Models trained on sparse, noisy core-level pathology offer little insight into their decisions and can fail under domain shift. Clinical use needs a traceable decision process and a flag for uncertain predictions.
  Methods: We introduce ProtoCouncil, in which three foundation encoders (MedSAM, DINOv3 and V-JEPA 2.1) learn case-based prototypes at three anatomical levels: whole gland, needle track and gland sectors. Prototypes are projected onto real training-core regions, and label-free fusion keeps every class logit as a bias plus an exact sum of prototype similarity contributions. Encoder disagreement triggers selective prediction.
  Results: In five-fold patient-level cross-validation (6,588 cores, 693 patients), ProtoCouncil reaches 82.9% AUROC for clinically significant PCa (csPCa, Grade Group $\geq$3), 4.3 points above the best previously published method. In zero-shot evaluation on two external cohorts, ProtoCouncil achieves higher all-cancer and csPCa AUROC than all previous methods. Nearest same-class exemplars match test-core tumor involvement better than random same-class cases. Externally, csPCa accuracy falls from 79% when encoders agree to 27% when two oppose the fused call. Abstaining on disagreement raises balanced accuracy by 7 and 15 points, while retaining 58% and 54% of the cores.
  Conclusion: ProtoCouncil grounds core-level predictions in exact similarity-based evidence from real, anatomically indexed training cases, which makes its reasoning interpretable. It achieves the highest csPCa AUROC across three cohorts and identifies unreliable predictions through encoder disagreement.

</details>


### [38] [UniRGBA: Unifying RGB and Alpha in Pretrained Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09069)
*Lei Xu,Zhuo Li,Yongyi Jin,Aoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniRGBA 提出统一视频 RGBA 生成框架，仅通过对预训练 RGB 扩散模型做最小改动（VAE 零初始化扩通道+DoRA），即可原生生成带 alpha 通道的透明视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型能生成逼真 RGB，但原生 alpha 通道视频生成仍是难题；现有方法依赖抠图式数据与非原生 RGBA 设计，专精真实物体场景，在空间非局部半透明的视觉特效内容上严重退化。

Method: 核心洞察是无需专用 alpha 编码器或复杂隐空间重构。UniRGBA-VAE 将因果视频 VAE 通过零初始化通道扩展扩为 4 通道，采用对称选择性解冻（5% 可训练参数）与以 KL 参考损失锚定的六项渐进式重建损失防止隐空间漂移，并配合先重建后引入轻量 GAN 的两阶段策略；随后对预训练 DiT 应用标准 DoRA，复用统一 4 通道隐变量，无需 alpha 专用结构改动。

Result: 在视觉特效 RGBA 基准上，UniRGBA 的 RGBA 重建显著优于 Wan-Alpha，同时保持 RGB 保真度；RGBA 文生视频任务优于 Wan-Alpha，RGB-only 指标持平或超过原始 Wan2.1 主干。

Conclusion: 原生 alpha 支持可在不损害底层 RGB 先验与架构通用性的前提下集成到现有视频扩散模型，验证了最小化扩展路径的可行性。

Abstract: Recent advances in video diffusion models have achieved remarkable photorealistic RGB synthesis, yet generating videos with a native alpha channel remains an open challenge. Existing approaches often rely on matting-style data and non-native RGBA designs, causing them to specialize in real-object scenes and to degrade severely on visual-effect content whose semi-transparency is spatially non-local and non-elliptical. We present UniRGBA, a unified framework whose key insight is that RGBA generation does not require a dedicated alpha encoder or complex latent-space restructuring---a minimal, targeted extension of the existing RGB pathway is sufficient. UniRGBA-VAE extends a causal video VAE to 4-channel RGBA via zero-initialized channel expansion, symmetric selective layer unfreezing (5% trainable), and a six-term progressive reconstruction loss anchored by a KL reference loss that prevents latent drift, followed by a two-stage schedule that introduces a lightweight GAN objective. We then apply standard DoRA to the pretrained DiT, reusing the unified 4-channel latent without any alpha-specific architectural changes. On a visual-effect RGBA benchmark, UniRGBA substantially outperforms Wan-Alpha on RGBA reconstruction while preserving RGB fidelity. For generation, UniRGBA outperforms Wan-Alpha on RGBA text-to-video tasks and matches or surpasses the original pretrained Wan2.1 backbone on RGB-only metrics, demonstrating that native alpha support can be integrated without compromising the underlying RGB prior or the model's architectural versatility.

</details>


### [39] [Emo-LiveSync: Real-Time Universal Lip Synchronization with Explicit Emotion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09074)
*Sangjoon Yu,Seungeun Lee,JoonHeyok Shin,Ji-Su Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于 LivePortrait 的高效通用唇形同步视频生成框架，通过从预训练 video-to-motion 模型蒸馏知识将音频对齐到隐式运动空间，实现实时推理与高保真效果，并支持风格化角色与情感表达控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GAN 方法高效但视觉保真度不足，扩散模型质量高但计算开销大；且多数方法仅适用于写实人脸，难以推广到风格化角色和非人类对象。作者希望兼顾质量与效率，并提升通用性。

Method: 以 warp-based 的 LivePortrait 肖像动画模型为基础，引入训练策略，通过从预训练 video-to-motion 模型蒸馏知识，将音频信号对齐到隐式运动空间；并设计 expressive control gate，实现对情感表情的显式操控。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在视觉质量与唇形同步准确度上均达到 SOTA，同时保证实时推理，并可自然泛化到多样风格化角色。

Conclusion: 该工作证明通过在隐式运动空间中做音频-运动对齐，可在不牺牲计算效率的前提下实现高质量、通用的唇形同步视频生成，并支持情感表达丰富化。

Abstract: Lip synchronization, the task of generating realistic lip movements synchronized with audio signals, is fundamental to applications such as film dubbing, virtual avatars, and digital content creation. Recent GAN-based methods offer computational efficiency but often lack visual fidelity, while diffusion-based approaches achieve high-quality results at the cost of substantial computational overhead. Moreover, most existing methods are limited to photo-realistic human faces, restricting their applicability to stylized characters and non-human entities. In this paper, we propose an efficient universal lip-sync video generation framework that achieves high-quality results without compromising computational efficiency. We build on LivePortrait, a warp-based portrait animation model, and introduce a training strategy that aligns audio signals to an implicit motion space by distilling knowledge from a pre-trained video-to-motion model. This enables real-time inference while maintaining high visual fidelity, and naturally generalizes to diverse stylistic characters beyond photo-realistic humans. Furthermore, we introduce an expressive control gate that enables explicit manipulation of emotional expressions for content enrichment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in both visual quality and lip-sync accuracy while ensuring real-time inference.

</details>


### [40] [GlobalPuzzle: Globally Informed Genetic Search for Eroded Jigsaw Puzzle Reassembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09077)
*Lunyan Wang,Chuanzhi Xu,Ziyuan Tao,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GlobalPuzzle 框架结合全局画布级评估、动作引导和遗传搜索，解决不规则侵蚀拼图重组难题，弥合"全局视觉直出"与"局部搜索"两类方法的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 GAP 基准上，侵蚀边界拼图极难，现有方法在 5×5 上完美组装率仅 0.3%。人类靠识别相邻件、评估当前组装、迭代执行加件/替换/整体移动等复杂操作来解谜。但现有方法存在根本局限：利用全局视觉信息的方法直接预测布局，难以执行此类迭代操作；基于搜索的方法虽能重排，却主要依赖成对边界兼容性，缺乏全局视觉信息来评估和引导搜索。

Method: 提出 GlobalPuzzle：将全局画布级评估、动作引导与遗传搜索相融合。把全局视觉信息引入候选组装的评估与优化，支持灵活的、全局感知的重建，从而消解仅凭侵蚀边界难以判断的模糊邻接关系。

Result: 摘要未给出定量实验结果，仅以 GAP 上 0.3% 完美率为对照基线来说明问题难度与新方法的动机。

Conclusion: 通过全局视觉评估与遗传搜索结合，GlobalPuzzle 弥补了全局预测类与局部搜索类方法的不足，有望提升侵蚀拼图重组的准确性与灵活性。

Abstract: Jigsaw puzzle reassembly remains a highly challenging task, particularly when piece boundaries are irregularly eroded to simulate damage to cultural artifacts. On the GAP benchmark, existing methods achieve a perfect assembly accuracy of only 0.3% on $5 \times 5$ puzzles, whereas humans can solve jigsaw puzzles with remarkably high accuracy. Humans typically approach puzzle solving by identifying potentially adjacent pieces, evaluating the current assembly, and iteratively performing complex operations such as adding pieces, replacing incorrectly placed pieces, and moving entire connected groups of pieces. However, existing methods face a fundamental limitation: approaches that leverage global visual information typically predict piece arrangements directly, making it difficult to explicitly perform such complex iterative operations, while search based methods can rearrange pieces but primarily rely on pairwise boundary compatibility, lacking global visual information to evaluate and guide the search process. To bridge this gap, we propose GlobalPuzzle, a jigsaw puzzle reassembly framework that integrates global canvas level evaluation, action guidance, and genetic search. By incorporating global visual information into the evaluation and optimization of candidate assemblies, GlobalPuzzle aims to resolve ambiguous adjacency relationships that are difficult to determine from irregularly eroded piece boundaries alone, enabling flexible, globally informed reconstruction.

</details>


### [41] [Training-Free Active Test-Time Adaptation for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09084)
*Jihwan Bang,Sumyeong Ahn,Hwanjun Song,Jae-Gil Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Active VLM-TTA（主动测试时适应）设定：VLM 可花费有限标注预算查询部分测试样本标签，但当前样本预测须在标签可用前给出，标签只能作为未来样本的延迟证据；并给出无需训练的即插即用模块 ACTOR，通过一致性-可靠性检查将不确定样本路由到基预测、标签查询或记忆纠正三条路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 测试时适应多在完全无监督下依赖伪标签、置信度或熵，对困难偏移样本相当于让模型"自证自修"，缺乏外部证据；而监督在线适应又假设标签可直接训练或纠正模型，过于理想。论文希望研究二者之间的中间部署现实。

Method: 形式化 Active VLM-TTA 的"仅未来可用"约束（预测先于标签使用，标注预算有限、有成本且延迟）。提出 ACTOR：训练免费的插件模块，基于已查询标签的记忆做一致性与可靠性检查，将不确定样本在基预测、标签查询、记忆纠正之间路由。

Result: 在十个跨域基准以及基于 ImageNet 的域迁移基准上，ACTOR 在 ResNet 与 ViT 骨干上一致提升强 VLM-TTA 基线，推断开销可忽略。

Conclusion: 在标签延迟且预算受限的实际部署条件下，保守复用稀疏标签是可行的；一个轻量、训练免费的路由模块即可稳定增益现有 VLM-TTA 方法，代码已开源。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) for vision-language models (VLMs) is usually studied in a fully unsupervised regime, where adaptation relies on pseudo-labels, confidence scores, or entropy computed by the model itself. This is a severe restriction on hard shifted samples: the model is asked to repair its own uncertain predictions without any external evidence. At the other extreme, supervised online adaptation assumes labels can directly train or correct the model. We study the intermediate deployment regime between these two extremes. In Active Test-time Adaptation for VLMs (Active VLM-TTA), a pretrained VLM may spend an explicit labeling budget on selected test samples, but the prediction for the current sample must be emitted before its label can be used; queried labels are therefore costed, delayed evidence for future samples rather than free correction for the present one. Under this future-only constraint, the central question is how to reuse sparse labels conservatively. We instantiate this setting with ACTive VLM-TTA for Online Retrieval (ACTOR), a training-free plug-in module that routes uncertain samples among base prediction, label query, and memory-based correction using a consistency-and-reliability check over previously queried labels. Across ten cross-domain benchmarks and a domain-transfer benchmark based on ImageNet, ACTOR consistently improves strong VLM-TTA baselines on ResNet and ViT backbones with negligible inference overhead. Our implementation is publicly available at https://github.com/kaist-dmlab/ACTOR.

</details>


### [42] [AdaAlloc: Adaptive Visual Token Allocation for Long-Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09092)
*Joungbin An,Kristen Grauman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AdaAlloc：一种免训练推理框架，将长视频问答视为视觉token分配问题，在固定预算下自适应地在全局概览与高分辨率局部证据之间分配视觉token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频问答迫使多模态大模型在严格视觉token预算下推理，形成"广泛时间覆盖"与"细粒度空间细节"的权衡。均匀采样覆盖广但易掩盖关键局部线索；字幕法或帧相似度检索基于有损代理，易漏掉运动、状态变化及前后关系。

Method: 将长视频推理重构为视觉token分配问题；提出AdaAlloc：先规划全局/局部token分配策略，再用时间定位找出候选证据片段并精炼为紧凑的时间记忆；策略同时控制帧采样密度与分辨率，将稀疏低分辨率的全局帧与更密集高分辨率的局部帧结合，最终由答案器基于全局上下文与精炼局部证据作答（免训练）。

Result: 在LVBench、MLVU、VideoMME和LongVideoBench上，AdaAlloc相较同等预算的均匀采样最多提升10.5个百分点，并在各自报告的基准上优于所对比的帧选择方法与agent类方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式的全局-局部token分配与时间定位相结合，可在不训练的前提下显著提升长视频问答效果，表明将预算分配作为核心设计维度是有效方向。

Abstract: Long-video question answering forces multimodal large language models to reason under strict visual-token budgets, creating a tradeoff between broad temporal coverage and fine-grained spatial detail. Uniform sampling preserves coverage but often obscures decisive local cues, while caption-based or frame-similarity retrieval operates over lossy proxies that can miss motion, state changes, and before-after relations. We recast long-video reasoning as a visual-token allocation problem: given a fixed budget, decide how much to spend on global context versus high-resolution local evidence, and where in the video to draw that local evidence from. We introduce AdaAlloc, a training-free inference framework that adaptively allocates visual tokens between a global video overview and localized visual detail. AdaAlloc first plans a global/local allocation policy, then uses temporal grounding to locate candidate evidence segments and refines them into a compact temporal memory. The selected policy controls both frame sampling density and resolution, combining sparse, lower-resolution global frames with denser, higher-resolution local frames drawn from the refined memory. The final answerer reasons over the allocated global context and refined local evidence. Across LVBench, MLVU, VideoMME, and LongVideoBench, AdaAlloc improves over budget-matched uniform sampling by up to 10.5 percentage points and outperforms the compared frame-selection and agentic methods on their reported benchmarks.

</details>


### [43] [Trajectory Forcing: Exploiting Diffusion Trajectories for Autoregressive Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09096)
*Bohan Wang,Shuo Chen,Yunzhi Li,Zhaozheng Chen,Junzhe Zhang,Hanwang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Trajectory Forcing (TraF)，利用前序视频块的去噪轨迹作为自回归上下文，提升视频扩散模型的生成长度与质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归扩散方法仅用前一个预测块作为上下文，丢弃了块生成过程中的中间预测（去噪历史）。而LLM等传统自回归模型以全部历史token为条件，二者存在差距。

Method: 将先前视频块的去噪轨迹作为上下文。两种训练范式：(1)有GT视频时，用高斯插值构造伪轨迹并由模型重新预测；(2)有教师模型时，从学生自身去噪 rollout 获取真实轨迹。第一种范式为第二种提供良好初始化。

Result: 5秒VBench基准Total 85.15，超过Self Forcing(+0.84)和Causal Forcing(+1.11)，质量与语义同时提升；30秒生成（6倍训练时长）在所有对比方法中整体质量最高。

Conclusion: 将完整去噪轨迹纳入自回归上下文是有效扩展视频扩散模型至长视频/流式生成的方向，且两级训练范式可协同提升效果。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) modeling is arguably the only way to extend a fixed-length video diffusion model to longer or even streaming videos. However, existing AR diffusion methods use only the previously predicted chunk as context for the next, discarding the intermediate predictions that constitute the chunk's generation history. In contrast, conventional AR models such as LLMs condition on the entire history of preceding tokens. To this end, we propose Trajectory Forcing (TraF), an AR diffusion method that exploits the denoising trajectories of previous video chunks as context. TraF operates under two training regimes: (1) with ground-truth videos, where pseudo-trajectories are constructed from Gaussian interpolations and re-predicted by the model, and (2) with a teacher model, where real trajectories are obtained from the student's own denoising rollout. The first regime naturally provides a strong initialization for the second. On the 5-second VBench benchmark, TraF achieves a Total score of 85.15, outperforming Self Forcing (+0.84) and Causal Forcing (+1.11), with Quality and Semantic improving simultaneously. On 30-second generation---6$\times$ the training horizon---TraF achieves the highest overall quality among all compared methods.

</details>


### [44] [BayesCrop: Real-Time Probabilistic Multi-Crop Video Reframing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09100)
*Cheng-Han Lee,Rajesh Sureddi,Neil Birkbeck,Yilin Wang,Balu Adsumilli,Alan Bovik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BayesCrop: 实时概率化多裁剪视频重构（reframing）框架，用双分量各向同性高斯混合建模裁剪偏好，配合锚帧预测+运动矢量传播与周期视角切换，112 FPS 实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 横屏内容适配到竖屏等异构显示格式的需求增长；但重构本身具有主观性，存在多个合理感兴趣区域，而现有方法只预测单一确定性裁剪，无法表达多解与不确定性。

Method: 用两分量空间各向同性高斯混合建模裁剪位置与尺度：每个分量给出一个代表性重构模式及其局部不确定性，支持双视角预测与额外候选采样；引入 anchor-and-propagate 策略，仅在稀疏锚帧做神经预测，其余帧用压缩码流中的运动矢量传播裁剪轨迹；多条轨迹通过轻量周期性视角切换合并为单一输出。

Result: 在 LIVE-YT-MC、LIVE-YT-VC++ 及多目标长视频基准上，单输出定位具竞争力，多裁剪参考覆盖率较高，假设多样且连贯，感知质量稳定；默认配置下达 112 FPS 实时吞吐；从学习到的分布采样可进一步提升参考覆盖率，优于仅用两个均值代表裁剪。

Conclusion: 以概率分布替代单一确定性裁剪是可行且高效的多裁剪视频重构范式；结合稀疏神经推理与码流运动矢量可实现实时部署，并提供可控的多样性与覆盖度。

Abstract: Automatic video reframing is increasingly important for adapting landscape content to heterogeneous display formats. Existing methods typically predict a single deterministic crop, despite the subjective nature of reframing and the presence of multiple plausible regions of interest. We present \textbf{BayesCrop}, a real-time probabilistic framework for multi-crop video reframing. BayesCrop represents crop preferences using a two-component spatially isotropic Gaussian mixture over crop position and scale. Each component defines a representative reframing mode together with its local uncertainty, enabling both dual-view prediction and sampling of additional plausible crop candidates. For efficient video processing, we introduce an anchor-and-propagate strategy that performs neural prediction only on sparse anchor frames and propagates crop trajectories using motion vectors available from the compressed video bitstream. The resulting trajectories can be combined into a single output using a lightweight periodic view-switching strategy. Experiments on LIVE-YT-MC, LIVE-YT-VC++, and a multi-target long-video benchmark demonstrate competitive single-output localization, high multi-crop reference coverage, diverse yet coherent crop hypotheses, and stable perceptual quality. BayesCrop achieves real-time throughput of 112 FPS at the default operating point, while sampling from the learned distributions further improves reference coverage beyond the two representative crop means.

</details>


### [45] [PathXAI: Evidence-guided structured pathology reporting by transferable tissue segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09117)
*Zhaorui Tan,Jun Aun Tan,Weixiang Zhong,Xiaoxi Wang,Tan Pan,Xingdong Xue,Li Liu,Jing Zhang,Jing Zhang,Weimiao Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PathXAI将分割得到的组织成分信息与H&E图像及可选领域知识结合，在冻结权重的前提下生成证据可追溯的结构化病理报告，在分割与报告生成指标上均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理报告需要把跨区域、跨切片的组织观察转化为具体诊断与形态学描述，但如何用显式的组织成分测量来指导H&E报告生成仍研究不足。

Method: 构建PathXAI：(1) 分割模块提取组织成分信息；(2) 将其与H&E图像、可选领域知识一同输入自托管预训练视觉语言模型，引导诊断推理、定向复查与结构化报告撰写；(3) 模型权重不更新。

Result: 分割模块在内部与外部测试的平均Dice超过基线；在1200例公开+151例私有病例上，与参考诊断及形态学描述的一致性高于仅用H&E的对照。公开队列中PathXAI-Report较Qwen+H&E的癌种匹配率高4.4个百分点，finding F1高5.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 显式组织成分信息可有效提升病理报告生成的诊断准确性与证据可追溯性，且在冻结预训练模型的情况下即可实现。

Abstract: Pathology reporting requires translating tissue observations across regions and slides into specific diagnoses and supporting morphological descriptions. However, the use of explicit tissue-composition measurements to guide H&E-based report generation remains underexplored. Thus, we developed PathXAI, which combines segmentation-derived tissue information with H&E images and optional domain knowledge to guide diagnostic reasoning, targeted reinspection and structured report writing. Its segmentation component exceeded baselines in mean Dice on internal and external tests. In automated report evaluation on 1,200 public and 151 private cases, PathXAI achieved higher agreement with reference diagnoses and documented morphology than the H&E-based comparators. On the public evaluation cohort, PathXAI-Report exceeded Qwen + H&E in cancer family match rate by 4.4 percentage points and in finding F1 by 5.7 percentage points. PathXAI generates evidence-linked reports with a self-hosted pretrained vision-language model, without updating its weights.

</details>


### [46] [Finite-sample Calibration Guarantees for Binary Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09120)
*Bruno Viti,Martin Holler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种具有形式化标定保证的二元分割后处理标定方法，通过考虑空间依赖的集中不等式推导出改进的理论界，实验验证其满足保证并优于现有后处理标定方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于机器学习的分割模型常存在标定不良问题，每像素预测的置信度不能准确反映真实概率，这在医学图像分割等高风险场景尤为关键；现有标定方法中仅少数能给出标定误差的理论保证（即高概率下低标定误差），因此需要能建立形式化保证的后处理标定方法。

Method: 提出一种事后（post-hoc）标定方法；从标准集中不等式出发，推导出显式考虑二元分割输出中固有空间依赖性的改进理论界，从而为该标定器建立正式标定保证。

Result: 实验表明所提出的标定器满足推导出的理论保证，并且优于现有的后处理标定方法。

Conclusion: 考虑空间依赖性的理论分析能提供更强的标定保证，所提后处理标定方法在理论保证与实证性能上均具优势。

Abstract: Machine learning based segmentation models are frequently miscalibrated, producing for each pixel a label prediction whose associated confidence does not accurately reflect the true underlying probability. This limitation is particularly critical in high-stakes applications such as medical image segmentation. Although several calibration methods have been proposed, only a small subset of them provides theoretical guarantees on the calibration error, i.e., they do not typically ensure that, with high probability, the model exhibits a low calibration error. In this work, we develop a post-hoc calibration method for which formal calibration guarantees can be established. To achieve this, we derive from standard concentration inequalities an improved theoretical bound that explicitly accounts for the spatial dependence inherent in binary segmentation outputs. We empirically demonstrate that the proposed calibrator satisfies the derived guarantee and outperforms existing post-hoc calibration approaches.

</details>


### [47] [CoMET: Tracking the Shared Pose Error of Roadside Messages for Asynchronous Vehicle-Infrastructure Cooperative 3D Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09121)
*Shuwei Sun,Jian Huang,Hui Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CoMET（共模误差跟踪），用卡尔曼滤波在时间上估计车路协同3D检测中转瞬的位姿平移误差，仅用决策时刻可得信息，在线纠正所有路侧框，提升异步V2X-Seq检测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路侧传感器能检测自车看不见的车辆，但位姿误差会整体平移同一条消息中的所有检测。论文研究仅用决策时刻信息（无真值、无未来帧、无逐对校准偏移）的异步车路协同3D检测，且发现即使零延迟也有38%的验证消息不存在可匹配的成对目标，因此需要跨时间估计该共同平移。

Method: CoMET在每个消息到达时匹配车辆与路侧检测，用互为最近邻的配对估计偏移，并对该发送方维护一个卡尔曼滤波器：有配对时更新，无配对时用滤波预测值前向保持。纠正施加于所有路侧框（包括自车未见目标）。估计器无学习参数，仅用一个冻结的检测头对融合结果排序。

Result: 在21条验证序列、0–400ms四种延迟下，CoMET平均BEV AP(IoU 0.7)为66.63，优于未纠正在线位姿的63.18和最佳中间融合基线62.96。在三种架构的五个检测器上，比在线位姿高2.2–3.5 AP，比仅用当前消息估计偏移高0.3–0.6。还发现基准中28%的验证消息用零偏移作占位，其余中45%的偏移搜索触到边界，影响仅路侧可见目标的标签。

Conclusion: 时序共同模误差估计是有效的在线位姿纠正手段；在两种替代标签参考下CoMET均优于未纠正在线位姿；用CoMET位姿和纠正标签重训中间融合在三架构上全部提升，其中两个显著。

Abstract: A roadside sensor can detect vehicles hidden from the ego vehicle, but a pose error shifts every detection in the same message. We study asynchronous vehicle-infrastructure cooperative 3D detection on V2X-Seq using only information available at decision time, without ground truth, future frames, or per-pair calibration offsets at inference. Objects seen by both sensors reveal the shared translation, but even at zero added delay, 38% of validation messages have no matched pair. We propose CoMET (Common-Mode Error Tracking), which estimates this translation over time. At each message arrival, CoMET matches vehicle and roadside detections, estimates the offset from mutually nearest pairs, and updates a Kalman filter for that sender. When no pair matches, the filter carries its estimate forward. The correction applies to all roadside boxes, including those unseen by the ego vehicle. The estimator has no learned parameters; a separate frozen head ranks the merged detections. Across four delays from 0 to 400 ms on 21 validation sequences, CoMET averages 66.63 bird's-eye-view AP at IoU 0.7, compared with 63.18 for the same late fusion using uncorrected online poses and 62.96 for the best intermediate-fusion baseline. Across five detectors from three architectures, CoMET gains 2.2 to 3.5 AP points over online poses and 0.3 to 0.6 over estimating the offset from the current message alone. We find that the benchmark uses a zero offset as a placeholder in 28% of validation messages and that its offset search reaches the boundary in 45% of the rest. These offsets affect the labels for objects seen only from the roadside. Under two alternative label references, CoMET improves on uncorrected online poses in every evaluated population. Retraining intermediate fusion with CoMET poses and corrected labels improves detection on all three architectures tested, with statistically significant gains on two.

</details>


### [48] [Look Closer, Align Better: Explicit Visual Attributes for Robust EEG-to-Image Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09123)
*Ke Xiao,Banghua Yang,Wenbin Guo,Fenqi Rong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AttrEcho 框架，用 EEG 相关的局部视觉属性增强预训练视觉特征，实现细粒度 EEG-to-image 检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 EEG-to-image 检索依赖预训练视觉编码器表示，但这些表示可能缺失区分语义相似图像所需的细粒度视觉细节。

Method: 先分析显式视觉属性的 EEG 可解码性及其超出预训练表示的互补预测价值；再提出 AttrEcho，包含 RAVF（通过可学习符号残差门将 EEG 相关局部视觉属性与空间对应预训练特征融合）和 FTA（学习各区域维度专属权重以聚合特征，用于与 EEG 表示做对比对齐）。

Result: THINGS-EEG 上：被试内 200 类检索 Top-1 94.8%、Top-5 99.7%；图库扩至 1600 张含同概念干扰项时 Top-1 保持 82.1%；跨被试检索 Top-1 25.3%、Top-5 53.0%。

Conclusion: EEG 相关视觉属性与空间对齐融合对细粒度 EEG-to-image 检索至关重要。

Abstract: EEG-to-image retrieval commonly aligns neural responses with representations from pretrained vision encoders. However, these representations may underrepresent fine-grained visual details needed to distinguish semantically similar images. To address this limitation, we first investigate the EEG decodability and complementary predictive value of explicit visual attributes, identifying visual cues that capture EEG-relevant information beyond pretrained representations. Building on these findings, we propose AttrEcho, a framework that enriches pretrained visual representations with complementary, spatially aligned visual attributes. Specifically, Region-Aligned Visual Attribute Fusion (RAVF) integrates EEG-relevant local visual attributes with spatially corresponding pretrained features through a learnable signed residual gate. We further employ Feature Token Aggregation (FTA), which learns dimension-specific weights across regions to aggregate refined visual features for contrastive alignment with EEG representations. Experiments on THINGS-EEG demonstrate that AttrEcho achieves 94.8% Top-1 and 99.7% Top-5 accuracy in intra-subject 200-way retrieval, while maintaining 82.1% Top-1 accuracy as the gallery expands to 1,600 images with same-concept distractors. AttrEcho also achieves 25.3% Top-1 and 53.0% Top-5 accuracy in inter-subject retrieval. These results highlight the importance of EEG-relevant visual attributes and spatially aligned fusion for fine-grained EEG-to-image retrieval.

</details>


### [49] [TouchAnnotator: Multi-Source Tactile Supervision with Privileged-View Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09129)
*Ao Li,Zhihao Yuan,Chubin Zhang,Kunrui Huang,Sheng Xu,Zengjue Chen,Junjie Wang,Jiawei Li,Yansong Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TouchAnnotator：仅用头戴第一视角视频即可生成密集触觉/接触标注的双臂标注器，通过多源触觉监督与特权视角蒸馏实现，可泛化到裸手、戴手套和机器人形态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一视角视频能大规模记录人-物交互，但很少包含隐藏接触与压力的触觉标签；而触觉数据采集昂贵、仅限特定手套/机器人形态。作者希望把第一视角视频转化为密集触觉标注，解决触觉标签稀缺与跨形态迁移问题。

Method: 1) 统一“规范手场”（canonical hand field），整合六数据集、三种形态的监督信号：手套压力、几何推导的人手接触、机器人指尖形变，并用源感知掩码保留有效目标。2) 训练时用EMA教师同时观察同步的第一视角与手腕视角，将手部局部表征与触觉预测蒸馏到仅用第一视角视频+估计姿态的学生模型。3) 引入紧凑的过去片段记忆提供时序上下文。

Result: 在EgoTouch、零样本FPHA迁移以及860段同步手套录制上评估；模型可迁移到裸手接触，并在裸手/手套/机器人三种形态下产生定性一致的触觉响应。在实测录制上，共享协议下时间精度提升5.38点，Volumetric IoU提升5.02点。

Conclusion: 异构的人-机器人监督结合特权训练视角，可将第一视角视频转化为密集触觉标注；推理阶段无需手腕相机或触觉硬件，具备跨形态泛化能力。

Abstract: Egocentric video captures human-object interaction at scale but rarely includes tactile labels for hidden contact and pressure. We introduce TouchAnnotator, an ego-only bimanual tactile annotator trained with multi-source tactile supervision and privileged-view distillation. A canonical hand field unifies glove pressure, geometry-derived human contact, and robot fingertip deformation from six datasets and three embodiments, while source-aware masks preserve valid targets. During training, an EMA teacher observes synchronized ego and wrist views and distills hand-local representations and tactile predictions into a student that uses only ego video and estimated pose at inference. Compact past-clip memory supplies temporal context. We evaluate on EgoTouch, zero-shot FPHA transfer, and 860 synchronized glove recordings. The model transfers to bare-hand contact and produces qualitatively coherent tactile responses across bare-hand, gloved-hand, and robot embodiments. On measured recordings, it improves temporal accuracy by 5.38 points and Volumetric IoU by 5.02 points under a shared protocol. These results show that heterogeneous human--robot supervision and privileged training views can turn egocentric video into dense tactile annotations.

</details>


### [50] [DINO-Nucleus: Multi-Level Self-Supervised Features for Nucleus Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09133)
*Mohammad Sarhangzadeh,Elyar Esmaeilzadeh,Doruk Oner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DINO-Nucleus：利用DINOv2多层自监督特征+LoRA参数高效微调，联合预测细胞核区域、实例流和中心点，实现细胞核实例分割与计数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量显微分析需要准确的细胞核实例分割与计数，但相互接触的细胞核难以分离，现有方法多依赖复杂模型或敏感的后处理步骤。

Method: 基于DINOv2多层Transformer自监督特征；联合预测细胞核区域、像素级实例流与显式中心点，并用一致性目标使预测流收敛至对应中心；通过轻量LoRA模块适配预训练骨干以降低微调成本。

Result: 在分割与计数任务上表现良好，且骨干适配参数高效。

Conclusion: 参数高效地适配多层自监督特征是显微图像细胞核实例分割的有效方案。

Abstract: Nucleus instance segmentation and counting are essential for quantitative microscopy, enabling reliable analysis of cell populations, tissue organization, and disease-related changes. However, accurately separating touching nuclei remains difficult, and many existing approaches rely on complex models or sensitive post-processing procedures. We present DINO-Nucleus, a parameter-efficient framework that leverages multi-level self-supervised DINOv2 representations for nucleus instance segmentation and counting. The model jointly predicts nucleus regions, pixel-wise instance flows, and explicit nucleus centers, while a consistency objective encourages the predicted flows to converge toward their corresponding centers. Multi-level transformer features provide complementary spatial and semantic information, and the pretrained backbone is adapted using lightweight LoRA modules to reduce fine-tuning cost. Experimental results show that DINO-Nucleus achieves strong segmentation and counting performance with parameter-efficient backbone adaptation. These findings demonstrate that parameter-efficient adaptation of multi-level self-supervised features can provide an effective solution for nucleus instance segmentation in microscopy images.

</details>


### [51] [FIND: Frequency Invariance Disentanglement for Test-Time Adaptation in LiDAR 3D Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09170)
*Dongxiao Li,Zixuan Hu,Ling-Yu Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FIND，一个在频域中解耦域不变内容与域特定干扰的测试时自适应（TTA）框架，用于LiDAR 3D目标检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR 3D检测在传感器配置和环境变化下存在域偏移，性能下降；现有TTA方法主要在空间域做自监督，但点云稀疏非欧几里得，易将语义内容与域风格混淆。

Method: 在频域中实现解耦。用B-Spline拟合构建局部自适应滤波器，嵌入双流架构：不变流（Invariant Stream）提取鲁棒特征引导伪标签生成；特定流（Specific Stream）对域扰动施加一致性约束。结合不变性驱动对齐与稳定性引导正则化。

Result: 在跨数据集自适应和鲁棒性损坏基准上大量实验，显著优于现有SOTA方法。

Conclusion: FIND通过频域解耦有效提取域不变特征并抑制域特定干扰，提升LiDAR 3D检测在域偏移下的鲁棒性，代码已开源。

Abstract: LiDAR-based 3D Object Detection (L3OD) is a fundamental 3D perception task, yet its performance is often compromised by domain shifts arising from diverse sensor configurations and environmental conditions. Existing Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) methods primarily leverage self-supervision in the spatial domain, yet they frequently confound semantic content with domain style due to the sparse, non-Euclidean nature of point clouds. To overcome this limitation, we propose FIND (Frequency INvariance Disentanglement), a novel TTA framework that precisely disentangles domain-invariant content from domain-specific interference within the frequency domain. Our methodology utilizes B-Spline fitting to construct locally adaptive filters integrated into a dual-stream architecture: an Invariant Stream extracts robust features to guide pseudo-labeling, while a Specific Stream enforces consistency against domain perturbations. By combining invariance-driven alignment with stability-guided regularization, our approach dynamically extracts robust domain-invariant features while suppressing domain-specific interference. Extensive experiments on cross-dataset adaptation and robust corruption benchmarks demonstrate that FIND significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/llldx03/FIND.

</details>


### [52] [Bootstrap Your Own AV-Proxies: Adaptive Contrastive and Prototype Learning for Audio-Visual Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09191)
*Junbo Zhang,Hang Su,Zhaofan Li,Hang Dong,Chao Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BYOAVP框架，用自监督音频增强(SSAE)对比学习和动态原型约束(DPC)解决音视语义鸿沟与融合噪声问题，在AVS基准上达SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音视频分割(AVS)面临单模态语义不完整、音视表示间存在语义鸿沟；传统融合解码难抑制融合噪声，而引入数据先验的方法增加建模复杂度、跨域泛化差。

Method: 设计自监督音频增强(SSAE)模块，用对比学习将音频表示与梯度阻断的视觉嵌入自适应对齐以缩小语义鸿沟；另设计动态原型约束(DPC)模块，通过动量式原型更新细化像素级类别感知，并借跨模态交互增强发声区域定位。

Result: 在2个AVS基准、6个子任务上取得SOTA性能，展现较强鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: BYOAVP以对比对齐+原型约束的简洁框架有效缓解融合噪声与语义鸿沟，兼顾性能与跨域泛化，为AVS提供新思路。

Abstract: Audio-Visual Segmentation (AVS) aims to accurately segment sounding objects in video frames by leveraging audio-visual correspondence cues. However, it remains challenging due to the intrinsic semantic incompleteness within a single modality and the semantic gap between audio and visual representations. Traditional feature-fusion decoding approaches struggle to suppress fusion noise effectively, while recent methods that incorporate data-dependent priors often increase the complexity of modeling audio-visual correlations, leading to poor cross-domain generalization. To address these issues, we propose a novel adaptive contrastive and prototype learning framework, BYOAVP, for AVS. Specifically, we design a Self-Supervised Audio Enhancement (SSAE) module that introduces contrastive learning to adaptively align audio representations with gradient-blocked visual embeddings, thus narrowing the semantic gap between modalities. Furthermore, a Dynamic Prototype Constraint (DPC) module is developed to refine pixel-wise category perception via momentum-based prototype updating, while enhancing localization of sounding regions through cross-modal interaction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves SOTA performance across two AVS benchmarks and six sub-tasks, exhibiting strong robustness and generalization ability.

</details>


### [53] [InstantHMR : Real-Time Human Mesh Recovery on Your Phone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09195)
*Mohamed Adjel,Kahina Chalabi,Vincent Bonnet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InstantHMR 是一组面向移动端推理的开源单图人体网格恢复回归器，基于 MHR 参数化模型、仅用公开标注训练，在 Galaxy S23 NPU 上 5.5 ms 恢复 6890 顶点网格，精度与最快方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动端实时 3D 人体网格恢复有机器人、生物力学、AR/VR 等大量应用，但现有 SOTA 要么在手机上太慢，要么为速度牺牲精度，缺少兼顾速度与精度的开源方案。

Method: 以 Momentum Human Rig (MHR) 参数化模型为基础构建单图回归器家族，仅使用 SAM 3D Body 数据集中的公开标注训练；并采用统一协议对 11 个 SOTA 方法（含 Meta 官方 MHR-to-SMPL 转换）复现评测，在 8 种目标平台（桌面 TensorRT 到两款手机 NPU）测延迟。

Result: 三星 Galaxy S23 NPU 上每人 5.5 ms 恢复 6890 顶点网格，NVIDIA RTX 4070 上 0.98 ms，速度超过所有对比方法；移动端比精度略优的唯一兼容方法快 12 倍；精度具竞争力：3DPW 40.0 mm PA-MPJPE、EMDB 48.8 mm、RICH 41.7 mm。

Conclusion: InstantHMR 实现了移动端精度与速度的良好折中，并开源代码与模型，同时提供统一协议的复现基准与多平台延迟数据，便于后续公平比较。

Abstract: Real-time 3D human mesh recovery from a single image on mobile devices is a challenging problem with many applications, including robotics, biomechanics, AR/VR, and exergames. Current state-of-the-art models are either too slow to run efficiently on mobile devices or sacrifice accuracy for speed. We present InstantHMR, a family of open-source single-image regressors suited for mobile inference, based on the Momentum Human Rig (MHR) parametric model and trained exclusively on publicly available annotations from the SAM 3D Body dataset. Our approach achieves remarkable performance on a Samsung Galaxy S23 NPU, recovering a 6,890-vertex body mesh in 5.5 ms per person, and 0.98 ms on an NVIDIA RTX 4070 GPU. This speed surpasses all existing methods we evaluated. On mobile, it is 12x faster than the single mobile-compatible method that slightly outperforms ours in accuracy, while achieving competitive accuracy with 40.0 mm PA-MPJPE on 3DPW, 48.8 mm on EMDB and 41.7 mm on RICH. To ensure fair comparisons, we re-evaluate 11 state-of-the-art methods under a unified protocol aligned with the closest MHR-based competitors, including Meta's official MHR-to-SMPL conversion, and report their reproduction results. Additionally, we measure latency across eight different targets, ranging from desktop TensorRT to two phone NPUs. Code and models are available at https://github.com/Naturalpad-studio/InstantHMR.

</details>


### [54] [HMMF-Net: A Lightweight Hierarchical Multi-Frame Multimodal Fusion Network for Accurate Fish Feeding Intensity Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09224)
*Phi-Hung Hoang,Van-Manh Tran,Nam-Thuan Trinh,Thi-Thu-Hong Phan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轻量级多模态HMMF-Net，融合视觉与声学信息进行鱼类摄食强度评估，准确率96.77%，可部署于边缘设备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动鱼类摄食强度评估(FFIA)对精准水产养殖与饲料成本优化很重要，但单模态方法易受声学噪声和视觉干扰影响；现有多模态框架依赖计算量大的3D卷积或Transformer，难以在边缘设备上运行。

Method: 视觉分支将时间上相隔的帧堆叠为多通道输入，送入适配的2D EfficientNetB0；声学分支用自适应多轴残差单元(AMARU)从log-mel谱中提取时间、频率和局部时频表示；通过分层双向信使跨模态融合(BMCF)，在中间表示层用紧凑信使token逐步交换两模态信息，降低密集跨模态注意力的计算开销。

Result: 在AV-FFIA数据集上五折交叉验证，测试折准确率96.77%、F1 96.88%，参数量3.987M，1.002 GFLOPs；相比最佳视觉配置(95.49%)误差降低28.38%。在Jetson Orin Nano上用TensorRT FP32部署保持精度，每2秒样本推理11.54ms，模型大小18.81MB。

Conclusion: HMMF-Net兼顾有效的多模态表示学习与计算效率，可在资源受限的边缘平台上实现准确、实用的鱼类摄食强度评估。

Abstract: Automated fish feeding intensity assessment (FFIA) is important for precision aquaculture and feed cost optimization. However, unimodal methods are vulnerable to acoustic noise and visual disturbances, while existing multimodal frameworks often rely on computationally intensive 3D convolutions or Transformers, limiting their use on edge devices. To address these limitations, we propose HMMF-Net, a lightweight multimodal architecture that combines early multi-frame visual encoding and multi-axis acoustic modeling through a hierarchical bidirectional messenger cross-modal fusion (BMCF) mechanism. The visual branch captures temporal variations in feeding behavior by stacking temporally distant frames as a multi-channel input to an adapted 2D EfficientNetB0 backbone. The acoustic branch employs adaptive multi-axis residual units (AMARU) to extract temporal, frequency, and local time-frequency representations from log-mel spectrograms. BMCF progressively exchanges information between the two modalities at intermediate representation levels through compact messenger tokens, reducing the computational overhead of dense cross-modal attention. Evaluated on the AV-FFIA dataset using five-fold cross-validation, HMMF-Net achieves 96.77% accuracy and 96.88% F1-score on the held-out test folds, with 3.987M parameters and 1.002 GFLOPs. Compared with the best-performing visual configuration (95.49% accuracy), HMMF-Net reduces the classification error by 28.38%. Deployment on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano using TensorRT with FP32 precision maintains comparable accuracy, with an inference time of 11.54 ms per 2-second sample and a model size of 18.81 MB. These results demonstrate that HMMF-Net combines effective multimodal representation learning with computational efficiency, enabling accurate and practical FFIA on resource-constrained edge platforms.

</details>


### [55] [T2I Models Rarely Invert Negation but Often Fail to Suppress Negated Concepts: Benchmarking Negation Suppression in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09304)
*Akansha Gautam,Aparna Bharati,Mayank Vatsa,Richa Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 T2I-NSI 基准与指标，量化文生图模型对显式否定概念（如"没有台灯的书桌"）的抑制能力，发现否定敏感性普遍很低，主要失败模式是"抑制不足"而非"反向生成"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文生图扩散模型常无法抑制被明确否定的概念，但缺乏系统化的基准和度量来量化这种"否定敏感性"。

Method: 构建 T2I-NSI 基准：每个否定提示配一个正面对应提示，按语言形式（词汇/语义、形态、句法）与目标类型（物体、属性、关系、组合、嵌套）分解否定；含 759,869 配对提示原子、5,082 物体目录、916,272 张图像；用 Basic CLIP 估计器和 Robust 集成（CLIP+VQA+开放词汇检测）评分。

Result: 否定敏感性普遍低：开源模型 NSI ≤ 0.038，GPT-Image-1.5 与 Gemini-2.5-Flash-Image 约 0.09；主导失败为抑制不足；反向解释几乎为零（45 万+评估中 0.00%）；8–19% 原子在两种提示下都渲染出概念，另有 9–25% 仅部分抑制。420 原子、9 名标注者的盲测人类研究印证该结论；匹配提示对比显示开源与闭源约 3.5 倍差距非提示生成伪影。

Conclusion: 现有模型对显式否定普遍不敏感，问题本质是"抑制不足"而非"语义反转"；开源与闭源存在约 3.5 倍差距，该基准与指标为否定理解研究提供可复用评测框架。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models often fail to suppress explicitly negated concepts, such as a lamp for "a desk without a lamp." We introduce T2I-NSI, a benchmark and metric for measuring negation sensitivity in text-to-image generation. It pairs each negated prompt with a canonical positive counterpart and factorizes negation by linguistic form (lexical/semantic, morphological, syntactic) and target type (objects, attributes, relations, compositions, and nested cases). The benchmark contains 759,869 paired prompt atoms over a 5,082-object catalog, with 916,272 images (911,982 open-weights, 4,290 closed-source), scored by a Basic CLIP estimator and a Robust ensemble of CLIP, VQA and open-vocabulary detection. Negation sensitivity is low: open-weights generators score NSI <= 0.038, while GPT-Image-1.5 and Gemini-2.5-Flash-Image reach about 0.09. The dominant failure is under-suppression rather than misdirection. Inverse interpretation is essentially absent (0.00% across over 450K atom-model evaluations), whereas models render the concept under both prompts on 8-19% of atoms and only partly suppress it on a further 9-25%; most remaining atoms are too weakly rendered to label. A blinded human study (420 atoms, nine annotators) corroborates the near-absence of inversion and the high persistence rate, and a matched-prompt comparison shows the open-versus-closed gap (~3.5x) is not a prompt-generator artifact. Code and data: https://github.com/IAB-IITJ/T2I-NSI

</details>


### [56] [Interpretable Context-Aware Multi-Label Retinal Classification with Dual-Hub Heterogeneous Graph Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09309)
*Proshanto Chanda,Munib Mesinovic,Tingting Zhu,Abhirup Banerjee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双枢纽异构图神经网络（GNN），通过跨模态消息传递耦合眼底空间特征与结构化临床协变量，实现多标签视网膜筛查的内在可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多标签视网膜筛查需同时捕捉共现病变与临床背景，但现有多模态融合方法不透明，缺乏可解释性。

Method: 构建双枢纽异构GNN，通过跨模态消息传递结合视觉与临床特征；采用空间偏置自注意力建模病灶级patch交互，并用模态无关的图注意力系数显式量化各视觉区域和临床风险因素对每个疾病节点的贡献，从而在正向聚合中直接暴露多级决策路径，而非依赖事后显著性近似。

Result: 在BRSET数据集（8,524名患者的16,266张照片）上，七种疾病Macro-AUPRC达76.1%，与纯图像（75.9%）及ResNet-50+MLP融合（75.9%）基线持平或更优；黄斑水肿（+3.5%）和糖尿病视网膜病变（+2.1%）提升最大。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持竞争力性能的同时提供固有可解释性，临床上下文对特定疾病有明显增益；但整体性能提升有限，需进一步验证泛化性。

Abstract: Multi-label retinal screening requires capturing co-occurring pathologies and clinical context, yet standard multimodal fusion remains opaque. We propose a dual-hub heterogeneous graph neural network (GNN) that couples spatial fundus features and structured clinical covariates through cross-modal message passing while providing inherent interpretability. Rather than relying on post-hoc saliency approximations, our architecture exposes multi-level decision pathways during forward aggregation: spatially biased self-attention captures lesion-level patch interactions, while modality-specific graph attention coefficients explicitly quantify the contribution of visual regions and clinical risk factors to each disease node. Evaluated on the BRSET dataset (16,266 photographs from 8,524 patients), the proposed model achieves 76.1% Macro-AUPRC across seven conditions, matching or exceeding image-only (75.9%) and ResNet-50+MLP fusion (75.9%) baselines, with largest contextual gains in macular edema (+3.5%) and diabetic retinopathy (+2.1%). Codebase is available at: https://github.com/proshanto-c/ca-mlc-refactor.

</details>


### [57] [Keyline Detection with YOLO: Runway Perception for Vision-Based Aircraft Landing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09316)
*Leander Fritsch,David Rügamer,Alexej Klushyn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出“物体关键线（keyline）检测”任务：为每个物体预测一组固定、语义有序的无限直线，用Hesse法向式参数化并扩展YOLO，提出OKLS作为评价指标、目标分配信号与回归损失，在跑道感知场景验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键点作为物体几何特征易受遮挡和截断影响；而物体边缘即使在部分遮挡或端点出视野时仍可观测，能提供互补信息，因此需要一种线级的关键点类比表示。

Method: 定义物体关键线检测：每条线与一个物体实例关联并由语义索引标识；将YOLO扩展为预测连续Hesse法向式[ρ, cosθ, sinθ]的直线参数；提出Object Keyline Similarity (OKLS)，同时用作评估指标、目标分配信号和回归损失；在基于视觉着陆的跑道感知中实例化。与四种线参数化对比。

Result: 在四种评估的线参数化中，该方法表示取得最高检测精度；关键线可作为额外输出通道直接集成进标准YOLO头，且不降低关键点性能、不增加明显延迟。

Conclusion: 关键线是关键点的有效线级补充，具备鲁棒性与易集成性，在跑道等含语义线性特征的场景中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Modern object detectors can predict keypoints as per-object geometric features, but keypoints are inherently vulnerable to occlusion and truncation. Object edges can provide complementary information, often remaining observable even when partially occluded or when their defining endpoints leave the field of view. We therefore introduce object keyline detection, the task of predicting a fixed, semantically ordered set of infinite lines per object instance. Unlike the outputs of general line-segment detectors, each keyline is associated with an object instance and identified by a semantic index, providing a line-level analog of keypoints. To solve this task, we extend YOLO with keyline prediction parameterized in continuous Hesse-normal form $[ρ,\cosθ,\sinθ]$, and introduce Object Keyline Similarity (OKLS), which serves as an evaluation metric, target assignment signal, and regression loss. We instantiate this formulation on runway perception in vision-based landing, a motivating example containing semantic linear features that remain partially observable throughout the approach. Experiments show that our representation achieves the highest detection accuracy among four evaluated line parameterizations, and keylines integrate directly into standard YOLO heads as additional output channels without degrading keypoint performance or adding meaningful latency.

</details>


### [58] [RegNC-Mamba3: Shared Structural Feature Learning Using Non-Causal State Space Principles for Multi-Modal Image Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09336)
*Zhikang Li,Yan Wu,Xin Hu,Yi Dai,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出非因果状态空间原理（NC-SSP）与RegNC-Mamba3多模态图像配准方法，通过消除扫描路径依赖并结合Log-Gabor核的频域调制，以粗到细框架实现更高精度、更低复杂度的跨模态配准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态图像配准的核心挑战在于以更高精度和更低计算复杂度提取共享结构特征。Mamba-3的SSP离散化更精确、截断误差更小且保持线性复杂度，但原始SSP为NLP设计，不适用于图像（尤其存在扫描路径依赖问题）。

Method: 1) 设计NC-SSP，通过相对权重归一化并对所有环形扫描路径聚合，构造无长程衰减的全局隐状态，消除积分计算对扫描路径的依赖，获得更大感受野和更高效率；2) 用可学习Log-Gabor核为每个注意力头施加不同方向的频域调制，构建复数序列用于隐状态计算与特征交互，增强跨模态共享结构提取；3) 基于以上构建粗到细配准方法RegNC-Mamba3。

Result: 在VIS-SAR和VIS-IR数据集上，RegNC-Mamba3优于所对比的基于检测器和半稠密的多模态配准方法。

Conclusion: 将SSM的状态空间原理适配到图像配准领域是有效的：非因果化与频域调制相结合可兼顾精度与效率，为多模态配准提供了新思路。

Abstract: The main challenge of multi-modal image registration is shared structural feature extraction with higher accuracy and lower computational complexity. Compared with a State Space Model (SSM), the State Space Principles (SSP) formulation of Mamba-3 uses a more accurate discretization with lower truncation error while maintaining linear complexity. However, vanilla SSP is designed for natural language processing (NLP) and is not directly suitable for images. To address this issue, we propose a novel non-causal SSP (NC-SSP) with shared structure awareness and develop a coarse-to-fine registration method named RegNC-Mamba3. First, NC-SSP is designed to eliminate the dependence of the integral computation on the scanning path. By combining relative-weight normalization with aggregation over all circular scan paths, NC-SSP constructs a globally hidden state without long-range decay, which provides a wider receptive field and higher efficiency than the original Mamba-3. Second, NC-SSP is modified to strengthen the extraction of cross-modal structural features. When computing hidden states and feature interactions, we construct complex-valued sequences in NC-SSP by applying learnable Log-Gabor kernels, with different orientations to each head for frequency-domain modulation. This improves the perception of shared structural information across modalities and increases both registration accuracy and quality. Experiments on VIS-SAR and VIS-IR demonstrate that RegNC-Mamba3 outperforms the compared detector-based and semi-dense multi-modal registration methods.

</details>


### [59] [HiMEM: Hippocampus-Inspired Memory Consolidation, Pruning, and Composition for Vision-Language-Action Models in 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09355)
*Boran Zhao,Yanbin Hu,Jun Ye,Zhenxian Hu,Yuxin Zhai,Jin Cui,Pengju Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiMEM 提出类海马体记忆巩固、情节记忆剪枝与灵活记忆组合三种机制，将大量情节记忆压缩为语义记忆并去除冗余，显著提升 VLA 模型在长程任务与跨场景泛化上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 记忆机制在复杂 3D 场景中存在严重记忆噪声：存储的情节记忆包含大量场景布局、物体配置和机器人状态的冗余细节，掩盖了可泛化的操作原则，阻碍跨任务迁移。

Method: 1) 受海马体启发的记忆巩固机制，将大量情节经验综合为紧凑的核心语义记忆，从中提炼可泛化原则；2) 情节记忆剪枝机制，删除已被语义记忆覆盖的冗余情节记忆，提高有效记忆容量；3) 灵活记忆组合机制，联合利用多条情节与语义记忆以增强跨场景泛化。

Result: 在 LIBERO 与 LIBERO-Plus 上验证有效，可适配不同 VLA 主干；在 LIBERO 上将 pi_0 的平均成功率从 91.88% 提升至 98.20%。

Conclusion: 通过模拟海马体的记忆巩固与剪枝，并辅以灵活的组合式记忆利用，HiMEM 有效抑制了复杂 3D 场景中的记忆噪声，兼顾长程任务执行与跨任务/跨场景泛化，且具备良好的主干通用性。

Abstract: Memory plays a crucial role in long-horizon task execution and cross-task generalization for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models operating in complex 3D scenes. However, existing VLA memory mechanisms suffer from memory noise, particularly in complex 3D scenes, as stored episodic memories contain extensive redundant details about scene layouts, object configurations, and robot-specific states, which obscure generalizable manipulation principles and hinder cross-task transfer. To address this challenge, we first introduce a hippocampus-inspired memory consolidation mechanism that synthesizes extensive episodic experiences into a compact set of essential semantic memories, enabling VLA models to derive generalizable principles from practical experience. Second, we propose an episodic memory pruning mechanism that removes redundant episodic memories whose contents are captured by consolidated semantic memories, thereby increasing effective memory capacity and improving long-horizon task execution. Finally, to handle complex task scenarios, we develop a flexible memory composition mechanism that jointly leverages multiple episodic and semantic memories to enhance cross-scenario generalization. Experiments on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus demonstrate the effectiveness of HiMEM across different VLA backbones, improving the average success rate of pi_0 from 91.88% to 98.20% on LIBERO.

</details>


### [60] [SPSC: Zero-Shot Cross-Subject EEG-to-Image Reconstruction via Semantic Preservation and Subspace Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09380)
*Yutang Lu,Shuai Jiang,Fei Su,Zhicheng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SPSC框架，通过对象掩码对比正则化(OMCR)与子空间候选门控(SCG)实现无标签零样本跨被试EEG图像重建的语义保持与子空间校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG信噪比低且存在跨被试语义漂移，零样本跨被试场景下源被试学到的EEG语义嵌入偏离目标被试图像嵌入流形，且目标无标签、无图像嵌入，重建极具挑战。

Method: 两阶段框架：训练阶段用对象掩码对比正则化(OMCR)，以对象级正样本约束条件空间，在重建先验与低层分支中保持对象级特征邻域；推理阶段构建子空间候选门控(SCG)，利用训练图像原型与目标无标签EEG的批统计量，在图像原型子空间内为每个任务选择候选，实现无标签语义校正。

Result: 在THINGS-EEG数据集十名被试的LOSO设置下，跨被试重建CLIP从0.552提升至0.713，Alex.5从0.612提升至0.745。

Conclusion: 语义校准后的条件能更好地支持无标签推理下目标被试的图像生成，显著提升零样本跨被试EEG重建性能。

Abstract: Pretrained vision-language models and diffusion generators have driven rapid progress in brain-to-image generation, while EEG-based reconstruction is still constrained by low signal-to-noise ratio and cross-subject semantic shifts. Zero-shot cross-subject reconstruction poses even greater challenges: EEG semantic embeddings learned from source subjects deviate from the manifold of image embeddings of the unseen target subject, particularly when neither target labels nor target image embeddings are available. To address this issue, we introduce the Semantic Preservation and Subspace Calibration (SPSC) framework, which first uses Object Mask Contrastive Regularization (OMCR) with object identity for backend training, and then constructs Subspace Candidate Gate (SCG) to achieve label-free semantic correction for target subjects during inference. Specifically, OMCR regularizes the conditioning space with object-level positive samples to preserve object-level feature neighborhoods within the reconstruction prior and low-level branch, while SCG exploits training image prototypes and batch statistics of unlabeled target EEG to select candidates for each task within subspaces of image prototypes. Under the leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) setting across ten subjects on the THINGS-EEG dataset, SPSC improves the cross-subject reconstruction from 0.552 to 0.713 on CLIP and from 0.612 to 0.745 on Alex.5. These significant gains indicate that the semantically calibrated conditions can better support generation for the target subjects under label-free inference.

</details>


### [61] [A Bridge from Paste to Place: Physics-Guided One-Step Image Composition via Object-Scene Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09387)
*Hongzhen Li,Youwei Pan,Yingfang Zhu,Sen Zhu,Fengjie Zhu,Leilei Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Paste2Place 是一个端到端、物理引导的 flow 模型，一步将粘贴输入桥接为真实图像合成结果，并配套构建大规模配对数据集与基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参考插入方法会改变物体姿态与外观，前景协调与阴影合成方法各只解决部分问题，缺少高效统一方案，也缺少大规模高质量数据集与基准。

Method: 提出物理引导的 flow-based 单步隐空间桥接模型，模型内显式进行 albedo-shading-residual 分解与重组；并用 Blender 渲染与验证流程生成带物理标注的配对数据，结合开源物体移除数据构造真实场景配对。

Result: 产出 86,273 对训练数据与 1,500 例基准；定性与定量实验显示在合成质量和推理速度上均有显著优势。

Conclusion: 工作明确了图像合成任务定义，给出有效统一方案，并填补了大规模高质量数据集与基准的空白。

Abstract: Realistic image composition must make a pasted object appear naturally present in a scene while preserving the fine details of both foreground and background. Reference-based insertion methods can change object pose and appearance, while foreground harmonization methods and shadow-specific synthesis methods each address only part of this task. An efficient, unified solution to this task remains an open challenge. We introduce Paste2Place, an end-to-end, physics-guided flow-based model that learns a direct, one-step latent bridge from pasted inputs to realistic composites, guided by explicit albedo-shading-residual decomposition and recomposition within the model. To support model training and evaluation, we develop a Blender rendering and validation pipeline to generate paired composites with physical annotations, and construct real-scene pairs from open-source object-removal data. This effort yields 86,273 training pairs and a 1,500-example benchmark. Extensive qualitative and quantitative experiments demonstrate that Paste2Place achieves substantial advantages in image composition quality and inference speed. Overall, our work provides a clear task definition and an effective solution for image composition, and fills a gap in large-scale, high-quality datasets and benchmarks for training and evaluating image composition.

</details>


### [62] [The Role Of Recurrent Refinement In Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09436)
*Joao D. S. Marques,David Calhas,Arlindo L. Oliveira*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出在分割主干上追加轻量循环精炼模块（CRF、现代Hopfield网络、卷积GRU），研究测试时迭代次数增加（含超出训练范围）的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈分割网络在大队列上表现良好，但在样本稀少且有成像伪影的受限临床场景中性能下降；人类视觉因皮层的循环与反馈连接而保持鲁棒，可随时间精炼视觉信息。

Method: 受视觉皮层循环/反馈机制启发，在现有分割主干后附加轻量循环精炼块，分别采用条件随机场（CRF）、现代Hopfield网络和卷积GRU三种实现；在测试阶段变化迭代次数（包括超过训练时的迭代次数）以观察其行为。

Result: 在Kvasir-SEG与ARCADE两个开源数据集上，新增参数不足1.5%，干净数据上提升最多1.4个百分点，噪声条件下提升最多17个百分点，并降低HD95。

Conclusion: 轻量循环精炼是提升分割网络在数据稀缺与噪声条件下鲁棒性的高效手段，且支持测试时迭代次数外推。

Abstract: While feed-forward segmentation networks perform well on large cohorts, they degrade in constrained clinical settings with few training samples and imaging artifacts. Human vision, by contrast, remains robust under these conditions, a property partly attributed to the recurrent and feedback connectivity of the visual cortex, which refines visual information over time. Inspired by this, we append lightweight recurrent refinement blocks to segmentation backbones, namely Conditional Random Fields (CRFs), modern Hopfield networks and convolutional GRUs, and study their behaviour as the number of iterations increases at test time (including beyond the training horizon). Evaluated on two open-source datasets, Kvasir- SEG and ARCADE, our recurrent blocks add less than 1.5% parameters and yield gains on clean data (up to 1.4 pp) and under noise (up to 17 pp) while reducing HD95. Code is available at https://github.com/joaodsmarques/recurrencehelpsegmentation .

</details>


### [63] [Overview of the ImageCLEFmedical 2026 GANs task: Exploring fingerprint detection, privacy leakage, and synthetic medical image generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09455)
*Alexandra-Georgiana Andrei,Mihai Gabriel Constantin,Mihai Dogariu,Dzmitry Karpenka,Yuri Prokopchuk,Ahmedkhan Radzhabov,Dan-Cristian Stanciu,Liviu-Daniel Ştefan,Vassili Kovalev,Henning Müller,Bogdan Ionescu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ImageCLEFmedical GANs 2026（第四届）设立三个子任务：检测训练GAN所用的真实图像、逆向识别生成合成图像的隐向量、生成兼顾保真与隐私的CT切片；共37队注册、10队提交结果，本文综述任务设置、数据、指标及参赛方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗数据用于训练生成模型存在隐私泄露与安全风险，需系统评测真实数据被"记住"的程度、隐空间映射的可逆性，以及合成图像在保真与隐私间的权衡。

Method: 提供真实与GAN合成肺部CT切片的基准数据集及真值标注；子任务1为成员推断/训练数据溯源，子任务2为隐向量匹配，子任务3为隐私保护CT生成；分别用Cohen's Kappa、匹配准确率、FID与隐私保护分数（PPS）组合评估。

Result: 37支队伍注册，10支队伍在若干子任务提交运行结果，6支队伍提交工作笔记；论文汇总各队方法与结果（摘要未给出具体数值）。

Conclusion: 该挑战赛持续为医疗合成图像的隐私与安全提供标准化评测平台，本届新增隐向量可逆性子任务并量化保真-隐私权衡。

Abstract: The 2026 ImageCLEFmedical GANs Task Exploring Fingerprint Detection, Privacy Leakage, and Synthetic Medical Image Generation continues to investigate privacy and security concerns around using patient data to generate synthetic medical images, now in its fourth edition. This edition introduces three complementary subtasks: the first extends prior editions by asking participants to detect which real images were used in training a Generative Adversarial Network to produce given synthetic outputs; the second is a novel subtask requiring participants to identify which latent vector produced each synthetic image, probing the faithfulness and reversibility of the latent-to-image mapping; the third asks participants to generate privacy-preserving CT slices, quantifying the trade-off between image fidelity and patient privacy. Ground-truth annotations and benchmark datasets of real and GAN-generated lung CT slices are provided for all subtasks. Evaluation is based on Cohen's Kappa for Subtask 1, matching accuracy for Subtask 2, and a combination of FID and Privacy Preservation Score (PPS) for Subtask 3. Overall, 37 teams registered for the task, with 10 teams submitting runs across subtasks and 6 teams submitting working notes papers. This paper presents an overview of the task setup, datasets, evaluation metrics, and summarises the approaches and results of participating teams.

</details>


### [64] [Left-Right-Consistent Iterative Stereo Matching via Cross-Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09458)
*Manuel Knott,Diogo Luvizon,Daniel Scharstein,Elias Vansteenkiste*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出跨注入（CI）机制，一种可即插即用的轻量扩展，让迭代式立体匹配模型同时输出左右视图视差并保持双向一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流立体匹配只预测左（参考）视图视差，但逐眼AR/VR渲染、新视角合成、双视图深度融合等应用需要左右两视图视差且二者一致。简单地对水平翻转并交换的输入再跑一次网络会得到两个独立预测，在透明表面、弱纹理区和遮挡边界处易不一致。

Method: 提出 cross-injection（CI）：在迭代式（基于细化）匹配器的精化阶段每一步，把显式带符号的左右不一致量作为额外输入线索注入，从而耦合两次预测过程。该方法轻量，可加到任意预训练的左视图迭代匹配器上。

Result: 在所有基准上显著降低左右不一致（FoundationStereo 在 Booster 上减半以上），同时基本保持物体边界锐利；在两个先进模型上也显示 CI 能降低单视图视差误差。

Conclusion: CI 是通用、轻量的扩展，即使在只需左视图视差的场景下也值得使用。

Abstract: State-of-the-art stereo matching models predominantly predict the disparity of only the left (reference) view. However, applications such as per-eye AR/VR rendering, novel view synthesis, or two-view depth fusion require disparity for both views, and need those two maps to agree. The usual workaround of running the network a second time on the horizontally flipped and swapped pair gives two independent predictions that can be inconsistent, especially on transparent surfaces, in textureless regions, and at occlusion boundaries. We introduce cross-injection (CI), a lightweight extension for iterative (refinement-based) models that can be added to any pretrained left-only iterative matcher. It couples the two passes by injecting the explicit, signed left-right inconsistency as an additional input cue at every step of the refinement stage. We empirically show that CI substantially reduces left-right disagreement on every benchmark (more than halving it on Booster for FoundationStereo), while largely preserving sharp object boundaries. Furthermore, using two state-of-the-art stereo matching models, we show that CI also reduces the single-view disparity error, making it a worthwhile extension even if only left disparity is needed.

</details>


### [65] [FusionCLIP: Multimodal Contrastive Pretraining for Fusion Plasma Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09493)
*Qiang Chen,Xiao Wang,Qingquan Yang,Hao Si,Zikang Yan,Guosheng Xu,Jin Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FusionCLIP多模态对比预训练框架，对齐可见光视频与多速率诊断信号，在EAST-MMD数据集三类任务上取得89.07%综合得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚变预训练方法主要通过重建或预测观测学习表征，未充分挖掘可见光视频与多元诊断信号之间的模态对应关系；同时等离子体区域仅占视频帧局部，且诊断变量采样率差异大，带来表征学习挑战。

Method: FusionCLIP框架包含两部分：Spatiotemporal Stability Selection筛除冗余、保留有信息量的视觉token；Temporal Query Encoder利用观测时间戳整合异步诊断信号；再通过对比学习对齐两种模态表征。

Result: 构建含1307次EAST放电的EAST-MMD数据集，在破裂预测、ELM分类、H98回归三个独立微调任务上综合得分89.07%，分别超过最佳通用预训练配置和去掉时空稳定性选择的域内对比预训练配置6.27和2.12个百分点；消融实验与注意力可视化验证了关键组件贡献。

Conclusion: 跨模态对比对齐视频与诊断信号是有效的聚变预训练范式，时空稳定性选择与时间戳感知编码器是性能提升的关键，数据集与结果验证了框架有效性。

Abstract: Visible-light videos and multivariate diagnostic signals provide complementary observations of tokamak discharges. However, existing fusion pretraining methods, primarily learn representations by reconstructing or predicting observations, leaving the correspondence between these modalities underexplored. Representation learning is further challenged by the fact that plasma-related regions occupy only part of each video frame and diagnostic variables have substantially different sampling rates. We propose FusionCLIP, a multimodal contrastive pretraining framework. Spatiotemporal Stability Selection retains informative visual tokens, while a Temporal Query Encoder integrates asynchronous diagnostic signals using their observation timestamps. Contrastive learning then aligns the two modality representations. We construct EAST Multimodal Diagnostics (EAST-MMD), comprising 1307 EAST discharges, and evaluate the model on three independently fine-tuned tasks: disruption prediction, edge-localized mode (ELM) classification, and $H_{98}$ regression. FusionCLIP achieves an aggregate score of 89.07\%, exceeding the best general-purpose pretraining configuration and an in-domain contrastive pretraining configuration without Spatiotemporal Stability Selection by 6.27 and 2.12 percentage points, respectively. Ablations and attention visualizations further support the contributions of its key components.

</details>


### [66] [BEiTScore++: Reference-free Image Captioning Evaluation with BEiT-3 and Conformalized Quantile Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09505)
*Gonçalo Gomes,Bruno Martins,Chrysoula Zerva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BEiTScore++，将BEiTScore图像描述评估指标扩展为不确定性感知版本，在单次前向传播中同时输出对齐分数与经共形校准的置信区间，在多基准上保持排序相关性，且区间宽度比MC dropout更贴合预测误差，并在图像退化与域外艺术图上表现出不确定性上升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实部署中评估指标面临输入歧义与域偏移，需要可靠的不确定性估计；现有BEiTScore只给分数，缺乏原则性的不确定性量化手段。

Method: 对BEiTScore进行不确定性感知扩展：建模人类评分的预测分布，单次前向即可完成打分与不确定性量化，并借助共形预测（conformal prediction）校准得到置信区间；与Monte Carlo dropout对比。

Result: 在多个含人工评分的数据集上，BEiTScore++保持与BEiTScore相当的排序相关性；在大多数基准上，其区间宽度比MC dropout更紧密地跟随预测误差；图像退化与域外艺术图上不确定性显著增大。

Conclusion: BEiTScore++可作为不确定性感知的图像-文本对齐评估实用工具，兼具准确打分与有原则的不确定性量化，且对退化与域偏移敏感。

Abstract: Reliable uncertainty estimation is important for deploying evaluation metrics in real-world settings, where both input ambiguity and domain shifts are common. In this work, we propose an uncertainty-aware extension of the BEiTScore image captioning metric, producing conformally calibrated confidence intervals alongside alignment scores. Our approach models predictive distributions over human judgments, enabling both accurate scoring and principled uncertainty quantification in a single forward pass. We evaluate our BEiTScore++ metric across multiple benchmarks with human-annotated ratings, showing that it preserves the ranking correlation of BEiTScore, while producing intervals whose width tracks the prediction error more closely than Monte Carlo dropout on most benchmarks. The estimated uncertainty also grows under image degradation and on out-of-domain artwork images, making BEiTScore++ a practical tool for uncertainty-aware evaluation of image-caption alignment.

</details>


### [67] [V-STORM: A Structure-Aware Grounded Chain of Thought Dataset for Faithful and Fine-Grained Spatio-Temporal Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09511)
*Dian Jiao,Yuqian Yuan,Jiaqi Zhu,Mingjian Gao,Siwen Tan,Yunqi Cao,Jinhui Li,Sijing Li,Zhenxuan Fan,Haoyang Wang,Wenqiao Zhang,Siliang Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 V-STORM 框架，将视频转为时空场景图并派生可验证的 Grounded Chain of Thought，构建260K训练集与3K人工校验基准，揭示MLLM"证据定位易、证据组合难"的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM 能识别视频中发生了什么，但细粒度时空推理仍很弱：难以精确定位证据出现的"何时何地"，也难以将证据组合成连贯推理链。核心障碍是缺乏可扩展的数据构建与评测流程，无法提供将推理步骤与显式时空证据关联的细粒度可验证监督。

Method: V-STORM 是一个结构感知框架：将视频转换为时空场景图（STSG），并基于经校验的证据路径派生出 Grounded Chain of Thought（GCoT），使每个推理步骤都可追溯到结构化、可验证的时空证据。在此框架下构建了 260K 样本训练集和 3K 人工验证基准，均带 GCoT 标注。

Result: 实验显示证据定位与证据组合之间存在明显分离：现有 MLLM 往往能识别相关时空线索，却无法将其整合为正确的多步推理——这一瓶颈被"只看答案"的评测大幅掩盖。此外，用 V-STORM 微调可显著提升答案准确率和 grounding 忠实度。

Conclusion: V-STORM 提供了可扩展的自监督数据构建与评测 pipeline，验证了结构化证据监督对忠实、有据可依的视频推理的有效性。数据集、基准和源码将公开。

Abstract: While MLLMs can often identify what happens in a video, they still struggle with fine-grained spatio-temporal reasoning, which requires precise grounding when and where the supporting evidence appears and composing such evidence into a coherent reasoning chain. This challenge is exacerbated by the lack of scalable data construction and evaluation pipelines that provide granular, verifiable supervision linking reasoning steps to explicit spatio-temporal evidence.To address this gap, we present V-STORM, a structure-aware framework that transforms videos into Spatio-Temporal Scene Graphs (STSGs) and derives Grounded Chain of Thought (GCoT) from validated evidence paths, making each reasoning step traceable to structured and verifiable spatio-temporal evidence.Under this framework, we construct a 260K-sample training set and a 3K-sample human-verified benchmark, both featuring GCoT annotations that link reasoning to explicit spatio-temporal evidence.Experiments reveal a clear separation between evidence localization and evidence composition: current MLLMs can often identify relevant spatio-temporal cues, yet still fail to integrate them into correct multi-step reasoning, exposing a bottleneck that answer-only evaluation largely obscures. Moreover, fine-tuning with V-STORM substantially improves both answer accuracy and grounding faithfulness, validating its effectiveness as supervision for faithful and evidence-grounded video reasoning. Our dataset, benchmark, and source code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [68] [Embedding Predictive Adaptation and Refinement for Multi-Modal Industrial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09521)
*Jaeho Cho,Jinsik Bang,Taehwan Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EPAR方法，通过嵌入预测自适应与噪声抑制网络改进基于记忆库的多模态工业异常检测，提升特征判别力和定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于记忆库的RGB-3D多模态IAD方法存在两问题：(i)冻结预训练编码器因域差距导致正常/异常特征区分不足，现有对比学习自适应难以捕捉精确定位所需的空间关系；(ii)由块级距离分数插值得到的像素级异常图粗糙，缺乏细粒度细节。

Method: 1) 通过预测掩码区域嵌入来适应编码器以捕捉空间关系，并结合块级方差-不变性-协方差(VIC)正则化，解决域差距造成的特征判别性差问题；2) 提出噪声抑制网络，学习每个块特征与最近正常原型间的关系差异而非异常外观，从而细化粗糙异常图，该关系信号对未见真实缺陷仍有效。

Result: 在少样本设置下表现优异，MVTec 3D-AD和Eyecandies数据集上AUPRO@1%分别提升+1.8%和+1.9%。

Conclusion: EPAR通过嵌入预测自适应与关系型噪声抑制精细化，有效解决多模态工业异常检测中的特征判别与定位精度问题，具备强实用性与少样本适应能力。

Abstract: Industrial Anomaly Detection (IAD) has evolved from 2D image-based approaches to multi-modal methods that leverage the complementary nature of RGB images and 3D point clouds. Among these, memory bank-based methods, which identify anomalies by the discrepancy between each query patch feature and its nearest normal prototype, have proven particularly effective. However, two limitations remain: (i) employing frozen pretrained encoders yields insufficient discrimination between normal and anomalous features due to the domain gap, while existing contrastive learning-based adaptation struggles to capture the spatial relationships required for precise localization; and (ii) pixel-level anomaly maps interpolated from patch-level distance scores remain coarse, lacking fine-grained detail. To address these limitations, we propose EPAR (Embedding Predictive Adaptation and Refinement). We adapt encoders by predicting the embeddings of masked regions to capture spatial relationships, together with patch-level Variance-Invariance-Covariance (VIC) regularization, resolving the poor discriminability between normal and anomalous features caused by the domain gap. We further propose a Noise Suppression Network that refines coarse maps by learning the relational discrepancy between each patch feature and its nearest normal prototype, rather than the anomaly's appearance. This relational signal remains valid for unseen real defects, despite being learned from synthetic anomalies. EPAR demonstrates strong practical applicability, excelling in few-shot settings and improving AUPRO@1% on MVTec 3D-AD and Eyecandies by +1.8% and +1.9%, respectively.

</details>


### [69] [RadarDINO: Distilling a Visual Foundation Model into mmWave Radar for Human Mesh Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09524)
*Furkan Mert Algan,Zhifan Ni,Eckehard Steinbach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RadarDINO：用DINOv3视觉语义蒸馏加显式几何监督，从毫米波雷达重建人体网格，在M4Human全部三个划分上超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波雷达做人体网格重建（HMR）兼顾隐私与光照鲁棒性，但高保真仍困难：稀疏点云法信息丢失、漏检；原始雷达张量法（如RT-Mesh）缺乏细粒度语义与显式局部人体建模。

Method: 将DINOv3教师模型的视觉语义经标定的RGB-雷达几何迁移到雷达体素，提供体素级与身体部位级监督，得到部位感知的雷达表示；并加入顶点损失与关节损失直接约束重建几何。

Result: 在M4Human数据集上，方法在全部三个评估划分上均优于SOTA基线。

Conclusion: 视觉语义蒸馏与显式几何监督相结合可有效提升雷达人体网格重建保真度，为隐私友好的HMR提供可行路径。

Abstract: Millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar offers a privacy-preserving and illumination-robust alternative for human mesh reconstruction (HMR). However, high-fidelity radar HMR remains challenging. Sparse point cloud-based methods suffer from information loss and missed detections, while raw radar tensor methods like RT-Mesh lack fine-grained semantics and explicit local body modeling. In this paper, we propose RadarDINO, a radar HMR framework that distills visual semantics from a DINOv3 teacher into 3D radar representations and introduces explicit geometric supervision. DINOv3 descriptors are transferred to radar voxels via calibrated RGB-radar geometry, providing voxel- and body-part-level supervision for part-aware radar representations. We further incorporate vertex- and joint-losses to directly constrain reconstructed geometry. On the M4Human dataset, our method outperforms the SOTA baseline across all three evaluation splits.

</details>


### [70] [ExQuery: Explicit Structured Query Priors for Temporal 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09527)
*Honglin Sun,Yifei Zhang,Junyang Zhang,Kenji Hashimoto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ExQuery 提出一种显式结构化查询框架，将3D检测查询拆分为共享语义切片与中心、尺寸、朝向、速度等物理状态切片，并在时序重建中复用该结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏查询式3D检测器把语义、几何与运动信息混在统一隐式查询中，即使使用显式3D锚点也如此，缺乏可解释性与结构化利用。

Method: 将解码查询组织为共享语义上下文切片和各物理状态专属切片，从先验编码到分支回归保持一致；重建时序查询时，用自车运动变换预测的物理状态、用分类得分形成软语义先验，再通过门控仿射调制与存储的隐式实例特征融合。

Result: 在 nuScenes 基准上取得有竞争力的检测性能，消融实验验证了各模块的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式结构化查询可有效提升时序3D检测中对语义与物理状态信息的利用，是一种可行且有效的方法。

Abstract: Sparse query-based 3D detectors commonly encode heterogeneous semantic, geometric, and motion information into unified latent queries, including when explicit 3D anchors are used. We introduce ExQuery, an explicit structured query framework for temporal 3D object detection. Each decoded query is organized into a shared semantic context slice and physical-state-specific slices for center, dimensions, orientation, and velocity. This structure is preserved from prior encoding to branch-specific regression and is reused to reconstruct temporal queries: predicted physical states are transformed by ego motion, classification scores form soft semantic priors, and the reconstructed query is fused with the stored latent instance feature through gated affine modulation. Experiments on the nuScenes benchmark show that our method achieves competitive detection performance, and ablation studies validate the effectiveness of our proposed modules.

</details>


### [71] [Multimodal Training with Knowledge Traction for Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09536)
*Xiao Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种引入外部知识（文本提示+大语言模型先验）的弱监督视频异常检测方法，结合字典学习压缩先验、LLM保证相似帧文本描述一致性，并用跨模态注意力融合特征，性能优于现有SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有弱监督视频异常检测方法主要依赖帧特征幅值判断异常，缺乏外部知识，鲁棒性与泛化性有限；同时图像描述模型对相似帧生成的文本描述存在不一致问题。

Method: 1) 利用文本提示与LLM构建任务特定先验信息，并用字典学习对其归一化与压缩；2) 借助LLM与辅助信息保证同一视频内相似帧文本描述的一致性；3) 提出融合先验知识与跨模态注意力的特征处理方式，学习多模态间关系以构建更准确可靠的特征表示。

Result: 实验结果表明该方法优于其他state-of-the-art方法。

Conclusion: 引入外部知识与跨模态注意力可提升弱监督视频异常检测的性能、鲁棒性与泛化能力，是一种有效的改进思路。

Abstract: The task of weakly supervised video anomaly detection is challenging. Existing approaches mainly model video frame features and determine whether anomalies are present based on their magnitudes. In this work, we propose a novel method that incorporates external knowledge into training to improve model performance, robustness, and generalization. We leverage textual prompts and a large language model (LLM) to establish prior information for the specific task, and employ dictionary learning to normalize and compress this information. Moreover, during experiments, we observed inconsistencies in the textual descriptions extracted from similar video frames by image caption models. To address this, we devise a method that leverages an LLM and auxiliary information to ensure consistency in the textual descriptions of similar frames within a video. Finally, we introduce a feature processing approach that incorporates prior knowledge and cross-modal attention mechanisms. This approach learns the relationships and characteristics among different modalities to construct more accurate and reliable feature representations, thereby improving the accuracy and stability of downstream tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that our method surpasses other state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [72] [Video Stitching from Multiple Moving Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09570)
*Shira Ifergane,Roy Amoyal,Shahar Benishay,Oren Freifeld*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MViS：轻量测试时优化的多视角视频对齐框架，联合处理多相机短时窗口帧并预测逐视角逐时刻单应矩阵，两阶段优化保证多视角与时序一致性，支持任意/时变视角交集图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图像拼接或视频稳像的几何视频对齐方法受限于两相机，或必须假设相机线性固定顺序，难以适用于多台自由移动相机拍摄同一场景的实际情形。

Method: 提出MViS框架，使用受空间变换器启发的网络联合处理所有视角在短时间窗内的帧，预测逐视角、逐时间的单应矩阵；优化分两阶段：先几何引导对齐，再基于外观细化，两阶段均施加多视角一致与时序一致约束；无需场景特定超参调优。

Result: 在两视角视频标准基准上达到或超过当前最优；并发布了一个包含两台以上自由移动相机的带标注数据集，建立了该复杂设定下的首个基准。

Conclusion: MViS能处理任意且时变的视角交集图，在快速相机运动下保持稳健，为多自由移动相机的视频对齐提供了轻量且无需调参的解决方案与新基准。

Abstract: We address geometric video alignment for the same scene captured by multiple freely moving cameras. Existing methods, which are based on image stitching or video stabilization, are typically limited to two cameras or must assume the restrictive setting of a fixed linear ordering of the cameras. We propose MViS, a lightweight test-time optimization framework for joint multi-view alignment with temporal consistency. MViS uses a spatial-transformer-inspired net to jointly process frames from all views within a short temporal window and predict per-view, per-time homographies. Optimization proceeds in two stages: geometry-guided alignment followed by appearance-based refinement, with both stages enforcing multi-view and temporal consistency across the sequence. The method requires no scene-specific hyperparameter tuning, handles arbitrary and time-varying view-intersection graphs, and remains robust under rapid camera motion. On a standard two-view video benchmark, MViS matches or surpasses the state of the art. To facilitate evaluation in more complex scenarios, we introduce an annotated dataset with more than two freely moving cameras and establish the first benchmark for this setting. Project page: https://bgu-cs-vil.github.io/MViS-project/ .

</details>


### [73] [MSR-ABC: Complementary Feature Fusion for Clear Cell Renal Cell Carcinoma Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09583)
*Yuan Liang,Sourav Bhattacharjee,Abraham Campbell*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MSR-ABC全局-局部框架，融合MedVAE、MedSigLIP与影像组学（radiomics），在ccRCC分型上取得内部0.806、外部0.879的平均AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT上对透明细胞肾细胞癌（ccRCC）分型，需要能刻画肿瘤不同表型的表征；但简单拼接重建特征、图文嵌入与影像组学描述子，难以充分挖掘其互补信息。

Method: 构建全局-局部的MSR-ABC框架：空间匹配的局部图像描述子经跨表征注意力交互；影像组学辅助监督促使池化局部表征保留定量属性；门控残差机制将局部信息与全局摘要整合；辅以区域感知池化以及视觉与软文本提示。

Result: 在396例KiTS23患者级划分与111例TCGA/TCIA外部队列上，三次训练种子平均AUROC内部0.806（95%患者自助法CI）、外部0.879；内部较全局MedVAE-影像组学与三源拼接分别高3.35与3.61个百分点。去除任一来源两队列均下降（去影像组学降幅最大）；单独去除跨表征注意力、辅助监督或门控融合亦降低内部AUROC。

Conclusion: 结果与集成架构中互补性贡献相一致，支持进一步研究结构化表征融合用于无创肾肿瘤表征。

Abstract: Clear cell renal cell carcinoma (ccRCC) classification from computed tomography (CT) may benefit from representations that capture different aspects of tumour phenotype. However, simply concatenating reconstruction-derived features, image--text embeddings, and radiomic descriptors may not fully exploit their complementary information. We propose MSR-ABC, a global--local framework integrating MedVAE, MedSigLIP, and radiomics. Spatially matched local image descriptors interact through cross-representation attention, while radiomic auxiliary supervision encourages the pooled local representation to retain quantitative tumour attributes. A gated residual mechanism integrates local information with global summaries. Area-aware pooling and visual and soft-text prompting complete the framework. We evaluate discrimination using a patient-level split of 396 KiTS23 cases and an external cohort of 111 TCGA/TCIA cases, reporting mean AUROC over three training seeds with 95% patient-bootstrap confidence intervals. MSR-ABC achieves mean AUROCs of 0.806 internally and 0.879 externally. Internally, it exceeds global MedVAE--radiomics and three-source concatenation by 3.35 and 3.61 AUROC percentage points, respectively. Removing any source lowers mean AUROC in both cohorts, with the largest decreases following radiomics removal. Individual removal of cross-representation attention, auxiliary supervision, or gated fusion also lowers internal mean AUROC. These findings are consistent with complementary contributions within the integrated architecture and support further investigation of structured representation fusion for non-invasive renal tumour characterisation.

</details>


### [74] [Dual-Region Slice Aggregation for CT-Based Renal Tumor Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09599)
*Yuan Liang,Sourav Bhattacharjee,Abraham Campbell*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种区域感知架构用于CT图像上透明细胞肾细胞癌(ccRCC)分类，联合肿瘤与瘤周0–10mm上下文，结合冻结DINOv3特征、位置感知切片聚合、固定PCA与影像组学门控融合，内部/外部AUC达0.8078/0.9094。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ccRCC是成人最常见的肾癌，需要可靠的CT分型。现有方法多仅用肿瘤区域特征，忽略瘤周上下文，且冻结视觉特征如何适配肾脏CT（区域读出与切片聚合）尚不清楚。

Method: 在共享冻结DINOv3特征图上分别池化肿瘤区域与0–10mm瘤周上下文；共享位置感知切片聚合器后接仅训练阶段拟合的固定PCA；所得表示通过两个标量特征门控与影像组学特征融合。以匹配的单肿瘤读出、早期PCA及重构作为对照消融。

Result: KiTS23内部测试AUC 0.8078±0.0154，111例外部队列AUC 0.9094±0.0015；较匹配的单肿瘤读出平均AUC分别提升1.50与2.34个百分点。早期PCA和重构对照内部平均AUC更低，而外部AUC在早期与晚期PCA间相近。

Conclusion: 区域读出（肿瘤+瘤周）与切片聚合共同作用，是将冻结视觉特征适配于肾脏CT的关键；该设计在内部与外部评估中均优于单肿瘤读出基线。
