Although this pattern was marked by low inter-annotator agreement (Krippendorff's $α= 0.070$), our annotation-quality checks suggested genuine evaluative subjectivity, plausibly reflecting distinct annotator decision schemes rather than annotation failure. In contrast, the LLM-as-a-Judge showed a strong pro-reasoning bias (98.3% of pairs vs. 55.2% of human votes), consistent across its five configurations and partly tracking sentence length. These findings suggest that, at the frontier, reasoning-related quality differences are small enough to be dominated by reader subjectivity, and that LLM-as-a-Judge evaluators can obscure this subjectivity in nuanced creative-generation tasks.

</details>


### [110] [The Invisible Gap: Romanized Indic Generation Fails Where Automatic Metrics Succeed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09085)
*Madhu Sudhan Rao Beesetty,Surbhi Sharma,Devesh Maheshwari,Lokesh Meesala*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 现有多语言NLP基准缺失"罗马化印地语/泰卢固语/卡纳达语"生成评测；BGE-M3、BERTScore等自动指标系统性误判其质量。作者用4个LLM、142条OpenAssistant对做诊断，发现GPT-5.5与Claude在人工12分制上差1.43分，而BGE-M3均值仅差0.004；并揭示Gemini脚本泄漏、Qwen退化为纯英文两类失败模式，提出"失败感知"评测策略与四要素评估组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数亿印度用户以拉丁字母罗马化方式（如"aap kaise hain"）与数字系统交互，但罗马化印度语生成完全缺席NLP基准与评测框架，缺乏可靠的评估手段。

Method: 在142条OpenAssistant查询-回复对上评测4个LLM（Claude Opus 4.8、GPT-5.5、Gemini 2.5-flash、Qwen3.5-35B）；对72条回复的诊断子集进行母语者12分制人工评分，并与BGE-M3、BERTScore等自动指标对比；统计脚本泄漏、英文坍缩等失败模式；提出对空输出、脚本污染和指令坍缩判零的失败感知评测策略。

Result: 人工评分中GPT-5.5比Claude Opus 4.8高1.43分（12分制），而全数据集BGE-M3均值仅相差0.004，证明自动指标与人类判断严重脱节。发现模型特异失败模式：Gemini在16.9%的泰卢固语回复中出现原生脚本泄漏，Qwen在16.2%的卡纳达语回复中坍缩为纯英文输出。

Conclusion: 评测罗马化印度语生成需要协同的评估组合：语义相似度、脚本合规性、失败覆盖率与母语者可接受度；单靠语义自动指标无法发现脚本污染与指令坍缩等关键失败。

Abstract: Hundreds of millions of Indians communicate with digital systems in romanized Latin characters -- typing "aap kaise hain" rather than native script -- yet romanized Indic generation is absent from NLP benchmarks and evaluation frameworks. We demonstrate that automatic metrics (BGE-M3, BERTScore) systematically misrepresent the quality of romanized Hindi, Telugu, and Kannada generation. We evaluate four LLMs (Claude Opus 4.8, GPT-5.5, Gemini 2.5-flash, Qwen3.5-35B) on 142 OpenAssistant query-response pairs. On a 72-response diagnostic subset, native speakers rate GPT-5.5 1.43 points higher than Claude Opus 4.8 on a 12-point rubric, whereas their full-dataset BGE-M3 means differ by only 0.004. Two distinct model-specific failure modes emerge: Gemini produces script spillage (16.9% of Telugu responses leak native script), while Qwen collapses to English-only output (16.2% of Kannada responses). We introduce a failure-aware evaluation policy that assigns zero to empty outputs, script-corrupted responses, and instruction collapses -- revealing failures metrics alone cannot detect. Our results show that evaluating romanized Indic generation requires a coordinated bundle: semantic similarity, script compliance, failure coverage, and native-speaker acceptability.

</details>


### [111] [MARS: Training Multilingual Search Agents for Retriever-Free Evidence Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09090)
*Rui Qi,Yunlong Liang,Yufeng Chen,Jinan Xu,Fandong Meng,Kaiyu Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出MARS，一个无需检索器的多语言搜索智能体，通过强化学习在直接语料交互（DCI）范式中直接用shell命令检索原始语料，并用语料原生实体锚定和冗余感知奖励解决跨语言检索错误与训练坍缩问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多语言RAG依赖可学习检索器和基于相似度的排序，易受跨语言检索错误和索引开销影响；转向retriever-free的DCI范式后，瓶颈变为多语言搜索动作生成：精确搜索对实体的表层形式敏感，且仅结果级监督对多轮搜索指导不足。

Method: 提出MARS多语言搜索智能体：1）corpus-native entity grounding，从语料证据中恢复可靠的词汇锚点，缓解检索失败；2）redundancy-aware search reward，鼓励简洁有效的搜索轨迹，解决多语言RL训练中的训练坍缩。

Result: 实验显示MARS持续优于强检索式基线和DCI基线，具备稳健的跨语言泛化能力，并显著减少冗余的语料交互。

Conclusion: MARS证明retriever-free的多语言DCI搜索结合RL可行且有效，实体锚定与冗余感知奖励是其成功关键。

Abstract: Existing multilingual retrieval-augmented generation relies heavily on learned retrievers and similarity-based ranking from multilingual collections, which are vulnerable to cross-lingual retrieval errors and indexing overhead. In this paper, we explore retriever-free multilingual search, where the model directly interacts with raw corpora through executable shell commands. This paradigm shifts the bottleneck to multilingual search action generation, where exact search is sensitive to entity surface forms and outcome-only supervision provides limited guidance for multi-turn search. To address these challenges, we propose MARS, a multilingual search agent in direct corpus interaction (DCI) with reinforcement learning (RL). MARS introduces corpus-native entity grounding to recover reliable lexical anchors from corpus evidence, mitigating failure to retrieve evidence. Meanwhile, we present a redundancy-aware search reward to encourage concise and effective search trajectories, addressing training collapse in RL of multilingual scenarios. Experiments show that \ours~consistently outperforms strong retrieval-based and DCI baselines, while demonstrating robust cross-lingual generalization and substantially reducing redundant corpus interactions.

</details>


### [112] [AlienBench: A Benchmark of Structural Novelty in Language Model Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09094)
*Jonas Oppenlaender,Johanna M. Silvennoinen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AlienBench 将认知心理学的"结构化想象任务"移植到语言模型上，用有明确地球默认参照的"外星生物特征偏离度"来量化创造力，评测 10 个模型，开源权重模型反而更"新颖"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有创造力评测让人类专家或 LLM 评委直接打"新颖性"分，但缺乏"何谓新颖（即偏离熟悉样本）"的固定参照系，评分不可比、易漂移。需要一个具有已知"熟悉默认值"的任务来度量偏离。

Method: 借鉴认知心理学的 structured imagination task：让模型描述外星生物，三位 LLM 评委从描述中抽取 10 个生物学特征并标注为"地球典型"或"偏离"，偏离特征数即为 0–10 分。每模型在 6 种提示条件（任务框架与星球环境不同）下各生成 30 条描述，共 180 条，取均值作为总分。评测 10 个模型（前沿到开源权重）。

Result: 总分区间 3.28（GPT-6 Luna）至 6.55（Ministral 3 14B）；开源权重模型平均得分高于闭源模型（5.40 vs. 4.58）。在基线提示下，模型在全部特征上的偏离频率均高于 100 名人类被试。

Conclusion: 基于"相对地球默认特征的偏离度"可构建有固定参照、可复现的创造力基准；结果表明模型比人类更"不落俗套"，且开源权重模型新颖度更高，同时全部提示、生成、评委输出与分数均已开源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for creative tasks such as writing, design, and idea generation. For these uses, it matters whether a model's ideas depart from familiar examples or stay close to them. Novelty is one standard measure of creativity, and current creativity evaluations ask human experts or LLM judges to rate how novel an output is. However, these ratings have no fixed reference for what counts as novel (or, in other terms, departure from the familiar). Measuring this departure requires a task with known familiar defaults. The structured imagination task from cognitive psychology is such a task. In this creativity test, people imagine a creature from another planet. The departure from features of Earth animals, such as bilateral symmetry and legs as mode of locomotion, is used as measure of creativity. We introduce AlienBench, a benchmark that adapts the structured imagination task to language models. Models describe alien creatures, and a panel of three LLM judges extracts ten biological features from the description and labels them as either Earth-typical or a departure. The sum of feature departures is the score of a description, from 0 to 10. Each model describes alien creatures under six prompt conditions that vary the framing of the task and the planetary environment. Each model generates 30 descriptions per condition (180 in total), and its overall score is the mean across these descriptions. We evaluate 10 language models, from frontier to open-weight models. Overall scores range from 3.28 (GPT-6 Luna) to 6.55 (Ministral 3 14B). Interestingly, open-weight models score higher than proprietary models in this task (5.40 vs. 4.58). Under the baseline prompt, the models depart from Earth defaults more often on average than 100 human participants on every feature. We release all prompts, generations, judge outputs, and scores.

</details>


### [113] [PhoPunct: A Large-Scale Benchmark of Transformer Architectures for Vietnamese Punctuation Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09107)
*Khoa D. Nguyen,Thai Bao Huynh,Binh T. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在22.1M token的越南语标点恢复基准上，固定训练预算比较17种架构，发现Bi-LSTM/CRF层并不能可靠提升macro F1，架构收益需与调参收益区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越南语标点恢复评测基准较小，难以判断架构选择随数据规模如何伸缩；且架构改进常与架构专属调参混杂，结论不可靠。

Method: 构建News与Novels语料（19.9M + 2.2M token，共22.1M标注token）；由4个预训练Transformer骨干搭配可选Bi-LSTM与CRF层构成17种架构，外加PhoBERT-large-Bi-LSTM-CRF模型PhoPunct；所有模型统一训练8个epoch、相同学习率、不做架构专属调参。

Result: 8组同域同骨干对照中有7组纯Transformer分类器达到或超过其Bi-LSTM-CRF版本；PhoPunct取得最佳News结果61.35；Novels最佳为XLM-R+CRF的50.18，超过PhoPunct的48.97。

Conclusion: 循环层与结构化预测头的价值依赖其优化设置，在固定预算下不必然带来增益；应明确区分架构本身改进与架构专属调参带来的改进。

Abstract: Vietnamese punctuation restoration is often evaluated on relatively small benchmarks, limiting conclusions about how architectural choices scale with data volume. We present a controlled benchmark on News and Novels corpora containing 22.1 million labeled tokens (19.9M News and 2.2M Novels). We evaluate 17 architectures formed from four pretrained Transformer backbones and optional bidirectional long short-term memory (Bi-LSTM) and conditional random field (CRF) layers, together with a PhoBERT-large-Bi-LSTM-CRF model, PhoPunct. Every model is trained for eight epochs with the same learning rate and without architecture-specific tuning. Under this fixed budget, adding Bi-LSTM and CRF layers does not reliably improve macro F1: in seven of eight matched domain-backbone comparisons, a plain Transformer classifier matches or outperforms its Bi-LSTM-CRF counterpart. PhoPunct obtains the best News result (61.35), whereas XLM-R with a CRF obtains the best Novels result (50.18), exceeding PhoPunct (48.97). These results show that the value of recurrent and structured-prediction heads depends on their optimization regime and motivate explicit separation between architectural improvements and improvements due to architecture-specific tuning. Our source code is available at: https://github.com/aisia-research-lab/PhoPunct

</details>


### [114] [Calibrated concept profiles: making the reading of a model's neurons a measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09196)
*Piotr Wilam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文把"直接读取神经元"从一种主观解读变成一种可量化测量：用声明的刺激总体生成两次提示电池，得到以神经元自身波动为单位的"校准概念画像"，并为每个数字提供噪声单位与随机水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性研究已从直接读神经元转向读取训练在其上的第二模型（探针、稀疏自编码器、crosscoder）。这种转向解决了混合语义、无单位阈值、跨研究不可迁移等真实问题，但牺牲了直接读取的独特优势：无需中间训练、能直接命名神经元、读数可重复可比。作者希望恢复这些优势同时保留测量的严谨性。

Method: 将神经元激活视为在刺激总体上的分布，并声明该总体：目标构造周边的每个因素按参考语料的出现率采样并记录。据此生成提示电池并抽取两次；计算校准概念画像（每个神经元一个数），以神经元在普通输入上的自身波动为单位，两次抽样给出噪声单位。读数前用一组固定检查在模型上验证电池，并测量画像在训练检查点间能分辨的最小变化。在 OpenCoder-1.5B 的 Python 任务上实现，并跟踪覆盖两万亿训练 token 的多个检查点。

Result: 该方法报告了替代方案所不具备的内容：每个数字的单位、每次比较的随机水平、以及每个检查点上承载某构造的具体神经元。实验跨两万亿 token 的检查点验证了画像的可分辨变化。

Conclusion: 直接读取神经元可以被确立为一种有单位、有噪声基准、可重复可比较的测量方法；提示电池、代码与全部结果数据均已公开。

Abstract: Interpretability has largely moved from reading a model's neurons directly to reading a second model trained on the first: a probe, a sparse autoencoder, a crosscoder. The move answered real faults -- mixed meanings, thresholds without a unit, readings that did not transfer from one study to the next -- but gave up what a direct reading alone provides: nothing trained in between, the neurons themselves named, and a reading that can be repeated and compared. Here the direct reading is made a measurement.
  A neuron's activation varies with the prompt, so its reading is a distribution over a population of stimuli. We declare that population: every factor around a target construct is drawn at its rate in a reference corpus and logged.
  From the declared distribution we generate a battery of prompts, and draw it twice. From the battery we compute the calibrated concept profile, one number per neuron, stated in units of the neuron's own spread on ordinary input; the two draws give every number a noise unit. Before the profile is read, a fixed set of checks licenses the battery on the model, and the smallest change the profile can resolve across training checkpoints is measured.
  Realised for Python on OpenCoder-1.5B and followed across checkpoints spanning two trillion training tokens, the profile gives what the alternatives do not report: a unit on every number, a chance level for every comparison, and which neurons carry a construct at every checkpoint.
  The battery, the code and the data behind every result are released.

</details>


### [115] [Representational drift in a code model: behaviour settles, which neurons carry it does not](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09204)
*Piotr Wilam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在OpenCoder-1.5B预训练过程中测量"表征漂移"的类比现象：对169个Python语法构造成分，功能（loss）在训练极早期即达下限，而承载该功能的神经元集合始终在变化，从不固定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经科学中"表征漂移"指行为不变而承载表征的神经元变化。作者希望在AI模型预训练中检验类似现象，并事先固定假设——承载某构造成分的神经元集合会随时间趋于稳定（settle）——以检验其是否成立。

Method: 在2万亿token、恒定学习率下预训练OpenCoder-1.5B，检查点间隔细至1000步；对169个Python构造成分逐一测量：每处变化均配噪声单元；通过在4B、84B、2T token处移除"承载神经元"并与随机移除对比，衡量其重要性；用注入实验（突发性settling或渐进性settling）检验能否以99%检出率识别集合稳定化。

Result: 16个（语法决定下一token的）构造成分，其loss在训练前4%内即达下限，但模型对承载神经元的依赖持续增长至训练结束（移除代价比随机移除分别多0.06、0.25、0.40 nats）。全部169个构造成分的承载集合在最后检查点仍在变化；若在84B–1.5T间突发稳定、或500B前渐进稳定，将以99%概率被检出，故稳定化确实不存在。84B时测得的集合在训练结束时移除，与随机集合不可区分。微观上呈"持续池+变化样本"：离开集合的神经元回归概率是匹配神经元的3倍，且无集合重新聚合。

Conclusion: 预先固定的"承载者会稳定"假设被驳斥：功能在早期固定，而实现该功能的神经元集合始终流动（"功能由当前神经元承载"）。作者讨论了候选原因，并提出可区分这些原因的关键实验。

Abstract: In neuroscience, representational drift is a change in which neurons carry a representation while behaviour stays the same; we measure its analogue in OpenCoder-1.5B during pretraining, for 169 Python constructs over two trillion tokens at a constant learning rate, with a noise unit on every change, at checkpoints as close as 1,000 training steps. For 16 of the 169, those whose grammar fixes a next token, the loss at those positions reaches its floor within the first 4% of training, while the model's reliance on the neurons carrying them grows to the end: removing them costs 0.06, 0.25 and 0.40 nats more than removing a random set, at 4 billion, 84 billion and 2 trillion tokens. Which neurons carry a construct never settles: for all 169 the carrier set is still changing at the last checkpoint, and an abrupt settling at any date from 84 billion to 1.5 trillion tokens, or a gradual one before 500 billion, would have been detected in 99% of injected cases. The set measured at 84 billion tokens, removed at the end, cannot be separated from a random set, so the function is carried by whichever neurons are current. Up close the change is a persisting pool and a changing sample: a neuron that leaves a construct's set returns at three times the rate of a matched neuron, and no set reassembles. The hypothesis we fixed in advance, that the carriers settle, is refuted; we discuss candidate causes and name the experiment that would separate them.

</details>


### [116] [Khmer PFGen-Bench: Building a Judge-Free Benchmark for Open-Ended Generation in a Low-Resource Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09222)
*Mengsay Loem,Kentaro Imajo,Ryota Ozaki,Masanori Hirano*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Khmer PFGen-Bench，首个针对高棉语（低资源语言）开放式多句生成的无评判模型（judge-free）基准，基于关键词规则与 n-gram 统计的打分器，并研究了如何构造有效且多样的参考回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式生成基准常用 LLM 作为评判者，但其在低资源语言上的可靠性存疑；因此希望构建不依赖 LLM 评判的基准。

Method: 将日语 PFGen-Bench 的打分器迁移至高棉语：用关键词规则与 LLM 生成参考的 n-gram 统计打分。由于该打分要求参考既有效又多样，作者实验分析参考构造方式，发现后训练模型参考更有效但多样性更低，混合不同模型家族可在相同参考数量下提升多样性；最终采用九个模型家族的后训练模型参考。

Result: 基准得到的模型排名在不同参考集合间保持稳定，且与母语者评分一致。

Conclusion: 为其他低资源语言构建无评判基准提供了可操作指导：关注参考的有效性与多样性的权衡，并通过混合模型家族提升多样性。

Abstract: Benchmarks for open-ended generation often rely on large language model (LLM) judges, whose reliability in low-resource languages is uncertain. We introduce Khmer PFGen-Bench, to our knowledge the first judge-free benchmark for multi-sentence answers to questions composed directly in Khmer, a low-resource language. Khmer PFGen-Bench adapts the scorer of PFGen-Bench, a benchmark for Japanese that scores responses with keyword rules and n-gram statistics from LLM-generated references. This scoring requires references that are both valid and varied, which LLMs may not produce in Khmer, so we examine how to build such references. We find that post-trained models can give more valid but less varied references than their base variants, and that mixing model families increases variety for the same number of references. Using references from post-trained models in nine families, the benchmark yields model rankings that are stable across reference sets and consistent with a native speaker's ratings. These findings offer guidance for building judge-free benchmarks in other low-resource languages.

</details>


### [117] [KLEAR: Knowledge-Matched LLM Evaluation in Korean across Application Requirements via Controlled Benchmark Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09223)
*Seoyeon Kim,Jeonghyun Kang,Hamin Koo,Barom Kang,Heondeuk Lee,Jaehyung Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 KLEAR 框架，通过构造“知识匹配对比集”（保持所需知识不变、改变应用难度）来区分大模型答错是由于缺乏知识还是应用知识失败，并据此揭示出即使提供知识仍存在的应用能力缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有韩语 LLM 基准的准确率无法区分错误来源：是缺少知识，还是未能正确应用知识。且现有基准中大多数题目仅靠事实回忆即可作答，而需要更多应用的题目往往知识本身也不同，难以隔离“应用能力”。

Method: KLEAR 在构造时先识别每个源问题背后的知识，形成显式“锚定知识包”，以此引导生成需要更复杂应用的新问题，从而保持知识不变而改变应用要求；生成候选题后经过自动与人工验证，并基于评估反馈迭代修订。

Result: 实验表明，所得知识匹配题集基本满足预期约束；在此基础上的受控评测显示，随着应用要求提高，模型出现即使已提供所需知识也仍然持续存在的失败。

Conclusion: KLEAR 提供了可分离知识与应用的受控韩语评测手段，证明应用能力是独立于知识掌握的瓶颈，为诊断 LLM 错误来源提供了新范式。

Abstract: Existing Korean benchmarks for Large Language Models (LLMs) evaluate performance across diverse knowledge domains, but their accuracy scores cannot distinguish whether incorrect responses arise from a lack of required knowledge or a failure to apply that knowledge correctly. Our analysis of existing Korean benchmarks reveals that most questions can be answered through factual recall alone, while those demanding greater application also differ in required knowledge, making it difficult to isolate application ability. To address this, we introduce KLEAR, a framework that constructs knowledge-matched contrasts by varying application demand while preserving the required knowledge. During construction, KLEAR identifies the knowledge underlying each source question as an explicit anchor bundle to guide generation of questions requiring more complex application. Generated candidates then undergo automated and human validation, with iterative revision based on evaluation feedback. Experiments demonstrate that the resulting knowledge-matched sets largely satisfy the intended constraints, enabling controlled evaluation across diverse LLMs. These evaluations reveal that increasing application demand exposes failures that persist even when the required knowledge is provided.

</details>


### [118] [DUET: Dual-Sparse Inference for Block Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09239)
*Siyuan Yan,Shen Gu,Xiaoxing Ma,Chun Cao,Jingwei Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Duet，一个免训练的双稀疏推理框架，通过前缀 KV 稀疏和早期查询稀疏加速扩散语言模型推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLM）推理开销大有两方面：一是随生成推进，对不断增长的历史前缀做注意力越来越昂贵；二是在每个去噪步中，当前生成块的所有位置都要完整经过 Transformer，而实际只有少数位置被确定。

Method: 利用每个生成块内历史注意力的集中性和跨步稳定性，构建可跨去噪步复用的紧凑历史 KV 子集（前缀 KV 稀疏）；并利用浅层表示预测需要更新的位置，只将这些查询传播到后续 Transformer 层，避免对不可能更新的位置做深层计算（早期查询稀疏）。整个方法无需训练。

Result: 在 32K 上下文长度下，LLaDA2.1-mini 上获得 1.34×/2.83× 的端到端/每步加速，SDAR-8B-Chat-b32 上为 2.41×/4.72×，同时保持有竞争力的准确率。

Conclusion: Duet 通过双稀疏策略有效降低 DLM 推理成本，在保持精度的同时实现显著加速。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) enable parallel token generation through iterative denoising, yet their inference remains costly due to repeated attention over a growing historical prefix and repeated processing of the entire current generation block. Historical attention becomes increasingly expensive as generation progresses, while all current-block positions are propagated through the Transformer at every denoising step even though only a small fraction are typically resolved at that step. In this paper, we introduce Duet, a training-free dual-sparse inference framework that addresses these challenges through prefix Key-Value (KV) sparsity and early query sparsity, respectively. Specifically, Duet exploits the concentration and cross-step stability of historical attention within each generation block to construct a compact historical KV subset that is reused across denoising steps. For query sparsity, Duet predicts update positions from shallow representations and propagates only the corresponding queries through the suffix Transformer layers, avoiding deep computation at positions unlikely to be updated in the current step. Experiments show that Duet maintains competitive accuracy while achieving 1.34$\times$/2.83$\times$ end-to-end/per-step speedups on LLaDA2.1-mini and 2.41$\times$/4.72$\times$ on SDAR-8B-Chat-b32 at a 32K context length.

</details>


### [119] [Multilingual Artificial Datasets for AI-Driven Cardiology: the CliTe and ParaCliTe corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09296)
*Lorenzo Pratesi,Eulalia Farre-Maduell,Machteld Boonstra,Michele Miranda,Jonas Mack,Eduard Rodenas-Alesina,Jonathan Kossoff,Corinne Allaart,Sébastien Bratières,Manuela-Daniela Danu,Isabella Fumarulo,Milou Gamage,Antonio Iaconelli,Pavel Leinveber,Felix Lindberg,Pavel Macháč,Vladimíra Macíková,Gianluigi Savarese,Raffaele Scorza,Bram van Es,Emma Weiss,Andrea Guala,Martin Krallinger,the DataTools4Heart NLP group*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出人工合成语料库的构建指南，发布了多语言心脏病学语料库CliTe、平行语料ParaCliTe及其自动翻译版本ParaCliTe Silver，并证明其可用于评估和改进心脏病学NLP模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏病学NLP发展需要可比、多语言且经过验证的语料，但受隐私限制此类资源极为稀缺，因此作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 依据公开的语料构建指南，来自7家欧洲医院的心脏科医生以捷克语、荷兰语、英语、意大利语、罗马尼亚语、西班牙语和瑞典语人工撰写虚构病历（保证临床多样性与性别平衡），形成CliTe；其中一个子集经多阶段人工翻译成其余6种语言，构成句级对齐的平行语料ParaCliTe；其余报告采用自动翻译，形成ParaCliTe Silver。

Result: CliTe包含699份人工心脏病学报告，以门诊记录（340份）、出院报告（263份）和住院记录（89份）为主，覆盖缺血性心脏病（199例）、心力衰竭（188例）和瓣膜病（79例）等。ParaCliTe含94位虚构患者的报告，7种语言句级对齐，共658份文档，以出院报告（322份）、门诊（196份）和住院（140份）记录为主，涉及心力衰竭（399份）、缺血性心脏病（287份）和瓣膜病（238份）。实证结果显示ParaCliTe具有实用性，同时揭示现有SOTA NLP模型在心脏病学领域适配上的局限。

Conclusion: 在详细指南支持下，构建多语言、公平且贴近真实的人工患者语料库以提升心脏病学NLP性能是可行的。

Abstract: Background and Aims. Comparable, multilingual, validated cardiology corpora are critical to advance trustworthy natural language processing (NLP) models for cardiology but such resources are scarce due to privacy concerns. This study aimed to cover this gap by presenting suitable artificial corpus creation guidelines and by releasing 3 multilanguage corpora. Methods. Following purpose-specific publicly-available corpus construction guidelines, cardiologists from 7 European hospitals generated artificial clinical records in Czech, Dutch, English, Italian, Romanian, Spanish and Swedish, ensuring clinical diversity and sex balance, resulting in the CliTe corpus. A CliTe subset underwent a multi-stage manual translation into the other 6 languages, resulting in the parallel ParaCliTe corpus, while the remaining reports were automatically translated, constituting ParaCliTe Silver. Results. CliTe is a collection of 699 artificial cardiology reports, mostly outpatient notes (340 documents), discharge reports (263), and inpatient notes (89), describing fictional patients with ischaemic heart disease (199 patients), heart failure (188), and valvular disease (79), among others. ParaCliTe contains reports from 94 fictitious patients, each available in seven languages with sentence-level alignment, resulting in 658 documents. The corpus mainly comprises discharge reports (322 documents), and outpatient (196) and inpatient (140) notes, describing patients with heart failure (399 documents), ischaemic heart disease (287), and valvular disease (238). Empirical results illustrate the utility of ParaCliTe and highlight the limitations of state-of-the-art NLP models adapted to the cardiology domain. Conclusions. The creation of multilingual, equitable and realistic corpora of artificial patients capable of strengthening NLP performance for cardiology is feasible when supported by detailed guidelines.

</details>


### [120] [Asymmetric Correspondence Learning for Lexical and Style Embeddings: Interpreting Latent Space via Acoustic Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09313)
*Michael Whealing,Robert Gaizauskas,Thomas Hain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出非对称对应学习(ACL)，用分割潜变量瓶颈把HuBERT特征蒸馏为声学词嵌入(AWE)与声学风格嵌入(ASE)，并揭示线性探针无法验证特征隔离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督语音嵌入同时编码词汇、说话人和韵律信息，特征难以隔离；而常用线性探针可能高估隔离效果。

Method: ACL：将对齐的HuBERT特征经分割潜变量对应瓶颈分别蒸馏为AWE和ASE；用探针/聚类评估，再用eGeMAPS上的组级置换特征重要性解释。

Result: AWE词汇聚类紧密(AP≈0.90, Fisher≈6.28)；ASE说话人探针97.9%但拓扑弥散(Fisher≈1.01)，仍保留显著词汇方差(Fisher≈0.86)却线性不可恢复(0.17%)。AWE按声道滤波划分，ASE按F0动态划分。

Conclusion: 标准诊断基准不足以保证特征隔离：潜空间中仍可保留大量探针无法检测的语音结构。

Abstract: Self-supervised speech embeddings jointly encode lexical, speaker, and prosodic information, complicating feature isolation. We introduce Asymmetric Correspondence Learning (ACL), distilling HuBERT features into separate Acoustic Word Embeddings (AWEs) and Acoustic Style Embeddings (ASEs) via a split-latent correspondence bottleneck. Through probing and clustering evaluations on single-word utterances, we demonstrate that substantial variance from extrinsic sources remains encoded within each latent space, despite linear probing indicating the information has been stripped. Whilst the AWE space exhibits cohesive lexical clustering (AP$\approx$0.90, Fisher$\approx$6.28), linear probing on the ASE space achieves 97.9\% speaker accuracy across a diffuse topology (Fisher$\approx$1.01), yet preserves substantial lexical variance (Fisher$\approx$0.86) that remains linearly unrecoverable (0.17\%). To explain these findings, we apply group-level permutation feature importance over eGeMAPS acoustic features, finding that AWEs partition by vocal tract filters and ASEs by $F_0$ dynamics. These findings suggest that standard diagnostic benchmarks may be insufficient for verifying feature isolation, as substantial speech structure can be retained even when it remains entirely hidden from probes.

</details>


### [121] [Syn2DRE: Synergistic Dual Teacher-Student Framework for Document-level Relation Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09342)
*Khai Phan Tran,Xue Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Syn2DRE：一种结合自监督与交叉监督的轻量级师生架构半监督框架，用于生成更可靠的伪标签，并采用统一端到端训练提升效率，在多个基准上优于先前半监督文档级关系抽取SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档级关系抽取（DocRE）依赖昂贵的大规模标注；伪标签方法虽可利用低质量数据，但现有方法存在效率低下和确认偏差（confirmation bias）问题。

Method: 提出Syn2DRE半监督框架：将自监督学习与交叉监督（cross-supervision）结合于轻量级教师-学生架构中以生成更可靠的伪标签；并引入统一的端到端训练策略以进一步提升框架效率。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，Syn2DRE一致优于先前最先进的半监督DocRE方法。

Conclusion: 通过轻量级师生交叉监督与端到端训练，Syn2DRE有效缓解了伪标签的确认偏差与效率问题，提升了半监督文档级关系抽取的性能。

Abstract: Document-level Relation Extraction (DocRE) aims to identify relations between entities across a document but typically relies on costly large-scale annotations. While pseudo-labelling enables the use of low-quality data, existing methods often suffer from inefficiency and confirmation bias. To address these challenges, we propose Syn2DRE, a semi-supervised framework that combines self-supervised learning with cross-supervision in a lightweight teacher-student architecture to produce more reliable pseudo-labels. Additionally, we introduce a unified end-to-end training strategy to further improve efficiency of the framework. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that Syn2DRE consistently outperforms prior state-of-the-art semi-supervised DocRE methods.

</details>


### [122] [A Spiking Neural Model of the Mental Lexicon: Representations, Similarity Structure, and Associative Retrieval in Perception and Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09402)
*Bernd J. Kröger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文在神经工程框架(NEF)与语义指针架构(SPA)中构建了脉冲神经网络实现的心理词典,含613个词条,支持感知与产生两条通路,并在全部词条背景下成功检索8个词。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对词汇通达的神经解释需要明确区分词汇表征结构、跨层级联想连接以及检索过程中产生的神经活动,现有模型常混淆这三者。

Method: 用关系图递归构建概念与语音形式表征,以lemma提供独立词汇身份;每条通路结合两种联想记忆变体(胜者通吃竞争选择与阈值传递),使用199,400个漏积分发放神经元实现。

Result: 形式化了层级内相似性、跨层联想检索及解码活动的解释;仿真在感知(语音→概念)与产生(概念→语音)两个方向上,针对全部613词条成功检索8个选定词。

Conclusion: 提供了词汇表征与神经检索机制的透明描述,并配有可获取的代码、词汇数据与在线仿真文档。

Abstract: A neural account of lexical access requires an explicit distinction between the structure of lexical representations, the associations linking representational levels, and the activity generated during retrieval. This paper specifies a spiking neural implementation of the mental lexicon within the Neural Engineering Framework and Semantic Pointer Architecture. Conceptual and phonological form representations are constructed recursively from relational graphs, whereas lemma representations provide independent lexical identities. The implementation contains 613 lexical entries and supports two directed processing pathways: perception maps a phonological form to its associated concept via a lemma, whereas production maps a concept to its associated phonological form via a lemma. Each pathway combines two associative-memory variants, winner-take-all (WTA) selection (competition) and thresholded transfer, and uses 199,400 leaky integrate-and-fire neurons. We formalize within-level similarity, cross-level associative retrieval, and the interpretation of decoded activity. Simulations show successful retrieval of eight selected words from the lexicon in both processing directions, with retrieval evaluated against all 613 lexical entries. These simulations use complete phonological forms as input for perception and complete concepts as input for production, illustrating lexical retrieval through the corresponding processing pathways. This paper provides a transparent description of lexical representations and neural retrieval mechanisms, supported by accessible code, lexical data, and online simulation documentation.

</details>


### [123] [An Effective Spoken Language Understanding Approach Leveraging Query-Adaptive Intent Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09491)
*Bing-Long Kuo,Yu-Hsuan Fang,Tien-Hong Lo,Hsueh-Wei Liao,Berlin Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: APEX是一种两阶段检索框架，先用意图级原型筛选候选意图，再在选定意图空间内检索上下文示例，从而提升免微调口语理解性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型可通过上下文学习实现免微调的口语理解（SLU），但直接基于查询的检索可能选出语义相似却意图无关的示例，影响效果。

Method: 提出Query-Adaptive Intent Detection via Prototype Extraction（APEX）：第一阶段用意图级原型选择候选意图，第二阶段在筛选出的意图空间内检索上下文示例；在SLURP数据集上以Exact Match Accuracy（EMA）评估，含完整转写与困惑度分层条件，并在ASR转写上做检索分析。

Result: 完整转写下，APEX相比查询式检索在Qwen3 4B和Gemma-3-4B上EMA分别提升4.34和3.97个百分点；高困惑度输入下分别提升7.66和2.13个百分点；原型引导的候选选择在多种输入条件下获得更高的真实意图覆盖率。

Conclusion: 原型引导的两阶段检索能更有效保留意图相关信息，在免微调SLU中稳定优于直接查询检索，且在高难度输入下收益更明显。

Abstract: Large language models enable fine-tuning-free spoken language understanding (SLU) through in-context learning, but direct query-based retrieval may select semantically similar yet intent irrelevant demonstrations. This paper presents Query-Adaptive Intent Detection via Prototype Extraction (APEX), a two-stage retrieval framework that first selects candidate intents using intent-level prototypes and then retrieves contextual exemplars within the selected intent space. Experiments on the SLURP dataset evaluate joint SLU performance using Exact Match Accuracy (EMA) under full-transcript and perplexity-stratified conditions, with additional retrieval analysis on ASR-generated transcripts. Compared with query-based retrieval, APEX improves EMA by 4.34 and 3.97 percentage points with Qwen3 4B and Gemma-3-4B, respectively, on full transcripts. Under high-perplexity inputs, APEX also yields EMA gains of 7.66 and 2.13 percentage points. Retrieval analysis further indicates that prototype-guided candidate selection achieves higher ground truth intent coverage across multiple input conditions.

</details>


### [124] [On the Effectiveness and Limitations of Open-Weight LLMs for Low-Resource Offensive Language Detection in Danish](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09506)
*Elnur Adl Zarabi,Amit Kumar,Adi Singh,Sierra Nelson,Megan Stubbs-Richardson,Taoran Ji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究开放权重LLM在丹麦语冒犯性语言检测（低资源任务）中的有效性与鲁棒性，涵盖四种模型家族、11种提示策略及BERT基线对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 丹麦语冒犯性语言检测是未被充分探索的低资源任务，需评估开放权重LLM在此任务上的表现、跨提示泛化能力及对类别不平衡等现实条件的鲁棒性。

Method: 评估Llama、Mistral、Gemma、Qwen四家族模型，采用涵盖五类的11种提示策略，考察跨提示泛化、丹麦语正字法变体及平衡/自然不平衡分布下的鲁棒性；并与三种预训练策略不同的BERT基线（MLM提示与监督微调）对比，另尝试轻量微调。

Result: 未经任务微调的LLM即可超越三个微调BERT基线，轻量微调带来进一步提升；无单一模型或提示类别占优，提示设计与预训练存在不可先验预测的交互；语义型提示迁移更稳健，格式约束型提示泛化差；æ/ø/å的ASCII转写普遍降低性能；平衡测试集最佳F1为0.84，自然偏斜下为0.62（偏斜训练）和0.70（平衡训练），相对下降26.2%与16.7%。

Conclusion: 开放权重LLM在丹麦语冒犯性检测上具竞争力且轻量微调有效，但提示设计需与模型预训练匹配，丹麦语特殊字符承载有效信号，类别不平衡仍是关键未解挑战。

Abstract: We study the effectiveness and robustness of open-weight large language models (LLMs) for Danish offensive language detection, an underexplored low-resource task. We evaluate four model families (Llama, Mistral, Gemma, and Qwen) across eleven prompting strategies spanning five design categories, cross-prompt generalization, Danish orthographic variation, and robustness under balanced and naturally imbalanced class distributions. We compare against three BERT-family baselines with distinct pre-training strategies, evaluated via both masked language model prompting and supervised fine-tuning. Even without task-specific fine-tuning, LLMs can surpass all three fine-tuned BERT baselines, and lightweight finetuning yields substantial further gains. No single model or prompt category dominates, underscoring that prompt design interacts with model pre-training in ways that cannot be predicted a priori. Semantically grounded prompts transfer more robustly, while format-constrained patterns consistently fail to generalize. ASCII transliteration of Danish-specific characters (æ, ø, å) generally degrades performance, indicating these characters encode meaningful signal. At the same time, the gap between a balanced test set (best F1 of 0.84) and the corpus's natural class skew (0.62 trained on skewed data, 0.70 trained on balanced data) -- relative drops of 26.2\% and 16.7\% -- shows that class imbalance remains a critical open challenge. Our code and configurations are available at https://github.com/PASHAADC/Danish-LLM-HateSpeech.

</details>


### [125] [Benchmarking Speaker Embedders in the Sub-Second Regime for Real-Time Voice AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09516)
*Abhishek Gautam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在严格亚秒级延迟下评测多种开源说话人嵌入器（TitaNet、ECAPA-TDNN、CAM++、SpeakerNet）在背景语音干扰下的表现，发现降噪预处理有害、截断音频有害、TitaNet约一半维度冗余，并给出部署建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时语音AI代理需从可辨识的背景人声（电视、家人、同事）中区分出活跃用户；传统降噪只去除平稳噪声，背景语音因与目标说话人频谱相似而漏过，导致下游说话人验证（SV）和ASR误接受。

Method: 构建受控基准（在真实通话静音段以调制幅度注入第二个真实说话人），结合加权人工标注的生产数据集，在亚秒级延迟约束下对TitaNet-Large/Small、ECAPA-TDNN、CAM++、SpeakerNet进行基准测试，并分析嵌入维度冗余性。

Result: 三点发现：①嵌入前降噪普遍有害，跨架构均提高EER；②将音频截断至短于完整VAD语音段会降低所有模型性能；③TitaNet约一半嵌入维度对本任务冗余，尾部维度编码语言、性别等群体属性，在同质用户群中区分价值低。

Conclusion: 部署建议：在原始音频上用完整VAD语音块做验证；阈值应偏向精确率而非F1；使用截断嵌入以降低相似度检索成本。

Abstract: Real-time Voice AI agents must separate the active user from intelligible background speech that survives conventional noise suppression. Background sources include television audio, family members, and colleagues. Production denoisers remove non-stationary environmental noise effectively. Background speech shares the spectral characteristics of the target and passes through, causing false acceptances in downstream speaker verification (SV) and automatic speech recognition (ASR). We benchmark open-source speaker embedders TitaNet-Large, TitaNet-Small, ECAPA-TDNN, CAM++, and SpeakerNet under a strict sub-second latency budget. Evaluation combines a controlled benchmark with a weighted human-annotated production set. Our controlled benchmark is built from real calls in which a second real speaker is injected into silence intervals at modulated amplitude. We report three findings. First, denoising before embedding is generally harmful and raises equal error rate (EER) across architectures. Second, truncating audio below the full voice-activity segment degrades every model. Third, roughly half of the TitaNet embedding is redundant for this task. Trailing dimensions encode population attributes such as language and gender. Such attributes carry little discriminative value in a demographically homogeneous user base. We distill these results into deployment guidance. Verification should run on raw audio with full voice-activity chunks. Thresholds should favor precision over F1. Truncated embeddings reduce the cost of similarity search.

</details>


### [126] [Query-Guided RoPE Modulation for Lost-in-the-Middle Mitigation in Long-Context LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09554)
*Peiyu Zhang,Heng Ping,Shinibali Bhattacharyya,Anwesan Pal,Nikos Kanakaris,Kevin Cheang,Shixuan Li,Wei Yang,Paul Bogdan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出免训练方法QGPM，用查询相关性重塑RoPE位置，缓解长上下文"lost-in-the-middle"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文模型常忽略输入中段信息（lost-in-the-middle），部分源于RoPE的远距离衰减削弱了远距离token间的位置信号。现有压缩删token、注意力重加权在RoPE削弱之后才起作用，RoPE级方法对所有查询施加统一重映射。

Method: QGPM为免训练方法：利用模型自身注意力头计算与查询的相关性；低层构建宽泛相关图，高层构建尖锐相关图；低相关token在快旋转平面上前进不足一个位置，将相关证据拉近查询；慢旋转平面保持原始布局，所有token位置不变。

Result: 在NoLiMa与RULER多上下文长度实验中，QGPM优于基模型和强基线，检索任务增益最大，并在多个骨干模型上泛化。

Conclusion: 查询引导的位置调制是一种有效且无需训练的RoPE级改进方案，可缓解中段信息丢失并具备良好通用性。

Abstract: Long-context language models often neglect information in the middle of the input, a phenomenon known as "lost-in-the-middle". Part of this gap may stem from the long-range decay of rotary position embedding (RoPE), which weakens the positional signal between distant tokens. Existing input compression deletes low-importance tokens, and attention reweighting acts only after RoPE has weakened this signal. RoPE-level methods modify positions directly but apply the same remapping to every query. To address these limitations, we propose QGPM (Query-Guided Positional Modulation), a training-free method that reshapes RoPE positions using query relevance from the model's own attention heads. QGPM builds a broad relevance map for lower layers and a sharp one for upper layers. Low-relevance tokens advance by less than one position on fast-rotating planes, pulling relevant evidence toward the query. Slow-rotating planes keep the original layout, with every token in place. Experiments on NoLiMa and RULER with multiple context lengths show that QGPM improves over base models and strong baselines, with the largest gains on retrieval tasks, and generalizes across multiple backbones. Our code is available at https://github.com/iZHpy/QGPM.

</details>


### [127] [RuACT: Recovering Inactive Rubrics as Adaptive Critics and Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09597)
*Yijun Li,Zhengxi Lu,Zhipiao Liu,Jinzheng Yu,Xutao Weng,Jian Shao,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对rubric强化学习中"不可达/饱和"两类无信号rubric，提出RuACT，分别当作教师（特权信息自蒸馏）与评论家（子rubric分解）来提供监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Group-relative优化下，全部失败（不可达）或全部通过的rubric（饱和）使组内奖励无差异、无学习信号；在Qwen3-4B医学训练中60.88%的query-rubric观测处于非激活状态。作者进一步发现两类状态缺失的监督不同：不可达rubric只需提示即可被满足（1.13%→91.25%），而饱和rubric下回答质量仍有差异。

Method: RuACT按缺失内容为每个非激活rubric分配角色：Rubric as Teacher 将不可达rubric作为特权信息，做奖励门控的on-policy自蒸馏；Rubric as Critic 将饱和rubric分解为共享父权重的子rubric，以区分当前回答。

Result: 在四个医学与科学基准上，Qwen3-4B与Qwen3-1.7B下均优于GRPO、rubric重加权和rubric引导探索，1.7B下取得最佳平均；初始不可达rubric的无引导恢复率由35.02%升至42.86%；子rubric细化将独立rollout上的奖励-质量对齐从57.74%提升至60.77%。

Conclusion: 将非激活rubric按缺失的监督类型重用作教师或评论家，可有效恢复学习信号并提升开放式任务的强化学习效果。

Abstract: Rubric-based reinforcement learning (RL) extends RL with verifiable rewards to open-ended tasks by scoring each response against query-specific rubrics. Under group-relative optimization, however, a rubric that every sampled response fails (unreachable) or passes (saturated) gives all responses the same outcome and no learning signal; in Qwen3-4B medical training, 60.88% of query-rubric observations are inactive. We find that the two states lack different supervision: stating an unreachable rubric in the prompt raises its satisfaction from 1.13% to 91.25%, whereas responses that all pass a saturated rubric still differ in quality. We therefore propose RuACT (Rubrics as Adaptive Critics and Teachers), which assigns each inactive rubric the role that supplies what it lacks. Rubric as Teacher uses unreachable rubrics as privileged information for reward-gated on-policy self-distillation, and Rubric as Critic decomposes saturated rubrics into child rubrics that separate current responses while sharing the parent's weight. RuACT outperforms GRPO, rubric reweighting, and rubric-guided exploration on all four medical and scientific benchmarks with Qwen3-4B and achieves the best average with Qwen3-1.7B. On initially unreachable rubrics, unguided recovery rises from 35.02% to 42.86%, while child refinement improves reward-quality alignment on independent rollouts from 57.74% to 60.77%. Code is available at https://github.com/ZJU-REAL/RuACT.

</details>


### [128] [Steerable Multi-Objective Group-Relative Preference Optimization with Jointly Trained Low-Rank Adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09634)
*Constantinos Karouzos,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LambdaMix：一种参数高效的偏好条件化策略族学习法，用组相对在线RL同时训练每个目标一个低秩适配器，单次训练即可通过偏好向量在推理时切换权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户偏好随场景变化，需要在多个竞争目标间调整权衡；但标准后训练通常只针对固定目标权重产出单一策略，缺乏灵活性。

Method: 在冻结基座上为每个目标联合训练一个低秩适配器；用一个偏好向量来选择生成响应的适配器混合，并加权各自归一化的奖励以更新适配器；基于组相对在线强化学习（GRPO类）。

Result: 在4个模型上评测"有用性vs无害性"及"解数学题同时遵循指令"两任务，LambdaMix的超体积均高于所有条件化与策略合并基线；比一组GRPO专家策略使用更少训练补全、存储更少适配器参数。固定架构与初始化时，同时变化适配器混合与目标权重比单独变化任一项获得更宽分数范围与更高覆盖。

Conclusion: 单次训练即可得到可调权衡的策略族，参数与样本效率优于专家策略集合，且混合与权重联合调节能更好覆盖帕累托前沿。

Abstract: Language model deployment often requires different trade-offs between competing objectives as user preferences change, yet standard post-training methods typically produce a single policy for a fixed objective weighting. We introduce LambdaMix, a new parameter efficient method for learning a preference conditioned family of policies with group-relative online reinforcement learning. LambdaMix jointly trains one low-rank adapter per objective over a frozen base model. One preference vector selects the adapter mixture that generates responses and weights the separately normalized rewards used to update the adapters. A single training run supports changing preferences at inference without further optimization. We experiment with four models on helpfulness and harmlessness and on solving math problems while following instructions. We also evaluate the two largest models on rewriting comments to preserve wording while reducing clues about personal attributes. On the first two tasks, LambdaMix achieves higher hypervolume than all evaluated conditioned and policy merging baselines across all four models. LambdaMix uses fewer training completions and stores fewer adapter parameters than a set of GRPO specialist policies. With architecture and initialization fixed, varying adapter mixtures and objective weights together gives wider score ranges and higher coverage than varying either alone.

</details>


### [129] [How Much Conversation History Does an AI Agent Need?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09662)
*Danny Kim,Fernando Koch*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究在BEAM 1,100题上固定检索排序与reader，比较五种段落预算与消息保留策略对得分和token的影响，发现得分随上下文增加而升但边际递减，且预算选择依赖证据保留与评测指标效度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究减少对话历史/上下文预算如何影响reader的token使用与基准分数，以及何时应如何选择记忆预算。

Method: 在1,100道BEAM问题上保持检索排序和reader不变，设置五种固定段落预算；另在有明确对话状态目标的受控任务中比较保留用户消息与token匹配的近期消息策略，测试Qwen与Mistral；并进行评分审计。

Result: 平均得分随段落预算增加而上升，但高预算下每增加token的增益递减；可回答问题通常随上下文增多得分更高；汇总弃答得分有时随预算缩小而上升。Qwen在保留用户消息时对直接需求的全状态匹配更好，但当答案依赖对早前提议的引用时匹配率下降；Mistral在各条件下接近下限。评分审计未能确定BEAM答案的正确性。

Conclusion: 实验刻画了所测记忆预算的token与得分后果；可靠的预算选择取决于保留每道题所需的证据，并验证所用答案质量度量的有效性。

Abstract: How does using less conversational history change reader-token use and benchmark scores? We examine this question on 1,100 BEAM questions. We held the retrieval ranking and reader constant across five fixed passage budgets, and mean assigned scores rose as the passage budget grew. The score gain per additional token became smaller at the upper tested budgets. Answerable questions generally received higher scores with more context. Pooled abstention scores sometimes rose as the passage budget shrank. We also tested message retention in a controlled task with explicit conversational-state targets and compared retained user messages with token-matched recency. Qwen matched the full state more often for direct requirements when user messages were retained. Its full-state matching declined when answers depended on references to earlier offers. Mistral produced near-floor results across the retention conditions. A scoring audit leaves the correctness of BEAM answers unresolved. These experiments characterize the token and score consequences of the tested memory budgets. Reliable budget selection depends on retaining the evidence each question requires and validating the measure used to assess answer quality.

</details>


### [130] [Language as Trait, Politeness as Strategy: Controlled Tests of Linguistic Effects in LLM Social-Deduction Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09768)
*Yuchen Li,Lisa Liu,Runqi Zou,Shuxuan Kuang,Qingquan Zhang,Julian Togelius*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 语言表达的复杂度与礼貌策略不改变LLM智能体对说话者的信任判断，也不影响投票或夜间行动；自然对局中88%的威胁面子言语为直接无修饰式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验同一话语的不同措辞（更简单/更复杂、更直接/更礼貌）是否会改变其他LLM智能体的判断与行为。

Method: 基于LURK Werewolf框架，将言语行为谓词与自然语言实现分离；仅改写实现并验证游戏内容不变，从存档检查点回放每日对局并与重采样对照组比较；跨说话者消息统一变换语言范围，逐句变换礼貌策略。共30局、4,335次回放。

Result: 两种操控均未改变信任；经多重比较校正后对投票和夜间行动无显著效应；最大未校正效应为语言范围偏离中性改写时约4个百分点的投票变化。自然对局中88%的威胁面子言语为直接无修饰式，与角色无关。

Conclusion: LLM智能体对措辞层面的语言差异（复杂度、礼貌）表现出稳健的抵抗力，判断主要由内容驱动；但在自发交互中，智能体倾向于使用直接的威胁面子言语。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents coordinate through conversation, and the same message can be phrased in simpler or more complex language, bluntly or politely. We test whether such differences change how other agents judge and act, using the LURK Werewolf framework, which separates each utterance's speech-act predicate from its natural-language realization. We rewrite only the realization, check that the game content is preserved, and replay each game day from saved checkpoints against a re-sampled control. We vary linguistic range consistently across a speaker's messages and politeness strategy per utterance. Across 30 games and 4,335 replays, neither manipulation changes the trust other agents place in the speaker, and no effect on votes or night actions survives correction for multiple comparisons; the largest uncorrected effect is that shifting range away from a neutral paraphrase changes about 4 percentage points more of the other players' votes. In natural play, 88% of face-threatening speech is bald on-record regardless of role.

</details>


### [131] [ROSE: Risk-Aware Orthogonal Subspace Navigation for Lifelong Knowledge Editing in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09770)
*Lingyun Song,Ziyao Chen,Kang Pan,Xiaolin Han,Yifei Zhang,Xiaoqi Wang,Yudai Pan,Xuequn Shang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ROSE框架，用风险感知正交子空间导航实现多模态大模型终身知识编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs需持续更新事实知识，但终身知识编辑面临严重的局部性侵蚀与灾难性遗忘：顺序编辑会破坏预训练能力及先前更新。

Method: ROSE将参数更新限制在已编辑子空间的正交补中以避免遗忘；引入风险引导机制，用动态计算的风险掩码保护模型稳定性所需的事实参数子空间；推理时采用多粒度知识整合策略（原型锚定的语义门控+扰动驱动的子空间融合），精准激活相关查询的编辑知识并减少无关输入干扰。

Result: 在两个主流多模态基准上，ROSE在五项关键指标上与SOTA方法相当，并在1000次顺序更新中保持出色稳定性。

Conclusion: ROSE有效缓解终身多模态知识编辑中的局部性侵蚀与灾难性遗忘，实现稳定且具竞争力的性能。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) require continuous factual updates to stay current, yet lifelong knowledge editing remains a daunting challenge. The primary obstacle is the severe Locality Erosion and Catastrophic Forgetting, where sequential edits inevitably interfere with pre-trained capabilities and prior updates. In this paper, we propose \textbf{ROSE}, a \textbf{R}isk-aware \textbf{O}rthogonal \textbf{S}ubspace navigation framework for lifelong knowledge \textbf{E}diting. ROSE addresses forgetting by aiming to restrict parameter updates to the orthogonal complement of previously edited subspaces. Crucially, it introduces a risk-guided mechanism that utilizes a dynamically computed risk mask to safeguard specific factual parameter subspaces essential for model stability. During inference, ROSE employs a multi-granularity knowledge integration strategy, featuring prototype-anchored semantic gating and perturbation-driven subspace fusion to precisely activate edited knowledge for relevant queries while limiting interference on unrelated inputs. Comprehensive experiments on two prominent multimodal benchmarks show that ROSE is competitive with state-of-the-art approaches across five key metrics, while maintaining outstanding stability over $1,000$ sequential updates. Our code is available at https://github.com/sunlitsong/ROSE.

</details>


### [132] [Are These Programmers Ripe Enough to Eat? Evaluating How LLMs Handle Lexical Knowledge Conflicts in Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09785)
*Dongxu Wu,Sara Court,Micha Elsner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建普通话-英语受控翻译任务，将提示中给出的词义视为"上下文知识"(CK)，系统研究词频、句子上下文与提示框架对九个大模型翻译输出的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部词汇资源可帮助LLM翻译低资源语言与专业领域文本，但这些资源可能与模型自身的先验词汇知识冲突。作者希望隔离并研究这种冲突。

Method: 构建受控的普通话到英语翻译任务：将常见源词在语义类别内及跨类别赋予新含义。将提示中提供的含义定义为上下文知识(CK)，系统评估词频、句子上下文和提示框架的影响；在9个模型上测试，并进行三项后续实验（情态弱化、重复、推理）。

Result: 源词词频越低、句子上下文越支持所给含义时，对CK的依赖越强；提示框架可降低CK依赖，但9个模型中有7个即使被明确要求忽略所给含义仍倾向采用CK；模型家族内更大模型更倾向利用CK。后续实验显示：情态弱化降低CK依赖，重复效应取决于提示布局，推理效应因模型而异。

Conclusion: 该受控研究揭示了CK依赖在不同条件和模型间的变化规律，为检测与评估词汇冲突、引导LLM翻译中的词汇选择提供了新方法方向。

Abstract: External lexical resources can help large language models (LLMs) translate texts in low-resource languages and specialized domains, but these resources may conflict with models' prior lexical knowledge. To isolate this conflict, we construct a controlled Mandarin-to-English translation task in which familiar source words are assigned new meanings within and across semantic categories. We treat these prompt-supplied meanings as contextual knowledge (CK) and systematically evaluate the effects of word frequency, sentence context, and prompt framing on the translated outputs. Across nine models, CK reliance is greater for less frequent source words and in sentence contexts that support the supplied meanings. Prompt framing can reduce CK reliance, but seven of nine models favor CK even when explicitly instructed to ignore the supplied meanings. Within model families, larger models are more likely than smaller models to utilize CK in their outputs. Three follow-up experiments further show that modal weakening lowers CK reliance, the effect of repetition depends on prompt layout, and the effect of reasoning varies across models. Overall, our controlled study reveals how CK reliance varies across conditions and models, motivating new methods to detect and evaluate lexical conflicts and guide models' lexical choices in LLM-based translation.

</details>


### [133] [Compaction, decay and reinforcement in a two-tier conversational memory: a paired evaluation against retrieval-augmented generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09807)
*Osamudiamen Edogun,Joshua Obigwe,Irfan Mehmood*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在LoCoMo基准上比较了两种长对话记忆策略——保留原始对话并检索（RAG） versus 将每轮压缩为原子事实再检索（compaction），在统一生成器、评判器与同库条件下用McNemar精确检验做配对比较。压缩法在四个可比类别上以71.4%对64.6%领先6.8分（p=5.5e-08）且省34%提示词token，但加入对抗类别后优势降至2.0且不显著。该损失呈结构性格局：答案越依赖原文措辞，压缩法越吃亏，说明它保留"断言了什么"却丢失"怎么说的"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具备记忆能力的对话智能体必须决定"保留什么"。一种主流做法是保留完整对话记录并检索（RAG），另一种是把每轮对话压缩成原子事实再检索。二者孰优孰劣缺乏严格受控的对比证据，本文即针对此问题设计配对实验。

Method: 在LoCoMo上让两组方案在单一窗口内回答全部1,985个问题，共用相同的记忆库、生成器与评判器，并将两组预测放在同一行，从而对512个分歧题使用McNemar精确检验。此外按"参考答案所需原文词数"与token重叠度进行分层分析，另报告四项带功效计算的负结果。

Result: 四个可比类别上压缩法71.4% vs 64.6%，领先6.8分（p=5.5e-08），提示词token少34%。纳入全部五类（含对抗集）后领先降至2.0且不显著，因基线在对抗类上领先14.6分。分层显示：参考答案需1–2个原文词时压缩法领先9.6分，超过8个词时落后3.1分，token重叠呈同向变化，表明评判器并非致因。

Conclusion: 压缩法与RAG各有适用面：压缩法保留事实所断言的"值"，丢弃了"表述方式"，故在答案为具体取值时占优，在答案依赖原文措辞时落败。对抗类问题是基线反超的主要来源。作者还给出四项负结果及其功效计算，提示方法选择应依据答案类型而非单一总体分数。

Abstract: A conversational agent that remembers has to decide what to keep. Retrieval-augmented generation keeps the transcript and searches it; the alternative is to compact each turn into atomic facts and search those. We compare the two on LoCoMo, with both arms answering all 1,985 questions in one window, against the same stores, with the same generator and judge, and both predictions on one row, which licenses McNemar's exact test on the 512 questions where they disagree.
  On the four categories comparable work reports, compaction scores 71.4 per cent against 64.6, a lead of 6.8 points (p = 5.5e-08) at 34 per cent fewer prompt tokens. On all five, including the adversarial set comparable work drops, the lead falls to 2.0 and stops being significant, because the baseline leads adversarial by 14.6.
  That loss has a structure. Our lead falls as the reference answer demands more source wording, 9.6 points ahead at one or two reference words and 3.1 behind past eight, with token overlap moving the same way, so the judge is not the cause. Compaction keeps what a fact asserts and discards how it was said, so it wins where the answer is a value and loses where it is a phrasing. We also report four negative results with their power calculations.

</details>


### [134] [CodeQuant2: Enhanced Outlier Mitigation and Adaptive Clustering for Low-Precision LRMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09868)
*Xingyu Liu,Xiangyang Yin,Tianhua Xia,Haiyu Wang,Sai Qian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CodeQuant2 是面向低精度大推理模型的聚类 LUT 量化框架，通过旋转缩放抑制激活离群、聚类建模非均匀权重、按层自适应码本，并改进校准样本选择（覆盖解码早期激活波动），配合定制 LUT CUDA 核，在推理基准上显著提升精度并较 BF16 提速至多 2.85×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型通过生成长推理链提升性能，但推理成本高，因而低精度量化愈发重要；然而激进量化会严重损害推理能力。作者观察到推理任务在最初解码步激活波动更大，忽略这些早期状态的校准会遗漏关键激活波动，因此需要针对推理场景的专门量化方案。

Method: 提出 CodeQuant2：基于聚类的查表（LUT）量化框架。1）用旋转与缩放缓解激活离群；2）用聚类表示非均匀权重分布；3）按各层量化难度自适应调整码本大小；4）基于推理期激活行为观察改进校准样本选择，使统计更贴近实际解码分布；5）配套定制 LUT CUDA 内核实现高效推理。

Result: 在推理基准上取得显著的精度提升，同时相对 BF16 实现最高 2.85× 加速。

Conclusion: 结合推理期激活特性进行校准与聚类 LUT 量化，可在极低精度下保持大推理模型的推理性能并获得实际加速，说明针对推理动态的量化与系统协同设计是关键。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) improve task performance by generating longer intermediate reasoning traces, but this substantially increases inference costs and makes low-precision quantization increasingly important. However, aggressively quantized models often perform poorly on reasoning tasks. We observe larger activation fluctuations during the first decoding steps of reasoning. Calibration that omits these early states may miss their activation fluctuations. To address these reasoning-specific quantization challenges, we propose \textbf{CodeQuant2}, a clustering-based LUT quantization framework for low-precision large reasoning models. CodeQuant2 mitigates activation outliers with rotation and scaling, uses clustering to represent non-uniform weight distributions, and adapts codebook sizes to the quantization difficulty of each layer. Guided by our observation of reasoning-time activation behavior, CodeQuant2 further improves calibration sample selection to better match actual decoding statistics. Combined with our customized LUT CUDA kernel, CodeQuant2 delivers substantial accuracy improvements on reasoning benchmarks while achieving up to $2.85\times$ speedup over BF16.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [Two-Layer Evolutionary Scheduling for Machine Learning Jobs in Multi-Cluster Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09093)
*Jingyuan Xu,Changwu Huang,Lemeng Pan,Ke Tang,Xin Yao*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: EVO-GP 是一个两层进化在线调度方法，将全局作业到集群分配与集群内 GPU 资源分配解耦，用于多集群 GPU 环境的机器学习作业调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模 ML 工作负载对 GPU 集群调度提出极高要求，现有启发式调度器难以应对多集群 GPU 环境的复杂性与动态性，导致负载不均、排队延迟长、与业务目标对齐差。

Method: 将多集群 ML 调度建模为在线多目标优化（最小化平均与最大加权排队时间、最大化 GPU 时间利用率）；提出两层进化在线调度：第一层用进化算法将到达作业分配到可行集群以促进负载均衡，第二层用离线遗传编程自动学习的优先级函数对作业排序并指导集群内 GPU 分配。

Result: 在真实数据与模拟工作负载上的大量实验表明，EVO-GP 持续优于最先进基线方法，并保持更好的适应性与可扩展性。

Conclusion: 解耦全局分配与集群内分配的进化在线调度可有效应对多集群 GPU 调度的多目标优化难题，性能优于现有基线。

Abstract: The rapid growth of large-scale machine learning (ML) workloads has led to unprecedented demands on GPU cluster scheduling systems. However, existing heuristic schedulers struggle to handle the complexity and dynamics of multi-cluster GPU environments, often leading to load imbalance, long queuing delays, and poor alignment to business objectives. This paper formulates the ML scheduling problem in multi-cluster GPU environments as online multi-objective optimization that minimizes the average and maximum weighted queuing times while maximizing GPU time utilization. To solve this optimization problem, we propose EVO-GP, a two-layer evolutionary online scheduling method that decouples global job-to-cluster allocation from intra-cluster GPU resource allocation. At the job-to-cluster allocation layer, an evolutionary algorithm assigns arriving jobs to feasible clusters to promote load balance across clusters. At the resource allocation layer, a priority function automatically learned offline via genetic programming is used to rank jobs and guide GPU allocation within each cluster. Extensive experiments on both real-world data and simulated workloads demonstrate that the proposed EVO-GP method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, while maintaining superior adaptability and scalability.

</details>


### [136] [OMCDE: Opposition-based Multi-Stage Matrix-Based Competitive Differential Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09167)
*Mohammad Sarhangzadeh,Shahryar Rahnamayan,Doruk Oner*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: OMCDE是一种基于矩阵的竞争型差分进化算法，在保持计算效率的同时提升连续全局优化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高性能差分进化变体往往引入额外复杂度和计算开销，本文希望在不牺牲效率的前提下提升优化表现。

Method: 提出OMCDE：融合竞争驱动的搜索动态、优化阶段间的自适应行为以及基于对立的学习初始化，并采用可并行化的矩阵框架实现。

Result: 在CEC 2017基准函数上，OMCDE相较于多种先进DE算法表现强劲，且保持高效与可扩展性。

Conclusion: OMCDE是求解复杂数值优化问题的一个有前景的替代方案。

Abstract: Differential Evolution has proven to be an effective algorithm for continuous global optimization, but it's modern high-performing variants often introduce additional complexity and computational cost. We propose OMCDE, a matrix-based competitive Differential Evolution algorithm that aims to improve optimization performance while maintaining computational efficiency. The proposed approach integrates competition-driven search dynamics, adaptive behavior between optimization stages, and opposition-based initialization within a parallelizable framework. Empirical results on widely used CEC 2017 functions show that OMCDE achieves strong performance compared to several state-of-the-art DE algorithms, while remaining efficient and scalable. These findings indicate that OMCDE is a promising alternative for solving complex numerical optimization problems.

</details>


### [137] [Bridging Spiking and Continuous Dynamics in Spiking Neural Networks: A Muscle-Inspired Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09295)
*Maryam Soltani,Philip N. Garner*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 论文提出"肌肉响应模型"(muscle-response model)——一种受生物肌肉整合神经脉冲启发的二阶动力学解码层，将脉冲神经网络的离散脉冲序列转换为连续信号，并在文本转语音任务上分析神经元数量与时间分辨率的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN产生的是离散脉冲序列，如何通过类生物机制将其转换为连续的生成/回归输出仍是一个未解决问题。生物系统中肌肉整合神经脉冲产生平滑协调运动，为此提供灵感。

Method: 设计一个生物启发的动力学层，利用二阶动力学对脉冲活动做时间积分，从而把脉冲序列映射为连续表示；在文本转语音任务中考察脉冲神经元数量与时间分辨率对生成信号质量的影响，并分析学习到的动力学特性。

Result: 在所评估配置下，增加表征容量（脉冲神经元数量）比提高时间分辨率对信号质量的影响更大；对学习动力学的分析显示，在没有对响应类型施加显式约束时，大多数神经元收敛到非振荡行为。

Conclusion: 结果展示了生物启发动力学解码器在连接脉冲神经计算与连续信号生成方面的潜力。

Abstract: Transforming the spike trains produced by spiking neural networks (SNNs) into continuous signals through biologically inspired mechanisms remains an open challenge in generative and regression tasks. In biological systems, a similar transformation occurs when muscles integrate neural spikes to produce smooth, coordinated movements. Inspired by this mechanism, we introduce a muscle-response model, a biologically motivated dynamical layer that converts spike trains into continuous representations. Our approach uses second-order dynamics to model the temporal integration of spiking activity. We investigate how the number of spiking neurons and temporal resolution affect the quality of the generated signals in a text-to-speech task, showing that increasing representational capacity has a greater impact than increasing temporal resolution under the evaluated configurations. Furthermore, an analysis of the learned dynamics reveals that, without explicit constraints on the response type, most neurons converge toward non-oscillatory behavior. These results demonstrate the potential of biologically inspired dynamical decoders for bridging spiking neural computation and continuous signal generation.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [138] [Improving Contextual Retrieval for Long Documents using Semantic Chunking and Hybrid Retrieval Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08498)
*Sakshi Garg,William B. Andreopoulos*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出融合知识图谱结构化推理与稠密向量检索的混合RAG框架，用多阶段流水线（摘要+语义分块）、动态知识图谱与分层检索机制解决多跳推理与上下文碎片化问题，在MultiHop-RAG上优于单一向量或图基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长篇幅、分布式文档信息量大，答案常需跨多源证据关联；标准RAG在多跳推理上表现差，且存在上下文碎片化问题。

Method: 多阶段流水线：基于Transformer的摘要与语义分块提升上下文保留；构建捕捉实体间显式关系的动态知识图谱，同时维护稠密向量索引；推理时采用分层机制，优先图遍历保证精度，必要时回退到向量检索。

Result: 在MultiHop-RAG数据集上，混合方法优于纯向量与纯图基线，获得更高Exact Match分数，复杂查询的检索准确率提升。

Conclusion: 结构化推理与向量检索相结合可构建更鲁棒的系统，有效应对复杂知识密集型问题。

Abstract: The vast amount of information available in lengthy, distributed documents makes finding relevant answers difficult, especially when the answer requires connecting evidence from multiple sources. Standard Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems often struggle with this type of "multi-hop" reasoning and frequently face issues with context fragmentation. In this paper, we propose a hybrid RAG framework that integrates the structured reasoning of knowledge graphs with the broad semantic coverage of dense vector retrieval. Our approach employs a multi-stage pipeline using transformer-based summarization and semantic chunking to improve context retention. We construct a dynamic knowledge graph to capture explicit relationships between entities while simultaneously maintaining a dense vector index. During inference, the system uses a hierarchical mechanism that prioritizes graph traversal for precision and falls back to vector retrieval when necessary. We evaluated our framework on the MultiHop-RAG dataset. The results show that this hybrid approach outperforms standalone vector and graph-based baselines, achieving higher Exact Match scores and improved retrieval accuracy for complex queries. This confirms that merging structured reasoning with standard vector retrieval creates a more robust system for handling complex knowledge-intensive questions.

</details>


### [139] [RedMall: Generative Recommendation for Large-Scale E-commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08519)
*Kunyang Zhou,Xin Jia,Yi Yang,Yongjun Yang,Manjie Xu,Honglei Zhang,Yan Zhu,Kai Zuo,Xu Tang,Zheng Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: RedMall是一个面向大规模电商的生成式推荐系统，通过层次化SID、层次依赖建模、多目标预测、曝光感知损失及GRPO+LambdaRank对齐，在离线与线上均取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐存在三大问题：1）语义ID（SID）被当作一维序列，忽略层间层次依赖；2）next-token预测无法显式优化会话级推荐；3）已有RL方法虽对齐用户偏好，却少有显式优化会话中正样本的排序。作者希望解决这些问题以适配大规模电商场景。

Method: 1）基于多模态表示的残差量化构造层次化SID；2）引入轻量SID编码器显式建模层间依赖；3）采用稀疏MoE、双门控注意力和多目标预测来扩展模型、增强兴趣表示并优化会话级推荐；4）设计曝光感知损失，将曝光未点击视为负反馈；5）结合GRPO与辅助LambdaRank损失，利用真实用户反馈与物品排序共同指导优化。

Result: 在Amazon Beauty、Amazon Toys、Amazon Food和MovieLens-1M四个公开数据集上，RedMall在报告的Recall和NDCG指标上均优于基线。线上A/B测试显示：电商广告场景ADVV提升2.24%、CTR提升0.59%；电商推荐场景GMV提升1.08%、日活买家（DAB）提升0.95%。

Conclusion: RedMall通过层次化SID建模、多目标与会话级优化以及排序对齐的强化学习，有效缓解了生成式推荐的三大瓶颈，并在公开数据集与真实线上业务中均验证了其有效性，具备大规模电商落地价值。

Abstract: Generative recommendation has shown considerable potential in industrial recommendation systems. However, the performance of existing generative methods is limited by several challenges. 1) Semantic IDs (SIDs) are treated as one-dimensional sequences, overlooking the hierarchical dependencies among different layers. 2) Next-token prediction used is unable to explicitly optimize session-wise recommendation. 3) Although reinforcement learning has been applied to align generated recommendations with user preferences, few existing methods explicitly optimize the ranking of positive items in session-wise recommendation. To address the above issues, we propose RedMall, a generative recommender designed for large-scale e-commerce. Firstly, RedMall constructs hierarchical SIDs through residual quantization over multimodal representations. Secondly, RedMall introduces a lightweight SID encoder to explicitly model layer-wise dependencies in SIDs. Moreover, RedMall incorporates sparse mixture-of-experts, dual gated attention, and multi-target prediction to scale model size, enhance user interest representation, and optimize session-wise recommendation. We further design an exposure-aware loss that treats exposed but unclicked items as negative feedback. To better align user preferences and list-wise ranking objectives, we combine Group Relative Policy Optimization with an auxiliary LambdaRank loss, using both real user feedback and item ranking to guide optimization. Experiments on four public datasets-Amazon Beauty, Amazon Toys, Amazon Food, and MovieLens-1M---show that RedMall outperforms the evaluated baselines across all reported Recall and NDCG metrics. Online A/B tests show improvements of 2.24% in advertiser value (ADVV) and 0.59% in click-through rate (CTR) for e-commerce advertising, as well as gains of 1.08% in GMV and 0.95% in daily active buyers (DAB) for e-commerce recommendations.

</details>


### [140] [CascadeRec: Deployed Adaptive LLM Compute Allocation for Multi-Tenant Content Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08566)
*Kai Zhao,Guilin Zhang,Xiang Cheng,Xiquan Cui,Henry Heng,Xu Chu,Alet Blanken*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CascadeRec：两级LLM重排级联，Tier-1轻量模型处理全部请求，路由器仅将可能受益的请求升级到Tier-2昂贵推理；生产A/B实验降低约10%推理成本，延迟影响极小且无质量回退。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM重排对推荐有效，但每个请求都用昂贵模型成本高，且对交互稀疏用户反而降低质量；需要在不牺牲质量的前提下控制推理成本。

Method: 两级级联架构：Tier-1轻模型对每请求重排，路由器判断何时升级至Tier-2（自洽性采样、多路径推理或更大模型）；部署于AWS EKS/GKE；辅以MovieLens-1M与Amazon-Electronics公开基准的片段级配对bootstrap分析。

Result: 生产A/B超一个月：相比统一使用昂贵层，推理成本降约10%，延迟影响极小，暂无质量回退。公开基准显示统一LLM重排使稀疏历史用户NDCG@10最多下降30%，唯一显著增益出现在最长历史片段；升级冷用户可能加重损失；哪些片段受益依领域而定。

Conclusion: 级联与片段门控（segment-gated）上线策略可低成本保留LLM重排收益；租户间负载不均衡是级联的驱动因素，需在服务层处理LLM输出解析失败；受益片段具有领域依赖性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are attractive rerankers for recommendation. Running an expensive model on every request is costly, and for users with sparse interaction histories it often lowers quality. We present CascadeRec, a two-tier LLM reranking cascade deployed in production at a large financial planning and analysis (FP&A) SaaS provider. It serves content recommendations to enterprise tenants on AWS EKS and GKE. A lightweight Tier-1 model reranks every request. A router escalates only the requests where extra inference-time compute is likely to help, and sends them to a more expensive Tier-2 procedure (self-consistency sampling, multi-path reasoning, or a larger model). The system has run in a production A/B experiment for over a month. Compared with uniform use of the expensive tier, CascadeRec has reduced inference cost by about 10%, with minimal latency impact and no quality regression observed to date. Tenant data cannot be released, so we also evaluate the pipeline on public benchmarks (MovieLens-1M and Amazon-Electronics). Segment-level analysis with paired-bootstrap confidence intervals shows that uniform LLM reranking lowers NDCG@10 for sparse-history users by up to 30%, and its only statistically significant gain appears on the segment with the longest histories. Escalating cold users can make the loss larger instead of repairing it. Which segments benefit from escalation depends on the domain, and this motivates the segment-gated rollout we use in production. We describe the deployment architecture, the measured benefits, and lessons learned, including inter-tenant load imbalance as a driver for cascading and the handling of LLM output-parsing failures at the serving layer.

</details>


### [141] [SteerGR: Steering Generative Recommendation via Sparse Autoencoders for Controllable Game Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08790)
*Sunkyung Lee,Xiaokai Wei,Da Long,Hyunsik Jeon,Jiajun Wu,Rithwik Seth,Jongwuk Lee,Michelle Gong,Gungor Polatkan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 SteerGR 框架，利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）实现生成式推荐的概念级可控，无需重训模型即可将推荐推向或远离目标游戏玩法概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐基于用户交互历史预测物品标识，但对推荐物品属性的显式控制研究不足；核心难点在于将人类可读概念与内部特征关联，使干预能真正改变推荐结果。

Method: 三阶段框架：(i) 概念定义与接地——用 LLM 定义游戏玩法概念；(ii) 特征发现与选择——依据概念关联发现候选特征，并通过调控效果选出最优特征；(iii) 稀疏特征干预——放大或抑制所选特征以引导推荐，无需重训推荐器。

Result: 在真实游戏数据集上，用两个工业级生成式推荐模型进行实验，所选特征能成功将推荐引导至目标概念或远离目标概念。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将基于 SAE 的引导方法从大语言模型拓展到工业生成式推荐，实现了概念级的推荐可控性。

Abstract: Generative recommendation aims to predict item identifiers from user interaction histories, but explicit control over the properties of recommended items remains underexplored. A key challenge is to connect human-readable concepts to internal features whose interventions effectively change the recommended items. To address this, we propose Steerable Generative Recommender (SteerGR), a framework that uses sparse autoencoders for concept-level control in game discovery. SteerGR proceeds through three stages: (i) Concept Definition and Grounding for defining play concepts with an LLM; (ii) Feature Discovery and Selection for discovering candidate features by their concept associations and selecting one by its steering effect; and (iii) Sparse Feature Intervention for amplifying or suppressing selected features to steer recommendations without retraining the recommender. Extensive experiments with two industrial generative recommendation models on a real-world game dataset show that the selected features successfully steer recommendations toward or away from target concepts. We believe this work takes a first step to extend SAE-based steering from LLMs to industrial generative recommendation.

</details>


### [142] [Generative Search and Recommendation at Shopify](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08804)
*Juhana Kangaspunta,Khalil Hajji,Darshan Fofadiya,Kyumin Park,Michael Garner,Anthony Maltsev,Ryan Synk,Jose Sepulveda,Joshua Crisologo,Shuying Sun,Akshay Soni*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SID-LLM 将搜索与推荐统一为生成语义 ID（由内容衍生 token 组成的短序列），在 Shopify 十亿级商品目录上实现生产可用的检索系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shopify 需在严格延迟约束下从不断变化的十亿级商品目录中检索相关商品；搜索与推荐通常分开建模，缺乏共享表示与训练目标。

Method: 1) 在目录数据与行为数据上联合预训练 SID-LLM，使搜索与推荐共享表示与目标；2) 用强化学习提升搜索相关性（judged relevance）；3) 从任务专属专家蒸馏以提升推荐质量（不损害搜索质量）；4) 服务端采用多 token 预测 + 轻量 ranker，降低延迟并提升检索质量。

Result: 作为独立搜索系统，质量与 Shopify 现有多模型搜索栈相当且满足生产延迟要求；接入搜索与推荐系统后，离线评测与线上 A/B 测试均显著提升。

Conclusion: 语义 ID 生成可作为大规模商品发现（搜索+推荐）的统一实用方案。

Abstract: Search and recommendation at Shopify require retrieving relevant products from a changing catalog of billions of products under strict latency constraints. We present SID-LLM, which casts both tasks as generating semantic IDs: short sequences of content-derived tokens representing products. We jointly pre-train SID-LLM on catalog and behavioral data, giving search and recommendation a shared representation and training objective. We show that reinforcement learning further improves judged search relevance. Furthermore, distillation from task-specific experts improves recommendation quality over joint supervised fine-tuning without a statistically detected loss in search quality. Finally, for efficient serving, we pair multi-token prediction with a lightweight ranker, improving retrieval quality over our autoregressive baseline while reducing latency. We evaluate SID-LLM both as a standalone retriever and as a component of Shopify's production search and recommendation systems. As a standalone search system, SID-LLM achieves quality comparable to Shopify's existing multi-model search stack while meeting production latency requirements. Integrating SID-LLM into the search and recommendation stacks provides significant gains in both offline evaluations and online A/B tests. Together, these results demonstrate the practical value of semantic-ID generation as a unified approach to product discovery at scale.

</details>


### [143] [SkinMind: Equipping Agentic RAG with Human-Inspired Graph Probing for Reliable Dermatology AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08935)
*Jing Hao,Weinan Zhou,Zhihong Lu,Yaling Shen,Yiming Xu,Yefeng Liu,Siyuan Yan,Shuaibo Li,Ming Dai,Junjun He,Zuyi Zhu,Jinjie Gu,Lijun Wu,Jian Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SkinMind：面向皮肤科的多模态智能体式RAG框架，通过图探测工具在皮肤科知识图谱（SkinMind-KG）上迭代检索文本与视觉证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮肤科AI需要跨异构文本与视觉来源的可验证证据，但传统RAG依赖对扁平语料的单次检索，难以自适应检索粒度、探索多跳关系、整合多模态临床证据。

Method: 将检索建模为编排器与图环境之间的迭代交互；提供8个层级化图探测工具（识别实体、查看节点信息、遍历关系与路径、检索源文本与视觉证据），采用渐进披露机制自适应决定检索模态、探索方向与证据粒度，并构建查询专属证据子图。同时基于6200万token皮肤科指南/教科书语料与42.3万张图像，在皮肤科医生参与设计的模式与冲突消解机制指导下构建SkinMind-KG（超100万节点、200万边）。

Result: 在六个皮肤科VQA基准、两种策略模型设置下，SkinMind相较直接回答分别提升8.70分（GPT-5.4-mini）和8.89分（Gemini-2.5-Flash），并持续优于其他RAG方法。

Conclusion: SkinMind与SkinMind-KG为皮肤科证据支撑的多模态推理提供了有效基础。

Abstract: Reliable dermatology AI requires access to verifiable evidence distributed across heterogeneous textual and visual sources. However, conventional retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) typically relies on one-shot retrieval from flat corpora, limiting its ability to adapt retrieval granularity, explore multi-hop relations, and integrate multimodal clinical evidence. We introduce SkinMind, a multimodal agentic RAG framework that equips policy models with human-inspired graph probing for autonomous evidence seeking. SkinMind organizes retrieval as an iterative interaction between an orchestrator and a graph environment. Through eight hierarchical graph-probing tools, the orchestrator progressively identifies relevant entities, inspects node-level information, traverses relations and paths, and retrieves source-text and visual evidence. This progressive-disclosure design allows the model to adaptively determine the retrieval modality, exploration direction, and evidence granularity while constructing a query-specific evidence subgraph. To support autonomous graph probing, we construct SkinMind-KG from a 62M-token corpus of dermatology guidelines and textbooks and 423K images, guided by a dermatologist-informed schema and a conflict-resolution mechanism. The resulting graph contains over 1.0M nodes and 2.0M edges, unifying textual, relational, and visual evidence for retrieval. Across six dermatology VQA benchmarks and two policy-model settings, SkinMind improves the average score over direct answering by 8.70 and 8.89 points with GPT-5.4-mini and Gemini-2.5-Flash, respectively, while consistently outperforming competing RAG methods. Together, SkinMind and SkinMind-KG provide an effective foundation for evidence-grounded multimodal reasoning in dermatology.

</details>


### [144] [Rethinking Latent Reasoning for Generative Recommendation: Beyond Teacher-Generated CoT and Recurrent Hidden-State Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09006)
*Yiwen Chen,Fuwei Zhang,Zehao Chen,Deqing Wang,Hehan Li,Peizhi Xu,Hanmeng Liu,Shuanglong Li,Xin Pei,Fuzhen Zhuang,Zhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PHiT 用从用户历史物品中直接提取的语义目标来监督“历史分析型”思考 token，无需昂贵 CoT 标注和循环隐状态反馈，仅靠 SFT 即在四个推荐基准上超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐中的潜在推理存在两大实际障碍：一是构造教师模型生成的思维链（CoT）监督成本高，二是循环隐状态反馈需要专门的执行循环，工程复杂。

Method: 先做步级诊断，衡量推荐 CoT 各步骤对下一物品预测和推理链连贯性的贡献，发现“历史分析”步骤是唯一在两方面均正向的步骤。据此提出 PHiT：聚合缓存的稀疏物品编码构造语义目标，监督不同词表思考 token 的上下文状态；所有思考 token 作为普通输入 ID 一次性在单次因果 prefill 中提供，去除循环末状态反馈。

Result: 仅用监督微调（SFT），PHiT 在四个推荐基准上超过对比基线；受控消融验证了“输入匹配的语义监督”的价值；成本测量显示目标构造快速，训练时间与仅推荐思考 token 的基线相当。

Conclusion: PHiT 通过重新设计监督来源与计算接口，绕开 CoT 标注和循环执行循环，实现了简单高效且有效的生成式推荐潜在推理。

Abstract: Latent reasoning in generative recommendation faces two practical barriers: constructing teacher-generated chain-of-thought (CoT) supervision is costly, and recurrent hidden-state feedback requires a specialized execution loop. We revisit both the supervision source and the computation interface. A step-level diagnosis measures how recommendation CoT supports next-item prediction and trace coherence. In the analyzed traces, history analysis is the only step with positive mean contributions on both dimensions, while later preference and intent steps have negative mean answer contributions. Guided by this finding, we propose PHiT (Prefilled, History-Informed Thought Tokens), which learns intermediate states from semantic targets extracted directly from observed item histories. PHiT aggregates cached sparse item encodings to construct these targets and supervises the contextual states of distinct vocabulary thought tokens. All thought tokens are supplied together as ordinary input IDs during a single causal prefill, removing the need for recurrent final-state feedback. With supervised fine-tuning alone, PHiT outperforms the compared baselines across four recommendation benchmarks. Controlled ablations support the value of input-matched semantic supervision, while cost measurements show fast target construction with training time comparable to the recommendation-only thought-token baseline.

</details>


### [145] [MV-IVF: Multi-Vector Retrieval via Multi-Vector Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09040)
*Kishen N Gowda,Laxman Dhulipala,Lars Gottesbüren,Rajesh Jayaram*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MV-IVF 直接在多头向量数据上构建原生 IVF 倒排索引，借助 MV-Lloyd（Chamfer 目标下的 Lloyd 式聚类）建树，查询时做 best-first beam 搜索并配合量化与缓存优化，在 BEIR/LoTTE 上于 90% Recall-10@10 下取得最高 8.03 倍低延迟与 173.5 倍 QPS 提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多头向量检索通过 MaxSim 等集合到集合交互提升检索质量，但代价高昂；现有系统多依赖代理表示或"过滤-重排"流水线，把候选生成退化为单向量检索，缺少对多头向量原生的索引方案。

Method: 提出 MV-Lloyd：Chamfer 目标下的 Lloyd 式点云聚类算法，关键观察是点云分配后，中心更新等价于对簇内展平嵌入做标准单向量聚类，从而可高效构建内部节点为"中心点云"的层次 IVF 树；查询时用 best-first beam 搜索定位叶子簇再打分，并用低比特量化、查询压缩和缓存友好的批处理加速；实现为 C++ 库 MVSIC 及统一评测框架。

Result: 在 BEIR 与 LoTTE 文本检索基准上，MV-IVF 具有良好延迟-召回权衡、最佳批量吞吐前沿，以及较优的构建时间与索引大小；在 90% Recall-10@10 处，单查询延迟最多降低 8.03 倍，QPS 最多提升 173.5 倍。

Conclusion: 原生 IVF 式索引对可扩展多头向量检索是实用且有效的，为多向量检索提供了区别于代理表示与过滤-重排的补充方向。

Abstract: Multi-vector retrieval represents each query and document as a set of embeddings and scores relevance via fine-grained set-to-set interactions such as MaxSim (Chamfer similarity). This expressiveness improves retrieval quality over single-vector representations, but makes search substantially more expensive. Existing systems address this challenge primarily through proxy representations or filter-and-rerank pipelines that reduce candidate generation to single-vector search. We study the complementary direction of building a native IVF-style index directly over multi-vector data.
  We introduce MV-IVF, an inverted-file index for multi-vector retrieval built on MV-Lloyd, a new Lloyd-style algorithm for clustering point clouds under a Chamfer objective. Our key observation is that once point clouds are assigned to clusters, the center-update step reduces to standard single-vector clustering over the flattened embeddings in each cluster. This enables efficient construction of a hierarchical IVF tree with center point clouds at internal nodes.
  At query time, MV-IVF performs best-first beam search over the tree to identify promising leaf clusters, then scores the documents in those leaves. We further accelerate search using low-bit quantization, query compression, and cache-efficient batched search. We implement MV-IVF in MVSIC, an optimized C++ library and unified evaluation framework for multi-vector retrieval that includes several major approaches.
