Across BEIR and LoTTE text retrieval benchmarks, MV-IVF achieves strong latency--recall tradeoffs, the best batched throughput frontiers, and favorable build-time and index-size profiles. At $90\%$ Recall-$10$@$10$, MV-IVF achieves up to $8.03\times$ lower single-query latency and up to $173.5\times$ higher QPS than the baselines, demonstrating that native IVF-style indexing is practical and effective for scalable multi-vector retrieval.

</details>


### [146] [PAGSL: Path-Augmenting Graph Structure Learning for explainable scholar recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09126)
*Jiale Xu,Mingshu Ai,Junjie Huang,Tao Jia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PAGSL：结合图增强与路径语义的可解释学者推荐模型，在三个学术数据集上推荐性能显著优于SOTA，且解释更具深度与个性化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释学者推荐框架受优化噪声与"枢纽节点偏差"困扰，生成的解释浅显、缺乏层级深度与个性化。

Method: 提出Path-Augmenting Graph Structure Learning (PAGSL)：通过图增强激活实体间潜在学术路径，用基于路径的神经网络编码路径进行推荐；设计带路径分数优化的掩码机制以生成简洁有信息量的解释；融合表示学习与语义路径知识，从两方面提供可解释结果，并采用异构卷积。

Result: 在Scholat、AMiner、ResearchGate三个数据集（约4.9万作者、33万余交互记录）上验证：F1提升6.31%、Accuracy提升6.29%、AUC提升5.95%；并给出可解释性的定量结果与可视化；消融实验验证异构卷积与路径表示学习的有效性。

Conclusion: PAGSL在推荐准确性与可解释性上均取得强表现，能缓解枢纽偏差并生成更简洁、有深度且个性化的解释。

Abstract: Explainable scholar recommendation aims to identify potential scholars of interest and provide reasonable explanations for the recommendations. However, existing frameworks struggle with optimization-induced noise and "hub node bias", yielding trivial explanations that lack hierarchical depth and personalization. To this end, we propose a novel explainable scholar recommendation model, Path-Augmenting Graph Structure Learning (named PAGSL). PAGSL combines representation learning and semantic path knowledge to ensure recommendation performance while providing interpretable results from two perspectives. Specifically, it activates latent academic paths among entities through graph augmentation and employs a path-based neural network to encode these paths for recommendation. A masking mechanism with path score optimization is designed to generate concise and informative explanations. We evaluate PAGSL on three academic datasets (Scholat, AMiner, and ResearchGate), comprising approximately 49,000 authors and over 330,000 interaction records. Experimental evaluations validate that PAGSL outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving significant improvements of 6.31\% in F1-score, 6.29\% in Accuracy and 5.95\% in AUC for recommendation. Crucially, in terms of explainability, we provide quantitative results and visualization effects. Furthermore, ablation studies validate the effectiveness of our heterogeneous convolution and path representation learning. PAGSL achieves strong performance in both recommendation accuracy and explainability.

</details>


### [147] [Structure and Lineage Evidence for Vietnamese Statutory Article Retrieval on DRiLL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09200)
*Duc-Vu Nguyen,Ngan Luu-Thuy Nguyen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 在越南语法条检索任务DRiLL上，引入条文身份/形式、同法支持、引用与谱系等特征，通过共享候选的选择器、listwise Qwen证据与学习器设置，将官方F2从71.77%提升至72.91%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究越南语法定条文检索，探索条文身份与形式、同法支持、引用与谱系等证据特征对检索性能的贡献，并量化其作用。

Method: 在DRiLL数据集上，采用共享候选的选择器、listwise Qwen证据以及学习器设置，并在五个随机种子下评估；通过决策树分裂增益（训练目标下降量）量化新增特征的贡献，并结合案例进行分析。

Result: 五个随机种子下官方F2由71.77%提升至72.91%，两条文问题上描述性增益最大；新增特征占所提选择器总分裂增益的18.20%，主要来自同法与引用证据；案例揭示基础条文与修正、伴随条款恢复、干扰项剔除及失败情形。

Conclusion: 结果支持在其他数据集、表示与学习器上进一步研究，但未确立单个特征的独立效应，也不依赖LightGBM、手工评分或显式图结构。

Abstract: We study Vietnamese statutory Article retrieval on DRiLL using features for provision identity and local form, same-law support, and citation and lineage evidence. Across five random seeds, selectors sharing candidates, listwise Qwen evidence, and learner settings improve official $F_2$ from 71.77% to 72.91%, with the largest descriptive gain on two-Article questions. Added features account for 18.20% of the proposed selector's total decision-tree split gain, measured as training-objective reduction, mainly from same-law and citation evidence. Cases reveal base provisions and amendments, companion recovery, distractor removal, and failures. These findings motivate research on other datasets, representations, and learners without establishing individual feature effects or requiring LightGBM, hand-crafted scores, or explicit graphs.

</details>


### [148] [Orthogonal Norm Growth in Two-Tower Retrieval: A Popularity Mechanism for Unified Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09294)
*Andrei Atamaniuk*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究统一搜索与推荐系统共享物品表征时，重复更新导致物品向量范数增长，并在未归一化点积检索下影响曝光。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一搜索和推荐系统会从两个交互流更新同一批物品向量，作者想弄清重复更新是否会放大物品向量范数，并在原始内积服务下造成检索偏差。

Method: 理论推导：分析仅余弦损失下输出梯度与当前物品向量正交（切于等范数球），给出其"实际移动仍保持切向"的充分条件，并指出这并非联合SGD的定理。实验：在MovieLens 32M与Amazon Books上做受控消融；在非线性塔（每物品ID向量+共享内容编码）中把范数变化分解为ID更新贡献与其他输出变化，并用匹配训练干预去除ID行更新带来的即时范数增长。

Result: 累积的切向移动与"频率—范数"关联同时出现，在原始点积（而非归一化物品）排序下带来额外的头部物品曝光；非线性塔中活跃物品的移动近似切向；去除ID更新即时范数增长的干预降低了范数相关曝光，但同时也损害了推荐效用。

Conclusion: 该条件性结果不能等同于联合SGD的定理；所提干预是诊断工具而非缓解方案；日志仅限推荐侧，因此该形式化提供的是共享表征审计方法，联合流量中的普遍性仍有待研究。

Abstract: Unified search and recommendation systems may update the same item vectors from both interaction streams. We study how repeated updates can increase an item vector's norm and affect retrieval under an unnormalized dot product. A cosine-only loss makes the output gradient perpendicular to the current item vector - tangent to its constant-norm sphere. Training changes encoder parameters, however, so the realized item-vector movement need not remain tangent. We derive sufficient conditions under which it does; every nonzero update then increases squared norm by its squared length. When the tasks share an item encoder, sequential updates from both can accumulate in the same norm, which raw-inner-product serving can expose for either task. This conditional result is not a theorem about joint SGD. Controlled ablations test these conditions. On MovieLens 32M and Amazon Books, accumulated tangent movement accompanies frequency-norm association and additional head-item exposure under raw-dot rather than normalized-item ranking. In a nonlinear tower combining a per-item ID vector with a shared content encoding, active item movement remains near-tangent. We separate its norm change into the ID-update contribution and other output changes, then use a matched training intervention that removes immediate norm growth caused by the ID-row update. The intervention reduces norm-related exposure but also recommendation utility, so it is diagnostic rather than a proposed mitigation. Our logs are recommendation-only: the formulation supplies a shared-representation audit, while prevalence in joint traffic remains open.

</details>


### [149] [SPARSEUP: Towards Better Recipes for Training Modern Learned Sparse Retrievers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09680)
*Thibault Formal,Pierre Erbacher,Cosimo Rulli*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出一套训练Splade类稀疏编码器的配方，基于LateOn骨干网络和数据训练出SPARSEUP，在BEIR-13上达到56.4平均nDCG@10，为150M参数以下公开词汇表稀疏编码器中最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习式稀疏嵌入（sparse embeddings）是有力的稠密检索替代方案，但性能落后于单向量和late interaction模型。其一大原因在于训练极为困难，通常需要非常严苛的收敛条件。本文旨在改善稀疏编码器的效率-效果权衡并提升训练稳定性。

Method: 1) 分析logit分布对训练稳定性和最终稀疏度的影响；2) 提出按位置的fan-out控制（per-position fan-out control），即硬性限制每个位置可输出的词汇维度数量；3) 采用简单的词汇表合并策略，去除冗余词项（如大小写与词边界变体）；4) 将整套配方应用于LateOn骨干与训练数据。

Result: 训练得到的SPARSEUP在BEIR-13上取得56.4平均nDCG@10，作者所知为150M参数以下公开词汇表稀疏编码器中的最高水平。

Conclusion: 通过系统性地处理logit分布、引入按位置fan-out硬约束以及词汇表合并，可在现代骨干与数据上稳定训练Splade类编码器，获得优于现有稀疏编码器的效率-效果权衡。

Abstract: Learned sparse embeddings are a powerful alternative to dense retrieval, yet they have fallen behind single-vector and late interaction models. One reason for their limited adoption is that they are notoriously difficult to train, generally requiring very tight conditions to converge. We present a recipe for training Splade-like encoders on modern backbones and data that achieves a better efficiency-effectiveness trade-off. We discuss the role of logit distribution and its impact on the training stability and the final sparsity of the encoder. We also introduce a new per-position fan-out control - a hard cap on how many vocabulary dimensions each position may emit. We further apply a simple vocabulary merging strategy that removes redundant entries - such as case and word-boundary variants of the same word - which are common in cased BPE-based backbones. We apply our recipe to the LateOn backbone and training data, obtaining SPARSEUP, a sparse encoder that reaches 56.4 average nDCG@10 on BEIR-13, the highest we know of among public vocabulary-based sparse encoders below 150M parameters.

</details>


### [150] [SAGE-PAIR: Source-Pretrained Routed Residual Adaptation for Sparse-Feedback Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09728)
*Weiqing Zeng,Zida Yang,Guquan You,Jiexin Zheng,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SAGE-PAIR通过从相邻源时段预训练低维全局残差及用户画像条件化的专家路由，将目标窗口的稀疏反馈用于校准而非重学习，在0.05-1.0%反馈下实现NDCG@10相对提升1.77%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统常在新服务窗口内、结果数据不足以重训模型时就上线；在0.05-1.0%极稀疏反馈下，微调与元学习仍需从稀少标签估计目标专属更新，更新方向难以辨识。

Method: 提出SAGE-PAIR分阶段适配器：在相邻源时段预训练低维全局残差，并基于源用户画像加软路由的残差专家；目标阶段仅用稀疏反馈校准该残差状态，而非重新学习推荐器。

Result: KuaiSAR-v2锁定测试集上，SAGE-PAIR(Full) NDCG@10相对提升1.77%；核心协议中路由专家较源预训练PAIR再增0.25%；匹配的LoRA与Adapter-Tuning基线仍接近源模型；适配与推理均为毫秒级。

Conclusion: 稀疏反馈适配的关键在于更新空间的可辨识性而非标签数量：源预训练提供可估计的修正方向，用户条件化路由在全局修正之上贡献一致且有区分的跨用户修正。

Abstract: Recommendation systems often enter a new serving window before enough outcomes are available to retrain the deployed model. We study this regime with 0.05-1.0% target feedback, a range that spans our stress tests while keeping supervision far below the degrees of freedom of the source recommender. At this scale, existing fine-tuning and meta-learning methods still estimate target-specific updates from extremely sparse feedback, leaving the update direction weakly identified. SAGE-PAIR instead transfers a source-supported residual correction space and only calibrates this space using target outcomes. We introduce SAGE-PAIR, a staged adapter that pretrains a low-dimensional global residual on an adjacent source period and adds a soft router over residual experts conditioned on source user profiles. Target feedback then calibrates this residual state rather than relearning the recommender. On a locked test split of KuaiSAR-v2, SAGE-PAIR (Full) achieves a 1.77% relative improvement in NDCG@10, while a core-protocol comparison shows a further 0.25% gain from routed experts beyond source-pretrained PAIR. Matched source-pretrained LoRA and Adapter-Tuning baselines remain near the source model, supporting the need for a different adaptation space. Thus, SAGE-PAIR improves ranking quality with millisecond-scale adaptation and inference costs. Sparse-feedback adaptation is fundamentally governed by the identifiability of the update space rather than by label count alone. Source pretraining supplies estimable correction directions for scarce labels, while user-conditioned routing contributes a consistent, differentiated cross-user correction on top of the global correction. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SAGE-PAIR-610E/.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [FasterRW: An Efficient Prior-Assisted Random Walk Solver for Thermal Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08490)
*Zixiao Wang,Tianshu Hou,Chenghan Wang,Zhen Zhuang,Leilei Jin,Tsung-Yi Ho,Farzan Farnia,Bei Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FasterRW 提出一种"延续值(continuation-value)"框架，用廉价的带噪先验替换截断后未知的温度尾部，把 Feynman–Kac 随机游走热仿真的截断偏差从取决于绝对温度变为取决于先验误差，并结合 kriging 精化多点查询，实现最高 28 倍加速且保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Feynman–Kac 随机游走可在不求解全局温度场的情况下估计局部温度，但在缺少 Dirichlet 边界的实际场景中，为控制截断残差需要极长路径，导致计算代价过高。

Method: 1) 证明"停时残差恒等式"：截断后期望尾部等于当前衰减权重乘停点处温度；2) 基于该恒等式构造先验校正的单点估计器，用廉价带噪先验替代未知延续值，并给出误差控制的阈值选取规则；3) 多点查询时，将跨查询的穿透尾部转为伪样本，在先验误差坐标下用 kriging 融合精化。

Result: 相比此前最先进方法，FasterRW 在保持精度的前提下最高实现 28 倍加速；代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过将截断偏差由绝对温度尺度转为先验误差尺度并引入无偏的尾部估计与空间统计精化，FasterRW 有效缓解了无边界热随机游走的截断瓶颈，为 IC 设计中的局部热估计提供了实用加速方案。

Abstract: Thermal simulation is becoming increasingly critical in modern IC design and manufacturing. Feynman--Kac random walk methods can estimate localized temperatures without solving the global field, but in practical settings without Dirichlet boundaries they require very long paths to control the truncation residual. This paper presents FasterRW, a continuation-value framework for accelerating Feynman--Kac thermal random walks. We prove a stopping-time residual identity showing that the expected tail after truncation equals the current attenuation weight multiplied by the temperature at the stopping location. This identity enables a prior-corrected single-point estimator: a cheap, noisy prior replaces the unknown continuation value, making the truncation bias scale with prior error rather than absolute temperature and yielding an error-controlled threshold-selection rule. For multi-point queries, FasterRW further converts cross-query pass-through tails into pseudo-samples and refines the resulting observations in prior-error coordinates through kriging. Compared with prior state-of-the-art methods, FasterRW achieves up to $28\times$ acceleration while maintaining accuracy. Our code is open-sourced at https://github.com/ShiningSord/FastRW.

</details>


### [152] [Embedding Consistent Recurrent Neural Networks in Finite Element Simulations for Path-Dependent Damage Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08494)
*Muhammed Adil Yatkın,Mihkel Kõrgesaar,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将训练好的RNN损伤准则用Fortran重写并嵌入Abaqus/Explicit的VUMAT中，提出"一致RNN（ConsRNN）"使预测在应变路径加密时收敛，实现结构尺度下的嵌入式断裂准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限元求解器中嵌入代理模型做钣金局部化物理预测时，必须保证预测与求解器的应变增量数目无关；而显式非线性代码中的增量数难以控制，SimpleRNN的预测会随增量数漂移，无法嵌入。

Method: 将训练好的RNN损伤准则用Fortran重实现，作为逐增量断裂准则嵌入VUMAT，随求解器推进网络状态；对比SimpleRNN与所提出的ConsRNN（其转移函数设计为随应变路径细化而收敛），二者均在双线性应变路径上训练，并在不同时间离散化与非线性历史下评估。

Result: SimpleRNN随增量数增加而漂移，不适合嵌入；ConsRNN在细化下收敛，但在多线性路径上相比SimpleRNN有明显固定分辨率精度损失。结构尺度上，嵌入的ConsRNN控制夹持钢板单元删除，峰值力与峰值位移与成熟的双参数断裂准则一致；峰前一致属构造使然（峰前无单元删除、本构路径相同），差异仅出现在峰后软化段。

Conclusion: 离散一致性是将循环代理模型嵌入显式FEM的决定性属性；本文首次在结构尺度上嵌入了采用非标准转移函数的定制循环单元。

Abstract: Capturing sheet-metal localisation physics with a trained surrogate inside a finite element (FEM) solver requires the surrogate to deliver consistent predictions irrespective of the solver's strain-increment count, which is difficult to control in non-linear explicit codes. We address this by re-implementing a trained recurrent neural network (RNN) damage criterion in Fortran and embed it as a live, increment-by-increment fracture criterion inside an Abaqus/Explicit user material subroutine (VUMAT), advancing the network state alongside the solver. Two architectures are compared: a SimpleRNN and the proposed Consistent RNN (ConsRNN), whose transition function is designed so that predictions converge as the strain path is refined rather than drifting with the increment count. Both are trained on bilinear strain paths and evaluated under varying temporal discretisations and nonlinear histories. SimpleRNN predictions drift as the increment count increases, which disqualifies it for embedding; ConsRNN converges under refinement, at a substantial fixed-resolution accuracy cost relative to SimpleRNN on multilinear paths. Deployed at structural scale, the embedded ConsRNN surrogate governs element deletion in a clamped steel plate and reaches the same peak force and peak displacement as an established two-parameter fracture criterion. This pre-peak agreement is expected by construction, since no element is deleted before the peak and the two runs therefore share an identical constitutive path; the responses diverge only on the post-peak softening branch. The results establish discretisation consistency as the decisive property for embedding recurrent surrogates in explicit FEM solvers, and report the first embedding of a customised, non-standard-transition recurrent cell at structural scale.

</details>


### [153] [Rethinking Data Selection as Distribution Matching on Semantic Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08495)
*Peng Sun,Yi Yang,Mingshuo Chen,Chunxiao Li,Yao Zhu,Huawen Shen,Tianfan Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QMAC提出一种质量感知的多频带特征函数匹配方法，将数据选择建模为分布匹配与质量过滤的联合优化问题，在4类数据、8个目标模型、44个基准上优于现有数据选择基线，部分设置下甚至超过全量数据训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令微调依赖海量训练数据，带来巨大计算与存储开销，且数据中存在冗余、噪声和低质量样本。现有数据选择方法多基于多样性、重要性或二者结合，未显式约束所选子集与全量数据在嵌入空间的分布一致性。

Method: QMAC在多个频率尺度上匹配子集与全量数据的经验特征函数，兼顾全局语义覆盖与局部扰动，同时利用任务相关的质量分数过滤噪声和低质量样本，实现分布匹配与质量过滤的联合优化。

Result: 在4种数据类型、8个目标模型、44个基准上系统评估。QMAC在标准图文指令数据上优于现有基线，在标准文本指令、图文推理和文本推理数据上显著超过随机选择，并在多种模型与预算组合下超过全量数据训练。

Conclusion: QMAC通过分布一致性约束与质量过滤的联合建模，实现了高效且有效的数据选择，能在大幅削减训练数据的同时保持甚至提升模型性能；代码将在论文录用后公开。

Abstract: Instruction tuning of large language models and large vision language models relies on massive training data, which incurs substantial computational and storage costs. Meanwhile, training data often contains redundancy, noise, and low quality samples. Existing data selection methods mostly rely on diversity, importance, or a combination of both, without explicitly constraining the distributional consistency between the selected subset and the full data in the embedding space. We propose QMAC, a quality aware multiband characteristic function matching method that formulates data selection as a joint optimization problem of distribution matching and quality filtering. QMAC matches the empirical characteristic functions of the selected subset and the full data across multiple frequency scales, capturing both global semantic coverage and local perturbations, while using task related quality scores to filter noisy and low quality samples. We systematically evaluate QMAC on 4 data types, 8 target models, and 44 benchmarks. Experimental results show that QMAC outperforms existing data selection baselines on standard image-text instruction data, substantially surpasses random selection on standard text instruction, image-text reasoning, and text reasoning data, and exceeds full data training in multiple model and budget combinations. The code will be made publicly available upon acceptance of the paper.

</details>


### [154] [Generative Reconstruction of Full-Field Sensor States for Safety-Critical Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08499)
*Umme Mahbuba Nabila,Linyu Lin,Majdi I. Radaideh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究面向HTTF高温试验设施，构建稀疏传感器场重建框架，在实验内与跨实验两种协议下评测六种方法；GNN-GRU VAE取得最低误差（MAE 11.73°C、RMSE 20.54°C、R²=0.98），section-context扩散模型跨实验迁移最优（RMSE 42.84°C、R²=0.91）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键数字孪生需要准确状态感知，但高温辐射环境中的热电偶会漂移、失效或间歇不可用，导致部分可观测性；因此需在稀疏传感器条件下重建不完整传感器场。

Method: 基于俄勒冈州立大学高温试验设施（HTTF），在实验内（PG-29 DCC）和跨实验（PG-27 PCC训练→PG-29测试）两种协议下评估六类方法：扩散模型、VAE、GAN三类条件生成模型，两种确定性RNN模型及岭回归；模型结合图神经网络空间编码与门控循环单元（GRU）时序建模，跨实验采用section-context条件化。

Result: 实验内：GNN-GRU VAE最优（MAE 11.73°C，RMSE 20.54°C，R²=0.98），确定性GNN-GRU与普通GRU的RMSE分别为25.26°C和35.22°C，表明空间建模有效；三种生成模型RMSE均比岭回归降低一半以上；已知传感器由91减至45时扩散模型仍保持R²=0.94。跨实验：section-context扩散模型迁移最佳（RMSE 42.84°C，R²=0.91）。

Conclusion: 时空生成建模对安全关键数字孪生中的缺失传感器重建具有显著价值，且结合图结构空间编码与section-context条件化可提升鲁棒性与跨实验泛化能力。

Abstract: Accurate reconstruction of incomplete sensor fields is critical for state awareness in safety-critical digital twins (DTs). Thermocouples in high-temperature and radiation environments may drift, fail, or become intermittently unavailable, causing partial observability. This study develops a sparse-sensor reconstruction framework for Oregon State University's High Temperature Test Facility (HTTF) and evaluates six methods under intra- and inter-experiment protocols: three conditional generative models (i.e., a diffusion model [DM], variational autoencoder [VAE], and generative adversarial network [GAN]), two deterministic recurrent neural-network models, and ridge regression. For intra-experiment reconstruction within the PG{-}29 depressurized conduction cooldown (DCC) test, the models combine graph-based spatial encoding with gated recurrent units. The graph neural network (GNN)--gated recurrent unit (GRU) VAE achieves the lowest error, with a mean absolute error (MAE) of $11.73,^\circ$C, a root mean square error (RMSE) of $20.54,^\circ$C, and an $R^2$ of 0.98. The deterministic GNN--GRU and plain GRU obtain RMSE values of $25.26,^\circ$C and $35.22,^\circ$C, respectively, demonstrating the benefit of spatial modeling. All three generative models reduce RMSE by more than half compared with ridge regression. The DM maintains an $R^2$ of 0.94 when known sensors decrease from 91 to 45. For inter-experiment reconstruction, models trained on PG{-}27 pressurized conduction cooldown (PCC) are evaluated on PG{-}29 using section-context conditioning. The section-context DM achieves the best transfer performance, with an RMSE of $42.84,^\circ$C and an $R^2$ of 0.91. These results demonstrate the value of spatiotemporal generative modeling for missing-sensor reconstruction in safety-critical DT applications.

</details>


### [155] [Adaptive Depth Aggregation in Graph Transformers for Long-Range Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08500)
*Yifeng Lu,Zhen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出图Transformer的残差流用固定系数汇聚各深度贡献，形成与节点相关的深度聚合不匹配；提出AttnRes（含Block AttnRes）实现节点自适应深度聚合，并在GPS/GRIT上于ECHO五个任务均降低MAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图Transformer只关注跨节点通信（全局或结构感知）的改进，但标准残差流以固定单位系数、跨节点共享的方式累加各深度计算。一旦中间贡献已产生，接口无法按节点显式重新加权，作者假设当中间贡献的相对效用在节点与结构上下文间存在差异时，会产生深度方向的聚合不匹配。

Method: 将图Transformer的残差计算形式化为因果的逐节点历史（图传播贡献与FFN贡献），使深度聚合成为显式接口选择；在此基础上用Attention Residuals（AttnRes）实现对该历史的节点自适应聚合，同时保留骨干网络的图传播与辅助状态机制。Block AttnRes通过对连续贡献池化，降低长历史的routing开销。将该接口实例化到GPS与GRIT上。

Result: 在ECHO全部五个任务上，两种变体的MAE均低于各自独立调优的骨干网络，且每个任务中至少有一种变体取得所有对比方法中的最低均值。仅路由的donor-swap干预实验支持了"深度聚合不匹配"假设，附加消融验证了关键设计选择。

Conclusion: 节点自适应的深度聚合可作为跨节点通信的有益补充，共同服务于长程图推理。

Abstract: Graph Transformers improve long-range node-to-node communication through global or structure-aware interactions. However, improving cross-node communication does not determine how computations produced at different depths should be combined. Standard residual streams accumulate successive computations with fixed unit coefficients shared across nodes. This fixed aggregation is a residual-interface constraint: once intermediate contributions have been produced, the interface cannot explicitly reweight them by node. We hypothesize that this constraint can create a depth-wise aggregation mismatch when the relative utility of these intermediate contributions varies across nodes and structural contexts. To study this hypothesis, we formalize Graph Transformer residual computation as a causal node-wise history of graph-propagation and feed-forward network (FFN) contributions, making depth aggregation an explicit interface choice. We realize node-adaptive aggregation over this history with Attention Residuals (AttnRes) while preserving the backbone's graph-propagation and auxiliary-state machinery. Block AttnRes reduces the routing cost of long histories relative to Full AttnRes by pooling consecutive contributions. We instantiate the interface in GPS and GRIT and evaluate it on all five ECHO tasks. Both variants achieve lower mean absolute error (MAE) than their independently tuned backbones on every task, and at least one obtains the lowest mean among all compared methods in each task. Routing-only donor-swap interventions support the mismatch hypothesis, while additional ablations examine the key design choices. Together, these results support node-adaptive depth aggregation as a useful complement to cross-node communication in long-range graph reasoning.

</details>


### [156] [Emergent Low-Dimensional Topological Dynamics Dictate Generalization and Escape Mechanics in Billion-Parameter Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08502)
*Dongqing Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出模型优化轨迹被动态约束在低维"执行子流形"（经验有效维度≤12）上的观点，据此推导出基于投影子空间持续同调（persistent homology）的拓扑泛化界，并给出严格鞍点指数逃逸的Morse-Betti机制；进而提出用随机Lanczos在活跃Hessian子空间内计算拓扑代理，衍生Top-SGD/Top-AdamW，在ImageNet上实现14.1%训练时间缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代过参数化网络泛化能力远超经典统计学习界，而现有理论无法解释。作者假设优化轨迹并不均匀探索ℝ^N，而是受约束于低维流形；希望用拓扑工具把"有效维度低"这一经验现象转化为可严谨表述的泛化界，并进一步指导优化实践。

Method: 1) 为参数空间引入正则化Hessian度量 g_ij = ∇_i∇_j L + εδ_ij；2) 在损失下水平集上分析随机Langevin扩散，基于投影子空间的持续同调推导拓扑泛化界；3) 将Morse指标跃迁与Betti数湮灭（β_1→0）联系，解释各向异性噪声下严格鞍点的指数逃逸；4) 用限定在活跃Hessian子空间的随机Lanczos迭代计算投影持续同调代理，规避维度灾难；5) 以活跃曲率特征动态调节学习率，构成Top-SGD与Top-AdamW。

Result: 在80+种架构（ResNet、ViT、7B级LLM）上验证框架；Top-AdamW/Top-SGD在ImageNet上将总墙钟训练时间减少14.1%，且已计入全部计算与内存开销；代码已开源。

Conclusion: 过参数化网络的优化轨迹处于低维执行子流形，使拓扑泛化分析与高效计算成为可能；该理论可转化为实用的优化器封装，在不增加净开销的前提下带来实际加速，为拓扑视角连接学习理论、优化与规模化训练提供了新范式。

Abstract: The remarkable generalization of modern over-parameterized neural networks defies classical statistical learning bounds. Here we establish that optimization trajectories in billion-parameter models do not sample the ambient dimension $\mathbb{R}^N$ homogeneously; instead, they are dynamically constrained to a low-dimensional execution submanifold $\mathcal{M}_{\text{exec}}$ (with empirically observed effective dimension $d_{\text{eff}} \le 12$). By equipping the parameter space with a regularized Hessian metric $g_{ij} = \nabla_i \nabla_j L + εδ_{ij}$ and analyzing stochastic Langevin diffusion on loss sublevel sets, we derive a rigorous Topological Generalization Bound grounded in projected subspace persistent homology. Furthermore, by linking Morse index transitions to Betti number annihilation ($β_1 \to 0$), we formulate an exact mathematical mechanism for exponential escape from strict saddles under anisotropic noise. We validate our framework across 80+ architectures (ResNets, Vision Transformers, and 7B LLMs). To bridge theoretical topology with computational feasibility, we resolve the curse of dimensionality by computing projected persistent homology proxies via randomized Lanczos iterations restricted strictly within active Hessian subspaces. Building upon this mathematical foundation, we introduce Topological-Adaptive Optimization (Top-SGD and Top-AdamW)---a family of wrapper algorithms that dynamically modulate learning rates using active curvature signatures. Extensive benchmarks demonstrate a 14.1\% reduction in total wall-clock training time on ImageNet with all computational and memory overheads fully accounted for. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/imslab-research/Topological-SGD.

</details>


### [157] [Does Country Matter? Evaluating Geographic Sensitivity of Large Language Models in Zero-Shot Chronic Kidney Disease Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08507)
*Sirajam Munira,Muhammad Ashad Kabir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究系统改变提示中的国家属性（25个全球语境），在孟加拉国社区CKD数据集（284人）上评估Qwen3 8B与Mistral 7B，发现非CKD预测高度稳定，而CKD阳性预测对地理语境敏感，且国家分组不反映流行病学相似性而似模型内部表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM具备强零样本推理能力，但对提示上下文敏感，影响部署可靠性。作者欲探究：在患者特征完全不变的情况下，提示中的地理语境是否独立影响基于LLM的慢性肾病（CKD）筛查结果。

Method: 使用孟加拉国社区CKD数据集（284人：112例CKD、172例非CKD），保持全部临床输入不变、系统性地替换25个国家属性；评估两个开放权重模型（Qwen3 8B、Mistral 7B）；在156名两模型基线均预测正确的"共同正确子集"上开展实验。

Result: 非CKD预测在地理语境下高度稳定；CKD阳性预测明显更敏感——Qwen3的CKD预测数从基线65最低降至50，Mistral变化较小但不可忽略。国家语境分组既不匹配已知流行病学/医疗相似性，更像反映各模型内部对国家token的表征方式。

Conclusion: 地理token能在患者输入完全相同的情况下系统性改变临床筛查结局，对LLM决策支持在多样化医疗场景中的部署具有重要警示意义。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show strong zero-shot reasoning capabilities, yet their sensitivity to prompt context raises questions about reliability in deployment. This study investigates whether geographic context embedded in prompts influences LLM-based chronic kidney disease (CKD) screening, independent of any change in patient features. Using a community-based CKD dataset from Bangladesh (284 participants with 112 CKD and 172 non-CKD), we systematically varied the country attribute across 25 global contexts while holding all clinical inputs constant, and evaluated two open-weight LLMs (Qwen3 8B and Mistral 7B). Experiments were conducted on a common-correct subset of 156 participants for whom both models produced correct baseline predictions. Results show that non-CKD predictions were highly stable across geographic contexts, with both models maintaining consistent non-CKD predictions in nearly all settings. In contrast, CKD-positive predictions were considerably more sensitive. For Qwen3, CKD prediction dropped from a baseline of 65 to as low as 50 across country contexts, while Mistral showed smaller but non-negligible variation. Country contexts did not group according to known epidemiological or healthcare similarities; instead, groupings appear to reflect how country tokens are internally represented within each model. These findings demonstrate that geographic tokens can systematically shift clinical screening outcomes despite identical patient inputs, with implications for the deployment of LLM-based decision support in diverse healthcare settings.

</details>


### [158] [Interpretable Multifidelity Learning with Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks for Spatiotemporal Nuclear Reactor Flow Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08512)
*Meredith Eaheart,Majdi I. Radaideh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究针对压水堆堆芯193个燃料组件瞬态质量流率预测，比较黑箱多保真前馈神经网络（MFNN）与可解释的多保真Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（MFKAN），并借助TabDDPM合成数据缓解CFD瞬态稀缺问题。结果表明MFKAN精度与MFNN相当，且能给出闭式符号方程；跨保真预测精度本质上受限于两保真度之间的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真CFD核反应堆仿真计算代价高昂，多保真代理建模可用低成本低保真数据加少量高保真数据降本；作者想探究预测全堆芯质量流率所需高保真数据能否减少，并检验可解释KAN在多保真设定下是否可替代黑箱神经网络。

Method: 以压水堆堆芯193个燃料组件的瞬态质量流率为对象，对比多保真前馈神经网络（MFNN）与多保真Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（MFKAN）；因每保真度仅有一条CFD瞬态，采用TabDDPM生成合成数据增强跨保真预测，并进行组件级相关分析与符号方程提取。

Result: 堆芯跨保真相关性弱，问题难度大，两架构绝对精度均不理想；MFKAN在单步前向预测上与MFNN相当并额外提供闭式符号方程。合成数据增强下KAN与FNN自评估精度相近（Pearson约0.73–0.81），但真实跨保真预测很差（0.03–0.06）；各组件预测相关性与真实跨保真相关性高度一致（KAN r=0.840–0.896，FNN r=0.804–0.903）。

Conclusion: KAN类多保真建模在各设定下精度与MFNN持续相当，并额外给出精度不劣于样条表示的符号方程；同时表明跨保真预测精度从根本上受限于保真度间固有相关性，而非模型选择。

Abstract: High-fidelity computational fluid dynamics (CFD) simulations of nuclear reactor systems are computationally expensive, motivating machine learning methods that reduce cost while preserving accuracy. Multifidelity surrogate modeling addresses this by combining inexpensive low-fidelity data with limited high-fidelity data. This work investigates whether the high-fidelity data required to predict the spatiotemporal mass flow rate across all 193 fuel assemblies of a pressurized water reactor (PWR) core can be reduced, comparing black-box multifidelity feedforward neural networks (MFNNs) with interpretable multifidelity Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (MFKANs). Cross-fidelity correlation across the core is weak, making this a challenging multifidelity problem, and neither architecture predicts the transient particularly well in absolute terms. MFKAN nonetheless performs comparably to MFNN for one-time-step-forward prediction while also providing closed-form symbolic equations unavailable with MFNN. To address the scarcity of CFD transients, with only one available per fidelity level, TabDDPM-generated synthetic data was used to augment cross-fidelity forecasting. KAN and FNN achieve comparable self-evaluation accuracy (Pearson correlation of roughly 0.73--0.81), but both perform poorly at true cross-fidelity forecasting (correlations of 0.03--0.06). Assembly-level analysis shows that each assembly's forecast correlation tracks its real cross-fidelity correlation for both architectures ($r=0.840$--$0.896$ for KAN, $r=0.804$--$0.903$ for FNN), confirming that forecasting accuracy is fundamentally limited by how correlated the fidelity levels already are. Overall, KAN-based multifidelity modeling achieves accuracy consistently comparable to MFNN across all settings, while additionally providing symbolic equations that match or exceed the spline representation's accuracy.

</details>


### [159] [What Are LLM Agents Actually Learning on Low-Budget Molecular Optimization Benchmarks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08522)
*Jiayu Qin,Zhengquan Luo,Changyou Chen,Zhiqiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在50次oracle调用的PMO任务上，LLM agent的大量"增益"主要来自可检索的任务信息而非在线优化；隐藏任务名与参考化合物后AUC从14.44骤降至4.85，仅基于确定性检索的LLM-free基线即可达12.16（84%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工作报告LLM agent在低预算分子优化上取得巨大提升，但作者怀疑这些提升可能源于任务信息泄漏与宽松的oracle设置，而非真正的在线优化能力，因此希望拆解各因素的真实贡献。

Method: 在PMO（50次oracle调用）上做受控消融：对比完整任务信息、隐藏任务名/参考化合物、身份盲化（仅告知oracle形式）以及无LLM的确定性检索基线；开展23任务2×2消融与8任务三随机种子复现。另在AutoDock Vina蛋白靶点基准上测试单编辑随机游走下界，并以无检索的种群agent与REINVENT做匹配预算对照。

Result: sum AUC：完整agent 14.44 → 隐藏信息后4.85，身份盲化5.39，纯检索基线12.16（达完整agent的84%）。Vina基准上单编辑随机游走三种子复合分0.636±0.008、对接改善−1.11±0.04 kcal/mol，构成强下界；正向对照中无检索种群agent在匹配50次调用AUC上超过REINVENT，100次时达到最佳。

Conclusion: 低预算分子优化中的LLM增益在很大程度上可由离线可检索信息与宽松oracle解释，需引入两项廉价合理性检查：检索安全的PMO提示词，以及基于对接优化必须报告的随机游走下界。

Abstract: Recent work reports large gains for LLM agents on low-budget molecular optimization. We measure the contributions of online optimization, retrievable task information, and permissive oracle landscapes. On PMO at 50 oracle calls, a GPT-4o mini agent's sum AUC falls from 14.44 to 4.85 after task names and curated reference compounds are hidden. An identity-blinded condition that states only the oracle form (e.g., similarity to a hidden reference) scores 5.39, while a deterministic retrieval-only baseline with no LLM reaches 12.16 (84% of the full agent). A full 23-task 2x2 ablation and a three-seed replication on eight tasks support this interpretation. On a protein-targeted benchmark with an AutoDock Vina composite oracle, a single-edit random walk is a strong lower bound; across three seeds it obtains composite 0.636 +/- 0.008 and docking improvement -1.11 +/- 0.04 kcal/mol. As a positive control, a retrieval-free population agent exceeds REINVENT at a matched 50-call AUC budget, with its strongest result at 100 calls. These results motivate two inexpensive sanity checks: retrieval-safe PMO prompts and a random-walk floor for docking-based optimization.

</details>


### [160] [Evolutionary Adaptive Metric Learning: A Multi-Objective Framework for Learning Local Distance Functions in Nearest Neighbor Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08549)
*Isaac Touza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 EAML，一种通过低维参数化学习样本相关正定度量的进化自适应度量学习框架，并从理论与实验两方面分析其适用条件与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近邻分类器的几何性质由距离函数决定，而固定距离或全局学习度量在整个输入空间使用同一几何结构，忽略了不同区域中相关特征方向可能不同的现实情况。

Method: EAML 将局部度量分解为两部分：由锚点间 softmax 插值生成的输入相关对角缩放，以及全局低秩相关分量，从而以低维方式参数化实例相关正定度量；采用实数编码精英遗传算法，以结合留一预测误差、鲁棒性、度量平滑性与正则项的标量目标进行优化。理论部分证明了正定性、度量等价性、最优解存在性与度量场 Lipschitz 连续性，并在明确假设下分析预测稳定性、泛化界与进化收敛性。

Result: 在具有区域相关特征的人工合成数据上，EAML 能恢复局部度量变化并优于全局度量；在 13 个公开数据集上用嵌套 5 折交叉验证评估时，EAML 在部分数据集上有提升，但整体未显著优于全局度量基线；额外实验显示，在冗余特征与测试时噪声条件下，全局对角度量可能更优。

Conclusion: 研究明确了进化式局部度量学习可获益的条件，同时指出其实际局限性：局部自适应并非普遍有效，需结合数据结构、噪声与冗余特征情况谨慎选用。

Abstract: Nearest neighbour classifiers inherit the geometry of their distance function. Fixed distances and globally learned metrics apply the same geometry throughout the input space, although relevant feature directions may vary across regions. We propose EAML (Evolutionary Adaptive Metric Learning), a framework that learns instance-dependent positive-definite metrics through a low-dimensional parameterization. Each local metric combines an input-dependent diagonal scaling, generated by softmax interpolation between anchor points, with a global low-rank correlation component. A real-coded elitist genetic algorithm optimizes the parameters using a scalar objective combining leave-one-out prediction error, robustness, metric smoothness, and regularization. We establish positive definiteness, metric equivalence, existence of an optimal solution, and Lipschitz continuity of the metric field. We further investigate prediction stability, generalization bounds, and evolutionary convergence under explicit assumptions. Experiments on synthetic data with region-dependent feature relevance show that EAML can recover local metric variations and outperform global metrics. Evaluation on thirteen public datasets using nested 5-fold cross-validation reveals a more nuanced picture: EAML improves performance on selected datasets but does not significantly outperform global metric baselines overall. Additional experiments show that global diagonal metrics can be preferable under nuisance features and test-time noise. These findings identify the conditions under which evolutionary local metric learning is beneficial and highlight its practical limitations.

</details>


### [161] [How Small Can We Go? Calibrating Activation-Cache Compression for SAE Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08551)
*Abraham Yeung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究激活缓存压缩对稀疏自编码器(SAE)特征的影响，发现保留特征比例缺乏清晰尺度，并据此提出以"重训相同字节为上限、换随机种子为下限"的校准式测量方法，用于评估各种压缩编解码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练SAE需要海量模型激活缓存，压缩缓存可节省存储，但压缩会破坏SAE特征。作者想弄清压缩对特征的影响程度，并指出现有指标（如保留特征百分比）没有明确尺度基准，难以判断压缩好坏，因此需要一个合理的测量方法。

Method: 设计两类测试：冻结SAE测试（把压缩缓存喂给原fp32训练的SAE，看特征激活是否保持，并用相关性和L2距离衡量）和重训测试（在压缩缓存上重训SAE，看原特征留存比例）。为建立尺度，采用校准法：重训相同字节设定上限，换随机种子设定下限，再以各站点自身下限为基准衡量每种压缩编解码器的影响。在GPT-2 small和Pythia-1.4B的TopK SAE上测试int4量化、PCA、随机投影、通道标准化及bf16前向等方案。

Result: 重训测试中留存特征比例无清晰尺度：GPT-2上fp16保留60.6%，而仅改动百万分之一的极小fp32步长却只保留70.5%；换随机种子仅保留23.0%。冻结SAE相关性与L2距离排序相近，且同一冻结读数在不同站点对应不同留存比例。在站点校准后，逐token朴素int4在全部测试中损失特征多于重设种子，PCA和随机投影在GPT-2上同样如此；量化前做通道标准化可使int4在相同文件大小下高于下限；最小预算下bf16前向略差于旋转8位存储。校准一个站点需4次训练和2次评估。

Conclusion: 压缩对SAE特征影响不能用单一留存百分比衡量，需以站点校准的下限/上限为参照。校准显示朴素int4、PCA、随机投影可能不如换种子，而通道标准化+int4和旋转8位存储更可取。校准成本为4次训练加2次评估。

Abstract: To train a sparse autoencoder (SAE), you need a massive cache of model activations. We look into the effects that compression has on an SAE in two ways: we keep the original fp32-trained SAE and pass the compressed cache into the same SAE to see how many feature activations stay the same (the frozen-SAE test), and we retrain an SAE on the compressed cache and see how many of the original features still exist (the retraining test). We found that in the retraining test, there is no clear scale for retained features. On a GPT-2 cache, rounding everything to fp16 keeps 60.6% of features, but changing one value in a million by the smallest step fp32 allows (a change eight orders of magnitude smaller) keeps only 70.5%. Meanwhile, changing the random seed keeps 23.0%. For the frozen-SAE test, the frozen SAE's correlation with the original increases in order of how precise the codecs are, but plain $L_2$ distance gives nearly the same ordering. We also see the same frozen reading correspond to different percentages retained at different sites. This leaves the question of the right measurement. We build one by calibrating: retraining on identical bytes sets the ceiling, and a reseed sets the floor. We then measure the effect of each compressed codec against its own site's floor. With TopK SAEs on GPT-2 small and Pythia-1.4B at research-scale budgets, naïve per-token int4 loses more features than a reseed everywhere we tested, as do PCA and random projection on GPT-2. Standardizing each channel before quantizing brings int4 back above the floor in terms of retained features, at the same raw file size. At our smallest budget, a bf16 forward pass, which some pipelines use to save memory, loses slightly more features than rotated 8-bit storage. Calibrating a site costs four training runs and two evaluation passes.

</details>


### [162] [Minimum-Sufficient Knowledge and Computation for Wind Energy Predictive Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08558)
*Purbak Sengupta,Aparna S. varde,Emil Jørgensen Njor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"最小充分知识与计算"(MSKC)原则,并在知识引导机器学习(KGML)的自适应知识路由上做概念验证:按状态选择满足决策风险与部署约束的最低成本路线,仅在需要时才升级。风电爬坡事件预测实验表明,在35.9%的关系知识注入率下,自适应路由与"始终注入"在指标上统计无差异,但知识调用减少64%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资源受限的信息物理决策系统普遍采用固定的感知、知识与计算流水线,忽视状态相关的信息需求,导致在简单状态下浪费算力与知识调用。KGML等方法也无条件注入外部知识,而非按需注入,与绿色IT和可持续部署目标不符。

Method: 提出MSKC原则:为每类决策状态从多条路线(低成本到高成本)中选择满足决策风险与部署约束的最省成本路线,不足时逐级升级。以KGML的自适应知识路由为切入点,构建状态感知门控,在风电场爬坡事件预测任务上比较局部、关系型、物理引导三类路线,使用两个风电场公开真实数据,并从理论上给出可行性保持、成本占优与严格期望节省的条件。

Result: 在35.9%的关系知识注入率下,自适应路由在报告指标上与始终注入统计不可区分,同时知识调用次数下降64%。理论上确立了可行性保持、成本占优和严格期望节省的成立条件,支持可持续、符合绿色IT的部署。

Conclusion: 结果支持MSKC的核心前提:并非所有状态都需要最大化的智能。对大数据流而言,分析过程本身即可按状态决定数据所需的知�识与计算量,从而实现资源感知、绿色高效的智能决策支持。

Abstract: Resource-constrained cyber-physical decision systems often apply fixed sensing, knowledge, and computational pipelines despite state-dependent information needs. We introduce Minimum-Sufficient Knowledge and Computation (MSKC), a resource-aware computing principle: select the least-costly route meeting decision-risk and deployment constraints, escalating only when cheaper routes are insufficient. As proof of concept, we isolate adaptive knowledge routing, the decision underlying Knowledge-Guided Machine Learning (KGML), which conventionally injects external knowledge unconditionally rather than only where needed. We instantiate this for wind ramp-event predictive analysis, an edge-oriented decision-support task, evaluating local, relational, and physics-informed routes with a state-aware gate on publicly available real data from two wind farms. We establish conditions for feasibility preservation, cost dominance, and strict expected savings over always-on routing. At a 35.9% relational-knowledge injection rate, adaptive routing is statistically indistinguishable from always-on injection on the reported metrics while reducing knowledge calls by 64%, supporting sustainable, Green-IT-aligned deployment. These results support MSKC's premise that uniformly maximal intelligence is unnecessary. This offers intelligent data analysis for big data streams: the analysis itself decides, per state, how much knowledge and computation the data warrant.

</details>


### [163] [PINNeval: A Comprehensive Evaluation Standard for Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08559)
*Kevin von Bargen,Hamid Ebrahimy,Tim Römer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一套新的PINN评估协议，将训练流程视为四组件的整体系统，并发布JAX工具包PINNpipe（含18种方法与9个基准问题），其结果挑战了现有基准结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PINN文献方法众多，但基准研究往往只孤立考察流程中的单一组件，且将其他组件设为过时的最小默认配置，所得结论未必能迁移到当前最优的竞争性流水线中。

Method: 将PINN训练流程系统拆分为四部分：域重要性自适应、损失平衡、网络架构与优化方案；提出新评估协议：(1)把四组件作为集成系统；(2)未研究的组件须采用有竞争力的实现；(3)明确规定指标与报告格式；(4)配套结构化文档以保证跨研究可追溯。

Result: 发布JAX工具包PINNpipe，集成当前文献中的18种方法与9个高效实现的基准问题；据此对照既有基准研究演示该协议，得到的结果挑战了若干常见假设。

Conclusion: PINN评估应从孤立组件转向整合式、有竞争力基线的可追溯协议，PINNpipe为社区提供了可复现的实践基础。

Abstract: The Physics-Informed Neural Network (PINN) literature has produced a rapidly expanding catalogue of methods. We divide a PINN training pipeline systematically into four components and observe that many existing benchmarks study these components in isolation. These components are domain-importance adaptation, loss balancing, neural network architecture, and optimization scheme. Moreover, the studies set the surrounding and not considered components of the pipeline to a minimal default that is no longer representative of current best practice approaches. Conclusions drawn under such conditions may not transfer to competitive pipelines. In this work, we propose a new evaluation protocol that (1) treats the four components as an integrated system; (2) crucially requires competitive implementations of the components not under investigation; (3) specifies the metrics and reporting format; and (4) is accompanied by structured documentation that ensures the traceability of evaluations across studies. Based on this comprehensive protocol, we release PINNpipe, an innovative JAX package containing 18 different methods from the current literature as well as nine efficiently implemented benchmark problems. Using PINNpipe, we illustrate our evaluation protocol in relation to established benchmark studies and report results that challenge common assumptions.

</details>


### [164] [A Taxonomy-Driven Synthetic Dataset for Retrieval Effectiveness and Fairness Evaluation in Hiring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09178)
*Warren Jouanneau,Morgane Hoffmann,Emma Jouffroy,Marc Palyart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个基于分类体系构建、完全合成且多语言的HR数据集与基准，同时评测检索效果与算法公平性；基于66万真实自由职业者画像生成含候选人档案、项目简报和反事实人口统计孪生样本的语料，发现SOTA嵌入模型在HR任务上表现偏离MTEB分数且存在性别化语言中的公平性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习招聘工具已普遍应用，但隐私法规与商业敏感性使公开基准测试在法律层面复杂困难，且通用基准无法反映HR场景对公平性的特殊要求。

Method: 构建 taxonomy 驱动的全合成HR数据集与基准：以66万真实自由职业者画像为基础，生成多语言语料（候选人档案、项目简报、反事实人口统计孪生），用于同时评估检索效果与算法公平性，并对SOTA嵌入模型进行审计。

Result: 嵌入模型在HR检索上的表现与通用MTEB分数出现偏离；在语法性别语言（如 gendered languages）中观察到公平性差异。

Conclusion: 通用基准不足以支撑HR场景，需要定制化评测才能识别在语义准确性与公平性之间取得Pareto最优的模型。

Abstract: While machine learning hiring tools are ubiquitous, the need for privacy regulations and commercial sensitivity makes public benchmarking legally complex. We address this challenge by introducing a taxonomy-driven, fully synthetic HR dataset and benchmark to simultaneously evaluate retrieval effectiveness and algorithmic fairness. Grounded in 660K real freelancer profiles, our multilingual corpus features candidate profiles, project briefs, and counterfactual demographic twins. Auditing state-of-the-art embedding models reveals performance divergence from general MTEB scores, alongside fairness disparities in grammatically gendered languages. This demonstrates that general benchmarks are insufficient for HR, requiring tailored evaluations to identify Pareto-optimal models that balance semantic accuracy and fairness.

</details>


### [165] [Not All Misalignment Is Alike: A Decomposed Benchmark for Emergent Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08569)
*Afonso Domingues,Mubashara Akhtar,Bhada Yun,Xin Chen,Lukas Fluri,Anna Hedström*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 EMERGE，一个将涌现性失配(EM)分解为八种具体失配行为的基准，在域外提示上配合匹配的良性对照进行评测，并追踪能力、连贯性与拒答率以排除混淆因素；应用于8个开源模型和13个微调数据集，发现总体失配率相近的模型其实行为画像差异很大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EM研究多沿用原论文的评测方式，其比率对少数评测问题、裁判模型和阈值高度敏感；评测还把失配与能力退化混为一谈，且几乎无法揭示失配的具体表现形式。此外，数据集、模型与微调配置的差异使不同研究结论难以横向比较。

Method: 构建分解式基准 EMERGE：从基座模型到微调模型跟踪八种不同失配行为的变化；在域外提示上评测，并与匹配的良性对照比较；同时测量能力、连贯性和拒答情况以控制潜在混淆因素；在8个开放权重模型与13个微调数据集上进行系统实验。

Result: 在8个模型、13个数据集上的实验表明，具有相似整体失配率的模型可能呈现显著不同的行为画像，单一失配分数无法刻画这些差异。

Conclusion: EMERGE 有望成为衡量涌现性失配的共享、稳健标准，使不同研究结果更可比，并推动该领域从"检测现象"走向"理解机制"。

Abstract: Emergent misalignment (EM), in which fine-tuning on a narrow task induces misaligned behaviour on unrelated prompts, has been demonstrated across a wide range of datasets, model families, and training setups. Yet, most studies continue to rely on the evaluation approach introduced in the original EM paper, producing rates that are sensitive to the small set of evaluation questions used, the choice of judge, and the thresholds applied. These evaluations can also conflate misalignment with capability loss and offer little insight into the specific ways misalignment manifests. Differences in datasets, models, and fine-tuning configurations further make findings difficult to compare across studies. We introduce EMERGE, a decomposed benchmark that tracks changes in eight distinct misaligned behaviours from base to fine-tuned models. It evaluates these behaviours on out-of-domain prompts against matched benign controls and tracks capability, coherence, and refusal to account for potential confounders. Applying EMERGE to 8 open-weight models and 13 fine-tuning datasets, we find that models with similar broad misalignment rates can exhibit very different behavioural profiles that a single score cannot capture. We hope EMERGE can help establish a shared and robust standard for measuring EM, making findings more comparable and helping move research from detecting the phenomenon towards understanding it.

</details>


### [166] [Benchmarking Machine Learning for Chaotic Three-Body Dynamics in Astrophysics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08634)
*Sheng Wan,Pavan Vynatheya,Duo Xu,Li Kevin Wenliang,Ye Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文构建混沌三体系统的预测任务层级，基准测试多种ML与物理基础模型，发现早期动力学可强预测稳定性，但长时轨迹精确预测仍困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML与生成式AI在物理理解上进展显著，但混沌三体系统作为经典难题，其可预测性问题尚缺系统基准。作者希望厘清不同预测任务（稳定性 vs 轨迹）的难度差异，并检验通用模型与物理基础模型在此场景下的表现。

Method: 在稳定/不稳定临界边界附近模拟三体系统轨迹；设计分层学习任务，核心为稳定性预测（给定时间范围内是否发散）与轨迹预测（预测精确轨迹）；使用从简单回归到SOTA物理基础模型的多种ML方法进行基准测试，并与Mardling-Aarseth稳定性判据等天体物理方法对比。

Result: 实验揭示混沌三体动力学存在清晰的任务难度次序：早期动力学历程即可实现较强的稳定性预测；而精确的长时程轨迹预测仍然困难。通用预测算法与物理基础模型各有表现，并可与传统天体物理判据进行比较。

Conclusion: 混沌三体问题中，稳定性预测相对可行而长期轨迹预测仍是挑战，说明任务应按难度分层评估；研究提供了公开代码与数据集，为物理ML基准与后续研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) and generative AI have recently made impressive progress in understanding physics. We explore a hierarchy of prediction problems stemming from chaotic three-body systems and benchmark existing ML approaches, including general-purpose prediction algorithms and those that build on top of foundational physics models. We first simulate the trajectories of triple systems near the critical boundary of stable/unstable initial conditions. We then design a hierarchy of learning tasks, focusing mainly on two broad problems: stability prediction and trajectory prediction. The former classifies whether a given triple system has diverged or not up to a specific horizon, while the latter predicts the exact trajectory, both given suitable input features. We benchmark a range of ML approaches, from simple regression methods to state-of-the-art foundational physics models, across these tasks, and compare them against astrophysical methods such as the Mardling-Aarseth stability criterion. Our experiments reveal a clear order of task difficulties in chaotic three-body dynamics: early dynamics enable strong stability prediction, while precise long-horizon trajectory forecasting remains difficult. Code and dataset are publicly available at https://github.com/Simonwan03/ThreeBodyMLBench.

</details>


### [167] [FondueQA: A Benchmark for Evaluating Parametric Compensation in Multi-Hop QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09274)
*Laurin Schmid,Suman Navaratnarajah,Koel Dutta Chowdhury,Steffen Eger,Josif Grabocka,Christian Frey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出FondueQA数据集，通过将多跳事实唯一对应到特定证据文档，实现可控的文档级证据消融，以研究检索证据不完整时大模型如何用参数化知识补偿。实验发现参数补偿强烈依赖实体流行度和缺失证据的结构角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多跳基准可能被污染，模型在缺失证据下答对既可能源于一般参数化知识，也可能是记忆了基准特定实例，难以区分。因此需要在可控条件下研究检索证据不完整时模型如何利用内部知识。

Method: 构建FondueQA数据集，将每个所需多跳事实唯一地定位到指定证据文档，从而支持文档级证据消融实验，研究不完整检索下的参数补偿能力。

Result: 参数补偿强烈依赖实体流行度与缺失证据的结构角色：涉及流行实体时补偿更成功；桥接证据（bridging evidence）可能比承载答案的文档更有用；模型可孤立回忆某缺失事实却在部分证据下无法使用，尤其当上下文含合理竞争实体时，Exact Match最多下降15个百分点。

Conclusion: 模型利用参数化知识补偿不完整证据的能力受实体流行度和证据结构角色影响显著，且存在"能回忆但不会用"的局限，为理解LLM内部知识与检索证据的交互提供了受控分析框架。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) show remarkable performance based on parametric knowledge, i.e., their ability to recall facts and associations internalized in a model's weights. However, studying this capability on established multi-hop benchmarks may be complicated by benchmark contamination, as many of these benchmarks have been publicly available long enough to plausibly appear in modern pretraining corpora. As a result, correct answers under missing evidence may reflect either general parametric knowledge or memorization of benchmark-specific instances. To study how models use internal knowledge when retrieval evidence is incomplete, we introduce FondueQA, in which every required multi-hop fact is uniquely localized to a designated evidence document. This enables controlled document-level evidence ablations to study parametric compensation under incomplete retrieval. Our empirical evaluation reveals that parametric compensation strongly depends on both entity popularity and the structural role of missing evidence. Generally, models compensate more successfully for facts involving popular entities. On FondueQA, we show that bridging evidence can be more useful than the answer-bearing document itself. Moreover, models may recall a missing fact in isolation but fail to use it under partial evidence, particularly when the context contains plausible competing entities, which can reduce Exact Match by up to 15 percentage points.

</details>


### [168] [Higher Order Component Attribution via Learned Surrogates in High Energy Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08656)
*Lauri Laatu,Aritra Bal,Benedikt Maier,Markus Klute,Michael Spannowsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KAN）的组件归因方法，将粒子Transformer（ParT）的内部机制回溯到喷注的物理描述符上，以检验多头注意力（MHA）和ParT架构是否真正学到了可区分的物理特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验高能物理中Transformer（尤以ParT为代表）在喷注鉴别上达到SOTA精度，但其有效性缺乏可解释性：尚不清楚MHA等机制是否学到了有物理意义的判别特征。

Method: 利用Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks（KAN）构建组件归因（component attribution）方法，把模型组件的贡献直接关联到喷注的物理描述符上，从而探查MHA与ParT架构内部的判别机制。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验结果，仅说明该方法用于探查MHA/ParT是否能学习到这些物理描述符作为判别特征。

Conclusion: 为高能物理中Transformer喷注鉴别器提供了面向物理描述符的可解释性分析框架，可用于验证注意力机制的物理合理性。

Abstract: Experimental High Energy Physics (HEP) has adopted the transformer architecture for identification of jets, particularly the state-of-the-art Particle Transformer (ParT) which injects explicit information about particle correlations into the attention mechanism. While able to achieve state-of-the-art results in accuracy, interpretability to its effectiveness has been limited. In this paper we propose component attribution methods using Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) that directly tie back to the physical descriptors of jets, to probe whether mechanisms such as Multi-Head Attention (MHA), or the ParT architecture, are able to learn these as discriminating features.

</details>


### [169] [Does a Trading Policy Need to Know What It Trades? Microstructure Invariance and Zero-Shot Transfer of Execution Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08701)
*Hieu Tran,Hong Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在逐笔订单回放的Nasdaq数据上，用微观结构不变性归一化训练一次执行策略，可零样本迁移到数千只未见股票、新时段、加密对和第二交易所；但不变单位相对原始单位优势不足一个基点，且近零迁移差距并非表征正确的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 执行算法通常逐资产调参，因为队列、成交率、买卖价差差异可达数个数量级。作者希望用强化学习做跨尺度上下文的零样本泛化，并检验微观结构不变性所提出的归一化（以各资产自身的时间、规模、价格单位计量后市场彼此相似）是否真的能支撑迁移。

Method: 基于公开Nasdaq数据的精确逐笔订单回放；在2019年954只股票上训练一次策略，任务是在排队等待与跨价差成交之间选择；在2020–21年1964只未见股票、2025–26年2629只股票、加密货币对及第二家交易所上原样评估。策略输入仅含近期成交率、成交规模、价差与最小变动价位成本。对比不变单位、原始单位、z-score与资产描述符，并与包含该股票训练数据的策略及池化比较器对照；另用合成市场和单摆实验验证比较器检验力。

Result: 仅凭近期市场特征、不含个股身份的策略，每股价差成本与"训练时见过该股"的策略相差0.015个价差（约为学习带来收益的五分之一），且该股自身历史无帮助；与第二交易所等权训练的策略差距相近，加密上差距更大。平均成本（费前）上，不变单位优于原始单位、z-score与资产描述符，但优势不足一个基点，其收益主要来自信息与共同成本尺度（可作为普通输入提供），且所测表征均无法外推到未见价差区间。接近零的池化迁移差距只能检测到劣质表征损失的一小部分。

Conclusion: 不变单位带来的是小幅但非零的迁移收益，而非决定性优势；"近零迁移差距"这一常用证据很弱，不能据此判定表征正确，需辅以更能暴露表征缺陷的对照检验。

Abstract: Execution algorithms are calibrated asset by asset, because the queues, trade rates and bid-ask spreads they face differ by orders of magnitude across assets. For reinforcement learning this is zero-shot generalisation across contexts of very different scales, and microstructure invariance proposes a normalisation fixed by theory: measured in each asset's own units of time, size and price, markets look alike. In an exact order-by-order replay of public Nasdaq data, we train policies once, on 954 stocks in 2019, to choose between waiting in the queue and crossing the spread, and evaluate them unchanged on 1,964 unseen stocks in 2020-21, 2,629 stocks in 2025-26, crypto pairs and a second exchange. First, a policy that sees a stock only through its recent trade rate, trade size, spread and price increment (tick) costs within 0.015 spreads per share (a fifth of what learning saves) of a policy whose training data included the stock, and the stock's own history does not help it; it comes within a similar margin of policies trained on the second exchange when every episode counts equally, and within a wider one on crypto. Second, in mean cost before fees, invariant units beat raw units, z-scores and asset descriptors, but by under a basis point: most of their advantage is information and a common cost scale that a learner can also take as plain inputs, and none of the representations tested extrapolates to unseen spread regimes. Third, a near-zero transfer gap to a pooled comparator is weak evidence that a representation is right: we show that such a comparator detects only a small share of what a poor representation loses, and confirm it in synthetic markets and on pendulums.

</details>


### [170] [Foundation-Model Residuals as Signals of Urban Bus Irregularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08703)
*Csaba I. Sidló,Jamil J. Ghazal,Domonkos Rózsay,Rita Rónai,András J. Molnár*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将预训练轨迹模型（UniTraj、TrajFM）在新城市（巴西尼泰罗伊）迁移时的残差视为揭示局部城市条件的系统性信号，而非单纯的性能缺陷，并用XAI分析残差与城市背景变量的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹基础模型跨城市迁移的误差通常只被当作性能局限；作者希望探究这些残差是否反映系统性的本地条件（如公交不规则性），并以"15分钟城市"等新城市范式为动机，评估模型在多样环境中的表现。

Method: 使用NetMob26的公交位置、票务、天气、网络与社会经济数据（尼泰罗伊），分析近期轨迹基础模型的空间残差与行程时间残差：检验高残差是否空间聚集、两类残差是否重叠，并用可解释AI识别哪些城市背景变量解释其分布；将高残差解释为公交不规则性的候选代理信号。

Result: 摘要未给出定量结果，仅说明研究设计检验了残差的空间聚集性、空间与行程时间残差的重叠性及背景变量的解释力。

Conclusion: 轨迹模型残差可作为探测城市交通不规则性的诊断工具，为评估新城市范式（如15分钟城市）提供超出标准移动性指标的分析视角。

Abstract: Pretrained trajectory models encode mobility patterns learned across cities, but their errors when transferred to a new city are usually treated only as performance limitations. We instead ask whether these residuals reveal systematic local conditions. Architectures such as UniTraj and TrajFM learn transferable trajectory representations, although cross-context transfer remains challenging. Using the NetMob26 bus location, ticketing, weather, network, and socioeconomic data for Niteroi, we analyze spatial and travel-time residuals of these recent trajectory foundation models. We test whether high residuals concentrate spatially, whether spatial and travel-time residuals overlap, and which urban-context variables explain their distribution.
  We interpret high residuals as candidate proxy signals of transit irregularity rather than direct evidence of service disruption, and analyze their contextual associations using Explainable AI (XAI). Insights into such varied environments are increasingly valuable for evaluating new urban paradigms; for instance, the 15-minute-city perspective serves as a key motivation for our approach, guiding our methods and expanding the analysis beyond standard mobility metrics.

</details>


### [171] [Dual-Stream LLM-Guided Reward Design for Reject-Inference-Aware Reinforcement Learning in E-Commerce Payment Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08706)
*Bo Qu,Zhurong Wang,Frank Zahradnik,Ming Ouyang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出双流奖励设计，用LLM演化奖励来缓解欺诈检测中的拒绝推断偏差，无需增加推理成本即显著提升美元加权精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产欺诈检测模型只能从被放行的交易中学习标签，被旧系统拦截的交易永远得不到欺诈标签，导致训练分布有偏（仅"已批准"样本）。这是典型的拒绝推断问题。

Method: 扩展Qu et al. (2025)的LLM引导奖励设计框架（LLM演化两步REINFORCE拦截策略的奖励）。让单个LLM设计双流奖励：流1对已标注交易用标准美元加权精度目标；流2对旧系统拦截的无标签交易，用最新梯度提升风险信号与旧系统原始评分做对比打分。不预设哪些信号重要，而是暴露一个证据超集，仅凭评估反馈让LLM自行选择；奖励写成代码，可直接审计。

Result: 在四个月样本外时间测试中，双流智能体在80%/85%/90%召回率下，美元加权精度分别比仅用标注的先前智能体高+4.7/+2.4/+3.0个点，比生产基线平均高+6.8个点。

Conclusion: 在架构与Qu et al. (2025)完全相同且不增加推理成本的前提下，将拒绝推断问题内嵌进奖励设计可有效缓解有偏训练，实现显著的精度提升，且选择过程可审计。

Abstract: Production fraud-detection models learn from a censored slice of their own traffic: transactions a legacy system blocked never receive fraud labels, so models train on a biased, approved-only distribution. We extend LLM-guided reward design, the framework of Qu et al. (2025) in which an LLM evolves the reward of a two-step REINFORCE fraud-blocking policy, to attack this reject-inference problem inside the reward. A single LLM designs a dual-stream reward: stream 1 applies the standard dollar-weighted precision objective to labeled transactions; stream 2 scores legacy-blocked, unlabeled transactions by contrasting the newest gradient-boosted risk signal against the legacy scores that drove the original block. Rather than prescribe which signals matter, we expose a superset of evidence and let the LLM decide for itself which to use, guided by evaluation feedback alone; because it writes the reward as code, that choice is directly auditable. On a held-out four-month out-of-time test, the dual-stream agent beats the prior labeled-only agent at every target recall, by +4.7, +2.4 and +3.0 points of dollar-weighted precision at 80%, 85% and 90% recall, and the production baseline by +6.8 points on average. The resulting policy is architecturally identical to that of Qu et al. (2025) and adds no inference cost.

</details>


### [172] [An Educated Guess: Deriving Statistically Aligned Gradient Estimates for Zeroth-Order Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08712)
*Justus Huebotter,Serge Thill,Marcel van Gerven,Nasir Ahmad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SZO-κ：利用前向激活几何构造零阶搜索方向，缓解高维随机扰动的梯度错位问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经形态芯片、异步系统和ASIC等新硬件难以支持反向传播所需的全局同步与精确可微。零阶优化（SPSA、权重扰动）只需推理即可估计梯度，但随机扰动的方向在高维空间与真实梯度错位（维数灾难），导致效果差。

Method: 不再使用盲目随机噪声，而是从前向传播中可获得的激活几何推导零阶搜索方向。提出谱零阶方法族 SZO-κ，其方向在显式随机假设下求解基于局部响应的梯度猜测目标，优先探索有特征支撑、更可能产生有信息损失响应的方向。

Result: 在多层网络与卷积网络上，系统分析了该方法相对标准扰动基线和反向传播的梯度对齐性、偏差-方差结构以及训练稳定性。

Conclusion: 利用前向激活几何可有效改善零阶梯度的方向对齐，使免反向传播的学习在非常规硬件上更具可行性。

Abstract: As novel hardware architectures are explored for the purposes of machine learning, including neuromorphic chips, asynchronous systems, and ASICs, effective learning algorithms are highly desirable. While backpropagation is the standard for differentiation on standard hardware, its requirement for global synchrony and exact differentiability limits the exploration of exotic algorithms on non-traditional substrates and devices. Zeroth-order (ZO) optimisation methods, such as SPSA and weight perturbation, offer a compelling alternative by estimating gradients through inference-only passes; however, these methods have historically suffered due to the `curse of dimensionality', where random perturbations become increasingly misaligned with the true gradient in high-dimensional spaces. In this work, we move beyond blind random noise by deriving zeroth-order search directions from activation geometry available during the forward pass. This leads to a family of Spectral Zeroth-Order methods, SZO-$κ$, whose directions solve local response-based gradient-guessing objectives under explicit stochastic assumptions. These methods explore feature-supported directions that are more likely to produce informative loss responses. Using multilayer and convolutional networks, we analyse gradient alignment, bias-variance structure, and training stability of these methods relative to standard perturbation baselines and backpropagation.

</details>


### [173] [Patching the Blind Spots: Reversible LLM Rule Overlay for Frozen Fraud Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08714)
*Bo Qu,Zhurong Wang,Frank Zahradnik,Ming Ouyang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种不修改已有GBDT欺诈分类器的“补丁”框架：由LLM基于SHAP低权重特征生成规则，经支持度筛选与校准后形成可加、可逆的log-lift叠加层，并以三模型等权logit平均降低方差；在真实电商欺诈检测中平均提升+2.26% ΔR@P与+1.60% ΔP@R，且在7个偏置偏移变体上全部提升ΔP@R。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业表格欺诈分类中GBDT虽为SOTA，但仍存在盲区；而重新训练已部署模型并不总是安全（如选择偏差数据下持续训练会退化）。因此需要一种不改动原模型、可安全叠加的补救手段。

Method: 以SHAP识别模型低估的特征作为锚点，让LLM提出规则；对含噪规则池做经验支持度筛选，并校准为加法式、可逆的log-lift叠加层，在产线截断阈值处生效。针对单个LLM收益高方差、跨数据集方向反转的问题，采用三个异构骨干的等权logit平均进行集成。

Result: 在真实电商欺诈检测器（选择偏差数据训练）上，继续训练即使精调也会退化；而叠加层在生产截断点（精度30–57%、召回23–63%）上平均获得+2.26% ΔR@P与+1.60% ΔP@R。在含7个设计型偏置偏移变体的公开基准上，所有变体ΔP@R均提升，ΔR@P基本持平。

Conclusion: 该叠加式规则补丁可在不重训、不改动原模型的前提下稳定弥补GBDT盲区，集成策略以牺牲峰值换取可控最坏情形，且每条修正都是可读的谓词，具备可解释性与可逆性。

Abstract: In industrial tabular fraud classification, gradient-boosted trees remain state-of-the-art, but even a well-trained model has blind spots, and retraining the deployed model is not always safe. We propose a framework that patches such a model without modifying it: LLMs propose rules anchored on features the model underweights by SHAP, and the resulting noisy pool is screened for empirical support and calibrated into an additive, reversible log-lift overlay that runs at the production cut-off. Single-LLM benefit is high-variance and inverts between datasets, so we pool three diverse backbones by equal-weight logit averaging, trading peak gain for a bounded worst case. On a real-world e-commerce fraud detector trained on selection-biased data, continued training regresses even when carefully tuned, whereas the overlay gains on average +2.26% recall at equal precision ($Δ$R@P) and +1.60% precision at equal recall ($Δ$P@R) over production cut-offs spanning 30-57% precision and 23-63% recall. On a public benchmark of seven designed bias-shift variants it raises $Δ$P@R on every variant and keeps $Δ$R@P near neutral. Each amendment is a readable predicate naming the fraud pattern it covers.

</details>


### [174] [On Differentially Private Mechanisms for Linear Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08737)
*Bardiya Aryanfard,Monika Henzinger,Farhood Rostamkhani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ContinualAdaSSP，首个在持续差分隐私下求解无约束最小二乘线性回归的机制，次优性差距约为 √d·polylog(n)，空间 O(d²log n)、单步更新时间 O(d²log n + d^ω)；并给出箱约束线性回归的首个下界，实验表明其精度显著优于逐步重跑批处理算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批处理设置下的差分隐私线性回归已被充分研究，但持续观测（continual observation）设置下仍缺乏有效方法：已有工作仅处理带约束的线性回归，且每步更新时间对 n 呈多项式级，实际不可用。因此需要同时兼顾无约束情形、低次优性和高效更新的机制。

Method: 提出 ContinualAdaSSP：在持续差分隐私框架下构造流式机制，每步对累计数据给出参数估计，利用 AdaSSP 类谱/子空间技巧控制噪声注入，使误差随 √d·polylog(n) 增长，并将每条新数据到来的更新代价压缩到 O(d²log n + d^ω)，空间保持 O(d²log n)。此外，通过对 Peter et al. (2024) 的指纹码（fingerprinting code）问题进行归约，推导箱约束最小二乘回归的下界。

Result: 理论：次优性差距 ∝ √d·polylog(n)，空间 O(d²log n)，更新时间 O(d²log n + d^ω)。下界：对 (1, O(1/n))-DP 为 min(n, √d/log^{1.5}d)，对 ε-DP 为 min(n, d/ε)，为箱约束情形首个下界。实验：在真实数据集上，其精度明显优于任何已知差分隐私机制，如每步重跑批处理算法。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了无约束线性回归在持续差分隐私下的空白，兼顾了精度、空间与更新时间效率，并通过新下界刻画了该问题的内在难度，实验验证了实用优势。

Abstract: We study the classic least-squares linear regression problem under differential privacy (DP) in the batch and the continual observation setting. In the latter setting, the mechanism receives a stream of $n$ data points $(x_i,y_i)$ ($x_i\in\mathbb{R}^d$ and $y_i\in\mathbb{R}$) and must output an estimate of the optimal parameters at each time step $i$. Although differentially private linear regression has been extensively studied in the batch setting, the problem remains underexplored for the continual setting, with prior work being restricted to constrained linear regression and update time polynomial in $n$.
  In this work, we introduce ContinualAdaSSP, the first mechanism for unconstrained linear regression under continual differential privacy. Our mechanism's suboptimality gap is proportional to $\sqrt{d}\operatorname{polylog}(n)$. It requires $O(d^2\log{n})$ space and $O(d^2\log{n}+d^ω)$ update time, where $d$ is the dimension of the input space.
  We also study fundamental limits of differentially private linear regression by showing the first lower bounds for the box-constrained least-squares linear regression problem. Our lower bounds are proportional to $\min(n,\frac{\sqrt{d}}{\log^{1.5}d})$ for $(1,O(\frac{1}{n}))$-DP, and $\min(n,\frac{d}ε)$ for $ε$-DP, and are derived using a reduction from a fingerprinting code problem from [Peter et al., 2024], providing new insights into the inherent hardness of this canonical task.
  Our experimental evaluation on real-world datasets shows that the accuracy of our continual observation mechanism clearly outperforms any other known differentially private mechanism for this problem such as rerunning the batch algorithm at each time step.

</details>


### [175] [Random Symbolic Boosting: A Geometric Approach to Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08753)
*José R. Ortegaa,José Manuel Muñoz Contreras,Leonardo Trujillo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出随机符号提升（RSB），用随机生成的语法树种群做 boosting 构建可解释的加性符号模型，无需代际进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号模型可解释性好，但通过代际进化构建计算代价高昂。

Method: 将语法树语义视作向量，证明高维空间可由足够多线性无关向量张成；在随机生成的语法树种群上做 boosting，直接操纵这些向量构建显式加性模型，并配合基于预测向量与目标向量夹角的几何自适应早停机制（与问题尺度无关、无需调参）。

Result: 在最小二乘下，boosting 对回归和分类均保证训练误差单调下降；在符号回归标准基准上处于精度与训练时间的 Pareto 最优点，在真实数据集上排名第一；在含 200 个问题的大型表格分类基准上取得有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: RSB 用随机语法树加 boosting 替代代际进化，兼顾可解释性、效率与精度，早停机制免调参且尺度无关。

Abstract: Symbolic models offer interpretability, but their construction through generational evolution can be computationally expensive. This article presents Random Symbolic Boosting (RSB), a method that constructs explicit additive symbolic models via boosting over populations of randomly generated syntax trees, eliminating the need for generational evolution. The idea combines two observations: high-dimensional spaces can be spanned by enough linearly independent vectors, and the semantics of syntax trees can be directly manipulated as such vectors. It is shown that, for Ordinary Least Squares, the boosting process guarantees a monotonic reduction of the training error for both regression and classification. Furthermore, an adaptive geometric early stopping mechanism is introduced. It is based on the angle between the prediction and target vectors, is independent of the problems scale, and requires no adjustments across tasks. Evaluated on a standard benchmark for symbolic regression, the method is located at the Pareto-optimal point between predictive accuracy and training time, ranking first on the real-world datasets. Additionally, it was tested on a large tabular classification benchmark with 200 problems, achieving competitive results.

</details>


### [176] [MEDALLION: Manifold Embedding Distillation and Attributions for Low-dimensional Latent InterpretatiON](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08767)
*Vicky Jung,Claire M. He,Irene Chang,Genevera I. Allen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MEDALLION通过知识蒸馏+神经特征归因解释t-SNE/UMAP等黑箱降维嵌入中哪些原始特征起作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降维广泛用于高维数据可视化，但解释t-SNE、UMAP等黑箱方法中哪些原始特征贡献于低维表示仍然困难。

Method: 先用神经编码器蒸馏给定嵌入，再对编码器应用多种现有神经特征归因方法，得到特征归因。

Result: 在MNIST和单细胞转录组学应用中，MEDALLION给出有信息量的特征归因，为嵌入提供额外洞见，并与现有可解释方法进行了性能比较。

Conclusion: 该框架可作为解释黑箱降维嵌入的通用工具。

Abstract: Dimensionality reduction is widely used to visualize high-dimensional data and guide scientific discovery, yet interpreting which original features contribute to low-dimensional representations remains challenging, particularly for black-box methods such as t-SNE and UMAP. We introduce \textbf{MEDALLION} (Manifold Embedding Distillation and Attributions for Low-dimensional Latent InterpretatiON), a novel framework for interpreting low-dimensional embeddings through knowledge distillation and neural feature attribution. MEDALLION distills any given embedding with a neural encoder which we explain via a broad range of existing neural feature attribution methods. Through experiments on MNIST and applications in single-cell transcriptomics, we show MEDALLION provides informative feature attributions that cast additional insights into the underlying embedding and compare its performance with existing interpretability approaches.

</details>


### [177] [On Lipschitz Explosion in Deep Neural Networks with Normalization: Consequences for Optimization and Adversarial Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08775)
*Ashkan Soleymani,Reyhaneh Hosseinpourkhoshkbari,Hadi Daneshmand,Patrick Jaillet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明：含归一化层（如BatchNorm）的深层网络，即使每层权重范数受控（‖Wℓ‖2≤1），输入Lipschitz常数与参数Lipschitz常数仍可随深度/参数维度指数增长，即便使用线性激活。这一"层稳定但整体指数不稳定"的现象使基于Lipschitz的优化、泛化与鲁棒性保证失效，并给出对抗脆弱的秩分离机制，实验予以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lipschitz常数是优化保证、泛化界和鲁棒性证书的核心量。实践中人们常用逐层范数控制（如‖Wℓ‖2≤1）和归一化层来"稳定"网络，但这类局部稳定判据是否真能约束整体稳定性尚不清楚。作者希望揭示：广泛用于稳定训练的归一化机制，可能制造在局部不可见的最坏情况不稳定性。

Method: 理论分析：针对含归一化层（以BatchNorm为典型）的深层网络，分别考察输入Lipschitz常数（对数据扰动的敏感性）与参数Lipschitz常数（对权重扰动的敏感性），在强逐层范数控制（‖Wℓ‖2≤1）甚至线性激活的设定下推导其随深度与参数维度的增长下界。据此推出对非光滑优化保证、泛化界与鲁棒性证书的影响，并刻画"秩创造型"扰动被更强放大的机制。实验：在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10上比较引入新奇异方向的扰动与保持原奇异子空间内同噪声水平扰动的效果。

Result: 理论结果：两个Lipschitz常数均可随深度（进而随参数维度）指数增长，线性激活下即成立，说明逐层范数界无法在局部反映该不稳定性。推论：参数Lipschitz爆炸使依赖Lipschitz的非光滑优化保证退化为关于维度的指数级界；输入Lipschitz爆炸使最坏情况下的Lipschitz型泛化界与鲁棒性证书变得空洞（vacuous）。理论还预测"秩创造"扰动比同噪声水平的子空间内扰动被放大得多。实验在三个数据集上支持该预测：秩创造型扰动导致准确率、置信度与margin的下降显著更剧烈。

Conclusion: 归一化层虽能稳定训练，却可能引入严重的、局部不可见的最坏情况不稳定性，使Lipschitz型理论保证在深层归一化网络中失效。该工作提示逐层范数控制不足以刻画整体稳定性，并给出对抗脆弱的秩分离解释，为更稳健的归一化设计与鲁棒性评估提供了新视角。

Abstract: Can a network look stable at every layer and still be exponentially unstable as a whole? The Lipschitz constant of a neural network is a basic stability quantity appearing in optimization guarantees, generalization bounds, and robustness certificates. Deep networks naturally admit two such notions, input Lipschitzness, measuring sensitivity to data perturbations, and parameter Lipschitzness, measuring sensitivity to weight perturbations. We prove that, for deep networks with normalization layers, with batch normalization as the canonical example, both Lipschitz constants can grow exponentially with depth and hence with parameter dimension, even under strong per-layer norm control such as $\|W_\ell\|_2\le 1$, and already for linear activations. Thus, a mechanism widely used to stabilize training can create severe worst-case instabilities that are invisible locally, i.e., from layerwise norm bounds alone. Parameter-Lipschitz explosion turns Lipschitz-dependent nonsmooth optimization guarantees into exponential-in-dimension bounds for deep normalized networks, while input-Lipschitz explosion makes worst-case Lipschitz-based generalization and robustness certificates vacuous. The latter also yields a concrete rank-separation mechanism for adversarial vulnerability. The theory predicts that perturbations introducing new singular directions should be amplified much more strongly than perturbations at the same noise level that remain within the input's original singular subspace. Experiments on MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, and CIFAR-10 support this prediction, showing that rank-creating perturbations cause substantially sharper drops in accuracy, confidence, and margins than same-subspace perturbations.

</details>


### [178] [Training Stability under Adaptive Curricula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08787)
*Johan Obando-Ceron,Walter Mayor,Michael Dennis,Aaron Courville,Pablo Samuel Castro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究无监督环境设计(UED)中"学生"（学习器）在自适应课程分布下是否仍可训练，发现并刻画了多种学习者侧病态现象，并通过干预证明提升学习者可训练性可在不改课程规则、不增加环境交互的情况下改善长期性能与零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往UED研究主要关注课程如何构建，而忽视了生成课程的分布是否让学习器保持可训练；作者希望检验在课程不断演化时学习器自身优化是否会崩溃，从而补充UED的设计原则。

Method: 在多种UED机制、不同环境复杂度与更长训练时程上进行系统性实证分析，识别学习器侧反复出现的病态现象，并施加受控干预以验证因果关系。

Result: 发现三类常见病态：参数增长、价值—结果排序退化、表征多样性丧失，它们伴随学习停滞和性能下降；受控干预缓解这些病态后学习效果提升；稳定学习器可改善长时程表现与零样本泛化。

Conclusion: UED的局限与传统深度RL中的优化失败相通，自适应课程会把学习器优化状态与其未来训练分布耦合；因此"学习者可训练性"应作为UED的补充设计原则——有效课程需要能持续从其所产生分布中学习的学生。

Abstract: Unsupervised Environment Design (UED) approaches train reinforcement learning (RL) agents on diverse curricula that may evolve with their capabilities. While prior work has largely focused on how these curricula are constructed, we study whether their students remain trainable under the resulting distributions. Across multiple UED mechanisms, environment complexities, and extended training horizons, we identify recurring learner-side pathologies, including parameter growth, degraded value--outcome ordering, and loss of representational diversity, that accompany stagnation and performance degradation. These findings connect limitations in UED to optimization failures previously identified in conventional deep RL, while showing how adaptive curricula couple the learner's optimization state to its future training distribution. Controlled interventions show that mitigating selected pathologies improves learning. Stabilizing the learner improves long-horizon performance and zero-shot generalization without changing the curriculum rule or increasing environment interaction. Our results establish learner trainability as a complementary design principle for UED. Effective curricula require students that remain capable of learning from the distributions they produce.

</details>


### [179] [Not All Latents Are Equal: Importance-Weighted Latent Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08788)
*Biraaj Rout,Jay Revolinsky,Han Liu,Harry Shomer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 图生成中离散扩散随节点数平方增长难以扩展，潜空间生成虽更可扩展但性能落后；作者发现原因是不同潜维度对图重建的重要性分布不均，提出按重要性重加权（LIR）改进损失，从而提升性能并加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散图扩散的计算量随节点数呈二次增长，难以用于大图；潜空间图生成通过去噪低维潜变量更易扩展到多节点，但实际性能不如离散方法。作者想弄清该性能差距的成因并加以弥补。

Method: 分析发现潜维度对图重建的影响高度不均，绝大部分影响集中在少数维度上，而现有损失对整个潜向量等权重重建，导致模型优化了大量与下游任务无关的信息。据此提出 Latent Importance Reweighting（LIR）：用解码器对潜变量的 Jacobian 衡量单个潜单元对图重建的相对影响，作为重要性权重对每个潜维度加权。方法模型无关，不引入额外参数或超参数。

Result: 在两个不同的自编码器、多个基准数据集上的实验表明，LIR 能提升生成性能，并使训练收敛更快。

Conclusion: 潜空间图生成落后于离散扩散的根源在于潜维度重要性分布不均与损失等权重建之间的错配；按解码器 Jacobian 计算的重要性对潜维度重加权是一种简单、通用且有效的改进手段。

Abstract: Graph generation has shown tremendous effectiveness in modeling complex scientific tasks ranging from molecule generation to optimized circuit design. Most recent advancements have developed models that learn to denoise noisy graphs using either discrete diffusion or flow matching. However, discrete graph diffusion scales quadratically with the number of nodes, making it difficult to use such models on larger graphs. On the other hand, latent graph generation instead seeks to denoise noisy graph latents, which scale better to more nodes due to their lower dimension. However, in practice, latent graph generation models lag behind their discrete counterparts in performance. We study this problem, finding that it is caused by the unequal distribution of that different latent dimensions have on graph reconstruction. the impact. We observe that the vast majority of impact is contained in only a smaller number of latent dimensions. Since equal emphasis is placed on reconstructing the entire latent in the loss function, existing models are optimized to reconstruct information irrelevant to the downstream task. To tackle this problem, we propose Latent Importance Reweighting ({\bf LIR)} which weights each latent dimensions via it's importance to final graph reconstruction. Importance is calculated as the relative influence of a single latent unit on graph reconstruction, which is represented via the Jacobian of the decoder relative to the latent. Our method is model-agnostic and requires no additional model parameters or hyperparameters. Experiments show that LIR can boost performance and lead to faster training convergence when applied to two different autoencoders on various benchmark datasets. Code: https://github.com/biraaj/GraphGenLatentReweight

</details>


### [180] [TopoSHAP: Shapley for Explainable and Efficient Topological Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08789)
*Mathilde Papillon,Nina Miolane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TopoSHAP框架，用Shapley值解释拓扑深度学习中的两种核心问题：哪些拓扑结构驱动预测（TopoSHAP-cell）、哪些架构组件对性能最重要（TopoSHAP-neighbor）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑深度学习（TDL）将图学习扩展到分子环、社交群体等高阶结构，但"哪些结构驱动预测"以及"哪些架构组件对性能最重要"这两类可解释性问题在跨拓扑域和跨架构上仍缺乏高效通用的方法。

Method: TopoSHAP-cell：为拓扑数据单元分配Shapley值并提取紧凑解释；TopoSHAP-neighbor：量化邻域对模型性能的贡献，并将其转化为后向消除搜索，用于在训练早期识别更小且有竞争力的架构。

Result: 在GraphXAI基准上，解释质量与图方法持平或更优，速度最高快112倍；揭示更优的拓扑特征设计，提升了解释恢复率与预测精度；架构搜索比传统TDL训练扫描最高便宜11.3倍。

Conclusion: TopoSHAP是一个高效、通用、可跨拓扑域与架构的统一解释框架，兼具解释质量、速度与实用价值。

Abstract: Topological deep learning (TDL) extends learning on graphs, such as molecules or social networks, to higher-order structures, such as rings in a molecule or groups in a social network. Explaining which of these structures drive a model's predictions, and which architectural components matter most for its performance, remains challenging. We introduce TopoSHAP, an efficient and general explanation framework that addresses both questions across topological domains and model architectures. TopoSHAP-cell assigns Shapley values to topological data cells and extracts compact explanations. These explanations match or outperform graph-based counterparts on GraphXAI benchmark data while running up to 112$\times$ faster.
