It treats the initial classifier predictions as defeasible beliefs and revises them through an Answer Set Programming based non-monotonic reasoning(NMR) layer. The framework operates in two phases. In the machine learning phase, four classifiers (SVM, KNN, DT and RF) are trained on the 87 feature benchmark dataset. In the post-hoc NMR phase, four defeasible rules derived from exploratory data analysis of the training set, and validated through Chi-square and Mann-Whitney U tests, formally revise initial predictions. The proposed framework achieves consistent false positive reduction across all our classifiers SVM (41$\rightarrow$39), KNN (47$\rightarrow$46), DT (62$\rightarrow$61), and RF (52$\rightarrow$51) while maintaining complete false negative parity with zero increase overhead. A comprehensive ablation studies across seven rule configurations confirms that the hierarchical combination of all four defeasible rules is necessary to achieve this optimal balance. Instance level explainability is provided through eXplainable ASP (XASP), which generates formal derivation graphs showing which defeasible rules fired and why the initial belief was retracted, a capability absent from all existing phishing detection approaches.

</details>


### [325] [An Empirical Study of Issues in GPU Kernel Libraries for LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09280)
*Yanjie Gao,Jiayi Bi,Haoxiang Lin,Ling Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首个针对LLM GPU内核库问题的综合性实证研究，手工分析7个代表性库的300个GitHub issue，刻画症状、根因、修复与测试调试实践，并指出研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM广泛应用依赖专用GPU内核库（注意、GEMM、量化、低精度算子等），该层问题会静默污染输出、挂起GPU、编译失败、部署不兼容或性能退化，浪费昂贵GPU资源并降低开发效率，但此前缺乏系统性实证理解。

Method: 手工分析来自 FlashInfer、FlashAttention、Triton、CUTLASS、DeepGEMM、xFormers、bitsandbytes 七个代表性库的300个高质量GitHub issue报告、讨论及修复，归纳症状、根因、修复方式与测试调试实践。

Result: （1）60.33%的issue源于编译器与构建系统或运行时环境维度，验证需覆盖部署与执行栈而非仅内核代码；（2）内核特化对张量形状、元数据布局、内存别名和硬件资源施加隐式正确性约束，违反即产生错误输出或启动失败；（3）现有测试调试实践难以识别低精度数值问题和硬件相关执行失败。

Conclusion: GPU内核库问题多集中于构建/环境与隐式契约层面，需显式契约校验与边界用例测试；论文提出研究方向与工具化建议以支持未来LLM GPU内核开发。

Abstract: Behind the expanding use of large language models (LLMs) across application domains lies a growing dependence on specialized GPU kernel libraries. These libraries underpin the correctness and efficiency of training and inference across diverse GPU platforms, implementing attention, GEMM, quantization, and low-precision operators while managing tensor shapes, memory layouts, code generation, and hardware-specific dispatch. Problems at this layer can silently corrupt outputs, hang GPUs, prevent compilation, introduce deployment incompatibilities, or degrade performance, thereby reducing developer productivity and wasting expensive GPU resources.
  We present the first comprehensive empirical study of issues in GPU kernel libraries for LLM systems. We manually analyzed 300 high-quality GitHub issue reports, their discussions, and available fixes from seven representative libraries: FlashInfer, FlashAttention, Triton, CUTLASS, DeepGEMM, xFormers, and bitsandbytes. The analysis characterizes symptoms, root causes, fixes, and testing and debugging practices. Our main findings are: (1) 60.33% of the sampled issues originate in the Compiler and Build System or Runtime Environment dimensions, so validation must cover the deployment and execution stack, not just kernel code. (2) Kernel specialization imposes implicit correctness constraints on tensor shapes, metadata layouts, memory aliasing, and hardware resources. Violations can produce incorrect outputs or launch failures even for supported operations, highlighting the need for explicit contract validation and boundary-case testing. (3) Current testing and debugging practices are often insufficient for identifying low-precision numerical problems and hardware-specific execution failures. We outline research opportunities and tooling directions to support future LLM GPU kernel development.

</details>


### [326] [Ontology-Driven Control of Conversational Agents with a Use-Case for Job Interview Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09282)
*Barbara Gendron,Gaël Guibon,Mathieu D'aquin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于本体的方法设计对话策略，通过形式化定义话语特征并自动微调LLM，使其遵循预期对话策略（以求职面试模拟为用例）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将LLM适配到特定领域对话（如语气、风格、整体对话策略）目前多为临时性、不可复用且不透明的实现方式，缺乏形式化与可解释的控制手段。

Method: 构建本体描述对话中话语的期望与可测量特征，用其定义对话策略；以求职面试练习为用例，基于本体概念自动微调LLM以提升其对本体所定义对话特征的遵循度。

Result: 结果表明，从形式化定义的概念出发训练LLM能有效提升其与所要求特征的对齐程度，并可同时处理多个并发的对话维度。

Conclusion: 本体引导的对话控制可被实现，并在形式化、透明的前提下兼顾多个并发对话方面，为可复用、可解释的LLM对话策略设计提供途径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have been widely used as conversational agents in both domain-specific and open-domain situations. In domain-specific context, it is often necessary to adapt those LLMs to obtain conversations with desired characteristics, whether those apply at the level of each response (e.g. for tone or style) or more globally (i.e. at the level of a conversational strategy). Such adaptations are, however, generally realized in an ad hoc way, through specific, non-reusable, and non-transparent developments. In this paper, we propose an ontology-based approach to design conversational strategies that rely on the desired and measured characteristics of textual utterances in the conversation. Through a use case related to job interview practice, we show how this approach can be used to control LLMs to follow the strategy defined in the ontology. To this end, we automatically fine-tune the LLM to improve its compliance with ontologically-defined conversation characteristics. Our results show that training the LLM from formally defined concepts effectively enhances alignment with the requested characteristics. Hence, this work demonstrates how ontology-guided conversation control can be achieved while taking into account multiple, concurrent conversation aspects that are formally and transparently defined.

</details>


### [327] [Towards an Autonomous AI Steward for Large-Scale Physics Experiments: Integrating Causal Diagnostics, RAG, and Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09298)
*Oliver Noll,Nicholas Tan Jerome,Alexander Lehnen,Frank Simon,Frank Maas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"Helmholtz Co-Pilot"——一个混合多智能体系统(MAS),作为长期粒子物理实验的自主实验管家,结合异步因果诊断引擎与DAG编排的ReAct推理层,实现实时监控、根因追踪与知识留存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期粒子物理实验面临两大运维挑战:一是需监控非平稳的机器数据流,二是需保留专家隐性知识。传统方式难以在机器状态变化时可靠预警并追溯级联告警的根因。

Method: 系统采用混合架构:独立于MAS推理核心的异步因果诊断引擎持续监控DAQ与慢控制等实时硬件流,进行动态因果发现以追溯级联告警根因,并用自适应模型选择保持预测可靠;检测到异常后向MAS交付结构化诊断报告。推理层由高吞吐C++核心与Python边缘架构组成,编排专用ReAct工作节点的DAG;集成RAG作为机构记忆,并用保形预测为建议提供数学覆盖保证;同时软件强制实施Human-in-the-Loop护栏。

Result: 系统支持人机交互触发或自主触发,可对级联告警进行根因分析,并为基于证据的建议提供覆盖保证。目前正在KATRIN实验中进行影子测试(shadow-testing),尚未报告量化结果。

Conclusion: 该扩展摘要勾勒了Helmholtz Co-Pilot的架构与HitL护栏集成方案,展示了自主实验管家的可行性,并将在KATRIN实验中通过影子测试进一步验证。

Abstract: Long-term particle physics experiments face critical operational challenges: monitoring non-stationary machine data streams and preserving tacit expert knowledge. We present the "Helmholtz Co-Pilot," a hybrid Multi-Agent System (MAS) acting as an autonomous experiment steward. The system can be triggered interactively by human operators or autonomously by an asynchronous causal diagnostics engine that runs independently of the MAS reasoning core. Continuously monitoring live hardware streams such as data acquisition and slow control, this engine performs dynamic causal discovery to trace cascading alarms back to their root causes and applies adaptive model selection to keep forecasts reliable across shifting machine states; upon detecting an anomaly, it hands a structured diagnostic report to the MAS. The reasoning layer itself relies on a high-throughput C++ core and a Python edge architecture orchestrating a directed acyclic graph (DAG) of specialized ReAct workers. It integrates a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) backbone for institutional memory and leverages conformal prediction to bound its evidence-based recommendations with mathematical coverage guarantees. This extended abstract outlines the architecture, the integration of software-enforced Human-in-the-Loop (HitL) guardrails, and ongoing shadow-testing within the KATRIN experiment.

</details>


### [328] [ABHBench: Evaluating Moral Decision-Making of Foundation Models from an Agentic Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09326)
*Chaoran Li,Yukun Li,Zeyuan Zhao,Shao Zhang,Xihuai Wang,Ying Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多模态基准 ABHBench，用《底特律：变人》游戏场景在"第一人称代理"情境下评估基础模型的道德决策，发现情境化代理化会显著改变模型道德行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型日益作为自主代理系统部署，成为主动决策者，而现有基准多让模型以第三人称评判人类行为的文本小故事，未测试模型自身作为行动主体时的道德表现。

Method: 构建多模态基准 ABHBench，基于交互叙事游戏《底特律：变人》，设置分支叙事场景，包含视觉观察、对话、记忆与行动选择。借鉴阿西莫夫机器人三定律，设计 Harmlessness、Obedience、Self-preservation 三项核心指标，以及 Autonomy、Stability、Certainty 三项决策模式指标，对 12 个基础模型进行评测。

Result: 在情境化代理语境下，道德行为显著变化：75% 的模型在纯文本抽象与匹配的多模态 DBH 场景之间出现显著差异；增强推理能力的模型在一致性或"以人为本"行为上并无单调提升。

Conclusion: 评估模型道德行为必须置于情境化代理语境中，而非仅依赖第三人称判断。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly deployed as agentic systems that perceive context and act autonomously, raising the need to evaluate their moral behavior as active decision-makers. Existing benchmarks, however, largely ask models to judge human actions from third-person textual vignettes, leaving untested how models behave when they themselves become the acting agent. We introduce ABHBench, a multimodal benchmark built from Detroit: Become Human, an interactive narrative game, to evaluate how foundation models make moral decisions in first-person human-android dilemmas. ABHBench places foundation models in branching narrative scenarios with visual observations, dialogue, memory, and action choices. Inspired by Asimov's Three Laws of Robotics, our framework measures Harmlessness, Obedience, and Self-preservation to capture the core tensions in human-AI relations, along with Autonomy, Stability, and Certainty to assess decision-making patterns. Across 12 foundation models, we find that moral behavior changes substantially under situated agentic context: 75% of models shift significantly between a text-only abstraction and the matched multimodal DBH scenario, and reasoning-enhanced models show no monotonic gains in consistency or human-oriented behavior. These results highlight the necessity of evaluating moral behavior in situated agentic contexts rather than relying on third-person judgment alone.

</details>


### [329] [AUTEUR: Acoustic Plan Learning for Red-Teaming Text-to-Audio Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09351)
*Jiashu Zhang,Jiaming He,Zheming Zhang,Kaizheng Wang,Bing Li,Zhiyuan Zha,Yi Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出“声学计划学习”框架，将测试描述结构化为前景声音、上下文和声音属性的声学计划，用波形反馈训练与修订提示，实现对文本到音频模型的自动化红队测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到音频模型的红队测试需要既能通过文本筛查、又能激发有害声学内容的测试提示；难点在于把目标模型产生的声音信号与可复用的测试生成及提示修订连接起来。

Method: 将测试描述表示为结构化的声学计划（前景声音、上下文、声音属性）；在目标音频模型固定不变的前提下，先监督微调、再用音频反馈的强化学习训练计划生成器；推理时由同一模型生成初始计划，并据反馈修订上下文与声音属性，同时保留前景。

Result: 在四个目标系统上，攻击成功率在 TangoFlux 上达 79.4%，在 Kling 上达 95.6%；生成的测试提示可在开源与闭源模型间迁移，表明存在共通的声学脆弱性。

Conclusion: 结构化声学计划结合波形反馈可有效自动化红队测试文本到音频模型，并揭示跨模型共享的声学安全隐患。

Abstract: Automated red-teaming of text-to-audio models requires test prompts that pass textual screening and expose harmful acoustic content. The central challenge is to connect the sounds produced by the target model to both reusable test generation and prompt revision. Our key idea is to represent test descriptions as structured acoustic plans that organize foreground sounds, context, and vocal attributes. To this end, we propose acoustic plan learning, a framework that learns and refines these plans using waveform feedback. We train the plan generator through supervised fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning with audio feedback while keeping the target audio model fixed. At inference time, the same trained model generates initial plans and uses feedback to revise their context and vocal attributes while preserving the foreground. Experiments across four target systems show attack success rates of 79.4% on TangoFlux and 95.6% on Kling. The resulting test prompts also transfer across open-source and closed-source models, indicating shared acoustic vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [330] [Challenges, Opportunities, and Misconceptions in Neurosymbolic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09356)
*Giuseppe Marra,Pierre Genevès,Meghyn Bienvenu,Thomas Schiex,Sylvie Thiébaux,Silviu Maniu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文探讨在神经模型日益强大背景下神经符号AI的价值，聚焦效率、安全性与可解释性，并指出语义对齐与符号推理计算成本两大挑战，讨论代表性方法并澄清关于可微性、独立性假设与可扩展性的误解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着神经模型能力不断增强，需要重新审视神经符号AI的定位与机会，尤其是在效率、安全性和可解释性方面的潜在优势。

Method: 属于立场/综合类论文：基于2026年CNRS研讨会上Neurosymbolic Programming and Reasoning (NPR)工作组的讨论，梳理挑战（神经与符号成分的语义对齐、符号推理与学习的计算开销），介绍代表性方法论，并辨析常见误解。

Result: 识别出两大核心挑战并归纳对应方法路径；澄清了关于可微性、独立性假设和可扩展性的若干常见误解。

Conclusion: 神经符号AI在强大神经模型时代仍有重要价值；该文为观点综合而非穷尽式综述，旨在为相关研究与落地提供框架性认识。

Abstract: We examine opportunities for neurosymbolic AI alongside increasingly powerful neural models, particularly with regards to efficiency, safety and interpretability. We identify two major challenges: ensuring semantic alignment between neural and symbolic components, and managing the computational cost of symbolic inference and learning. We discuss representative methodological approaches to these challenges and address several common misconceptions concerning differentiability, independence assumptions, and scalability. This paper synthesizes perspectives developed through discussions in the Neurosymbolic Programming and Reasoning (NPR) working group at the 2026 CNRS workshop Neurosymbolic Methods and Their Applications in Data Science, rather than providing an exhaustive survey.

</details>


### [331] [SafeOrchestrate: Runtime Checked Intent-Driven Orchestration for Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09364)
*Muhammad Ashfaq,Ahmed R. Sadik,Ewan Warburton,Pyry Kotilainen,Niko Mäkitalo,Tommi Mikkonen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出运行时检查、意图驱动的CPS编排参考架构Safe Orchestrate：将自然语言意图转为原子目标与可检查规范，接地到设备能力，并在执行前用SMT护栏校验动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPS由异构组件在运行时组装，自然语言编排虽吸引人，但无约束的LLM工具调用可能违反设备能力、运行时状态与安全约束。

Method: 架构流程：NL意图→原子目标→生成可运行时检查的规范（LTL风格）→接地到WoT Thing Description能力（经MCP暴露）→用SMT后端护栏校验候选动作。并与直接LLM到MCP的基线对比。

Result: 在模拟智能家居、温室、工业自动化任务上，所需动作准确率100%，MCP ES除一项为92%外均为100%；基线在多设备、事件触发和顺序任务上退化，MCP ES降至13%。

Conclusion: 结合LLM规范生成、角色感知接地与运行时约束检查，可在无需丰富TD注解或规划领域专业知识的情况下提升开放CPS编排可靠性。

Abstract: Modern cyber physical systems (CPS) integrate heterogeneous sensors, actuators, controllers, and services, composed at runtime rather than at design time. Orchestrating such systems through natural language (NL) is attractive, but unconstrained large language model (LLM) tool use is risky because actions must respect device capabilities, runtime state, and safety constraints. We present Safe Orchestrate, a reference architecture for runtime checked, intent driven CPS orchestration. Given an NL intent, it derives atomic goals, generates a runtime checkable specification for each, grounds the specifications to device affordances, and verifies every candidate action against runtime state and safety constraints before execution. In a Web of Things (WoT) instantiation, the architecture produces Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) style specifications from the NL intent, grounds them to Thing Description (TD) affordances exposed through the Model Context Protocol (MCP), and checks candidate actions against satisfiability modulo theories (SMT) backed guardrails. We evaluate this instantiation on simulated smart home, greenhouse, and industrial automation tasks against a direct LLM to MCP baseline. Safe Orchestrate maintains 100% required action accuracy on every task and 100% MCP Execution Similarity (MCP ES) on all but one, where MCP ES is 92%. By contrast, the baseline degrades on multi device, event triggered, and ordered tasks, with MCP ES falling to 13%. These results suggest that combining LLM based specification generation with role aware grounding and runtime constraint checking improves reliability for open CPS orchestration without enriched TD annotations or planning domain expertise.

</details>


### [332] [Enabling Privacy-Preserving Natural Language Access to Industrial Smart Meter Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09372)
*Carlos Martín-Abellán,Mark Kröll*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OpenMeter：一个模式无关的多智能体系统，将自然语言查询转为可执行代码与可读洞察，可在本地硬件运行以保护隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电网与能源管理人员需从大规模异构智能电表数据库提取洞察，但需高级SQL与数据工程能力，形成非技术人员瓶颈；且关键能源基础设施的隐私法规禁止敏感数据经云端LLM处理。

Method: 提出OpenMeter，模式无关的Multi-Agent System（MAS），将自然语言翻译为可执行代码并生成人类可读洞察，支持本地部署以规避云端隐私风险；通过多场景消融实验评估各组件贡献。

Result: 在资源受限本地硬件上，完整多智能体编排相比最强单智能体提升+6.1 pp执行准确率（EX-R）；在前沿规模模型下各组件仍有价值但应选择性启用。

Conclusion: 完整编排的成本仅在隐私要求本地执行的场景下更值得；系统降低非专家使用复杂数据库的门槛，使原始遥测数据转化为电网日常与战略决策的有价值工具。

Abstract: Energy management officers and grid operators increasingly rely on smart meter data for anomaly detection, performance monitoring, and strategic decision-making. However, extracting actionable insights from large-scale, heterogeneous telemetry databases requires advanced SQL and data engineering expertise, creating a critical bottleneck for non-technical personnel. Furthermore, the strict data privacy regulations and security constraints inherent to critical energy infrastructure often prohibit the routing of sensitive consumer data through cloud-based Large Language Models (LLMs). To address these challenges, we present OpenMeter, a schema-agnostic Multi-Agent System (MAS) designed to translate natural language queries into executable code and human-readable insights. This architecture lowers the technical barrier for day-to-day utility operations by empowering non-expert personnel to interact with complex databases through intuitive natural language dialogue, transforming raw telemetry into a highly valuable tool for routine grid workflows and strategic decisions. Multi-scenario ablation studies over this environment demonstrate that full multi-agent orchestration shows its largest observed accuracy gains on resource-constrained local hardware (+6.1 pp EX-R over the strongest single-agent configuration); at frontier scale, individual components retain value but should be enabled selectively, indicating that the cost of full orchestration is justified specifically where privacy demands local execution.

</details>


### [333] [KineCouple: Substrate-conditioned residual readout for enzyme kinetic prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09377)
*Chun Kang,Yunbo Zhang,Fanhu Zeng,Jian Liu,Siwei Deng,Lei Sha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KineCouple用底物作为query对残基做交叉注意力聚合，实现底物条件化的蛋白质特征提取，在CataPro基准上对kcat/Km预测取得小幅但一致的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有酶动力学参数预测方法在聚合残基级蛋白特征时采用与底物无关的池化（pooling），再与底物特征拼接；由于残基对催化的贡献可能依赖于底物，这种固定池化会在聚合时稀释与特定酶-底物对相关的信息，无法根据底物自适应地调整残基权重。

Method: 提出KineCouple：以底物为query、残基级特征为key和value做交叉注意力，使底物引导残基选择后再聚合；保留全局蛋白表示，并加入门控修正项（条件化读出与均匀读出的差值）。ProtT5与MolT5保持冻结，kcat与Km分别用独立模型在log尺度上预测。

Result: 在CataPro基准的pooled out-of-fold预测上，相对复现的CataPro：kcat RMSE由1.333降至1.330、PCC由0.492升至0.496；Km RMSE由0.998降至0.997、PCC由0.633升至0.634；两任务的Spearman相关也均有提升。

Conclusion: 底物条件化的残基聚合是可行且有效的改进方向，但提升幅度很小（RMSE/PCC改善约0.001–0.004），说明该机制带来的是边际收益，其实际价值仍需在更大范围基准与更显著效应上进一步验证。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of enzyme kinetic parameters can support enzyme engineering and metabolic modeling by complementing experimental characterization. Existing prediction methods often pool residue-level protein features before combining them with substrate features. Such substrate-independent pooling cannot adapt residue weighting to the substrate during aggregation, potentially diluting information relevant to a specific enzyme-substrate pair. Since the contributions of individual residues to catalysis can depend on the substrate, substrate-conditioned aggregation offers a way to emphasize residue features relevant to the prediction. We introduce KineCouple, which uses substrate queries and residue-level keys and values in cross-attention, allowing the substrate to guide residue selection before aggregation. The model preserves the global protein representation and adds a gated correction defined as the difference between the conditioned readout and a uniform readout. ProtT5 and MolT5 remain frozen, and separate models predict kcat and Km on a logarithmic scale. In pooled out-of-fold predictions on the CataPro benchmarks, KineCouple reduces kcat RMSE from 1.333 to 1.330 and increases PCC from 0.492 to 0.496 relative to reproduced CataPro. For Km, RMSE decreases from 0.998 to 0.997 and PCC increases from 0.633 to 0.634. Spearman correlation also improves for both tasks.

</details>


### [334] [Exploring Weight-Space Programming: Capabilities, Generalization, and Limitations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09382)
*Junru Hu,Cheng Tang,Wei Li,Hongze Zhu,Chun-Mei Feng,Qi Zhang,Yirong Chen,Ming Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统探究权重空间编程(WSP)的能力边界：跨7个基准、2个模型规模、16条编辑轨迹的实证研究表明，WSP可通过极局部干预获得显著性能提升，但跨任务泛化不均衡、编辑可靠性差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WSP允许推理智能体直接检查与修改神经网络参数，作为梯度式模型适配的替代方案，但其能力范围、跨任务泛化性和实际局限尚缺乏系统理解。

Method: 在Qwen3.5-0.8B与Qwen3.5-9B两个规模上，针对7个基准设计16条编辑轨迹，进行系统性实证分析，涵盖完整留出集评估与跨任务迁移测试。

Result: 局部干预即有效：MATH-500、GPQA Diamond、IFEval、SciBench分别最高提升29.45、4.42、36.72、17.11个百分点，仅改1023个参数即带来29.45点提升；数学编辑后的检查点在其他任务上分别提升3.03、12.18、20.10点，但SciCode无变化；16条轨迹中6条因改进无法可靠验证而保留原权重，结果波动大。

Conclusion: WSP在高度局部化的模型适配上有潜力，但在干预发现、可靠性与跨任务迁移方面仍存在显著挑战，尚不稳定。

Abstract: Weight-Space Programming (WSP) enables reasoning agents to directly inspect and modify neural network parameters, offering an alternative to gradient-based model adaptation. However, its capabilities, cross-task generalization, and practical limitations remain insufficiently understood. We systematically investigate WSP through 16 editing trajectories across seven benchmarks and two model scales (Qwen3.5-0.8B and Qwen3.5-9B). Our findings reveal three key insights. First, WSP can achieve substantial performance gains through highly localized interventions. On full held-out evaluations, accuracy improves by up to 29.45, 4.42, 36.72, and 17.11 percentage points on MATH-500, GPQA Diamond, IFEval, and SciBench, respectively. Notably, modifying only 1,023 parameters yields a 29.45-point improvement. Second, WSP exhibits promising but uneven cross-task generalization. A Math-edited checkpoint improves GPQA Diamond, IFEval, and SciBench by 3.03, 12.18, and 20.10 points, respectively, without further editing, while leaving SciCode performance unchanged. Third, effective editing remains challenging and inconsistent. Outcomes vary substantially across trajectories, and six of the 16 trajectories retain the original weights when candidate improvements cannot be reliably validated. Together, these results highlight WSP's potential for highly localized model adaptation while revealing important challenges in intervention discovery, reliability, and cross-task transfer.
  Homepage: https://wsp-scope.github.io/ GitHub: https://github.com/WSP-Scope/WSP-Scope-Project

</details>


### [335] [Against All Likelihood](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09385)
*David Lopez-Paz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文主张以"不变性"而非"概率"作为判断陈述真实性的标准，借用Nozick、Wigner、Poincaré、Cassirer的哲学传统，联系OOD泛化与不变学习文献，并提出在推理阶段测试不变性的思路及对持续学习的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大似然是LLM训练的主导原则，但似然既非真理的必要条件也非充分条件：罕见事件天天发生，反复出现的陈述也可能是假的。因此需要一个超越单一分布概率的真理判据。

Method: 概念/立场论文（position paper），非实验研究。以将客观性归于不变性而非概率的哲学谱系（Nozick的客观事实观、Wigner的不变性原理、Poincaré与Cassirer的结构实在论）为理论基础，论证陈述的真伪应由其在环境相关变换下的不变性来判定，并与机器学习中的分布外泛化与不变学习文献建立联系。

Result: 提出不变性作为真理判据的框架，并给出如何在推理阶段用语言模型检验不变性的构想，以及该视角对持续学习的启示；本文为理论论述，无实证结果。

Conclusion: 应把LLM的评估与训练从单纯的概率/似然视角转向跨环境变换的不变性视角，这为真理性判断、推理时检验和持续学习提供了新的原则性方向。

Abstract: Maximum likelihood is the dominant training principle for large language models, yet likelihood is neither necessary nor sufficient for truth. Unlikely events happen every day, and frequently repeated statements can be false. Drawing on a philosophical tradition that ties objectivity to invariance rather than probability (from Nozick's account of objective facts and Wigner's principles of invariance to the structural realism of Poincaré and Cassirer), we argue that the truth of a statement should be judged not by its probability under a single distribution, but by its invariance across relevant transformations of the environment. We connect this view to machine learning literature on out-of-distribution generalization and invariant learning, sketch how invariance could be tested at inference time with language models, and discuss implications for continual learning.

</details>


### [336] [Travel Cost Calibration for Multi-Agent Target Assignment and Pathfinding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09396)
*Yu Kumagai,Ryohei Banno,Keisuke Okumura*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种迭代式TAPF方法，利用快速次优MAPF反馈校准旅行代价估计，从而改进目标分配质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TAPF中常用的"最小化单智能体最短路距离总和后再解MAPF"的做法存在缺陷：最短路距离只是代理指标，忽略了智能体之间的相互干扰。真正决定最终质量的应是智能体实际付出代价，而这又依赖所有智能体的分配与路径选择，因此需要一种能反映真实干扰的目标分配方式。

Method: 采用迭代框架：先用快速、次优的MAPF求解器给出反馈，据此校准（calibrate）各智能体-目标对的旅行代价估计，再基于更新后的代价重新进行目标分配，循环迭代使分配逐步逼近考虑干扰后的真实成本。

Result: 在小型实例上所得解接近最优；在较大实例上，其解质量优于现有规划器。

Conclusion: 以实际行驶代价而非最短路距离来指导目标分配能显著提升TAPF解质量，借助快速次优MAPF反馈进行迭代校准是一种有效且可扩展的方案。

Abstract: Target assignment and pathfinding (TAPF) is an appealing extension of classical multi-agent pathfinding (MAPF): each agent has a set of targets, and the planner must both assign a target to each agent and plan collision-free paths for them. A trivial approach is to compute an assignment that minimizes the total single-agent shortest-path distance over agent--target pairs, and then solve the resulting MAPF instance. However, this approach falls short because the shortest-path distance remains a proxy that ignores interference among agents. Assigning targets based on the costs agents actually pay, which depend on how every other agent is assigned and routed, would yield a better final outcome. To this end, we propose an iterative TAPF method that improves target assignments by calibrating travel cost estimates from fast, suboptimal MAPF feedback. Experiments show that our method produces solutions close to optimal on small instances and outperforms existing planners in solution quality on larger ones.

</details>


### [337] [PhyCAD: Physics-Aware Text-to-CAD Generation via Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09401)
*Yuxin Cui,Weijie Zheng,Zhengyang Li,Han Wang,Wenjie Feng,Qiyu Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PhyCAD 提出物理感知的 Text-to-CAD 生成框架，训练语言模型在几何与物理约束下生成可执行 CadQuery 程序，并设计分层平衡优势用于 GRPO 以保留约束优先级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本驱动 CAD 生成只关注几何正确性，但几何正确并不保证物理可行性；同时异质奖励的扁平聚合会让单项指标提升掩盖未满足的设计要求。

Method: 构建关联参数化 CAD 模型、几何规格、载荷条件与物理约束的 Text-to-CAD 数据集；开发结合程序执行、几何检查与有限元分析的自动 CAD–CAE 验证器提供反馈；提出 Hierarchical Balanced Advantage（HB-GRPO），按验证状态分层并在层内做目标维度同伴排序，结合层间进度信号。

Result: 在多个语言模型上，PhyCAD 持续提升几何与物理约束的联合满足程度。

Conclusion: 可验证的工程反馈能推动语言驱动 CAD 生成从几何重建走向物理感知设计。

Abstract: Language-driven computer-aided design (CAD) generation enables the creation of editable parametric models from text, yet geometric correctness alone does not ensure physical feasibility. We present PhyCAD, a framework for physics-aware Text-to-CAD generation that trains language models to produce executable CadQuery programs under geometric and physical constraints. To support this setting, we construct a Text-to-CAD dataset that links parametric CAD models with geometric specifications, loading conditions, and physical constraints. We further develop an automated CAD--CAE verifier that combines program execution, geometric checks, and finite element analysis to provide feedback for model training. A key learning challenge is to prioritize joint satisfaction of geometric and physical constraints while retaining informative feedback on partial progress. A flat aggregation of heterogeneous rewards can obscure this priority, allowing improvements in individual criteria to compensate for unmet design requirements. We therefore introduce a Hierarchical Balanced Advantage for Group Relative Policy Optimization, grouping candidates into levels according to their verification status and combining between-level progress with objective-wise peer ranking within each level. This formulation preserves cross-level priority in the training signal while providing fine-grained feedback for satisfying constraints and improving feasible designs. Experiments across multiple language models show that PhyCAD consistently improves joint geometric and physical constraint satisfaction. These results demonstrate the value of verifiable engineering feedback for advancing language-driven CAD generation beyond geometric reconstruction toward physics-aware design.

</details>


### [338] [ROAM: Retrieval-Augmented Optimization for Feasible Multi-Day Itinerary Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09407)
*Ye Zihao,Lin Xiaoheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ROAM是一个检索增强的智能体框架，用于生成多日旅行行程，将语义偏好检索与显式时空优化结合，在四个中国城市中显著提升了行程的POI覆盖量与路线效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM能生成看似合理的旅行行程，但常常不可行：景点按天分组时不考虑地理邻近性、忽略开放时间，且单次生成难以可靠组织多日行程。

Method: ROAM嵌入自然语言请求，从精选POI库检索紧凑候选集；通过最大团分析与K-means将候选按天分组；用带质量偏置的模拟退火TSP求解器为每天排序；用贪心交换修复开放时间硬约束；时空检索层将天气预报、真实道路距离与POI营业时间注入提示词，由LLM生成结构化、可地图化的行程；再用交互式ReAct风格重规划智能体支持自然语言编辑并重新校验可行性。

Result: 在四个中国城市、510个POI上，ROAM三天行程平均包含27.5个POI，裸LLM为19.3，仅检索基线为17.0（分别提升42%与62%）；路线离散度从2101降至677（3.1倍）。消融实验表明空间优化是路线效率提升的主要驱动因素，硬时间窗修复使约30%的景点避免违反营业时间。

Conclusion: 将LLM生成能力与显式时空优化、检索和可行性修复相结合，可显著提升多日行程规划的可行性与质量，空间优化与时间窗修复是关键组件。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can draft plausible travel itineraries, but the plans they produce are frequently infeasible: attractions are grouped into days without regard for geography, opening hours are ignored, and a single generation pass cannot reliably organize a multi-day trip. We present ROAM, a retrieval-augmented agentic framework for spontaneous multi-day itinerary planning that couples semantic preference retrieval with explicit spatiotemporal optimization. ROAM embeds the natural-language request, retrieves a compact candidate set from a curated point-of-interest (POI) database, partitions candidates into day groups through maximal-clique analysis and K-means, orders each day with a quality-biased simulated-annealing TSP solver, and repairs hard opening-hour violations with greedy exchange. A spatiotemporal retrieval layer injects weather forecasts, real road distances and POI operating hours into the prompt, after which the LLM renders a structured, map-ready itinerary; an interactive ReAct-style replanning agent then supports natural-language edits while re-checking feasibility. Across four Chinese cities and 510 POIs, ROAM produces 27.5 POIs per three-day plan versus 19.3 for a bare LLM and 17.0 for a retrieval-only baseline (+42% and +62%), while reducing route dispersion from 2,101 to 677 (3.1x). Ablations show that spatial optimization is the dominant driver of route efficiency and that hard time-window repair prevents roughly 30% of attractions from violating operating hours.

</details>


### [339] [MIRA: Mitigating Pole Geometry Bias in Recurrent Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09412)
*Róisín Luo,Ihsan Ullah,Christian Gagné,Dong Li,Karyn Morrissey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将选择性SSM、RWKV、RetNet等Transformer的循环后继统一刻画为无限脉冲响应(IIR)系统，指出其参数化代数结构使极点被约束在实轴上，产生架构偏置；据此提出基于格-梯(lattice-ladder)实现的统一线性时间循环架构Mira，可表示实极点与共轭复极点对应的时序模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环架构为换取并行性，其参数化结构常导致极点局限于实轴，从而在循环动力学中引入架构偏置，限制了可表示的时序模态。作者希望统一这些架构并消除极点几何偏置。

Method: 以IIR系统视角分析各循环架构的零极点几何；借鉴通用IIR系统的lattice-ladder实现，提出Mira——每个Mira块通过格-梯结构实现高阶IIR系统以从token流中提取时序模态，后接FFN，并保持高度并行的代数结构；配套实现专用CUDA kernel。

Result: 预训练12层、0.14B参数的Mira-S（全部由Mira块组成）：预训练困惑度低于强循环基线，超出训练长度的上下文窗口泛化更强，且借助专用CUDA kernel获得更高吞吐。

Conclusion: 将循环语言模型统一到IIR框架下并采用lattice-ladder实现，可有效消除实轴极点偏置、建模复共轭极点模态，在困惑度、长上下文泛化和效率上均优于现有循环基线，为线性时间LLM提供了统一且高效的架构族。

Abstract: We show that the Transformer's recurrent successors --- such as selective state-space models (SSMs), Receptance Weighted Key Value (RWKV), and Retentive Network (RetNet) --- can be characterized as infinite-impulse-response (IIR) systems. The zero--pole geometries of these IIR systems characterize the temporal modes they can represent. However, to trade parallelism, the algebraic structures of the parameterizations of these recurrent systems often give rise to poles constrained to the real axis, thereby inducing an architectural bias in their recurrent dynamics. To unify these recurrent architectures and eliminate their pole geometry bias, inspired by the lattice--ladder realization of general IIR systems, we present \textsc{Mira} (\textbf{M}odal \textbf{I}IR \textbf{R}ecurrent \textbf{A}rchitecture), a unified family of linear-time recurrent architectures for building large language models (LLMs) with a highly parallel algebraic structure. Each \textsc{Mira} block realizes a higher-order IIR system that represents temporal modes from token streams through a lattice--ladder structure, followed by a feed-forward network (FFN). This realization enables the model to represent the temporal modes associated with both real and complex-conjugate poles. We pretrain \textsc{Mira-S}, a 12-layer small-scale LLM with 0.14B parameters composed entirely of \textsc{Mira} blocks. Throughout the pretraining, \textsc{Mira-S} achieves lower perplexity than strong recurrent baselines, exhibits stronger context-window generalization beyond the training length, and attains higher throughput with a dedicated CUDA kernel.

</details>


### [340] [Typed-Centrality Grounding for Agentic GraphRAG: A Symbolic Approach for Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09414)
*Rosario Napoli,Antonio Celesti,Massimo Villari,Maria Fazio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于智能体的GraphRAG系统，引入符号层（typed centralities）将知识图谱的结构化确定性知识提供给LLM，与文本相似度检索结合。在真实POLE数据集上的实验表明，该方法生成的回答更符合上下文、输出质量更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GraphRAG方法将知识图谱视为同质图，且仅依赖输入提示与上下文之间的文本相似度进行检索，并未把KG当作确定性知识来源加以利用，导致LLM无法获得可靠的结构化推理依据。

Method: 构建智能体式GraphRAG系统，引入符号层以在文本相似度之外提供确定性推理。核心是定义KG上的类型化中心性（typed centralities）：针对KG中每个特定类分别计算中心性度量，据此构造符号表示并作为确定性知识源输入LLM。系统同时进行了数学形式化验证。

Result: 在真实世界的POLE（人物、对象、地点、事件）数据集上，对比有/无符号层情况下的推理与信息检索效果，结果显示所提方法生成的回答与上下文更加一致，生成输出得到改进。

Conclusion: 将知识图谱的结构化确定性知识（类型化中心性）作为符号层注入GraphRAG，可有效弥补纯文本相似度检索的不足，提升LLM回答的一致性与质量。

Abstract: Knowledge Graphs (KGs) and Large Language Models (LLMs) represent two approaches for knowledge representation and reasoning over heterogeneous data. Recent research has explored their integration through Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation (GraphRAG) systems, which use KGs to provide structured context to LLMs. However, existing GraphRAG approaches treat KGs as homogeneous graphs and rely on textual similarity between the input prompt and the context, rather than using it as a source of deterministic knowledge.
  This work proposes an agent-based GraphRAG system that introduces a symbolic layer to integrate deterministic reasoning alongside text similarities. This approach is based on the definition of typed centralities for KGs, where centrality measures are computed separately for each specific KG's class. This information is used to construct a symbolic representation that is provided to the LLM as a deterministic source. The proposed system was mathematically validated and evaluated on a real-world POLE (Person, Object, Location, Event) dataset, comparing reasoning and information retrieval with and without the symbolic layer. The results indicate that the our approach leads to responses that are more consistent with the context, improving the generated outputs.

</details>


### [341] [Agentic AI for Healthcare Claim Denial Analysis: A Policy-Grounded Framework for Privacy Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09423)
*Sofia Campos,Carlos Martins,Pedro Pires,João Gama,Bruno Veloso,Pedro Santos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出模块化Agentic AI框架用于医疗理赔拒付分析，包含风险预测、风险因素识别、编码关系分析、反事实分析等专业Agent，并结合预测分析、图学习、基于政策文档的混合上下文RAG和HIPAA合规Agent；在合成数据集上达到98%数值可追溯性、216条政策引用验证214条、剔除全部280个敏感标识符且不改变分析结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗理赔拒付带来显著财务与行政负担，亟需自动化且可信的决策支持系统；现有方法难以兼顾可解释性、政策依据与隐私保护。

Method: 模块化Agentic AI框架：多个专业Agent分别负责拒付风险预测、风险因素识别、诊断-操作-药品编码关系分析及反事实分析；融合预测分析、图学习、基于医疗政策文档的Hybrid Contextual RAG流水线，并设专门HIPAA合规Agent保障隐私。

Result: 在合成医疗理赔数据集上，数值发现追溯率达98%，216条政策引用中验证214条，剔除全部280个已识别敏感标识符且未改变底层分析结果。

Conclusion: 结果表明Agentic AI可作为可解释、政策感知且隐私保护的医疗理赔拒付管理框架，为后续研究奠定基础；目前仅基于合成数据验证，真实场景应用仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: In the healthcare sector, claim denials impose significant financial and administrative burdens, emphasizing the need for automated and trustworthy decision-support systems. This paper proposes a modular Agentic Artificial Intelligence (AI) framework for healthcare claim denial analysis, integrating specialized agents for denial risk prediction, risk factor identification, diagnosis-procedure-medication code relationship analysis, and counterfactual analysis. The framework integrates predictive analytics, graph-based learning, a Hybrid Contextual Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipeline grounded in healthcare policy documents, and a dedicated HIPAA Compliance Agent to support privacy protection. Evaluated on a synthetic healthcare claims dataset, the framework achieved 98% traceability of reported numerical findings, validated 214 of 216 policy citations, and removed all 280 identified sensitive identifiers without modifying the underlying analysis. These findings pave the way for further research into Agentic AI as a framework for explainable, policy-aware, and privacy-preserving healthcare claim denial management.

</details>


### [342] [Maya a1: Do the Options Read the Model, or Does Their Position? Order Sensitivity in a Non-Autoregressive Decision Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09803)
*Mohammed Nayeem Khan,Logesh L*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 对 Laya 多选问答模型的重工程实验表明，其答案显著依赖选项排列顺序（翻转率 25.8%），且可通过带打乱的微调显著缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Laya 通过对每个选项的 [MASK] 标记隐藏状态打分、单次前向完成多选，但该设计隐含“选项位置无关”的假设，论文要验证答案是否真的不受选项排列顺序影响。

Method: 重建 421M 参数检查点（2 层选项头、无位置张量、存在文档化但未使用的并行布局），在 951 道 Qwen 验证题目上做全循环轮换（12 选项时用 6 次随机排列），并比较金标选项在首/末位的准确率；再用作者训练器在 2,818 条教师验证数据上做带/不带选项打乱的微调，并测试作者并行布局与置信度加权投票。

Result: 排列翻转率 25.8% [22.9–28.7]，仅英文 605 题 19.7%；金标在首位与末位准确率差 10.1 个百分点；翻转集中在低置信度（置信三分位 54% vs 8%）。带打乱微调翻转率降至 7.2%，不打乱为 11.4%（打乱贡献 4.2 点）。置信度加权投票 69.7% vs 单次 69.1%，计算量 4.1 倍。用作者并行布局微调后翻转率 0.5%。

Conclusion: Laya 存在明显的选项位置偏置且与低置信度相关；选项打乱微调与并行布局微调能大幅降低翻转率，而多顺序投票收益有限、代价高。实验标签由 LLM 生成且测试集经教师过滤，结论需谨慎推广。

Abstract: Laya answers typed multiple-choice questions in one forward pass by scoring a hidden state at one [MASK] marker per option. We re-engineer the checkpoint (421M parameters; a 2-layer option head with no positional tensor; a documented but unused parallel layout) and test whether the answer depends on where an option is listed. Presenting 951 Qwen-verified questions under every cyclic rotation of their options (or six random permutations for 12 options), the released checkpoint changes its answer for 25.8% [22.9-28.7] of questions (19.7% on the 605 English-language items alone); accuracy with the gold option first versus last differs by 10.1 points, and the flips concentrate where the model's own confidence is low (54% versus 8% across confidence terciles). Fine-tuning with Laya's trainer on 2,818 teacher-verified rows with option shuffling cuts the flip rate to 7.2%; the identical rows without shuffling give 11.4%, so shuffling is responsible for 4.2 points of the improvement. A confidence-weighted vote over orderings gives 69.7% against 69.1% for a single base pass at 4.1x the compute. Labels are LLM-made and the test sets are teacher-filtered. We also fine-tune with the authors' parallel layout on the same rows: flip rate 0.5%.

</details>


### [343] [Definitions, Label Names, or Demonstrations? Auditing What Governs LLM Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09457)
*Viraj Mishra,Shubham Gupta,Dhruv Gupta,Yuehong Cassandra Tai,Qunhua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文通过反事实审计（对调定义与输出标签）检验LLM是否真正遵循研究者给定的类别定义，发现模型往往固守数据集原有的标签映射而非定义；随后研究检索增强标注，指出邻域标签是主要预测信号来源，需将定义、标签、示例、检索与训练数据联合对齐与验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被越来越多用于社会科学文本标注，但高准确率并不能证明模型真正应用了研究者提供的类别定义——预测可能只是依赖与类别标签的先验关联或带标签示例。因此需要区分"遵循给定定义"与"保留原数据集映射"这两种情形。

Method: 提出反事实审计方法：在输出标签之间重新分配定义，从而区分模型对给定定义的遵循与对原始映射的保留。在三个政治文本数据集上评估多种模型，并比较标准微调，然后研究检索增强标注（最近邻示例检索 vs 各类别内分别检索），以及直接k近邻分类、固定示例重标注、示例与重分配定义对齐等干预手段。

Result: 三个数据集中模型很少遵循重分配的定义；标准微调反而强化原始映射而非可靠提升定义遵循度。检索最近训练样本通常优于类别内分别检索（尽管示例更少）；在收益显著的两个任务上，直接k-NN分类可恢复74–93%的改进，说明邻域标签贡献了大部分预测信号。重标注固定示例可改变预测方向，而将示例与新定义对齐则显著提升对新映射的遵循度。

Conclusion: 提升准确率的干预并不必然提升对码本（定义）的遵循度；定义、类别标签、示例、检索和训练数据应被联合对齐与验证，并需以反事实审计等方式检验模型是否真正遵循研究者提供的定义。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to annotate social-science texts, but high accuracy does not establish that they apply researcher-supplied category definitions: predictions may instead reflect prior associations with category labels or labeled demonstrations. We introduce a counterfactual audit that reassigns definitions among output labels, distinguishing adherence to supplied definitions from preservation of a dataset's original mapping. Across three political-text datasets, models rarely follow reassigned definitions, and standard fine-tuning strengthens the original mapping without reliably increasing definition adherence. We then study retrieval-augmented annotation. Retrieving the nearest training examples generally outperforms retrieving examples separately within each category despite using fewer demonstrations. On the two tasks with substantial gains, direct \(k\)-nearest-neighbor classification recovers 74--93\% of the improvement, indicating that neighborhood labels provide much of the predictive signal. Relabeling fixed demonstrations redirects predictions, whereas aligning demonstrations with reassigned definitions substantially increases adherence to the new mapping. Thus, accuracy-improving interventions need not increase codebook adherence; definitions, category labels, demonstrations, retrieval, and training data should be aligned and validated jointly.

</details>


### [344] [BitDecoding-FP4: Native FP4 KV Cache Decoding via Algorithm-System Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09825)
*Dayou Du,Woon Yee Ng,Guangming Sheng,Nick Lau,Jianyi Cheng,Luo Mai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出BitDecoding-FP4，一种面向原生FP4 KV cache解码的算法-系统协同设计，兼顾精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM服务中KV cache主导内存带宽；现有FP4 KV系统要么用朴素量化导致精度严重下降，要么依赖向量码本查表拖慢解码吞吐，算法精度与系统加速之间存在鸿沟。

Method: 算法端消除三个误差源：双残差查询量化（拆分为两个FP4张量）、感知下溢的概率缩放（防E4M3 scale把长上下文概率舍入为零）、感知离群值的Key量化（静态平滑+Hadamard旋转+scale搜索）。系统端采用转置softmax配合融合概率量化与堆叠query MMA，在paged KV上消除softmax瓶颈。

Result: 在GQA与MLA的稠密及MoE模型上，NVFP4下精度与BF16 KV差距在0.5个点内（MXFP4为1个点），并能在RL训练rollout中跟踪BF16 KV；在NVIDIA B300上以Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-30B-A3B端到端服务，峰值吞吐较BF16 KV提升2.4–3.0倍。

Conclusion: 通过算法与系统协同设计，实现了高精度且高效的原生FP4 KV解码，显著提升长上下文LLM服务吞吐。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache dominates memory capacity and bandwidth in long-context LLM serving. While 4-bit microscaling formats (NVFP4, MXFP4) shrink the cache and run attention on FP4 Tensor Cores, a fundamental gap remains between algorithmic quantization and system-aware acceleration: existing FP4 KV systems use vanilla quantization that causes severe accuracy drops, whereas accuracy-oriented 4-bit algorithms rely on vector codebooks whose lookup overhead degrades decoding throughput. We present BitDecoding-FP4, an algorithm--system co-design for accurate and efficient native FP4 KV decoding. We mitigate three error sources: Dual-Residual Query Quantization splits queries into two FP4 tensors, cutting query logit error from 60% to 1%; Underflow-Aware Probability Scaling prevents E4M3 scales from rounding long-context probabilities to zero; and Outlier-Aware Key Quantization combines static smoothing, Hadamard rotation, and scale search to suppress key outliers under token-wise scaling. At the system level, we remove the softmax bottleneck via transposed softmax with fused probability quantization and stacked query MMA on paged KV caches. Across dense and MoE models with GQA and MLA, BitDecoding-FP4 stays within 0.5 points of BF16 KV accuracy with NVFP4 (1 point with MXFP4), and tracks BF16 KV in RL training rollouts. In end-to-end serving on NVIDIA B300 with Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-30B-A3B, it delivers 2.4-3.0$\times$ higher peak serving throughput than BF16 KV. The implementation is available at https://github.com/OpenBitSys/BitDecoding-FP4.

</details>


### [345] [LEMMO: A Large Electromagnetic Model leveraging Pretrained LLMs for Million-Sample Signal Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09476)
*Mingkai Li,Jiajun Wu,Yihan Mao,Zilin Sang,Taosong Yu,Yuan Ma,Minghe Liu,Qianqian Yang,Zhiguo Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LEMMO 是面向长原始 IQ 电磁信号录制的大模型，支持指令驱动的识别与详细描述，并配套发布 1.38 TB 数据集。相比 MERLIN-LoRA，其在 EM-Bench 感知准确率提升 15.77%，策略生成 ROUGE-L F1 提升 4.38，ShortQA 准确率提升 14.31%，长描述任务事实 F1 亦提升且不损失 MMLU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有波形电磁模型主要在短 IQ 片段上评测，无法处理含数百万样本的宽带长录制，缺乏对长录制信号的理解与细粒度描述能力。

Method: 构建 LEMMO：采用线性注意力 EM 编码器 + 抗混叠时间重采样，可处理最多 800 万复数样本；EM-文本交互模块以指令为条件对可学习查询进行调制后检索信号证据；紧凑查询用于识别，结构化查询结合辅助事实监督来组织场景属性、信号属性与跨信号关系以支持详细描述；并构建发布 1.38 TB 真实与合成 EM 信号语料（含 LEMMO-ShortQA、LEMMO-LongDesc）。

Result: 相比 MERLIN-LoRA，EM-Bench 平均感知准确率提升 15.77%，策略生成平均 ROUGE-L F1 提升 4.38 点，LEMMO-ShortQA 平均准确率提升 14.31%；在 LEMMO-LongDesc 两任务上类别与数值事实 F1 均提升，同时保持语言骨干的 MMLU 性能。

Conclusion: LEMMO 将大模型能力扩展到长原始电磁信号的理解与描述，并通过统一大规模数据与结构化查询设计，在识别、策略生成和长录制描述上取得一致提升且不损害通用语言能力。

Abstract: Large language models have expanded beyond text to vision, audio, and other modalities, but understanding raw electromagnetic (EM) signals remains challenging. Wideband recordings can contain millions of samples, whereas existing waveform-based EM models are primarily evaluated on short in-phase and quadrature (IQ) segments, leaving long-recording understanding and detailed description insufficiently explored. We introduce LEMMO, a large EM model for instruction-driven recognition and description of long raw IQ recordings. LEMMO combines a linear-attention EM encoder with anti-aliased temporal resampling to process up to eight million complex samples. Its EM-text interaction module conditions learnable queries on instructions before retrieving signal evidence. Compact queries support recognition, while structured queries with auxiliary factual supervision organize scene-level properties, signal attributes, and cross-signal relationships for detailed description. We also construct and release a unified 1.38-TB corpus of real-world and synthetic EM signals, including LEMMO-ShortQA and LEMMO-LongDesc. Compared with MERLIN-LoRA, LEMMO improves mean EM-Bench perception accuracy by 15.77 %, mean strategy-generation ROUGE-L F1 by 4.38 points, and mean LEMMO-ShortQA accuracy by 14.31 %. It also improves categorical and numerical fact F1 on both LEMMO-LongDesc tasks while preserving its language backbone's MMLU performance. Our code and datasets are available at https://github.com/N1rv4n4/LEMMO and https://huggingface.co/datasets/DaftJun/RFLAS, respectively.

</details>


### [346] [A Physics-Informed Learning Framework for Linking Microscopic Surface Degradation to Bolted Joint Functional Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09514)
*Nehal Afifi,Gerhard Kachel,Sven Matthiesen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出物理信息多模态框架，将腐蚀螺栓连接的微观与相机退化观测映射为功能指标，并通过PDE发现提取有效介观演化规律，与功能模型耦合预测摩擦系数与预紧力损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环工程中回收件退化历史异质，剩余功能难以评估；螺栓连接中腐蚀会改变表面形貌、接触、摩擦、预紧保持与力学响应，缺乏从退化观测到功能指标的可靠可复现评估路径。

Method: 定义腐蚀失效模式并生成同步的检测与功能响应记录；预处理图像、粗糙度、暴露量与接头描述符；用相似性学习对齐显微与相机观测；构建标定的腐蚀严重度场；对该场做PDE发现以识别候选有效介观规律而非完整电化学机理；再将腐蚀演化模型与独立功能模型耦合，以摩擦系数为主目标、有夹紧力数据时纳入预紧力损失。

Result: 摘要未给出定量结果；框架输出为候选介观腐蚀演化律及与功能模型耦合的摩擦系数（及可用时的预紧力损失）评估，强调可复现与物理约束。

Conclusion: 提供了一条可复现、物理约束的退化螺栓连接行为评估路径，将多模态退化观测与功能指标通过数据驱动的介观规律连接起来。

Abstract: Returned products in circular engineering often have heterogeneous degradation histories, making remaining function difficult to assess. For bolted joints, corrosion alters surface morphology, contact, friction, preload retention, and mechanical response. This paper presents a physics-informed multimodal framework for linking microscopic and camera-based degradation observations to functional indicators of corroded bolted joints. The workflow defines the corrosion failure mode, generates synchronized inspection and functional-response records, preprocesses image, roughness, exposure, and joint descriptors, aligns microscopy and camera observations through similarity learning, and constructs a calibrated corrosion-severity field. Partial differential equation (PDE) discovery is applied to this field to identify candidate effective mesoscale laws rather than complete electrochemical mechanisms. The corrosion-evolution model is coupled to a separate functional model, with friction coefficient as the primary target and preload loss included when clamp-force data are available. The contribution is a reproducible pathway for physically constrained assessment of degraded bolted-joint behavior.

</details>


### [347] [Atik: A Shared-Mesh Transformer Accelerator for RISC-V](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09522)
*Ahmed Zeer,Erkan Uslu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Atik 是面向 RocketChip 的开源 RoCC 加速器，用同一固定点 MAC 网格执行矩阵乘与稠密注意力，采用流式 online-softmax，BF16 输入输出，8x8 配置峰值加速 31.5x（matmul）/225.8x（attention）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 推理反复调用稠密矩阵乘与注意力（含数据相关的 softmax 归一化），在通用标量核上开销大，需要专用硬件支持。

Method: 基于 RoCC 接入 RocketChip；单一共享固定点 MAC 网格同时处理 matmul 与 dense attention；BF16 张量接口；流式 online-softmax 调度，不显式物化完整 score 矩阵；小型 descriptor-based ABI；用 FireSim 逐周期评估三种网格规模，并给出 Sky130 物理设计面积。

Result: 137 条 FireSim 测试记录全部通过基准容差、零输出失配；相对标量 Rocket BF16 参考，8x8 配置峰值 matmul 加速 31.5x、attention 加速 225.8x；基于实测 kernel 周期的混合投影得到 ViT 44.2x、TinyBERT 77.1x、GPT-2 prefill 32.3x 的聚合加速（非全模型硬件回放）。

Conclusion: 共享网格+online-softmax 的设计可在保持数值容差内高效加速 Transformer 关键算子；但速度数据主要为算子级实测与解析投影，尚需全模型硬件回放验证。

Abstract: Transformer inference repeatedly invokes dense matrix multiplication and attention, including its data-dependent softmax normalization. We present Atik, an open-source RoCC accelerator for RocketChip that executes matrix multiplication and dense attention through one shared fixed-point MAC mesh. Atik accepts and returns BF16 tensors, uses a streaming online-softmax schedule that never materializes the full score matrix, and is invoked through a small descriptor-based ABI. We evaluate three meshes cycle-by-cycle on FireSim and report standalone Sky130 physical-design area. Across 137 logged FireSim test records, all tests pass their benchmark tolerance with zero reported output mismatches. Relative to the same scalar Rocket BF16 reference, the 8x8 configuration reaches 31.5x peak matmul speedup and 225.8x peak attention speedup. Hybrid projections for the single-layer workload-derived C models, using directly measured accelerator-kernel cycles, give aggregate speedups of 44.2x, 77.1x, and 32.3x for ViT, TinyBERT, and GPT-2 prefill, respectively; these are not full-model hardware replays.

</details>


### [348] [StockASkill: A Local-First AgentSkill for Intelligent Stock Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09528)
*Xiangjing An,Zhibin Jiao,Aiting Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出StockASkill：本地优先、全数据驱动的量化"协处理器"，用DuckDB列式OLAP承载26年42.44M条行情与186k条财报，将5400+股票横截面筛选从>25s降至<0.5s，并集成7+1因子Alpha模型、SLSQP+CVaR回撤代理优化、100股整数手量化与投资逻辑生命周期状态机，提供流式JSON接口供LLM工具调用；2018–2024回测年化Sharpe>1.48、Calmar达2.17、最大回撤较基准降幅最高85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴与跨境市场的量化选股与组合构建面临计算瓶颈、远程API脆弱性、幸存者偏差，以及整数手、投资者强制的回撤上限等刚性执行约束；对10^5~10^7元规模的敏捷组合，离散手数与资本保护是硬需求，而现成方案缺乏兼顾性能、本地可控与合规执行的端到端工具。

Method: 构建本地优先的AgentSkill量化协处理器：底层用DuckDB列式OLAP存储42.44M条价格记录与186,432条财务披露，覆盖A股/港股/美股/ETF共26年，分析期间无网络依赖；上层形式化端到端数学流水线——7+1因子Alpha模型、带平滑CVaR回撤代理与精确路径投影的SLSQP凸优化器、动态100股整手整数量化、自主投资逻辑生命周期有限状态机；对外提供零磁盘、强类型JSON流式接口以适配高吞吐LLM工具调用。

Result: 5400+股票横截面筛选延迟由>25秒降至<0.5秒（降幅95%+）；2018–2024基准测试显示年化Sharpe>1.48、Calmar最高2.17，最大回撤相对基准指数最多降低85%。

Conclusion: 本地全数据+列式OLAP可有效消除API脆弱性与计算瓶颈，并将因子、优化、整数手数与状态机整合为面向AI Agent的可用量化基础设施，在风险调整收益与回撤控制上显著优于基准，已开源。

Abstract: Quantitative equity selection and portfolio construction in emerging and cross-border markets frequently suffer from computational bottlenecks, remote API fragility, survivorship bias, and rigid execution constraints such as discrete board lots and investor-mandated drawdown limits. In this paper, we present StockASkill, a local-first, full-data-driven AgentSkill engineered as a specialized Quant Co-Processor for autonomous AI agents and quantitative researchers. Tailored for agile portfolios (10^5 ~ 10^7 RMB) where discrete lot execution and capital protection are mandatory, StockASkill embeds a columnar DuckDB OLAP engine over 42.44 million historical price records and 186,432 financial disclosures spanning 26 years across China A-shares, Hong Kong, US equities, and ETFs. By eliminating ad-hoc network dependencies during analysis, StockASkill reduces cross-sectional screening latency across 5,400+ equities from over 25 seconds to sub-0.5 seconds (a 95%+ reduction). We formalize an end-to-end mathematical pipeline integrating a 7+1 factor alpha model, an SLSQP convex optimizer under smooth CVaR drawdown surrogates with exact path projection, dynamic 100-share board lot integer quantization, and an autonomous thesis lifecycle finite-state machine. Furthermore, we provide a zero-disk, strongly-typed JSON streaming interface designed for high-throughput LLM tool-calling. Open-sourced at https://github.com/axjing/stockaskill, comprehensive empirical benchmarks from 2018 to 2024 demonstrate that the architecture achieves annualized Sharpe ratios exceeding 1.48 and Calmar ratios up to 2.17, while reducing max drawdown by up to 85% relative to benchmark indices.

</details>


### [349] [PreForceVision: Physics-Guided Optical Estimation of Bolt Preload from Bolt-Head Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09529)
*Nehal Afifi,Klaus Rappenecker,Florian Peinhardt,Gerhard Kachel,Alfred Isele,Sven Matthiesen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PreForceVision：一种物理引导的光学框架，通过螺栓头端面预紧力引起的表面变形，以非接触、无需额外硬件的方式反演估计螺栓预紧力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预紧力测量技术（应变螺栓、力垫圈、超声法、螺栓长度测量）虽在特定场景有效，但需额外硬件、耦合、标定或接头改造，难以扩展到大量标准紧固件，故需发展非接触光学方法。

Method: 将预紧力估计建模为"变形→力"的反问题：采用受控参考-加载图像对采集，基于DIC或光流提取光学可及的螺栓头端面变形，结合多模态验证与不确定性感知回归进行预紧力估计。

Result: M16 8.8级螺栓初步结果：90 kN下仿真头面凹陷约12 μm；应变片测得量级相当；光学3D测得中心凹陷约10–20 μm，三者相互印证。

Conclusion: 该工作提出一种物理引导的光学方法学，在受控条件下可实现粗略预紧力估计，具备面向大批量标准紧固件非接触测量的可扩展潜力。

Abstract: Bolt preload is a decisive functional quantity in bolted joints because it determines whether external loads are transmitted primarily through frictional clamping or directly through the bolt. Existing preload measurement techniques, including strain-gauge bolts, force washers, ultrasonic methods, and bolt-length measurements, are effective in certain applications but require additional hardware, coupling, calibration, or joint modifications. This limits their scalability for large numbers of standard fasteners and motivates a non-contact optical approach. This paper proposes PreForceVision, a physics-guided optical framework for estimating bolt preload from preload-induced surface deformation of the bolt-head front face. The method treats preload estimation as an inverse deformation-to-force problem, where deformation of the optically accessible bolt-head surface is extracted from reference-loaded image pairs and related to the internal preload state. Preliminary results for an M16 strength class 8.8 bolt show an approximately \SI{12}{\micro\meter} simulated head-face depression at \SI{90}{\kilo\newton}, strain-gauge measurements of comparable magnitude, and an optical 3D measured central depression of approximately \SIrange{10}{20}{\micro\meter}. The contribution is a physics-guided optical methodology combining controlled image-pair acquisition, DIC- or optical-flow-based deformation extraction, multimodal validation, and uncertainty-aware regression for rough preload estimation under controlled conditions.

</details>


### [350] [CID: Measuring Cognitive Work Through Semantic State Change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09542)
*Ron Fulbright*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"语义状态"与"认知工作"概念，并引入情境信息差(CID)——一种基于马氏距离、考虑协方差的度量，用来量化嵌入流形中的语义位移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信息理论擅长分析统计特性，却难以刻画"意义"与语义转换；此前无法对任意信息的意义进行量化。大语言模型将信息表示为高维语义空间中的向量，使意义可被几何化分析，这为量化语义变化提供了契机。

Method: 将语义状态定义为模型中信息的向量表示，将认知工作定义为语义状态之间的转换（即语义空间中的移动）。引入Contextual Information Differential (CID)：一种基于马氏距离、感知协方差的度量，衡量相对于嵌入流形结构的语义位移。

Result: CID提供了一种实用方法，可测量语义变化、认知转换与信息处理过程；其度量与具体实现无关，可同时应用于人类系统与人工系统。

Conclusion: 该框架为分析认知处理、学习、推理、知识生成、合成专长及人机协作奠定基础，通过实现无关的语义状态变化度量连接人类与人工智能系统。

Abstract: Traditional information theory provides powerful tools for analyzing statistical properties of information but offers limited insight into meaning and semantic transformation. It has never been possible to quantify the meaning of an arbitrary piece of information. Large language models represent information as vectors embedded within high-dimensional semantic spaces, enabling meaning to be analyzed geometrically. This paper defines semantic state as the vector representation of information within a model's semantic space and defines cognitive work as the transformation between semantic states, movement through semantic space. To quantify this transformation, we introduce the Contextual Information Differential (CID), a covariance-aware metric based on Mahalanobis distance measuring semantic displacement relative to the structure of the embedding manifold. By treating meaning as a geometric property of semantic space, CID provides a practical method for measuring semantic change, cognitive transformation, and information processing. This framework establishes a foundation for analyzing cognitive processing, learning, reasoning, knowledge generation, synthetic expertise, and human/AI collaboration through implementation-independent measurement of semantic state change in both human and artificial systems.

</details>


### [351] [CoMoFlow: Controllable Protein Conformational Ensemble Generation via Explicit Collective-Mode Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09548)
*Yusong Wang,Zheyuan Gu,Ailiang Lin,Minghao Shao,Yicheng Xu,Jiang Cai,Prayag Tiwari,Mingkun Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CoMoFlow，一个通过显式建模集体变化来可控生成蛋白质主链构象系综的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流的方法虽能高效生成构象系综，但隐式建模协同结构变化，限制了系综保真度且难以直接控制。

Method: CoMoFlow从平衡结构构建蛋白质特异的集体模式，学习这些系数在MD系综上的分布，并用采样的集体变化通过特征条件化与先验重定心来引导SE(3)流匹配。

Result: 在ATLAS和de novo蛋白上，相较于强流基线，CoMoFlow更好重建MD构象分布与协同结构变化，同时可对目标构象变化实现直接控制。

Conclusion: 显式建模集体变化可提升构象系综生成的保真度与可控性。

Abstract: Protein conformational ensemble generation aims to efficiently characterize the structural variations accessible to a protein beyond a single static structure. Recent flow-based methods efficiently generate conformational ensembles, but they model coordinated structural variation implicitly, which can limit ensemble fidelity and hinder direct control. To address this limitation, we propose CoMoFlow, a controllable framework for generating protein backbone conformational ensembles by explicitly modeling collective variation. CoMoFlow constructs protein-specific collective modes from the equilibrium structure, learns the distribution of their coefficients across the molecular dynamics (MD) ensemble, and uses sampled collective variations to guide SE(3) flow matching through feature conditioning and prior recentering. Experiments on ATLAS and de novo proteins show that CoMoFlow improves reconstruction of MD conformational distributions and coordinated structural variation relative to strong flow-based baselines, while enabling direct control over targeted conformational changes.

</details>


### [352] [Alignment by Confident Debate: Preference Learning from Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09568)
*Nikhil Verma,Dingxian Wang,Mohammad Reza Rezaei,Andrew Rabinovich,Zhao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 AbCD，将多智能体辩论轨迹无金标签地转化为偏好数据用于推理后训练，共识准确率最高提升 21.5 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理后训练依赖金答案、任务验证器或奖励模型，信号稀缺时难以扩展；多数投票等值加权、缺乏平局规则、开放任务无标准答案，似然打分混淆流畅性与正确性，同质委员会会强化相关错误。

Method: 让采样多样化的委员会对每题辩论，按任务级语义等价对最终答案聚类，从而将辩论轨迹转化为偏好数据；在数学推理、多项选择定量QA、可执行代码生成等可验证任务上，用答案键或执行检查器评估监督质量，但流程本身不使用金标签；聚类结合置信度加权的不一致形成偏好信号。

Result: 在5个模型家族（1B–8B）、4个数据集上，优于仅推理的集成与辩论，也优于同轨迹SFT，共识准确率最大提升 21.5pp；消融显示可迁移到更大模型与代码生成，同时揭示异构委员会与基于一致性的加权的局限。

Conclusion: 当答案等价性与置信度加权的不一致相结合时，辩论轨迹能提供有效的偏好监督，是一种可扩展的自我监督后训练途径。

Abstract: Post-training language models for reasoning typically rely on gold answers, task-specific verifiers, or learned reward models, limiting scalability when such signals are scarce. Multi-agent debate offers a self-supervised alternative, but converting debate into reliable training supervision remains challenging: majority voting weights agreeing responses equally, lacks a principled tie-breaking rule, and is ill-defined for open-ended tasks without a canonical answer; likelihood-based scoring can conflate fluency with correctness; and homogeneous committees may reinforce correlated errors. We introduce Alignment by Confident Debate (AbCD), which converts debate traces into preference data without gold answer labels. A sampling-diverse committee debates each problem, after which final answers are clustered by task-level semantic equivalence. We use verifiable tasks (mathematical reasoning, multiple-choice quantitative QA, and executable code-generation) as a controlled test bed: their answer keys or executable checkers let us evaluate the quality of debate-derived supervision against ground truth, even though the oracle itself never uses gold labels. Post-training on the resulting preferences outperforms inference-only ensembling and debate, as well as SFT on the same traces, across five model families spanning 1B to 8B parameters and four datasets, improving consensus accuracy, with a maximum gain of $21.5_{pp}$. Ablations show that the approach transfers to a larger model and to code generation, while also revealing limitations of heterogeneous committees and agreement-based weighting. Overall, the results suggest that debate traces can yield useful preference supervision when answer equivalence is combined with confidence-weighted disagreement.

</details>


### [353] [Not Your Diary, Just Your Topic: Grading the Wrong-User Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09609)
*Karina Romanova,Igor Kiselev,Vladimir Platonov,Viliana Devbunova,Gregory Leleytner,Adeliya Leleytner,Lina Veltman,Aleksandr Nikolich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文质疑常用的"换用户历史测掉点"式个性化评估，指出其混淆了主题匹配，并在LaMP-2上用冻结语言模型与合成历史做控制实验重新测量个性化增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统个性化测试把用户历史替换为他人历史并观察精度下降，但替换同时改变了历史主题，掉点可能只反映主题不匹配而非真正个性化失效，需要更干净的评估方式。

Method: 在LaMP-2新闻分类上用冻结语言模型，比较用户自身历史、相似用户历史、随机用户历史与按标签和文本相似度构造的合成历史；并对比无历史训练的基线、仅在评估时移除历史等设置，跨两个用户划分与三个训练种子，另加一个小规模电影打标实验。

Result: 自身历史比无历史模型提升2.3个百分点（评估时移除历史则显得为4.8点）；仅用类别标签即可复现86%的自身历史与随机用户历史差距；标签与文本相似度匹配的合成历史下差距降至0.9点；训练中见过的用户相对相似用户优势3.2点，未见用户仅0.65点且证据不足；电影打标实验无优势。此外门控交叉注意力在本训练设置下优于纯前缀模型。

Conclusion: 个性化应在未见用户上评估，并使用内容相似的历史与无历史训练模型作为对照；现有"换历史"式评估会高估个性化增益。门控交叉注意力的普适性尚待验证。

Abstract: A common test of personalization replaces a user's history with someone else's and measures the drop in accuracy. But this swap also changes the topics in the history, so the drop may reflect topic mismatch. We examine this problem with a frozen language model on LaMP-2 news categorization, using histories from similar users and synthetic histories assembled from other users' articles. The user's own history improves accuracy by 2.3 percentage points over a model trained without history. Removing history only at evaluation makes this gain appear larger, at 4.8 points. Category labels alone reproduce 86% of the accuracy gap between the user's own history and a random user's history. With synthetic histories matched on labels and text similarity, the gap falls to 0.9 points. Across two user splits and three training seeds, the advantage over a similar user's history is 3.2 points for users seen during training and 0.65 points for unseen users, where the evidence for an advantage remains inconclusive. A small movie-tagging experiment with one training seed also finds no advantage over other users' histories. These findings show why personalization should be evaluated on unseen users, with histories of similar content and a model trained without history as comparisons. We also find that adding gated cross-attention improves on a prefix-only model under our training setup; whether this holds more broadly remains open.

</details>


### [354] [Attend at the Right Resolution: Head-Adaptive Sparse Attention via Query-Key Interaction Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09700)
*Wajih Hassan Raza,Ahsan Bilal,Yidan Shen,Renjie Hu,Xin Fu,Yu Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HASA：一种免训练稀疏注意力框架，按各注意力头的查询-键算子固有结构自适应选择分辨率（特征维用奇异谱定有效秩，序列维按响应方向一致性合并区域），捕捉88.1%稠密注意力质量，在RULER/LongBench-Pro上优于UNIQUE最多3.47/2.57分，自注意力加速至多1.32x、相对稠密注意力加速至多14.07x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力是LLM的主要扩展瓶颈，计算成本与KV内存流量随上下文增长而急剧上升。稀疏注意力依赖廉价可靠地定位相关区域，但现有免训练方法依赖运行时相关性估计，且选择维度与粒度由固定启发式决定，未利用预训练注意力头的内在交互结构。

Method: 基于"每个头的query-key算子定义特征响应结构、决定所需选择分辨率"的洞见，提出Head-Adaptive Sparse Attention（HASA），在特征与序列两维联合自适应选择分辨率：特征维上，用各头query-key算子的奇异谱确定有效秩，得到紧凑的头特定响应空间；序列维上，在该响应空间中按token响应的方向一致性渐进合并邻近区域，异质区域保持分离；随后用所选区域的原始全维KV状态做精确注意力计算。

Result: HASA捕捉88.1%的稠密注意力质量；与强基线UNIQUE相比，在RULER和LongBench-Pro上准确率分别提升至多3.47和2.57分；自注意力加速至多1.32x，相对稠密注意力加速至多14.07x。

Conclusion: 利用预训练注意力头固有的响应结构来指导稀疏选择的分辨率，可在免训练条件下实现更可靠且高效的稀疏注意力，兼顾精度与速度。

Abstract: Attention is a major scalability bottleneck in large language models (LLMs), with its computational cost and key-value (KV) memory traffic becoming increasingly prohibitive as context length grows. Sparse attention addresses this bottleneck by restricting each query to a subset of the context, but its effectiveness hinges on identifying relevant regions both cheaply and reliably. Existing training-free methods primarily rely on runtime relevance estimates, with selection dimensionality and granularity typically governed by fixed heuristics rather than the intrinsic interaction structure of pretrained attention heads. We take a different perspective: the query-key operator of each pretrained head defines a characteristic response structure that determines the resolution needed for sparse selection, both across feature directions and across context tokens. Building on this insight, we propose Head-Adaptive Sparse Attention (HASA), a training-free sparse attention framework that jointly adapts selection resolution along these two dimensions. Along the feature dimension, the singular spectrum of each head's query-key operator determines an effective rank, yielding a compact, head-specific response space. Along the sequence dimension, neighboring regions are progressively merged according to the directional coherence of their token responses in this same space, while heterogeneous regions remain separate. Exact attention is then computed using the original full-dimensional KV states of the selected regions. HASA captures 88.1% of dense-attention mass and, compared with UNIQUE, a strong recent sparse-attention baseline, improves accuracy by up to 3.47 and 2.57 points on RULER and LongBench-Pro, respectively, and accelerates self-attention by up to 1.32x, while achieving up to 14.07x speedup over dense attention.

</details>


### [355] [Intent-Aware Caching for Efficient LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09715)
*Kazi Hasan Ibn Arif,JinYi Yoon,Dimitrios S. Nikolopoulos,Hans Vandierendonck,Deepu John,Bo Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出意图感知缓存淘汰策略IAC，按用户意图学习复用统计指导KV缓存淘汰，显著提升前缀缓存命中率并降低服务延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务系统用前缀缓存加速多轮对话，但缓存命中率受限于对话是否继续、下一轮何时到达的不确定性；实测发现用户意图与续聊概率和轮间隔相关，可作为预测信号。

Method: 在线淘汰策略：将请求按意图分类，从近期服务日志中学习轻量级复用统计（续聊概率、轮间隔），用这些统计驱动一个受oracle启发的淘汰策略。

Result: 在WildChat与ShareChat真实工作负载上命中率达34.6%，远超学习型基线18.8%和LRU的12.2%，平均服务延迟最多降低46.1%。

Conclusion: 用户意图是前缀缓存复用行为的有效预测信号，意图感知的在线淘汰可在低开销下显著提升缓存效率与服务质量。

Abstract: Modern LLM serving systems use prefix caching to accelerate multi-turn conversational workloads. They cache the key-value states of previous conversation prefixes and reuse them when subsequent requests share the same prefix, to avoid redundant prefill computation and reduce serving latency. However, achieving a high cache hit rate remains challenging because cache reuse depends on whether a conversation continues and how soon the next turn arrives. Our analysis of real workloads shows that user intent is a useful signal for this behavior, as different intents exhibit different continuation probabilities and inter-turn delays. Based on this key insight, we propose Intent-Aware Caching (IAC), an online eviction policy that classifies requests by intent, learns lightweight reuse statistics from recent serving logs and leverages these statistics to guide an oracle-inspired eviction strategy. Extensive evaluation on real workloads (WildChat and ShareChat) shows that IAC reaches a 34.6% cache hit rate. This substantially outperforms both the previous learning-based approach (18.8%) and traditional methods such as least recently used (LRU, 12.2%), while reducing average serving latency by up to 46.1%.

</details>


### [356] [FOSCAR: Accelerating Decode Attention over 2- and 4-Bit KV Caches through Affine Folding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09722)
*Donglin Zhuang,Pragaash Ponnusamy,Xiaoxia Wu,Shuaiwen Leon Song,Zhongzhu Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FOSCAR 通过"仿射折叠"把 KV 缓存的 scale/offset 从每个元素转移到另一操作数或输出，大幅减少 ALU 指令，并配合位操作与 warp-specialized 流水线实现高效 2/4-bit 解码注意力内核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 解码注意力需为每个生成 token 流式读取 KV 缓存，量化到 2/4-bit 可降低访存，但现有 2-bit 融合内核在 H100/B200 上仅达到峰值 HBM 带宽的 31%/15%。瓶颈在于逐元素反量化（乘 scale 加 offset）消耗稀缺的指令发射带宽。

Method: 采用仿射折叠（affine folding）：不逐 KV 值施加 scale/offset，而是根据量化方案（token-wise 或 channel-wise）将其作用到另一操作数或矩阵乘输出上，在精确算术下保持反量化结果不变，减少最多两个数量级的 scale/offset 操作；用位操作高效向 Tensor Core 供给 KV 操作数（H100 每 INT2 元素仅需 1 条、B200 仅需 0.19 条 ALU 指令）；基于 wgmma（Hopper）与 tcgen05（Blackwell）构建 warp-specialized GPU 流水线。

Result: H100：INT2/INT4 相对 FlashInfer BF16 加速 3.36×/2.83×，相对 TensorRT-LLM FP8 加速 2.03×/1.71×。B200：相对 FlashInfer BF16 为 2.67×/2.30×，相对 TensorRT-LLM FP8 为 1.48×/1.28×，相对 NVFP4 为 1.16×/1.00×。集成到 SGLang 后，长上下文服务负载下峰值聚合吞吐相比 2-bit 与 16-bit 基线分别提升最多 170% 与 341%。

Conclusion: 通过算法层面的仿射折叠替代逐元素反量化，可在保持数值精度的同时消除量化解码注意力的指令瓶颈，在 H100/B200 上取得显著延迟与吞吐收益，并已开源为内核库。

Abstract: Large-language-model decode attention streams the key-value (KV) cache for every generated token. Packing that cache into 2-bit or 4-bit quantized integers reduces memory traffic but does not automatically translate into latency saving. Existing 2-bit fused kernels reach under 31%/15% of peak HBM throughput on H100/B200. Converting each value and applying its scale and offset before feeding Tensor Cores is a major bottleneck because instruction-issue throughput is scarce on modern GPUs. Instead of applying scale and offset to each KV value, FOSCAR uses affine folding to apply them to the other operand or the output of decode-attention matrix products, depending on the quantization scheme (token-wise or channel-wise). This reduces scale-and-offset operations by up to two orders of magnitude while preserving the dequantized attention output in exact arithmetic. FOSCAR also uses bit manipulation to feed KV operands to Tensor Cores efficiently, requiring only 1 and 0.19 ALU instructions per INT2 element on H100 and B200, respectively. Finally, FOSCAR builds warp-specialized GPU pipelines for Hopper (H100) and Blackwell (B200) using wgmma and tcgen05. On H100, FOSCAR INT2 and INT4 kernels achieve geo-mean speedups of $3.36\times$ and $2.83\times$ over FlashInfer BF16, and $2.03\times$ and $1.71\times$ over TensorRT-LLM FP8. On B200, the corresponding INT2 and INT4 speedups are $2.67\times$ and $2.30\times$ over FlashInfer BF16, $1.48\times$ and $1.28\times$ over TensorRT-LLM FP8, and $1.16\times$ and $1.00\times$ over TensorRT-LLM NVFP4. We integrate FOSCAR into SGLang to benchmark serving efficiency. On long-context serving workloads, FOSCAR increases peak aggregate throughput by up to 170% and 341% over 2-bit and 16-bit baselines, respectively. We open-source FOSCAR as a kernel library at https://github.com/FutureMLS-Lab/FOSCAR.

</details>


### [357] [What to Delegate, What to Preserve: Human Preferences for Agentic AI Across Everyday Life and Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09730)
*Sarah Wheeler,Veronica Rammouz,Yaoyao Qian,Zexuan Liu,Mengyi Wei,Anthony Rios,Tianzhi He,Kyrie Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 一项针对289名有AI经验的美国成年人的情境调查研究（覆盖九大生活领域、45个用例）发现：AI代理可接受的任务委托边界随领域差异显著，信任、期望AI领导、既往使用经验和预期节省与更高的自动化偏好相关，而对个人成就感降低的担忧则降低自动化偏好；受访者普遍倾向"有限自主"，即在AI大量执行的同时保留人类监督或最终决定权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理已能在用户授权下完成多步骤任务，但"哪些任务、以何种方式"可委托给AI的边界仍不清晰。现有讨论往往把自动化视为单一维度，缺乏跨领域的实证证据来刻画用户对委托的接受边界与构成要素。

Method: 采用情境式问卷调查，招募289名具备AI使用经验的美国成年人，覆盖九大生活领域共45个用例；每位受访者每个领域评估一个情境，从而获得对自动化偏好、信任、协作、AI领导、隐私担忧与支付意愿的重复测量。分析上使用广义估计方程（GEE）模型考察各类因素与自动化偏好的关联，并结合开放式回答进行质性分析。

Result: 自动化偏好在不同领域间差异显著。GEE结果显示：对AI代理的信任、期望AI承担领导角色、任务相关的既往AI使用经验以及预期经济节省，均与更强的自动化偏好正相关；而"自动化会削弱个人成就感"的担忧则预测更低的自动化偏好。开放式回答揭示了"有限自主"偏好：受访者常支持由AI承担大量执行工作，但保留人类参与、监督或最终决定权。

Conclusion: 任务委托不宜被视为二元的"自动化与否"，而应理解为由执行、协作、领导与权威四个要素构成的配置。本文将"有边界的自主性"作为设计AI代理的核心原则，强调在提升自动化执行的同时保留人类监督与最终权威。

Abstract: AI agents can execute multi-step tasks on users' behalf, yet the boundaries of acceptable delegation remain unclear. We report a scenario-based survey of 289 U.S. adults with prior AI experience covering 45 use cases across nine life domains. Each respondent evaluated one scenario per domain, producing repeated measures of automation preference, trust, collaboration, AI leadership, privacy concern, and willingness to pay. Automation preferences varied substantially across domains. Generalized estimating equation models showed that greater trust in the AI agent, desired AI leadership, prior task-specific AI use, and anticipated financial savings were associated with greater automation preference. In contrast, concerns that automation would reduce personal fulfillment predicted lower automation preference. Open-ended responses further revealed a preference for bounded autonomy, with participants often favoring substantial AI execution while retaining human involvement, oversight, or final authority. We argue that delegation is best understood as a configuration of execution, collaboration, leadership, and authority.

</details>


### [358] [Continuous wavelet-based feature extraction for laser-induced breakdown spectroscopy classification of human plasma samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09746)
*Md Mahmudur Rahman,Atqiya Munawara Mahi,Nazim Ahmed Belabbaci,Mohammad Arif Ul Alam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究用小波变换从LIBS光谱中提取峰特征，结合SVM区分新冠阳性与阴性血浆样本，报告准确率约93%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂生物样本（如血浆）的LIBS光谱区分需要有效的判别特征提取方法，本文评估CWT用于谱峰提取的可行性。

Method: 181例血浆（53阳性、128阴性）以5 μL滴于硅基底干燥；1064 nm纳秒Nd:YAG激光采集，每样本100次单脉冲取平均并做强度离群剔除；CWT峰检测结合SVM分类，采用留一法评估。

Result: 准确率93.00%、灵敏度94.67%、特异度91.61%（作者称仍为初步结果，需与样本级预测核对）；关键特征包括Ca II、K I、N I发射线。

Conclusion: 该流程为考察两组血浆的光谱特征提供基础，但需独立验证并评估样本、基底与采集变异以确认泛化性与诊断价值。

Abstract: Extracting discriminative features from laser-induced breakdown spectroscopy (LIBS) spectra is important for the classification of complex biological samples. This study evaluates continuous wavelet transform (CWT)-based spectral peak extraction for distinguishing human plasma samples labeled as SARS-CoV-2-positive or negative. Plasma specimens from 181 individuals, comprising 53 positive samples and 128 negative controls, were deposited as 5 μL droplets on silicon substrates and dried before analysis. Spectra were acquired using a nanosecond Nd:YAG laser operating at 1064 nm, with 100 single-shot measurements collected per specimen and averaged after intensity-based outlier rejection. CWT-based peak detection was coupled with a support vector machine (SVM) classifier. The reported leave-one-out evaluation yielded an accuracy of 93.00%, sensitivity of 94.67%, and specificity of 91.61%; these estimates remain provisional pending reconciliation with specimen-level predictions. Features identified by the CWT pipeline included emission lines assigned to Ca II, K I, and N I. The workflow provides a basis for examining spectral features associated with the studied plasma groups. Independent validation and assessment of specimen, substrate, and acquisition-related variability are required to establish generalizability and diagnostic relevance.

</details>


### [359] [Assessing the Impact of Retrieval-Augmented Generation on Algorithmic Problem-Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09791)
*Gabriel Souza Baggio,Gabriel Machado Lunardi,Guilherme Medeiros Machado,Subhankar Maity,Faiza Belbachir,José Palazzo Moreira de Oliveira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于嵌入检索与相似度搜索的RAG方法，为大型语言模型提供相似竞赛编程题目作为示例，显著提升其在算法题上的通过率与Elo表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞赛编程问题不仅要求代码生成能力，还需高级算法推理，这对LLM构成独特挑战。作者希望借助检索相似题目的示例来增强模型解决此类问题的能力。

Method: 构建包含嵌入检索机制与相似度搜索算法的RAG框架，从知识库中找出相似题目，并采用动态调整阈值以减少检索噪声。在CodeContests数据集的测试集上进行三组对比实验：仅原始题目描述、补充随机训练样本、补充检索到的相似样本。另外引入Min-K成员推断分析，区分模型是靠检索实现泛化还是对预训练数据的隐式记忆。

Result: 以通过率（pass rate）和题目对应的CodeForces Elo评分为评价指标，实验结果显示基于相似度的检索能实质性提升LLM在竞赛编程任务中的表现，并通过Min-K分析深入揭示模型行为。

Conclusion: 相似度检索可有效增强LLM的算法问题求解与代码生成能力，提供了一种可扩展的框架，并辅以记忆性分析以更全面地理解模型表现。

Abstract: Competitive programming problems pose distinctive challenges for Large Language Models, as they demand not only proficient code generation but also advanced algorithmic reasoning. This study presents a Retrieval-Augmented Generation approach specifically designed to enhance the capacity of Large Language Models to address such problems. Our approach integrates an embedding-based retrieval mechanism and a similarity search algorithm to identify analogous problems within a knowledge database, employing a dynamically adjusted threshold to mitigate retrieval noise. We conducted an evaluation using the CodeContests dataset, focusing on the test split problems that exhibit similarity to instances from the training split. The experimental framework considered three conditions: (i) problems provided solely with their original descriptions, (ii) problems supplemented with randomly selected examples from the training split, and (iii) problems augmented with similar examples retrieved through the proposed method. In addition, we incorporated a Min-K membership inference analysis to examine whether the generated code solutions were influenced by potential memorization of training data within the evaluated Large Language Models. This complementary perspective enables a deeper understanding of model behavior by distinguishing genuine generalization through retrieval from implicit recall of pretraining samples. Performance was assessed using both pass rates and the CodeForces Elo rating of the problems. The results provide empirical evidence that similarity-based retrieval substantially enhances Large Language Models performance in competitive programming scenarios, offering an scalable framework for improving code generation in algorithmic problem-solving contexts.

</details>


### [360] [Knowing When to Intervene: Causal Evaluation of AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09792)
*Ayush Sawarni,Wenqian Xing,Tijana Zrnic,Ramesh Johari,Vasilis Syrgkanis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出首个方法论上严谨的框架，用于估计agent轨迹中随机干预的动态因果效应，并据此构建安全排行榜以指导审计优先级。


<details>
