  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有agentic AI评估依赖静态benchmark，无法反映部署时审计干预的价值。判断是否应在agent轨迹中途介入（验证输出或引导行为）本质是因果推断问题，但具有动态性挑战：干预效果依赖当前上下文、环境及agent未来行为。

Method: 构建评估轨迹内随机干预因果效应的框架；仅用少量随机干预（如验证、筛查）下收集的轨迹数据，估计一类丰富（上下文相关）干预策略的动态因果效应及其结构参数。

Result: 在tau-bench Retail、ToolSandbox、ImpossibleBench上实验，发现干预效应在不同agent动作与状态间差异显著；所估结构参数可构建反映各类agent动作风险的安全排行榜。

Conclusion: 工作指出因果推断与agentic评估交叉处的丰富研究议程，对AI agent可靠安全部署至关重要。

Abstract: Current evaluations of agentic AI systems primarily rely on benchmarks. However, the static nature of benchmarks makes them uninformative about the value of auditing interventions at deployment time. For reliable deployment, we often need to determine within an agent's trajectory whether or not to intervene, e.g., by verifying the agent's current output or steering it toward different behavior, using either human review or more costly models or time-consuming agentic supervision. The value of such interventions is fundamentally a causal inference question, asking whether the causal effect of such an intervention is beneficial for the final outcome. Estimating this causal effect is particularly challenging due to the dynamic nature of the problem: the impact of an intervention depends on the current context and environment, as well as the agent's future behavior. We develop the first methodologically grounded framework for evaluating the causal effects of randomized interventions within agent trajectories. By leveraging a relatively small dataset of agent trajectories collected under randomized interventions such as verification or screening, we can estimate the dynamic causal effect of a rich class of (contextual) intervention policies. The structural parameters that we estimate can also be used to construct safety leaderboards that capture the risk of various agentic actions, which can be used by practitioners for audit prioritization. Applying our methods to tau-bench Retail, ToolSandbox, and ImpossibleBench, we find that intervention effects vary substantially across agent actions and states. More broadly, our work suggests a rich research agenda at the intersection of causal inference and agentic evaluation, which we believe is essential for the reliable and safe deployment of AI agents in the real world.

</details>


### [361] [Test-Time Compute Scales Better with Parallel Agent Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09798)
*Junlin Wang,Haozhe Chen,Ang Li,Rahul Chalamala,Zhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"并行智能体团队"(parallel agent teams)：并行采样多个小团队并取最优团队结果，在12个长时序任务上优于Best-of-N和单个大团队，且性价比更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展可提升固定模型性能。现有两种智能体计算扩展方式各有缺陷：Best-of-N独立采样很快饱和；单个大团队协作虽得分更高，但规模增大后停止提升。作者希望兼得两者优势。

Method: 并行采样多个小型智能体团队，让各团队内部协作，最后保留表现最好的团队结果。在GPU内核、形式证明、漏洞利用开发等12个长时序任务上评测，使用27B模型、100个智能体预算，并与Best-of-N及单个100人团队对比。同时用简单统计模型解释原因。

Result: 10人并行团队在12个任务中的10个上胜过并行智能体采样；在运行过大规模团队的4个任务上，全部胜过单个100人团队。在相同美元成本下，并行团队在一半任务上超过前沿模型。统计模型显示：独立智能体均值低，单个大团队无方差增益，并行小团队兼顾高均值与方差增益。

Conclusion: 并行智能体团队是更有效的测试时计算扩展方式，通过在高均值与方差收益之间取得平衡来提升性能。

Abstract: Test-time scaling improves the performance of a fixed model by spending more computation at inference. There are two common ways to spend it on agents when a verifier can score every attempt. Best-of-N sampling runs many independent agents and keeps the best result but saturates quickly. One large team of collaborating agents can score higher but stops improving as it grows. Can we get the best of both? Our method, parallel agent teams, does so by sampling many small teams in parallel and keeping the best team's result. We test it on 12 long-horizon tasks from GPU kernels and formal proofs to exploit development. With a 27B model and a budget of 100 agents, parallel teams of ten beat parallel agents on 10 of 12 tasks and beat one team of 100 on all four tasks where we ran such large teams. At equal dollar cost the same parallel teams beat a frontier model on half the tasks. A simple statistical model shows that individual agents lose because their many samples have a low mean and that one large team loses because a single sample gets no gain from variance. Sampling many small teams in parallel balances a high mean with the gain from variance. Parallel agent teams thus offer a more effective way to scale test-time compute.

</details>


### [362] [Generative Latent Priors for Interpreting and Steering Protein Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09818)
*Daniel Hinjos,Lucía Urcelay*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将生成式隐先验（GLP）扩散模型迁移到ESM-2激活（8M与650M），既作为蛋白表征的可解释性透镜，也作为编辑先验进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流大模型可解释方法依赖强结构假设；GLP作为互补方案，直接学习冻结模型激活的分布而无需假设。作者希望验证GLP在蛋白质语言模型上是否兼具"透镜"和"编辑先验"双重价值。

Method: 在UniRef50上训练GLP拟合ESM-2-8M/650M的激活；以单GLP"元神经元"作探针，对比InterPLM发布的SAE特征及连续属性回归；并做证伪测试；再用GLP作编辑先验，对DiffMean引导三个生化属性做流形上校正，对多轮解码后序列重新嵌入评估结构合理性，并补充SAE钳制结构引导实验。

Result: 生成质量随深度与算力平滑提升；两个宿主上单GLP元神经元均为Swiss-Prot离散概念最强探针，优于InterPLM的SAE特征；连续属性上8M领先但650M不领先；证伪测试表明优势主要源于"集中化"而非新信息。作为编辑先验，流形上校正使DiffMean在三个生化属性上有效并恢复流形性，但仅限孤立情形；多轮解码后重嵌入的序列结构合理性受损，SAE钳制结构引导中也仅Helix基序成功。

Conclusion: GLP是有竞争力的可解释透镜（尤其在离散概念上），但作为编辑先验在自然性与结构合理性上失败；失败归因于重写覆盖度与解码器而非先验本身，明确了其在"编辑发现"与"探索发现"前必须解决的问题。已公开训练好的GLP模型。

Abstract: Dominant methods for interpreting large models impose strong structural assumptions. Generative Latent Priors (GLP) are a complementary approach: a diffusion model that learns the distribution of a frozen model's activations directly, imposing no assumptions. We port GLP to ESM-2 activations (UniRef50) and evaluate it at two host scales (ESM-2-8M and -650M), both as a lens for protein representations and as a prior for editing them. Generation quality scales smoothly with depth and compute. As a lens, single GLP "meta-neurons" are the strongest probes of discrete Swiss-Prot concepts at both hosts, beating InterPLM's released SAE features, and lead on continuous properties at 8M though not at 650M; falsification tests show this is mostly concentration, not new information. As an editing prior, the on-manifold correction makes DiffMean steering of three biochemical properties effective and restores on-manifoldness, but only in isolation: re-embedding the sequences actually produced after several rounds of decoding, the structural plausibility is compromised. This is mainly reproduced in additional SAE-based clamping structural steering experiments, where only Helix motif succeeds. The naturalness failure is traced to the rewriting coverage and the decoder rather than the prior, highlighting what the method must clear before it can serve for both discovery-by-editing and discovery-by-exploration. Trained GLPs are released on huggingface.co/collections/HPAI-BSC/glprot.

</details>


### [363] [Can Agentic Systems Close the Gap in Radiology with Production AI Tools?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09836)
*Aubin Charley,Julien Khlaut,Charles Corbière,Pierre Manceron,Corentin Dancette,Léo Machado*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出标准化的放射学智能体框架，用FDA获批设备工具目录模拟报告生成，量化工具可用性与编排能力对全覆盖报告的互补制约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射学AI分两条路线：专科模型窄而准、通用模型广但易幻觉与测量不准。用智能体系统结合二者可通向全面报告，但已发表系统局限于单一模态或部位。

Method: 定义标准化框架，规范编排器调用模块化工具生成结构化报告；在多模态报告语料上模拟生成，工具输出取自参考报告以隔离工具误差。设置"已获批设备目录"与"无限目录"两种条件，规划输入分别为临床指征或目标报告；并将漏检归因分类。

Result: 仅用获批目录+临床指征时，编排器仅回收不到三分之一的临床可操作发现；无限目录显著提升，基于目标报告规划进一步提升。漏检主因是获批设备未覆盖该模态的类别，约三分之一属"已提供却未调用"。补充最常缺失工具可弥合大部分差距，较小编排器即使有无限目录也丧失全部收益。

Conclusion: 工具可用性与有效编排是全面AI辅助放射报告的两个互补约束，二者缺一不可。

Abstract: Radiology AI has developed along two paths: specialized models that perform well on narrow tasks but cover few clinical findings, and generalist models that address a broader range of tasks but remain prone to hallucination and inaccurate measurements. Combining the two through agentic systems offers a route towards comprehensive radiology reporting, but published systems are tied to one modality or body part. We describe a radiology agentic framework that standardizes how an orchestrator calls modular tools to produce a structured report, and use it to measure what such a system can do with the tools from FDA-cleared radiology AI devices. Report generation is simulated on a large multi-modality report corpus, with the orchestrator given either a catalog of finding classes consolidated from the cleared devices, each available on the modalities it is cleared for, or an unlimited catalog in which any class can be requested, and planning either from the clinical indication or from the target report. Tool outputs are simulated from the reference report, so the comparison isolates tool availability and orchestration from tool error. With the cleared catalog and the indication alone, the orchestrator recovers under a third of the clinically actionable findings; recovery improves substantially with the unlimited catalog, and further when the plan is written from the target report. Attributing every missed finding to a cause shows that most are classes no cleared device offers for the study's modality, and that a third are classes or tools that were offered and not called. Adding the most frequently missing tools closes most of the gap to the unlimited catalog on held-out reports, whereas a smaller orchestrator with the unlimited catalog gives up the whole gain. Tool availability and effective orchestration are thus complementary constraints on comprehensive AI-assisted radiology reporting.

</details>


### [364] [Ensemble Methodology for Reference-Free Confidence Estimation in LLM-based Code Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09846)
*Marco Colombo,Balaji A Jayaram,Sowmyakumari Selvaraj,Sumit Tokkar,Animesh Kumar,Aravinda Prasad Sistla,Ravishankar Shetty,Rigel Gjomemo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种无需人工参考摘要即可评估LLM生成COBOL代码摘要正确性的方法：利用多模型间语义一致性作为真实性信号，并用LLM作为评判者进行成对比较，再通过校准聚合规则整合证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COBOL等遗留语言的代码摘要缺乏人工撰写的参考摘要，传统依赖参考文本或字符串/嵌入相似度的评估手段受限，因此需要一个可扩展、无需人工参考的正确性估计方案。

Method: 三步流程：(i) 由多个不同模型在受控条件下生成摘要；(ii) 由LLM评估器对摘要两两比较并输出结构化判断；(iii) 采用校准的聚合规则，合并支持性证据，仅对已被相互印证的矛盾进行惩罚。

Result: 摘要未给出具体量化结果或数据集指标，仅通过用例说明该方法的可行性与流程设计。

Conclusion: 模型间语义一致性可作为真实性的有效代理，配合LLM成对评判与校准聚合，可在无参考场景下估计COBOL摘要正确性，为遗留代码场景的LLM输出质量评估提供参考。

Abstract: This paper describes an approach and use case to estimate the correctness of LLM-generated COBOL summaries without access to human-authored references. The core intuition is that inter-model agreement on semantics is informative about truth, and that an LLM-as-judge can provide higher-fidelity pairwise assessments than traditional string- or embedding-based metrics. We operationalise this intuition with (i) controlled summary generation from diverse models; (ii) a LLM evaluator that compares pairs of summaries and emits structured judgments; and (iii) a calibrated aggregation rule that combines supportive evidence while penalising only corroborated contradictions.

</details>


### [365] [Flow-Guided Target-Space Alignment via Path Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09863)
*Ruizhi Yuan,Zeqiu Yu,Wei Gao,Wei Chen,Xiwei Tang,Lu Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlowAlign：将条件流匹配从推理时传输模型改造为训练时的对齐信号，用于把源模态映射到固定目标模态空间，同时保持目标分布全局结构与配对样本级对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态对齐常需映射到由目标模态直接定义、并用于下游分析的固定空间。现有目标函数各有所缺：对比损失只关心相对相似性，分布匹配只改善边缘一致性，逐点回归忽视目标空间路径几何，难以同时兼顾全局结构与实例级对应。

Method: 提出 FlowAlign，复用条件流匹配学到的流场作为训练期对齐引导：用该流场指导源侧表示与固定目标空间对齐，使源表示既保持配对对应又与目标空间全局结构相容。理论上从端点精度与局部目标空间几何两方面分析其改善配对对应的机制。

Result: 在合成数据、配对单细胞 multiome 基准及图文对齐实验中，FlowAlign 提升了实例级对应精度，同时在多种模态设置下保持目标空间结构。

Conclusion: 将流匹配用作训练时对齐信号是兼顾实例级对应与目标空间结构的有效方案，适用多种源–目标模态对齐场景。

Abstract: Many multimodal alignment problems require mapping a source modality into a fixed space defined by a target modality, where the target space is not merely an intermediate embedding but is used directly for downstream analysis. In this setting, successful alignment should preserve both the global structure of the target distribution and the instance-level correspondence observed in paired source--target training data. Existing objectives typically address only part of this goal: contrastive losses encourage relative similarity, distribution-matching losses improve marginal agreement, and pointwise regression fits each source sample to its paired target without explicitly modeling target-space path geometry. We propose FlowAlign, a flow-guided target-space alignment method that repurposes conditional flow matching as a training-time alignment signal rather than an inference-time transport model. FlowAlign uses the learned flow field to guide the alignment of source-side representations with the fixed target space, encouraging them to preserve paired correspondence while remaining compatible with the global target-space structure. We further provide theoretical analysis showing how FlowAlign helps improve paired correspondence by controlling endpoint accuracy and capturing local target-space geometry. Experiments on synthetic data, paired single-cell multiome benchmarks, and image--text alignment demonstrate that FlowAlign improves instance level correspondence while preserving target-space structure across different modality settings.

</details>


### [366] [On the Reliability of Majority-Vote Pseudo-Labels in Test-Time Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09872)
*Haoyu Wang,Ru Wang,Yuanyuan Wang,Jiaxian Guo,Mingming Gong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对TTRL中多数投票伪标签的可靠性问题，提出Prompt-Conditional Distributionally Robust DPO (PC-Dr.DPO)，通过基于投票可靠性的鲁棒重加权与缓存机制，在30个设置中的24个达到或超越最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时强化学习(TTRL)用自生成伪标签将预训练语言模型适配到无标签目标域，但其伪标签可靠性理解不足；作者希望厘清多数投票监督的误差来源，并缓解其中可处理的部分。

Method: 将多数投票监督的误差分解为总体多数偏置(population-majority bias)与依赖提示的有限rollout波动(finite-rollout variation)。前者无外部监督通常不可识别，故聚焦后者下游影响；提出PC-Dr.DPO，将提示内的鲁棒重加权以观测到的投票可靠性为条件，使采样敏感提示的更新更保守；并引入缓存机制减少在线适配中的自监督遗忘。

Result: 在六个数学与知识密集型推理基准、五种模型骨干上，PC-Dr.DPO在30个设置中的24个达到或超过最强TTRL基线。

Conclusion: 多数投票偏置不可识别但有限rollout波动可缓解；提示条件化鲁棒DPO结合缓存机制是提升TTRL可靠性的有效途径。

Abstract: Test-time reinforcement learning (TTRL) adapts pretrained language models to unlabeled target-domain data using self-generated pseudo-labels, whose reliability remains poorly understood. We analyze majority-vote supervision for TTRL with online Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and decompose its error into population-majority bias and prompt-dependent finite-rollout variation. Because population-majority bias is generally unidentifiable without external supervision, we focus on mitigating the downstream effects of finite-rollout variation. We propose \emph{Prompt-Conditional Distributionally Robust DPO (PC-Dr.DPO)}, which conditions within-prompt robust reweighting on observed vote reliability to make updates from sampling-sensitive prompts more conservative. We further introduce a cache-based mechanism to reduce self-supervised forgetting during online adaptation. Across six mathematical and knowledge-intensive reasoning benchmarks and five model backbones, PC-Dr.DPO matches or outperforms the strongest TTRL baseline in 24 of 30 settings.

</details>


### [367] [CPU-ARC: Agentic Reasoning Caching for on-CPU Reasoning Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09891)
*Peter Belcak,Greg Heinrich,Saurav Muralidharan,Shizhe Diao,Pavlo Molchanov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出「智能体推理缓存（ARCs）」，从LLM推理轨迹中构建可CPU执行的推理缓存，将「解题」与「执行解法」解耦，在新问题实例上只需执行已推理好的方案，性能与前沿LLM持平，成本降低16-291倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM不断「重复造轮子」：它们把任务求解与解法的执行混为一谈，导致每个问题都必须从零重新推理，效率低且成本高。作者认为是时候把「推理一次」与「反复使用解法」分离开来。

Method: agentic reasoning caching：从LLM的推理轨迹（reasoning traces）中提取并构建鲁棒的、可在CPU上直接执行的智能体推理缓存（agentic reasoning caches, ARCs）。一旦某任务被推理通，其解法可被转换为可执行程序，应用于新的问题实例时几乎无需语言模型参与。

Result: 在一系列困难的数学与科学任务上，ARCs与前沿LLM性能相当（on-par）；同时完全可解释、可持续训练、对输入扰动鲁棒，调用成本仅为源模型的1/16至1/291。

Conclusion: 将LLM推理产物编译为可执行缓存是提升效率的有效范式：推理一次、执行多次，兼顾性能、可解释性、鲁棒性与极低成本。

Abstract: Large language models reinvent the wheel all the time. They conflate task-solving with the execution of the solutions, which forces them to solve every problem from scratch. We show that once a task has been reasoned through, the application of its solution to a new problem instance can be done very efficiently and with little involvement from language models. We introduce agentic reasoning caching, a method for constructing robust CPU-executable agentic reasoning caches (ARCs) from LLM reasoning traces. ARCs show on-par performance with frontier LLMs across a range of difficult mathematical and scientific tasks, are fully explainable, continually trainable, robust to input perturbations, and 16-291x cheaper to invoke than the source model.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [368] [LLM-Augmented Flow for Project Conformation and Task Complexity Evaluation Based on the TLM Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08931)
*Maksim Sysoev,Alexander Semenov,Ilia Cheshev,Alexey Gevorkyan,Danil Somov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于LLM智能体工具的需求文档生成与任务复杂度打分框架（依据Tran等2004模型），并在金融科技真实商业项目上做可行性研究：用LLM辅助完成Discovery阶段并自动推导故事点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM技术快速发展，引发一个核心问题：Discovery阶段（尤其HLD/LLD，含PRD/BRD/FSD等需求文档产物）能否大部分由LLM工具完成，并据此更精确地推导故事点（SP）。

Method: 构建一个结构化框架，用于规范需求文档生成并打分任务复杂度；框架基于Tran、Lévesque与Meunier(2004)提出的模型，由智能体（agentic）工具执行；在金融科技领域的一个真实商业项目上应用该框架。

Result: 作为可行性研究（非验证性研究）呈现。因未记录所考察任务的实际开发工作量，所以得到的复杂度分数仅作为该工具的"输出结果"报告，而非经验证的工作量估计。

Conclusion: 该框架在真实项目中可运行，具备可行性，但缺少开发工作量数据，无法验证复杂度分数的准确性；未来需补充真实工作量对照以完成验证。

Abstract: The software development process, from inception to a working product or solution, can be divided into two major phases: discovery (Discovery) and delivery (Delivery). The Discovery is further subdivided into High-Level Discovery (HLD) and Low-Level Discovery (LLD). During HLD, management identifies the business solution or product to be integrated into the company's infrastructure. LLD, in turn, produces requirement documentation artifacts, including the Product Requirements Document (PRD), Business Requirements Document (BRD), and Functional Specifications Document (FSD). Story Points (SP) are a widely used modern approach to measuring the effort required to accomplish predetermined tasks, and the artifacts listed above help derive SP more precisely. With the rapid growth of LLM technologies, one of the main questions that arises is whether the whole Discovery phase, or most of it, can be completed with the help of LLM tools. In this paper, we test a framework that structures requirement documentation generation and task complexity scoring, based on the model introduced by (Tran, Lévesque, and Meunier 2004) and executed with the help of agentic tools. The approach is applied to a real-world commercial project in the fintech domain as a feasibility study rather than a validation: development effort was not recorded for the tasks examined, and the resulting complexity scores are therefore reported as the output of the instrument rather than as validated estimates of effort.

</details>


### [369] [DRAFT-Bench: Evaluating Frontier Agents Across the Professional Design Lifecycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08933)
*Rong Pan,Jianqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出DRAFT-Bench，从设计生命周期角度评测通用智能体在需求挖掘、创意探索、受约束编辑、迭代修改与生产交付上的能力。比较Codex/Astra与Claude Code在35份设计简报、43种条件下以及40场景RFQ任务上的表现，发现结构完成度高但语义合规存在违规，需区分执行、需求符合与专业验收三个层次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测多聚焦单点任务完成，缺乏对真实设计工作中跨生命周期能力（意图理解、创意探索、修订执行、交付准备）的系统评估。

Method: 构建DRAFT-Bench，采用Requirement-to-Artifact框架将演进需求、受保护属性、产物版本和交付义务关联；在35份设计简报×43条件下对比Astra与Claude Code，另设40场景RFQ赛道，并进行结构性与语义性检查。

Result: Astra完成43/43设计条件，Claude完成35/43；所有计分核心运行均获结构满分，但28个共同计分核心条件中语义检查显示Astra 11例、Claude 5例违规（属模型判断，非专家确认）。RFQ覆盖率高但成功排序随队列变化；SKU扩展、修订依赖与白墨文件暴露义务缺失、输入不足与评估歧义。

Conclusion: 应将执行完成度、需求合规性与专业验收区分开来；赛道未配对、工具不同，不能据此推断RFQ到交付的成功、工厂验收或纯粹的模型排名。

Abstract: Can frontier general-purpose agents understand client intent, explore creative directions, execute revisions, and prepare production deliverables? We introduce DRAFT-Bench (Design Reliability Across Feedback and Tasks), a lifecycle-oriented evaluation of requirement elicitation, creative exploration, constrained editing, iterative revision, and production handoff. Its Requirement-to-Artifact framework links evolving requirements, protected properties, artifact versions, and delivery obligations. We compare native Codex/Astra and Claude Code across 35 design briefs under 43 conditions, alongside a separate 40-scenario RFQ track. Astra completes 43/43 design conditions and Claude 35/43. All scored core runs receive full structural credit, yet on the 28 core conditions scored for both systems, semantic checks flag violations in 11 for Astra and 5 for Claude; these are model judgments, not expert-confirmed failure rates. RFQ coverage is high, but success ordering changes across cohorts. SKU expansion, revision dependencies, and White ink files expose ambiguity among unmet obligations, missing inputs, and evaluator interpretations. These findings separate execution, requirement compliance, and professional acceptance. Unpaired tracks and different tools preclude claims of RFQ-to-delivery success, factory acceptance, or a pure model ranking.

</details>


### [370] [MongoBench: Do LLMs Know What a MongoDB Query Will Do?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09065)
*Qibai Chen,Haoting Qiu,Yezhou Cheng,Mengxiao Qian*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出MongoBench基准，专门评测LLM智能体在执行MongoDB find()查询前预测其结果（返回匹配_id集合）的能力，而非仅看查询是否合法或答案是否正确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测只看查询是否有效、最终答案是否正确，无法判断智能体是否真正理解查询的实际语义后果；这种"后果预测"能力需要独立评测。

Method: 构建300条MongoBench题项，每条组合3-4个文档化的MongoDB匹配陷阱，由三家厂商模型对称提案以便按来源分层；以真实mongod服务器产生ground truth；评测四个模型（三家前沿系统+一个开源推理模型），并进行失败模式分析、规则表注入实验和委托执行（tool delegation）审计。

Result: 准确率70.1%–92.1%（22个百分点差距）；每个出题模型对自己所写题项误判10%–31%；错误更易由所涉语义陷阱（范围比较、null）预测，而非文档数量或规则数等表层指标；注入验证规则表效果不均；委托执行虽能给出可靠匹配集合，但某一模型在平均28%的题项上其执行过滤未通过规范检查，并错答约22题，其余三模型全部通过。

Conclusion: 后果预测能力应作为独立评测维度；能写查询不等同于能预测其效果，且执行委托需额外校验。基准、mongod oracle与评测代码已开源。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly operate databases on their own: they generate queries and run them against live data. Two checks are commonly used to judge such an agent - whether it produces a valid query, and whether it reaches the right final answer - but neither tells us whether the agent knows what its query will actually do before it runs. We argue that this consequence prediction ability must be evaluated in its own right, and we build MongoBench to measure it: given a collection of documents and a MongoDB find() filter, a model must return the exact set of matching _ids, with ground truth from a real mongod server. The 300 items each compose three or four documented matching pitfalls and are proposed symmetrically by three vendors' models, so results stratify by item source. Evaluating four models - frontier systems from three vendors and one open-weight reasoner - accuracy ranges from 70.1% to 92.1%, a 22-point spread. Each proposer mispredicts 10-31% of the items it wrote itself, so producing a query does not imply predicting its effect. A failure-mode analysis shows errors are better predicted by which semantic pitfall a query exercises - range comparisons and null - than by surface measures such as document count or the number of composed rules. Prepending a verified rule sheet helps unevenly; executing the original filter against the engine yields reliable match sets, but delegation must be checked: on 28% of items (averaged over three runs) one model's executed filters never passed a canonical check against the intended query, and it answered about 22 of them wrong, while the other three passed on every item. We release the benchmark, the mongod oracle, and the evaluation code with all prompts at https://github.com/research-repo-bai/MongoBench.

</details>


### [371] [When Routine Operations Break Architectural Pattern Relationships in LLM Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09088)
*Boming Xia,Qinghua Lu,Dino Sejdinovic,Liming Zhu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文研究LLM agent harness中架构模式关系在运行时如何被操作（重装插件、保存规则等）静默破坏，提出PatternOps评估方法，并应用于5个harness、3种模式关系、14个独立插件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent harness把工具中介、状态管理和安全护栏分散到原生服务、独立插件与配置中。日常运维操作可能在无代码变更的情况下改变运行顺序或保留状态，从而破坏架构模式所依赖的关系，但现有实践缺乏对此类运行时破坏的系统性评估手段。

Method: 提出PatternOps评估流程：从模式描述、provider文档和部署配置中提取需求，追踪到实现这些需求的provider及做决策的记录，再在受支持的操作下比较不同的责任分配方案。研究对象为5个已发布的harness中Guardrail Enforcement Point、Autonomy Controller、Blackboard三种模式的五种关系，主样本为14个独立编写的插件。

Result: 这些操作会静默破坏关系：插件被重新应用或allow规则保存后，所有插件仍处于激活状态，但策略不再评估编辑，导致一条被禁止的编辑得以执行。哪类替代方案合格取决于具体需求：能维持所需顺序、或保持/重建所需状态的分配在其测试路径和操作序列上成立，而其他方案虽能阻止禁止效果，却漏掉了在别处被拒绝的编辑。

Conclusion: 在这些harness中，模式关系是运行系统及其历史的属性，而非静态代码属性。架构师应基于其部署实际使用的决策路径、状态生命周期和操作序列来评估模式关系。

Abstract: LLM agent harnesses distribute tool mediation, state management and safety guardrails across native services, independently written plugins and configuration. Routine operations can change the runtime order or retained state that architectural pattern relationships require, without any code change. We study five relationships associated with the Guardrail Enforcement Point, Autonomy Controller and Blackboard patterns across five released harnesses, analysing a main sample of fourteen independently written plugins. Using PatternOps, our assessment procedure, we trace requirements from pattern descriptions, provider documentation and deployment profiles to the providers that realise them and the records that decide them. We then compare alternative allocations under supported operations. Such operations broke relationships silently: after a plugin was reapplied or an allow rule saved, policy stopped evaluating edits while every plugin stayed active, and a prohibited edit executed. Which alternatives qualified depended on the requirement. Allocations that kept the required order, or kept or rebuilt the needed state, held on their tested paths and operation sequences, while others prevented prohibited effects but missed edits rejected elsewhere. In these harnesses, a pattern relationship is a property of the running system and its history, so architects should evaluate it against the decision paths, state lifetimes and operation sequences their deployments use.

</details>


### [372] [GreyZone: Production-quality Agentic Application Threats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09161)
*Ruoyu Zhou,Shuyao Jiang,Haifeng Ruan,Abhik Roychoudhury*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出GreyZone基准，用于在真实开源agent应用环境中评估自动化红队服务生成并交付攻击的能力，并以Promptfoo在Letta Code和Clawith上的两个案例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent在编码、银行、电商等领域广泛部署，能调用工具并改变应用状态，安全测试对可靠部署至关重要；但现有工作多聚焦受攻击的subject agent（如AgentDojo），缺乏基于生产级agent环境、评估红队服务本身的基准。

Method: 选取独立开发的、具备访问权限、机密性和操作授权安全边界的开源agent应用；暴露包括多agent交互与多模态输入在内的多样攻击面；保留生产相关workflow，并对攻击结果进行独立验证；以Promptfoo在Letta Code与Clawith上开展两个初步案例研究，考察攻击交付、agent行为与验证结果。

Result: 给出两个案例研究的初步结果，展示攻击交付过程、agent行为表现及经独立验证的安全策略违规结果；同时发布包含各应用subject agent与攻击面的基准。

Conclusion: GreyZone以真实应用环境为依托、以验证性违规为评估标准，填补了红队服务评测基准的空白，并公开基准以支持agent安全测试方法研究。

Abstract: Agents are increasingly deployed across domains such as coding, banking, and commerce. Their ability to invoke tools and change application state makes security testing important for reliable deployment. Although agent security has been studied through various adversarial testing approaches, benchmarks grounded in production agent environments remain limited. We introduce GreyZone, a benchmark for evaluating automated red-teaming services against real-world agent applications. Unlike benchmarks such as AgentDojo, which primarily evaluate subject agents under attack, GreyZone evaluates services' ability to generate and deliver attacks that cause verified security-policy violations. We select independently developed, open-source agent applications with security boundaries concerning access permissions, confidentiality, and action authorization. These applications expose diverse attack surfaces, including those arising from multi-agent interactions and multimodal inputs. By grounding evaluation in application environments that preserve production-relevant workflows and independently verifying outcomes, GreyZone supports research on automated agent security testing. Specifically, we present two preliminary case studies using Promptfoo on Letta Code and Clawith to examine attack delivery, agent behavior, and verified outcomes. We release the benchmark, including subject agents and attack surfaces for each application, to support further research on agent testing methods.

</details>


### [373] [On the Limits of Execution Traces for Repairing Assertion Failures in LLM-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09212)
*Muntasir Adnan,Carlos C. N. Kuhn*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出断点调试（BPD）：用运行时变量值对失败程序进行行内标注后反馈给LLM修复，从信息视角论证断言失败的反馈信息量小，并在BigCodeBench上以26.7%胜22.4%超越重采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迭代修复后残留失败多为断言失败（BigCodeBench 79%、HumanEval 全部、LiveCodeBench 93%），但断言失败几乎不指示故障所在，反馈信息量不足。

Method: 提出BPD：将失败程序连同其变量运行时值以内联注释形式返回给LLM；在迭代修复5次仍失败的任务上，比较BPD与重采样各再花5次生成的修复效果，并分析只追踪首个失败测试、追踪全部失败测试及token开销。

Result: BigCodeBench上BPD修复26.7%对重采样22.4%，且优势随模型规模增大；未改动代码重提交从28%降至8%。HumanEval上BPD略优，LiveCodeBench上重采样反优（6.5%对4.9%）；仅用首个失败测试的追踪即优于全追踪且token更少；LiveCodeBench即便公开测试也无收益。

Conclusion: 结果符合信息视角：trace只在测试暴露失败输入且故障为实现疏漏时有帮助；否则修复循环应重采样而非追踪。

Abstract: Iterative repair returns a failed program's error to a large language model (LLM) and asks it to fix it. After iterative repair, the remaining failures are mostly assertion failures: with four Qwen2.5 models, they make up 79% of residual failures on BigCodeBench, all on HumanEval and 93% on LiveCodeBench. We argue, through an information view of the feedback, that an assertion failure says little about where the fault lies. We propose Breakpoint Debugging (BPD), which returns the failed program annotated inline with its variables' runtime values. We take the tasks that iterative repair failed to fix with five repair attempts and compare two ways of spending five further generations on them: BPD, and resampling. On BigCodeBench, BPD repairs 26.7% of these tasks against 22.4%, and its advantage grows with model size. On HumanEval, BPD outperforms resampling but only slightly, and on LiveCodeBench, fresh sampling repairs more (6.5% against 4.9%). Furthermore, BPD reduces unchanged-code resubmissions from 28% to 8% on BigCodeBench. Using only the first failing test's trace, it outperforms tracing all failing tests while using fewer tokens than both all-trace BPD and plain iterative repair. On LiveCodeBench, BPD gives no gain even on the public tests, so a visible failing input is not enough for a trace to help. These results match the information view: a trace helps only when the test exposes a failing input, and the fault is an implementation slip; otherwise, a repair loop should resample rather than trace.

</details>


### [374] [BOXBOX: Recursive Harness Self-Improvement for Long-Running Performance Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09218)
*Yuhao Tan,Hanyun Jiang,Fangkai Yang,Yuan Yao,Taolue Chen,Bo Qiao,Yu Kang,Lu Wang,Pu Zhao,Xiaoxing Ma,Qingwei Lin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出长时性能优化基准 PerfRace 与可在线热更新的 Agent 框架 BOXBOX，使智能体能在任务进行中递归改进自身 harness，取得 1.70x Speedup@4h，优于最强基线 1.54x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线 harness 优化依赖参考答案，为留出任务做静态优化，但真实任务多为开放式，长时运行还会暴露新困难，离线优化后的 harness 难以应对。因此需研究智能体能否在任务进行中利用任务特定经验递归改进 harness。

Method: 构建 PerfRace 基准，用于软件项目的长时间性能优化；提出 BOXBOX，支持智能体触发的 harness 修订与内核级热加载，同时保留执行上下文。在匹配测试时间预算下与 Frozen Harness、离线 Harness Evolution、Harness Scaling 对比。

Result: BOXBOX 在 PerfRace 上以更高的任务时间与模型成本效率取得更大加速，Speedup@4h 为 1.70x，最强基线为 1.54x。

Conclusion: 在线、任务内的 harness 自我修订优于离线优化与静态 harness，是一种更适配开放式长时任务的智能体优化范式。

Abstract: Automated harness optimization improves agents by learning from trajectories and task feedback. Existing offline methods use reference answers to optimize harnesses for reuse on held-out tasks. However, many real-world tasks are open-ended, while long runs expose new difficulties that offline-optimized harnesses may not address. To study harness optimization in these settings, we introduce PerfRace, a benchmark for long-running performance optimization of software projects, and investigate whether agents can use task-specific experience to recursively improve their harnesses during an ongoing task. To this end, we introduce BOXBOX, which enables agent-triggered harness revision and kernel-managed hot reloading while preserving execution context. We evaluate BOXBOX on PerfRace under matched test-time budgets, comparing it with Frozen Harness, offline Harness Evolution, and Harness Scaling. BOXBOX achieves higher speedup with more efficient use of task time and model cost, reaching a Speedup@4h of 1.70x, compared with 1.54x for the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [375] [Integrating MBSE Principles with AI to Improve the Quality of Requirements in SMEs Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09234)
*Iryna Volosheniuk,Claude Baron,Jean-Michel Bruel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向中小企业(SME)的轻量级MBSE方法，聚焦需求细化、可追溯性与早期V&amp;V，结合AI辅助分析改进需求质量，并通过工业案例验证可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MBSE虽能改善系统工程实践（需求管理、可追溯性、早期验证与确认），但对中小企业而言落地困难，因其缺乏适配自身规模与约束的结构化流程和解决方案。

Method: 将MBSE原则与AI辅助分析相结合，构建轻量级MBSE方法，保持与现有工具和实践的兼容；开发概念验证(proof of concept)并在工业案例中应用评估。

Result: 结果表明可在SME情境下支持早期V&amp;V活动，同时提升跨工程制品的可追溯性。

Conclusion: 轻量级、集成化的MBSE方法有助于中小企业渐进式采纳MBSE，从而提升整体工程效率。

Abstract: Model-Based Systems Engineering (MBSE) is a promising approach to improve systems engineering practices, particularly by enhancing requirement management, traceability, and early validation and verification (V\&V). However, its adoption remains challenging, especially for small and medium-sized enterprises (SMEs), which often lack structured processes and solutions adapted to their scale and constraints. Our goal is to propose a lightweight MBSE methodology tailored to the needs and constraints of SMEs. This paper focuses specifically on requirements refinement, traceability, and early V\&V. The approach integrates MBSE principles with artificial intelligence-assisted analysis to improve requirements quality while remaining compatible with existing tools and practices. A proof of concept is developed and applied in an industrial case study to evaluate the feasibility of the approach. The results demonstrate how early V\&V activities can be supported within an SME context, while improving traceability across engineering artifacts. These findings highlight the potential of a lightweight, integrated MBSE approach to support the progressive adoption of MBSE in SMEs, thereby improving overall engineering efficiency.

</details>


### [376] [Navigating Complexity: A User-Centered Evaluation of Project Management Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09260)
*Yasin Ghadimi Zaker,Hamid Reza Ghaeini*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究采用混合方法（以SUS量表为核心，141名专业人员问卷）实证评估Jira、Trello、Monday.com、Asana与Microsoft Project的UX，发现功能性与可用性存在显著权衡：Trello得分最高(77.5)，Microsoft Project最低(51.2)，最优工具高度依赖团队规模与项目复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字化转型中企业依赖专用软件管理复杂项目，但工具选型往往重功能与成本、轻视用户体验，导致采用率低、用户受挫与项目延误，亟需实证证据支撑以UX为导向的选型决策。

Method: 采用混合方法研究：以系统可用性量表（SUS）为主要量化工具，通过在线问卷收集141名有经验的专业人员数据，对五款主流项目管理工具在不同项目规模下的UX进行对比评估，并结合统计分析考察团队规模与项目复杂度对UX结果的影响。

Result: 发现功能性与可用性之间存在显著权衡。Trello的SUS得分最高（77.5），在小型项目与敏捷团队中体验最佳；Microsoft Project最低（51.2），尽管功能强大但可用性挑战明显；Jira表现均衡，功能强且复杂度可控。统计分析确认最优工具选择高度依赖情境，团队规模与项目复杂度影响UX产出。

Conclusion: UX并非次要特性，而是软件采纳与项目成效的关键成功因素。研究提供了将工具选型与项目情境相匹配的循证框架，为从业者提供可操作建议，并为软件开发者指出设计改进机会。

Abstract: In the age of digital transformation, companies are increasingly relying on specialized software to manage the complexity of projects. However, when selecting tools, features and costs often take precedence over user experience (UX), leading to low adoption rates, user frustration, and project delays. This study empirically evaluates the user experience of leading project management tools (Jira, Trello, Monday.com, Asana, and Microsoft Project) across various project scales using a mixed methods approach. The System Usability Scale (SUS) was used as the primary quantitative instrument, with data collected from 141 experienced professionals via online questionnaires. The results reveal a significant trade-off between functionality and usability. Trello achieved the highest SUS score (77.5), indicating a superior user experience for small projects and agile teams, while Microsoft Project scored the lowest (51.2), suggesting significant usability challenges despite its robust features. Jira demonstrated a balanced profile, offering powerful features with manageable complexity. Statistical analyses confirmed that the choice of the optimal tool is highly context dependent, with team size and project complexity influencing UX outcomes. The findings underscore that UX is not a secondary feature but a critical success factor for software acceptance and project effectiveness. This study provides an evidence-based framework for aligning tool selection with the project context, offers practitioners actionable insights, and identifies design opportunities for software developers.

</details>


### [377] [Wake 5: A Python Framework for Solidity Testing, Fuzzing, and Static Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09307)
*Josef Gattermayer,Michal Převrátil*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Wake 5 是开源 Python 智能合约测试框架，用进程内 EVM 替代 JSON-RPC 提升吞吐；论文证明有状态模糊测试的瓶颈是输入生成先验而非吞吐，LLM 智能体结合约束求解器可解完所有 153 个断言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能合约模糊测试器的对比研究往往混淆了吞吐量与输入构造设计两个维度，缺少对"到底什么才是限制有状态模糊测试效果的因素"的清晰定位。

Method: 提出 Wake 5：以进程内 revm 取代外部节点 JSON-RPC 连接，并通过 MCP 服务器将静态分析暴露给语言模型智能体。在 Daedaluzz 迷宫基准（153 个可达断言、守卫常量 < 2^32）上对比 Foundry、Echidna，做八小时长跑实验与生成器概率分布分析；另由 LLM 智能体配合约束求解器自动编写 harness，并在生产级 Go 代码库审计中做静态分析工具消融实验。

Result: Wake 5 达 29,931 笔交易/秒，与 Foundry（30,186）相当，是 Echidna（2,600）的 11.5 倍。但八小时内三者断言命中数中位数仅 75/71/70，并集 93。Wake 的整数生成器有 51% 概率质量落在 2^32 以下，说明浅层断言耗尽后瓶颈是生成器先验而非吞吐。LLM 智能体编写的 harness 在五次 Wake 运行中均达成 153/153，耗时 33–79 分钟。审计消融显示静态分析工具对三个模型的召回率均无提升、精确率全部提升、LLM 成本最多增加 12.7%。

Conclusion: 有状态模糊测试的约束来自输入生成先验，而非执行速度；LLM 智能体加约束求解可显著超越高速模糊器。论文还讨论了相关预言机与非确定性完成问题，并主张模糊测试器的角色正从"搜索"转向"执行"。

Abstract: Smart-contract fuzzers differ by an order of magnitude in throughput and by design in how they construct inputs, yet published comparisons rarely separate the two. We present Wake 5, an open-source Python framework for Solidity testing, fuzzing and static analysis, and use it to locate the binding constraint of stateful smart-contract fuzzing. Wake 5 replaces the JSON-RPC connection to an external node with an in-process EVM (revm) and exposes its static analysis to language-model agents through a Model Context Protocol (MCP) server. On the Daedaluzz maze benchmark (153 reachable assertions, guard constants below 2^32), Wake 5 executes 29,931 transactions per second, on par with Foundry (30,186) and 11.5x faster than Echidna (2,600). In eight-hour campaigns, however, the three tools plateau at medians of 75, 71 and 70 assertions, and their union reaches 93. We show that Wake's integer generator places 51% of its probability mass below 2^32 and that, once shallow assertions are exhausted, the generator prior rather than throughput bounds every tool. A harness written by a language-model agent, which derives inputs with a constraint solver, reached all 153 assertions in each of five campaigns executed by Wake, in 33 to 79 minutes. A complementary ablation in an agentic audit of a production Go codebase found that static-analysis tools improved recall for none of three models, raised precision for all three, and increased language-model cost by up to 12.7%. We discuss correlated oracles and non-deterministic completion, and argue that the fuzzer's role is shifting from search to execution.

</details>


### [378] [comfort-db: A Python toolkit to access, preprocess and explore the oceanographic COMFORT dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09353)
*Yvonne Jenniges,Are Olsen,Boris Peter Koch,Sebastian Maneth*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: comfort-db 是一个 Python 库，用于便捷访问和处理 COMFORT v2 SQLite 海洋观测数据集（含十个数据集、1772–2020 年共 4.58 亿条现场测量），并提供预处理、单位转换、网格化与探索性分析功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COMFORT 数据集规模庞大（4.58 亿条测量、跨 250 余年），直接操作 SQLite 数据库对用户门槛高，缺乏统一的加载、清洗、网格化和分析工具，因此需要一个专门的封装库降低使用难度。

Method: 以 Python 库形式封装 SQLite 数据库，提供：变量缩放与基于空间/时间/质量的筛选；加载时或通过 converter 对象进行单位转换；支持网格化并可选结合水深数据剔除陆地网格；提供绘图函数、交互式 Dash 应用以及海洋剖面和水团统计计算等探索性分析功能。

Result: 实现了开源的 comfort-db 库（MIT 许可），可从 PyPI 和 GitHub 获取，覆盖数据访问、预处理、网格化与通用及字段特定的探索性分析能力。

Conclusion: comfort-db 为 COMFORT 海洋观测数据集提供了易用的一体化工具链，显著简化了大规模海洋数据的获取、预处理与分析流程，并以开源形式发布便于社区使用与扩展。

Abstract: comfort-db is a Python library that facilitates working with the COMFORT SQLite dataset, version 2. The dataset assembles global oceanographic measurements from ten datasets and contains a total of 458,724,734 in situ measurements from the years 1772 to 2020. comfort-db enables easy access to the database and provides preprocessing and exploratory analysis utilities. Preprocessing supports variable scaling and filtering by space, time and quality. Unit conversions can be applied during data loading or separately via a converter object. Gridding is supported and optionally works with bathymetry data to exclude grid cells on land. Moreover, the library supports general and field-specific exploratory data analysis through plotting functions, an interactive Dash app and functions to compute oceanographic profile and water mass statistics. comfort-db is open source (MIT license) and available from PyPI and GitHub.

</details>


### [379] [Tracybot: Preserving Developer Intent by Tracing Agent-Generated Code to Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09395)
*Răzvan Albu,Aliaksei Khval,Maxine Orlova,Danis Music,Filipe Araújo Veras Rosa,Lirong Yi,Ranim Khojah*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Tracybot 是一个开源工具，集成 VS Code、Git 和编码智能体，将 LLM 辅助开发中产生代码的对话（Tasklet）与行级代码变更关联，并可导出为决策记录，以保留设计意图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 编码智能体在开发中做出越来越多设计决策，但这些决策及意图仅存在于开发者的对话中，与所产生的代码没有任何关联，导致意图和决策信息流失。

Method: 采用设计科学研究，经两次迭代开发出与 VS Code、Git 和编码智能体集成的工具 Tracybot。其核心概念为 Tasklet：由可选的规划提示加上产出代码的构建提示组成。功能包括 (i) 将智能体的行级代码变更映射到产生它的 Tasklet，(ii) 在需要时（如涉及架构决策）将 Tasklet 导出为决策记录。

Result: 通过焦点小组（7 人）和问卷（24 人）评估可用性与易用性：参与者认为 Tracybot 对代码审查等活动有用，多数认为 Tasklet 的设计与呈现能清晰表达开发者意图。

Conclusion: Tracybot 能将对话中的决策与代码变更显式关联并支持决策记录导出，被用户感知为有用且易理解，为 LLM 辅助开发中的意图追踪提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are now widely used in software development, and LLM-based coding agents are given increasing autonomy. With coding agents, many design decisions are made in the conversation between the developer and the agent. Typically, the developer starts a conversation with an agent and states their intent, the agent proposes options if needed, and the developer plans the change accordingly. Then, the resulting code is committed. However, in such scenarios, the decisions and the intent behind the changes remain in the conversation, with no link to the code they produced. In this paper, we present Tracybot, an open-source tool that integrates with VS Code, Git, and coding agents to keep track of relevant conversations in an LLM-assisted development process. Tracybot captures each change as a Tasklet which consists of optional planning prompts used to plan a change, together with a build prompt that produces it. Tracybot has two main features: (i) it maps line-level code changes made by coding agents to the Tasklets that produced them, and (ii) it allows the developer to export a Tasklet as a decision record when needed, for example when the decision concerns the architecture of the project. We developed the tool over two iterations of a design science study, while focusing the evaluation on the usability and user-friendliness of the tool. In a focus group (7 participants) and a survey (24 participants), we found that Tracybot was perceived as useful for activities like code review, and most participants found our Tasklet design and presentation to be a clear way to represent the developer's intent. Tool: https://github.com/TracyTeam/tracybot. Video: https://youtu.be/Vg_a1F5eO04.

</details>


### [380] [Deliberate Pacing for Human Oversight in Agentic Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09550)
*Tobias Reichel,Michèle Stassen,Anamaria Mojica-Hanke,Maria Teresa Baldassarre,Sebastian Baltes,Alexander Serebrenik,Steffen Herbold*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"刻意节奏"（deliberate pacing）愿景：有意调节自主软件开发推进的时机与方式，以保留有意义的人类监督机会，并给出动因与研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI 使开发过程可在无持续人类参与下推进，但开发者能否以及何时进行有意义的监督成为关键问题；完全的持续自主与频繁人工干预都并非普适可行。

Method: 采用探索性多声部文献综述（multivocal literature review），分析 20 篇来源，归纳出驱动该愿景的三类因素。

Result: 识别出三大因素：人类参与相对成本的变化、监督机会出现的时机、以及软件生命周期中各节奏决策之间的相互依赖。

Conclusion: 节奏应在"理解并影响关键决策的机会"与"人类注意力、开发时间和返工成本"之间取得平衡；并提出研究议程：何时刻意节奏有益、干预时机如何影响监督效果、节奏决策如何跨开发活动相互作用。

Abstract: Agentic AI increasingly enables software development activities to proceed without continuous human involvement. While this autonomy may accelerate development, it also raises questions about when developers can meaningfully oversee software development done by agents. We propose deliberate pacing: intentionally adjusting when and how autonomous development proceeds to preserve opportunities for meaningful oversight. Drawing on an exploratory multivocal literature review of 20 sources, we identify three factors motivating this vision: changing relative costs of human involvement, the timing of oversight opportunities, and interdependencies between pacing decisions across the software development life cycle. We argue that neither uninterrupted autonomy nor frequent human intervention is universal. Instead, pacing should balance opportunities to understand and influence consequential decisions against costs in human attention, development time, and rework. We outline a research agenda to investigate when deliberate pacing is beneficial, how intervention timing affects oversight, and how pacing decisions interact across development activities.

</details>


### [381] [TensorMutation: Learning Weight-Space Mutations of Deep Learning Models on Neural Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09650)
*Zaheed Ahmed,Philip Makedonski,Jens Grabowski*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TensorMutation 将训练好的模型表示为神经图，用突变条件 GNN 离线学习并预测预训练突变算子引起的参数变化，从而无需重训练即可生成可执行突变体，部分复现真实故障效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习突变测试存在"成本—真实性"权衡：预训练式突变产生真实但计算昂贵的突变体，而现有训练后算子虽高效却不够贴近真实故障。本文质疑该权衡是否必然，探究能否从已训练模型中直接预测故障引导式预训练突变算子的权重空间影响。

Method: 构建含25,252个原始—突变体对的语料（71个MLP分类器、11个故障引导预训练算子、含25个新整理对象）；TensorMutation把模型转为神经图，用突变条件GNN预测参数变化，GNN离线训练后可跨未见模型复用，无需重训练即生成可执行突变体，并引入直接权重空间监督。

Result: 在9个留出对象上部分复现预训练突变的行为效应，直接权重空间监督显著提升权重空间保真度；在12个真实故障对象上获得最高中位行为相似度，相对DeepMutation++的提升统计显著（Holm校正p=0.018），与DeepCrime及范数匹配随机对照的差异不显著。

Conclusion: 故障引导预训练突变在权重空间中留下可学习且可迁移到未见模型的结构，说明成本—真实性权衡可被部分打破。

Abstract: Mutation testing for deep learning (DL) faces a cost-realism trade-off. The pre-training approach produces realistic but computationally expensive mutants, whereas existing post-training operators modify trained models efficiently but produce mutants that less closely resemble real faults. This study asks whether the trade-off is necessary: can the weight-space effect of a fault-informed pre-training mutation operator be predicted from a trained model, without retraining it? We answer this question with TensorMutation, which represents a trained model as a neural graph and uses a mutation-conditioned graph neural network (GNN) to predict parameter changes. The GNN is trained offline and reused across unseen models so that TensorMutation can generate executable mutants without retraining. We construct a corpus of 25,252 original-mutant pairs from 71 multilayer perceptron (MLP) classifiers using 11 fault-informed pre-training operators, including 25 newly curated subjects. TensorMutation partially reproduces the behavioral effects of pre-training mutations on nine held-out subjects, and direct weight-space supervision significantly improves weight-space fidelity. On 12 real-fault subjects, TensorMutation achieves the highest median behavioral similarity among the evaluated approaches. The improvement over the post-training tool DeepMutation++ is statistically significant (Holm-adjusted p=0.018), whereas the differences from the pre-training tool DeepCrime and the norm-matched random control are not significant. These results show that fault-informed pre-training mutations leave learnable structure in weight space that transfers to unseen models.

</details>


### [382] [QAOA for Layered Architecture Recovery: Feasibility, Limits, and the Role of Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09744)
*Julien Cardinal,Andrew Chedid,Imen Benzarti,Ghizlane El Boussaidi,Christophe Pere,Jean-Marc Robert*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文将分层架构恢复(LAR)建模为QUBO问题并用QAOA求解，发现小规模单块QAOA可行，24量子位失效，而影响引导分解可将最优率从6.7%提升至93.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LAR传统上被定义为组合优化问题并由经典搜索求解，作者希望探究量子近似优化算法(QAOA)能否应用于LAR，以及其实际局限性。

Method: 将LAR表述为二次半指派问题(Quadratic Semi-Assignment Problem)，转化为每个包必须恰好分到一层的惩罚式QUBO，并编码为Ising哈密顿量由QAOA求解；对超出单电路量子比特预算的实例，采用影响引导的影响引导分解，将问题分解为一系列较小子问题求解。

Result: 在三个真实Java系统(15、18、24量子位)上评估：单块QAOA在两个较小系统上可行且准确，但在24量子位实例上失败；准确率主要由采样预算而非电路深度决定；与Tabu Search基线相比有竞争力但未超越；分解可将24量子位实例最优率从6.7%提升到93.3%，其准确率取决于子问题规模，且与单块管线不同，随采样预算减少反而提升。

Conclusion: 在当前含噪中等规模(NISQ)阶段，将QAOA应用于软件架构恢复的关键在于问题分解，而非更大电路或更多采样。

Abstract: Layered architecture recovery (LAR) reconstructs a system's architectural layers from its package dependencies, and is defined as a combinatorial optimisation problem solved with classical search. We investigate whether the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) can be applied to LAR, and with what practical limitations. We formulate LAR as a Quadratic Semi-Assignment Problem, transform it into a Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimisation (QUBO) problem in which each package is penalised unless it is assigned to exactly one layer, and encode it as an Ising Hamiltonian solved by QAOA. To reach instances beyond the qubit budget of a single circuit, we also apply an impact-guided decomposition that solves the problem as a sequence of smaller sub-problems. We evaluate the approach on three real Java systems ($15$, $18$, and $24$ qubits), studying the effect of circuit depth and sampling budget (RQ1), a comparison with a classical Tabu Search baseline (RQ2), robustness to simulated quantum noise (RQ3), and the decomposition (RQ4). We find that monolithic QAOA is feasible and accurate on the two smaller systems but fails on the $24$-qubit instance, that the sampling budget rather than circuit depth governs its accuracy, and that it is competitive with but does not outperform Tabu Search. Decomposition restores reliable recovery on the $24$-qubit instance, raising the optimal rate from $6.7\%$ to $93.3\%$; its accuracy is governed by the size of each sub-problem and, unlike the monolithic pipeline, improves as the sampling budget is reduced. Our results indicate that, in the current noisy intermediate-scale regime, problem decomposition, rather than larger circuits or more sampling, is the key to applying QAOA to software architecture recovery.

</details>


### [383] [AnimWeb-Bench: Benchmarking Animated Webpage Code Generation from Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09769)
*Zhongyi Zhang,Jingyu Xiao,Shuqing Li,Michael R. Lyu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出首个从视频生成动画网页代码的基准 AnimWeb-Bench，含124个真实网页、7类应用域、33种动画类型，并提出"静态外观保真度+动画保真度"二维评测协议，评测14个MLLM与3个编码代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web动画极为普遍（CSS animation 属性出现在超四分之三网页上），但现有网页代码生成基准仅针对静态截图、用户触发交互或孤立动效，MLLM与编码代理重建完整动画网页的能力尚未被系统评测。

Method: 构建 AnimWeb-Bench：124个真实网页，覆盖7个应用域、33种动画类型（91.9%页面含多种动画），每页配参考视频与原始静态资源。提出与人类对齐的二维评测协议：静态外观保真度在关键帧上衡量结构、文字、颜色与视觉相似度；动画保真度跨帧追踪UI组件，比较位置、尺寸、速度与外观。

Result: 评测14个MLLM和3个编码代理，发现：(1)复现网页动画显著难于复现静态外观；(2)显式帧检查与渲染反馈可提升编码代理的重建质量；(3)快速连续变化比离散状态切换更难复现。

Conclusion: 填补动画网页代码生成评测空白，揭示动画重建的核心难点与改进方向（帧检查、渲染反馈），代码与数据集已公开。

Abstract: Animation is pervasive on the Web, the CSS animation property alone appears on over three-quarters of webpages. Yet existing webpage code generation benchmarks target static screenshots, user-triggered interactions, or isolated motion graphics, leaving the ability of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and coding agents to reconstruct complete animated webpages largely unexplored. To fill this gap, we introduce AnimWeb-Bench, the first benchmark for animated webpage code generation from videos. It comprises 124 real-world webpages spanning 7 application domains and 33 animation types, with 91.9% of pages combining multiple types, each paired with a reference video and the static assets of the original webpage. We further propose a human-aligned, two-dimensional evaluation protocol: Static Appearance Fidelity measures structure, text, color, and visual similarity on aligned keyframes, while Animation Fidelity tracks UI components across frames and compares their position, size, velocity, and appearance with those in the reference video. Evaluating 14 MLLMs and three coding agents reveals: (1) reproducing webpage animations is substantially more difficult than reproducing static appearance; (2) explicit frame inspection and render feedback improve the reconstruction quality of coding agents; and (3) rapid, continuous changes are harder to reproduce than discrete state transitions. Our code and dataset are available at https://starry1001.github.io/AnimWeb-Bench-website/ .

</details>


### [384] [When Instruction Files Go Stale: Documentation Debt and Hidden Cost in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09802)
*Tural Mehtiyev,Biruk Tadesse,Lawrence Arkoh,Wesley Assunção*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究仓库指令文件（如 AGENTS.md/CLAUDE.md）的"过期文档债"对 LLM 编码智能体的影响：用 3 条真实过期指令×3 个版本×22 个维护任务，分析 732 条轨迹发现智能体会盲目遵循过期指引并付出额外成本，但从不修复指令文件本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AGENTS.md、CLAUDE.md 等仓库指令文件在每个会话中被读取，如同所有文档一样会随代码演进而过时（如测试目录迁移而文件仍指向旧路径）。这种"文档债"的利息是后续每次会话的额外成本，而仅以任务成功率衡量的评估会掩盖这一成本，导致过期文件长期存在。作者因此追问：是什么让智能体遵循过期指令？代价多大？能否恢复？

Method: 从活跃开源仓库中深选 3 条真实过期指令，分别代表三类指引：测试命令、代码位置、添加组件的流程。每条指令设三种版本（保持过期、改为当前正确路径、删除），代码与任务固定不变，共 22 个维护任务；分析两个模型共 732 条轨迹，细粒度到单次工具调用层面。

Result: 当过期指引与任务相关时智能体会遵循它，即便别处已有正确信息。在过期测试命令场景下，Claude Sonnet 5.5 每次运行多出约 2 次工具调用（比修正版多约三分之一）；另两类场景未检测到显著差异。智能体通常能从即时失败中恢复，但均未修复底层指令文件。修正指令可消除对废弃路径的操作，删除指令可阻止大部分此类操作。

Conclusion: 仅看任务成功率会掩盖文档债的成本，评估智能体应超越最终结果、关注过程行为。同时，智能体缺乏主动修复过期指令文件的能力/行为，需要专门机制来维护指令文件。

Abstract: LLM-based coding agents such as Claude Code are guided by repository instruction files (also called context files or agent configuration files) like AGENTS.md and CLAUDE.md, which are read in every agent session. As any documentation artifact, these files can fall behind the code and become stale (e.g., a test folder moves, but the file still points to the old one). In technical debt terms, such a stale instruction is documentation debt, and its interest is the cost it adds to every later agent session. Evaluations that check only task success can miss this cost, and the stale file can persist. In this study, we ask what leads agents to act on a stale instruction, how it affects their behavior and at what cost, and how they recover. We study three real-world stale instructions from active open-source repositories in depth, each a different kind of guidance: a test command, a code location, and a procedure for adding a component. Agents fix 22 maintenance tasks under three versions of each instruction (left stale, corrected to the current path, and removed), with the code and the task held fixed, and we analyze 732 trajectories of two models at the level of individual tool calls. We find that agents follow stale guidance when it is relevant to their tasks, sometimes even when correct information is available elsewhere. On the stale test command, Claude Sonnet 5.5 needed about two extra tool calls per run, a third more than with the corrected command; in the other cases, we could not detect a difference. Although agents generally recovered from the immediate failures, none repaired the underlying instruction file. Correcting the instructions eliminated operations on obsolete paths, while removing them prevented most. Our findings show that task success alone can hide the costs of documentation debt, highlighting the need to evaluate agent behavior beyond final outcomes.

</details>


### [385] [Agents on the Patch Path: Mining AI-Associated Security Remediation in GitHub Security Advisories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09808)
*Claudio Segal,Paulo Segal,Hudson Silva Borges,Paulo Silveira,Daniel S. Menasché,Eduardo Santana de Almeida,Felipe de Sant'Anna Paixão,Joanna C. S. Santos,Anton Kocheturov,Gaurav Srivastava,Raphael Machado*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: We mine local Git history of GHSA-referenced commits to find evidence of coding-agent involvement, link it to 469 candidate advisories across 189 repositories, and decompose review-timing differences into disclosure-route composition vs. within-route effects.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Autonomous coding agents are increasingly used in software development, but their role in real-world security remediation is poorly understood—especially whether agent-assisted fixes flow through vulnerability review pipelines differently.

Method: A local-Git mining pipeline detecting agent evidence via known agent accounts, explicit commit-message attribution, repository instruction files, and agent configuration artifacts; findings are linked to commits referenced by reviewed GHSAs. Timing analysis uses an exact route–timing decomposition to separate disclosure-route composition from within-route review lag, with comparisons against same-month advisories and a historical baseline.

Result: 469 valid candidate advisories across 189 repositories (448 with direct attribution, 21 with repository-level evidence only). Claude is the most observed tool family, then Copilot and Cursor. Candidate advisories show modestly longer mean review lag than same-month advisories, driven mainly by within-route timing; versus the historical baseline they show substantially shorter lags, with both route composition and within-route timing contributing. High- and critical-severity vulnerabilities are included.

Conclusion: Coding-agent involvement is already observable across hundreds of security advisories and repositories. Distinguishing direct attribution from repository-level adoption evidence, and separating route-composition from within-route timing effects, yields an empirical characterization of agent-associated remediation in the GHSA review pipeline.

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents are increasingly involved in software development, but their participation in real-world security remediation remains poorly understood. We investigate observable evidence of coding-agent involvement in commits referenced by reviewed GitHub Security Advisories (GHSAs), characterize the associated vulnerabilities, and examine review timing in relation to advisory disclosure routes.
  We develop a local-Git mining pipeline that identifies agent evidence through known agent accounts, explicit commit-message attribution, repository instruction files, and agent configuration artifacts. Linking these findings to GHSA-referenced commits yields 469 valid candidate advisories across 189 repositories, including 448 with direct attribution and 21 supported only by repository-level evidence. Claude is the most frequently observed tool family, followed by Copilot and Cursor.
  We further compare review timing against other advisories and historical baselines using an exact route--timing decomposition. Relative to advisories published in the same months, candidate advisories exhibit a modestly longer mean review lag, primarily associated with within-route timing rather than disclosure-route composition. Compared with a historical baseline, they exhibit substantially shorter mean review lags, with both route composition and within-route timing contributing to the difference.
  These findings demonstrate that coding-agent involvement is already observable across hundreds of security advisories and repositories, including high- and critical-severity vulnerabilities. By distinguishing direct attribution from repository-level adoption evidence and separating route-composition effects from within-route timing differences, we provide an empirical characterization of agent-associated remediation in the GHSA review pipeline.

</details>


### [386] [An Empirical Study on Clustering-based Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm for Regression Test Case Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09869)
*Antonio Trovato,Martin Beseda,Dario Di Nucci*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文扩展评估了量子方法QAOA-TCS在回归测试用例选择问题上的性能，与经典和多目标/单目标前沿方法对比，并考察其对量子噪声的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理想回归测试需重跑全部测试套件，但计算开销过大不可行，因此需要测试用例选择等优化方法。现有经典方法（Additional Greedy、DIV-GA）计算成本高，量子方法（SelectQA、BootQA、IGDec-QAOA）又受当前量子硬件物理限制。

Method: 在回归测试用例选择问题上使用QAOA-TCS，进行全面性能评估：与经典（Additional Greedy、DIV-GA）和量子（SelectQA、BootQA、IGDec-QAOA）前沿方法对比，并在不同噪声水平下测试其鲁棒性，分别考察多目标与单目标两种建模。

Result: QAOA-TCS在多数情况下优于现有多目标方法，且在高噪声下仍保持稳定；单目标建模下各QAOA类方法表现相当。多目标场景下量子退火方法SelectQA对大规模问题最高效；单目标场景下，在真实噪声模拟器上运行时QAOA-TCS在效果与效率间取得最佳折中。

Conclusion: QAOA-TCS是多目标测试用例选择的有竞争力方案且抗噪性好，单目标下QAOA类方法表现相当，SelectQA在大规模多目标问题上效率最优。但该类分析受各算法运行计算环境不一致的限制，结论需谨慎解读。

Abstract: Regression testing assesses whether software components that have already been developed and tested continue to work correctly after modifications. Ideally, regression testing re-executes the entire test suite after each modification, but this is infeasible in real-world scenarios because of the computational burden; therefore, optimized approaches, such as Test Case Selection, have been developed. The state of the art in regression testing optimization includes both classical approaches, such as Additional Greedy and DIV-GA, and quantum approaches, such as SelectQA, BootQA, and IGDec-QAOA. However, while the former incur high computational costs, the latter are constrained by the physical limitations of currently available quantum machines.
  This paper extends the evaluation of QAOA-TCS, our quantum approach for the test case selection problem. We thoroughly evaluate its performance, compare it with state-of-the-art classical and quantum approaches, and assess its robustness to quantum noise.
  The results show that QAOA-TCS outperforms state-of-the-art multi-objective approaches in most cases and maintains stable performance even under high noise levels. In the single-objective formulation, QAOA-based methods perform comparably. In terms of efficiency, our previous method based on quantum annealing, namely SelectQA, is the most efficient for large problems in the multi-objective formulation, whereas QAOA-TCS offers the best trade-off between effectiveness and efficiency in the single-objective formulation when run on realistic noisy simulators. However, such analysis is constrained by the different computational environments used to run the algorithms.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [387] [UniMore: Fine-Grained Supervision for Unified Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08996)
*Haoyue Bai,Yinyi Luo,Yicheng He,Qingsong Wen,Sharon Li,Jindong Wang*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 提出 UniMore 后训练方法，通过从图文对细粒度内容中构造互补的生成与理解任务，缓解统一多模态模型的"特异性损失"，在多类理解与生成基准上均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型虽整合理解与生成，但其输出存在"特异性损失"：生成图像只有正确物体却属性错误，描述只识别场景却缺少区分性细节。作者认为需要同时指明"要实现的视觉内容"和"用于区分的描述差异"的监督信号。

Method: 从图文对的细粒度内容中派生两项互补任务。生成路线：由对应短语预测有定位的图像裁剪，使条件与视觉目标范围匹配，并沿用原生视觉目标；理解路线：在完整图像条件下，既拟合正描述，又对省略或篡改细节的描述做排序对比。两条路线独立优化，学到的更新合并进同一统一模型，且不改变推理流程。

Result: 在多个理解与生成基准上均取得提升。

Conclusion: 从细粒度图文对中构造的接地生成与细节敏感的描述排序是有效的后训练监督，可同时增强统一模型的生成与理解特异性，且无需改动推理结构。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models combine image understanding and generation within one system, creating an opportunity to improve both capabilities through paired image--text supervision. We examine the outputs of a pretrained unified model in both directions and observe a loss of specificity: generated images can contain the requested objects with incorrect attributes, while captions identify a scene without expressing its distinguishing details. These failures call for supervision that specifies both the visual content to realize and the descriptive differences to distinguish. We propose UniMore, a post-training method that derives these complementary learning tasks from the fine-grained content of image--text pairs. The generation route predicts grounded crops from their corresponding phrases, matching the scope of the condition and visual target while using the native visual objective. The understanding route combines positive-caption fitting with ranking against captions that omit or alter details, conditioned on the full image. The two routes are optimized independently, and their learned updates are combined into a single unified model without changing its inference procedure. Experiments show gains across multiple understanding and generation benchmarks.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [388] [Fair Selection of Generalized Nash Equilibria: A Comparability Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09060)
*Sophie Hall,Heinrich H. Nax,Saverio Bolognani*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文指出传统变分广义纳什均衡(v-GNE)隐含假设了较强的基数单位可比性，并提出公平GNE(f-GNE)框架，通过显式优化与可比性匹配的公平指标来选择均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNE问题通常存在连续均衡集合，需要选取一个均衡，而该选择决定了共享约束成本如何在智能体间分配，常被期望是公平的。v-GNE因对所有人收取统一影子价格而被视为公平，但这一公平性假设缺乏理论审视。

Method: 借助社会选择理论中的"可比性"概念进行分析：证明GNE集本身无需智能体间成本可比，而v-GNE隐含假设了基数单位可比性（较强概念）。据此提出f-GNE，显式优化与当前可比性水平相匹配的公平指标，并给出单约束和线性二次博弈下的构造性求解方法。

Result: 揭示了v-GNE公平性主张的理论前提；建立了f-GNE框架并给出单约束与线性二次博弈的可计算方法；以电动汽车充电博弈作为贯穿全文的示例。

Conclusion: 均衡选择并非中性，v-GNE的公平性依赖于较强的可比性假设。f-GNE通过将公平指标与可比性对齐，提供了更严谨、可定制的公平均衡选择方案。

Abstract: Generalized Nash equilibrium (GNE) problems model self-interested agents coupled through their costs and shared constraints, and arise throughout control applications that allocate a scarce common resource. Such problems typically admit a continuum of equilibria, so any implementation must \emph{select} one. This selection is not neutral: it decides how the cost of the shared constraint is distributed among the agents, a decision often expected to be fair. The variational GNE (v-GNE), which charges all agents a uniform shadow price, is the conventional choice and is frequently advocated as fair. Through the lens of \emph{comparability} from social choice theory, we show that the GNE set itself requires no inter-agent comparability of costs, whereas the v-GNE implicitly assumes cardinal unit comparability, one of the stronger notions. We then introduce the fair GNE (f-GNE), which selects an equilibrium by explicitly optimizing a fairness metric matched to the comparability at hand, and give constructive routes to compute it for single-constraint and linear-quadratic games. An electric-vehicle charging game serves as a running example throughout this self-contained tutorial.

</details>


### [389] [Attaining Optimal Values under Sure Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09244)
*Takuto Yamauchi,Kenji Tei*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 研究有限随机博弈中硬规范（含概率0路径）下,有限控制器能否达到/逼近最优值的计算复杂度,并给出Muller硬条件下的最优有限实现内存上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 博弈中"每个低于值的阈值都有有限控制器达成,但值本身可能无有限控制器达成",需要对"给定阈值可达""未指定上确界可达""求一个精确值"三类数值问题给出精确复杂度分类,并弄清硬规范下的内存需求。

Method: Max用纯有限控制器,Min用纯公共历史策略;针对显式硬、布尔软Streett条件分析严格/非严格供给阈值问题;证明中保持值与有限可达性,并编码自适应查询而不计算未知子博弈值;内存界通过构造单一控制器从原sure region出发。

Result: 阈值问题coNP完全;未供给上确界可达性P^NP完全;单个精确约分式FP^NP完全(多项式度量归约);同样构造还给出普通随机Streett博弈(允许随机历史策略)的值比较与精确输出下界。Muller硬条件+[0,1]有理奖励下,用2^{O(n log(n+1))}Q(1+log R)个模式;Q、R联合依赖在固定n≥6、Q,R≥2时最坏情况紧。

Conclusion: 给出随机博弈硬规范数值问题的完整复杂度刻画及有限最优实现内存上界;关于竞技场规模的最优依赖与高效通用合成仍未解决。

Abstract: A game may have, for each target below its value, a finite controller meeting that target, yet no finite controller attaining the value itself. We study finite stochastic games with a hard specification that must hold on every supported execution, including paths of probability zero. Max uses a pure finite controller; Min may use any pure public-history strategy. We distinguish three numerical questions: can a supplied target be met, can the unspecified optimum be attained, and what is one exact value? For explicit hard and Boolean soft Streett conditions, the strict and non-strict supplied-threshold problems are coNP-complete, attainment of the unsupplied supremum is $\mathrm P^{\mathrm{NP}}$-complete, and one exact reduced fraction is $\mathrm{FP}^{\mathrm{NP}}$-complete under polynomial metric reductions. The proofs preserve both the value and its finite attainability when fixing a single opponent, and encode adaptive queries without computing unknown subgame values. The same construction yields matching value-comparison and exact-output lower bounds for ordinary stochastic Streett games with unrestricted randomized history strategies. We also bound the memory needed for optimal finite implementations for arbitrary Muller hard conditions and rational rewards in $[0,1]$ determined by infinitely recurring states. One controller is sure from the entire original sure region and attains at every finitely attained start, with no attainment premise on the other states. For $n$ states in that region it uses $2^{O(n\log(n+1))}Q(1+\log R)$ modes, where $Q$ is the product of the least common denominators of full original chance rows sourced in that sure region and $R$ clears all reward values. Its joint dependence on $Q$ and $R$ is worst-case sharp at each fixed $n\ge6$ when $Q,R\ge2$. Optimal dependence on arena size and efficient general synthesis are not resolved here.

</details>


### [390] [Optimal Regret for Profit Maximization in Bilateral Trade](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09558)
*Matteo Castiglioni,Anna Lunghi,Alberto Marchesi*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 研究重复双边贸易中，中间商在买卖双方估值来自未知联合分布、只能观察到是否成交的反馈下学习双侧定价，证明双侧学习在遗憾上并不比单侧更难，达到 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$ 遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典动态定价的 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$ 遗憾率是在一维（单侧）情形下得到的。双边贸易需要同时学习卖方接受价与买方支付价，且反馈仅为成交与否的二元信号，自然的问题是：从一维到二维是否引入了额外的学习困难（维度灾难）。

Method: 建模为重复双边贸易：中间商每轮向卖方报买入价、向买方报卖出价，双方估值来自未知联合分布，仅当双方都接受时成交，中间商赚取价差。算法在此仅观测成交与否（bandit 反馈）的条件下，针对最优固定价格对进行学习，并分析其遗憾上界。

Result: 设计了遗憾为 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$ 的算法，与最优固定价格对相比，在对数因子内匹配一维动态定价的经典下界，即二维情形未出现维度灾难。

Conclusion: 双侧市场学习在遗憾意义上与单侧一样容易，经典一维遗憾率可延续到二维，进而引出这一"无维度灾难"现象是否能推广到任意高维的开放问题。

Abstract: Learning to profit from trade requires learning both sides of a market: what a seller is willing to accept and what a buyer is willing to pay. We study repeated bilateral trade, where an intermediary posts a purchase price to a seller and a sale price to a buyer whose valuations are drawn from an unknown joint distribution. Trade occurs only if both parties accept their respective prices, in which case the intermediary earns the difference between the two prices. The intermediary observes only whether trade occurs, without observing either valuation. We show that learning both sides of the market is no harder, in terms of regret, than learning just one. Specifically, we design an algorithm achieving regret $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$ against the best fixed pair of prices, matching, up to logarithmic factors, the classical lower bound for one-dimensional dynamic pricing. Our result shows that the classical one-dimensional regret rate survives the transition to two dimensions, raising the question of whether this absence of a curse of dimensionality extends to arbitrarily high dimensions.

</details>


### [391] [Contextual Search and Bilateral Trade with a Trace-Square-Root Potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09615)
*Romain Cosson,Federico Fusco,Anupam Gupta,Stefano Leonardi,Renato Paes Leme,Matteo Russo*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 提出统一的"势函数"方法，用一位反馈同时解决上下文搜索与双边贸易，在贝叶斯设置和对抗性最坏情况下都取得阶数最优的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文搜索（contextual search）和双边贸易（bilateral trade）此前只得到 O(d log d) 和 O(d·6^d) 级别的遗憾保证，与已知下界差距很大，缺少统一、紧致的方法。

Method: 从贝叶斯视角出发，目标/估值由已知先验采样，决策者基于后验分布做预测；构造基于 Tr√Σ（协方差矩阵迹的平方根）的势函数，并取其"对偶"形式适配经典的对抗性最坏情况设置（目标与估值从单位欧氏球中被对手选取）。

Result: 贝叶斯上下文搜索总损失 O(Tr√Σ)；贝叶斯双边贸易遗憾 O(Tr√Σ_S + Tr√Σ_B)；对抗设置下两问题均达到紧的 O(√d) 遗憾，与该方向已有下界仅差常数因子。

Conclusion: 统一的势函数框架同时统一并显著改进了两个问题的理论保证，从 d log d / d·6^d 提升到紧的 √d 量级，达到阶数最优。

Abstract: We present a unified potential method for contextual search and bilateral trade with one-bit feedback and obtain order-tight algorithms for both problems. Our framework starts from a Bayesian perspective in which the unknown target (for contextual search) and the unknown pair of valuations (for bilateral trade) are sampled from a known prior distribution. In that model, the decision maker bases its prediction on the posterior distribution, obtained by conditioning on feedback received in previous rounds.
  For Bayesian contextual search, we present an algorithm with a total loss bounded by $O(\mathrm{Tr} \sqrt Σ)$, where $Σ$ is the covariance matrix of the target's prior; and for contextual bilateral trade, our algorithm's regret is bounded by $O(\mathrm{Tr} \sqrt{Σ_S}+\mathrm{Tr} \sqrt{Σ_B})$, where $Σ_S$ and $Σ_B$ are the covariance matrices of the seller's and buyer's valuations.
  We then use ideas from this Bayesian setting and adapt a ``dual'' of this trace-square-root potential to the classical worst-case setting, where the target and valuations are chosen adversarially from the unit Euclidean ball. For both contextual search and bilateral trade, we obtain tight $O(\sqrt d)$-regret algorithms. These results match the existing lower bounds up to a constant factor and improve on the previous guarantees of $O(d\log d)$ for contextual search and of $O(d6^d)$ for bilateral trade.

</details>


### [392] [Strategic Competition and Convergence in LLM Leaderboards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09780)
*Naveen Raman,Fei Fang,Yiling Chen*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 论文将排行榜竞争建模为博弈，分析LLM开发者何时走向能力“单一种植”（monoculture）、何时形成差异化优势，并用LM Arena和OpenRouter数据实证检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排行榜广受关注且影响模型开发者的资源投入，但竞争是否导致模型能力趋同（monoculture）或绩效趋同，尚缺乏系统分析。

Method: 将排行榜竞争建模为博弈：开发者在各能力维度上分配资源以最大化胜率；理论分析生产函数相似性、比较优势与边际收益递减的影响，并结合LM Arena与OpenRouter数据做实证。

Result: 理论表明相似生产函数导致相似能力画像，而比较优势可维持差异化；边际收益递减使竞争前沿日益拥挤。实证发现开发者会在落后品类上改进更多，同时保留品类特有优势；模型前沿愈发拥挤，模型使用模式日益多样化。

Conclusion: 竞争加剧未必产生可互换的模型，反而可以维持各具优势的竞争性模型。

Abstract: Leaderboards are a popular way to compare large language model (LLM) capabilities and can influence how model developers invest resources. We ask when competition on leaderboards leads developers to produce models with similar capability profiles, which we call monoculture, or similar performance levels. We study this by formulating leaderboard competition as a game in which model developers invest resources across capabilities to maximize their win rate. We show that similar production functions can induce similar capability profiles, while comparative advantage can sustain distinct capability profiles. We then study convergence in performance levels and show that diminishing returns can lead to an increasingly crowded competitive frontier. Using data from LM Arena and OpenRouter, we find that developers improve more in categories where they lag competitors, while still retaining category-specific advantages. At the same time, we find that the frontier of models becomes increasingly crowded, alongside increasingly diversified model usage patterns. Our results suggest that increasing competition does not need to produce interchangeable models, but can instead sustain competitive models with distinct strengths.

</details>
